### 325 subject events

- **1912.02** `vienna-life-birth-letter-from-the-dead/the-letter-is-real` tags: infancy, family, secret, letter, drama
  邮差把一封边角磨白的信塞进门缝，收信人是一个已经死了三年的名字。Josef Bauer把Klara放在膝上，拆开信，里面只有一句：“孩子像不像他？”Emil Weiss在门口探头，问是不是寄错了；Josef Bauer把信折回去，说“没有寄错”。
  _effects: flag +letter_from_dead_relative; knowledge “一封写给死去亲人的信问：孩子像不像他。”; mood -0.04000000000000001_

- **1912.07** `p22-comic-christening::ctx-vienna-1912::milieu-tenement/the-wrong-name` tags: infancy, family, christening, church, neighbor, comic, tender, drama
  神父念的是另一张纸条上的名字。Josef Bauer小声纠正，Klara正好吐出一口奶，落在登记簿边上；神父用袖子擦了擦，说这样也算盖过章了，只是纸角留下一小片湿痕。
  _effects: mood -0.009999999999999995; knowledge “洗礼时神父差点念错孩子的名字。”; flag +wrong_christening_name; cash -1 (a christening candle)_

- **1913.01** `vienna-life-infant-first-smile/smile-at-the-lamp` tags: infancy, family, first, warm
  Josef Bauer把Klara抱到窗边，煤汽灯罩上有一圈晃动的光。孩子盯着看了很久，忽然笑出声，口水滴在Josef Bauer的袖口上。屋里的人都停了一下，像是听见了什么了不起的消息。
  _effects: mood 0.04000000000000001; health 0.009999999999999898 (a good day)_

- **1913.02** `vienna-life-toddler-first-words/first-word-door` tags: infancy, family, first, comic
  门外有人上楼，Klara忽然指着门说：“爸爸。”Josef Bauer看了看空门口，没有纠正。那天晚上，孩子把家里每个人的名字都叫了一遍，叫错两个，却叫得很认真。
  _effects: skill; mood 0.020000000000000004; flag +first_word_door_

- **1913.12** `p22-warm-fathers-letter::ctx-klosterneuburg-rural-1912::milieu-tenement/the-neighbour-remembers` tags: infancy, family, letter, uncle, warm, melancholy, memory, drama
  送信的邻居说，叔叔年轻时在桥边救过一个落水的孩子，自己却冻坏了肺。Josef Bauer听完把信折好，放进糖罐底下；Klara伸手去拍罐盖，拍得很响，像在替那封信回答。
  _effects: state_add p22-family-legend state:world-vienna-1912:42; mood 0.01999999999999999; relation acquaintance with person-phase15-Klara-world-vienna-1912; knowledge “叔叔曾经在桥边救过一个孩子。”_

- **1914.01** `vienna-life-toddler-wrong-cradle/the-swap-story-spreads` tags: infancy, family, rumour, identity, comic, drama
  三天后，整条楼道都在说这家的孩子“抱错了”。Josef Bauer抱着Klara去敲Emil Weiss的门，Emil Weiss不开，只在门里说“我也是听来的”。Klara在母亲怀里睡着了，口水把她的领口洇出一小片深色。
  _effects: flag +cradle_swap_rumour; relation rival with person-emil-weiss; mood -0.05000000000000002_

- **1914.01** `state_tick:p22-family-legend/the-story-changes-hands/told-at-the-table` tags: p22-family-legend, family_legend, family, story, secret, inheritance, promise, memory
  又过了一年，Klara家的故事在饭桌上换了新的讲法：多了一句笑话，少了一处哭声。听的人把汤匙放下，等下一句；讲故事的人却先去看窗子有没有关好，谁也没有先动筷子。
  _effects: state_parameter p22-family-legend.warmth 60 -> 63; state_parameter p22-family-legend.secrecy 30 -> 28; state_parameter p22-family-legend.promise 55 -> 57; mood 0.01_

- **1914.03** `statistical_infection`
  Klara在Haus der Tante in Klosterneuburg病倒了，肺炎让身体发热、虚弱，接下来的日子只能卧床。

- **1914.06** `statistical_illness_worsened`
  Klara的肺炎没有完全好转，咳嗽和发热又拖了一个月，家里人轮流守着。

- **1914.08** `statistical_recovery`
  Klara从肺炎中慢慢恢复，能下床喝水，但身体还虚弱。

- **1915.01** `vienna-life-child-family-legend/lost-relative` tags: childhood, family, secret, story
  Josef Bauer在收拾箱子时翻出一张没有署名的照片，说那是家里一个“去了很远地方”的人。Klara问还回不回来，Josef Bauer把照片翻过去，说先把名字记住，别在饭桌上问。
  _effects: knowledge “家里有一张没有署名的照片，照片上的人去了很远的地方。”; flag +family_legend_photo; mood -0.01_

- **1915.02** `state_reaction:p22-family-legend/someone-asks-again` tags: p22-family-legend, family_legend, family, story, secret, inheritance, promise, memory
  又有人问起Klara家的那件旧事。大人先笑了一下，才说“都是很早以前了”；问的人点点头，眼睛却往摇篮那边看，手指在杯沿上停了一会儿，谁也没有接话，窗外有风。
  _effects: state_parameter p22-family-legend.secrecy 28 -> 32; state_parameter p22-family-legend.warmth 63 -> 65; mood 0.01_

- **1915.02** `p22-comic-mistaken-child::ctx-klosterneuburg-rural-1912::milieu-tenement/the-cherries-under-the-stall` tags: childhood, family, market, neighbor, mistaken_identity, comic, tender, drama
  市场尽头，一个老太太抓住Klara的手腕，说“你可回来了”。Klara低头看见摊子底下露出两只鞋；真正的孩子正躲在那里吃樱桃，嘴边全是红的，手里还攥着老太太的篮子。
  _effects: mood 0.04; relation acquaintance with person-phase15-Klara-world-vienna-1912; flag +mistaken_for_grandson; cash 1 (a handful of cherries)_

- **1915.02** `p22-strange-grandmother-voice::ctx-klosterneuburg-rural-1912::milieu-tenement/the-child-is-playing` tags: childhood, family, grandmother, memory, omen, strange, eerie, drama
  Josef Bauer认定孩子在学大人说话，笑着拍手。Klara愣了一下，又把那句话说得更响；Elisabeth Bauer也跟着学，三个人的声音在厨房里撞来撞去，最后谁也说不清原句是什么，一起笑了。
  _effects: mood 0.020000000000000004; relation kin with person-phase15-Klara-world-vienna-1912; flag +saying_is_a_game_

- **1915.02** `vienna-early-childhood.stairwell-fall/landlord-complaint` tags: childhood, family, home, street
  摔伤之后两天，房东女人在门厅拦住Josef Bauer，楼里的Hermann Kral正提着菜篮站在门边。房东女人说楼梯间不许孩子嬉闹，铁扶手磕坏要赔。Josef Bauer把裹着布条的Klara往身后一拉，回了句“灯都没有，谁看得见台阶”。房东女人把话记在房租本边上，说下回修灯的钱从房租里扣。
  _effects: mood -0.07; flag +stairs_accident_noted_

- **1916.01** `vienna-life-child-first-friend/shared-toy` tags: childhood, friendship, family, comic
  Emil在楼道里滚一只铁环，Klara站在门口看。Emil把铁环推过来，说“你推一次，我推一次”。两个人从楼梯口推到院子里，铁环倒下的声音把午睡的大人吵醒了一排。
  _effects: relation friend with person-phase15-Emil-world-vienna-1912; skill; mood 0.05_

- **1916.02** `vienna-life-child-inherited-wish/the-caregiver-cries` tags: childhood, family, wish, grief, uncanny, drama
  Josef Bauer没有回答，起身去洗碗，水开得很大。Klara跟到厨房，拽了拽衣角，说“你别哭，我不说了”。Josef Bauer蹲下来，把孩子抱得很紧，说“你说，你继续说”。
  _effects: mood -0.04999999999999999; flag +inherited_wish_grief_

- **1916.02** `p22-drama-inheritance-papers::ctx-klosterneuburg-rural-1912::milieu-tenement/the-seal-is-wrong` tags: childhood, family, inheritance, papers, secret, official, dramatic, dark
  Klara把墨水瓶碰倒在继承文书上。Josef Bauer一边擦一边看见，纸角的火漆印是去年的，文书却写着十年前的日期。公证人伸手去够那张纸，慢了一步，脸一下子白了。
  _effects: knowledge “继承文书上的火漆印比文书日期新。”; flag +wrong_seal; mood -0.02999999999999997; cash -2 (ink and a ruined envelope); state_add p22-family-legend state:world-vienna-1912:56_

- **1916.06** `p9-childhood-household.half-bed/chalk-line` tags: childhood, family, household, home, siblings, illness, neighbors, injury
  “过线了。”Klara用粉笔敲了敲床板。Emil不服，翻身时故意把腿伸过来。Klara顶了一下，Emil又顶回来。被子被蹬到床脚，谁也没去捡，天亮时两个人都缩着肩膀，眼圈发青。
  _effects: health -0.030000000000000027 (夜里推搡没睡踏实); relation kin with person-phase15-Emil-world-vienna-1912_

- **1918.01** `p9-gap-childhood-chores.mind-sibling/sibling-carried-afternoon` tags: childhood, household, chores, school, task, daily, sibling, care
  井台边的石沿湿得发亮，Ferdinand Bauer一挣，Klara只能把Ferdinand Bauer抱离地面。院子里晒的床单还没收，Klara背着Ferdinand Bauer在布影里来回走，用一块碎布给Ferdinand Bauer擦鼻涕。等Ferdinand Bauer睡沉，Klara的肩窝已经压出一道红痕，端碗时手指直抖。
  _effects: health -0.030000000000000027 (背弟妹走了一下午，胳膊酸痛); skill; relation kin with person:evt:world-vienna-1912:000513:child_

- **1918.02** `iw18-secret.cellar-door::ctx-klosterneuburg-rural-1912::milieu-tenement/the-door-was-just-coal` tags: childhood, secret, family, cellar, knowledge, fear, rural, agriculture
  地窖里只有煤、旧箱子和一只缺腿的凳子，Klara转了一圈，什么秘密也没找到。上楼时Josef Bauer正在切洋葱，问孩子下去做什么；Klara说找坛子，Josef Bauer便让孩子把手洗了来帮忙，案板上的洋葱辣得两人都流泪。
  _effects: mood 0.010000000000000009; food; flag +cellar_was_only_coal_

- **1918.02** `iw18-friend.chair-by-the-stove::ctx-klosterneuburg-rural-1912::milieu-tenement/the-adult-calls-it-a-phase` tags: childhood, play, imaginary_friend, solitude, family, wonder, rural, agriculture
  Klara说朋友今天不饿，Josef Bauer把盘子收走，说“等你长大就好了”。那天下午Klara没有再提朋友，只把空椅子推到墙角，又在椅背上搭了一条围巾。晚上熄灯后，Klara对着墙角小声说了很久的话。
  _effects: state_add iw18-imaginary-friend state:world-vienna-1912:81; mood -0.020000000000000004; flag +imaginary_friend_denied_by_adult; flag +imaginary_friend_active_

- **1918.06** `kernel-child.household-rules::ctx-klosterneuburg-rural-1912::milieu-tenement/edu-rules-hides-bread` tags: childhood, family, food, rules, everyday, rural, agriculture, small_town
  锅里只剩一层薄汤。Klara趁Josef Bauer去院里提水，掰下半个面包塞进嘴里，又把剩下的藏进围裙口袋。晚饭时Josef Bauer数了面包，没有骂，只把Klara的那份挪到自己盘边。夜里Klara听见灶台那边有轻轻的哭声。
  _effects: food; relation kin with person-josef-bauer; flag +hid_bread; mood -0.05_

- **1918.10** `iw18-play.kite-on-roof::ctx-klosterneuburg-rural-1912::milieu-tenement/the-kite-is-lost` tags: childhood, play, kite, roof, neighbor, comic, rural, agriculture
  风把风筝往屋脊另一边推，竹骨卡在两片瓦之间。Klara和Lena仰头看到脖子发酸，最后只看见一截纸尾巴在烟里抖。Lena说再糊一只，Klara说纸不够了；两个人蹲在墙根，把剩下的线绕回木轴上。
  _effects: mood -0.03; relation friend with person-phase15-Lena-world-vienna-1912; flag +kite_lost_on_roof_

- **1919.01** `state_tick:iw18-imaginary-friend/friend-year/fading` tags: iw18-imaginary-friend, childhood, play, imaginary_friend, solitude, imagination, secret, state
  Klara这一年很少再和那把椅子说话。学校里有了新的同桌，放学后要在楼梯间跳格子；有一天Klara把书包扔在椅面上，才想起自己已经三天没有给朋友留位置。椅面凉得很快，像一块普通木头。
  _effects: state_parameter iw18-imaginary-friend.closeness 50 -> 42; state_parameter iw18-imaginary-friend.boldness 30 -> 28; mood -0.010000000000000002_

- **1919.01** `p9-gap-childhood-chores.market-line/line-half-flour` tags: childhood, household, chores, school, task, daily, market, food
  面粉袋压在胸口，越抱越沉。Klara前面的大婶一直往后靠，Klara就把膝盖顶住对方的篮子，硬留出半步。买到半袋面粉后，Klara的鞋跟留在人堆里，找回来时鞋面全是泥。回家路上布袋换了两次肩，赤脚踩过石板时脚底发麻。
  _effects: food; health -0.020000000000000018 (赤脚走石板路磨伤脚底); skill; relation acquaintance with person-materialized-world-vienna-1912-24_

- **1919.02** `kernel-child.street-play::ctx-klosterneuburg-rural-1912::milieu-tenement/edu-play-sprains-ankle` tags: childhood, neighborhood, play, injury, street, rural, agriculture, small_town
  Klara从墙上跳下来，脚踝一歪，坐在地上起不来。Emil跑去叫Josef Bauer，两个人把Klara架回家。Josef Bauer用冷布裹住脚踝，嘴里骂，手却很轻。第二天，Klara拄着扫帚站在窗前，看别人踢球。脚踝肿了三天，鞋都穿不进去。
  _effects: health -0.06000000000000005 (sprained ankle from jumping off a wall); condition; relation friend with person-phase15-Emil-world-vienna-1912; relation kin with person-josef-bauer; flag +sprained_ankle_

- **1919.04** `kernel-child.punishment::ctx-klosterneuburg-rural-1912::milieu-tenement/edu-punish-belt` tags: childhood, family, punishment, neighbor, conflict, rural, agriculture, small_town
  Klara打碎了厨房的水罐，碎片扫进簸箕时还在响。Josef Bauer解下皮带，让Klara转过身。三下之后，Josef Bauer的手停在半空，最后把皮带扔回椅背，自己去院里提水。Klara站着没动，听见水桶碰井沿的声音。
  _effects: health -0.040000000000000036 (beaten with a belt); relation kin with person-josef-bauer; flag +beaten_for_broken_jar; mood -0.1; relation acquaintance with person-materialized-world-vienna-1912-2_

  又有人问起Klara家的那件旧事。大人先笑了一下，才说“都是很早以前了”；问的人点点头，眼睛却往摇篮那边看，手指在杯沿上停了一会儿，谁也没有接话，窗外有风。
  _effects: state_parameter p22-family-legend.secrecy 77 -> 81; state_parameter p22-family-legend.warmth 35 -> 37; mood 0.010000000000000002_

  Klara把空椅子上的围巾叠好，放进床下的木箱，又把箱子推回最里面。Emil在楼下喊第二遍时，Klara应了一声，跑下去时没有回头。那天晚上，木箱里只传出一声很轻的碰响，像有人翻了个身。
  _effects: state_remove iw18-imaginary-friend state:world-vienna-1912:81; mood -0.02; relation friend with person-phase15-Emil-world-vienna-1912; flag +imaginary_friend_boxed; flag -imaginary_friend_active_

- **1920.01** `p9-gap-childhood-chores.mind-sibling/sibling-shelter-missed-river` tags: childhood, household, chores, school, task, daily, sibling, care
  巷口的喊声第二次传来时，Klara正用膝盖压住木盆边沿，给Ferdinand Bauer挡穿堂风。Ferdinand Bauer在旧布底下翻了个身，Klara立刻停住，等呼吸又匀了才敢动。Josef Bauer推门进来，先看见盆里睡着的Ferdinand Bauer，再看见Klara冻红的脚背，把灶上留的汤热了热。
  _effects: mood -0.020000000000000004; relation kin with person:evt:world-vienna-1912:000513:child; relation kin with person-josef-bauer; relation acquaintance with person-materialized-world-vienna-1912-11_

- **1921.01** `p9-gap-childhood-chores.laundry-help/laundry-three-heller` tags: childhood, household, chores, school, task, daily, laundry, work
  “拽平，别留褶。”Josef Bauer在绳子那头喊。Klara踮着脚把床单拉直，碱水把指缝泡得发白。收工时天已经黑了，Josef Bauer数了三赫勒，又在围裙上擦干手才递过来。Klara把钱塞进袜筒，回家路上一直攥着，虎口的裂口沾了汗，火辣辣地疼。
  _effects: cash 0.03 (帮邻居晾床单挣的三赫勒); health -0.030000000000000027 (碱水泡手，虎口裂开); relation acquaintance with person-josef-bauer; relation acquaintance with person-materialized-world-vienna-1912-4_

  Ferdinand Hofer来家里说Klara的算术跟得上，不该缺课。Katharina Bauer把学费的事说了一遍，Ferdinand Hofer说可以免一半。Klara坐在门槛上，一手攥着课本，一手攥着没剥完的豆子。
  _effects: flag +kept_child_in_school; skill; relation friend with person-materialized-world-vienna-1912-34; relation rival with person-katharina-bauer; education primary; relation acquaintance with person-materialized-world-vienna-1912-38; relation acquaintance with person-materialized-world-vienna-1912-34_

- **1922.01** `p9-gap-childhood-chores.market-line/line-both-families` tags: childhood, household, chores, school, task, daily, market, food
  Luise Lechner把Klara拽到队伍前头，往自己身前塞，说“孩子占不了多大地儿”。窗口一次递出两包面包，Klara把邻居那包夹在胳肢窝，自己这包抱在胸前。到Haus der Tante in Klosterneuburg门口，Klara蹲下把两包并排，按Luise Lechner说的份量分开，连找零的铜板也分成两小堆。
  _effects: food; relation acquaintance with person-materialized-world-vienna-1912-25; skill; relation acquaintance with person-materialized-world-vienna-1912-25_

- **1922.09** `vienna-school-childhood.spelling-bee/spelling-bee.fail` tags: school, social, childhood
  Klara拼到一半停住，Anna Bauer的尺子落在桌角上。放学后楼梯间只剩Klara一个人，把“Notwendigkeit”抄了二十遍，墨水瓶见了底。
  _effects: mood -0.06; skill; relation acquaintance with person-anna-bauer_

- **1922.11** `vienna-childhood-street.market-errand/errand-lost-coin` tags: childhood, street, social, home
  菜摊前的地面是湿的。Klara弯腰捡土豆时，一枚铜板从指缝里滑出去，顺着水沟格栅掉了下去。Klara把剩下的钱摊在柜台上，让摊主帮忙数，还是少了三十克鲁泽。晚上Katharina Bauer把钱一枚枚排开，问了三遍怎么少的，Klara每答一次，声音就低一点。
  _effects: cash -0.3 (铜板掉进下水道); relation kin with person-katharina-bauer; mood -0.06_

- **1923.01** `vienna-life-school-day/hungry-in-class` tags: childhood, school, hunger, injury
  Klara早上只喝了一碗清汤，第二节课时眼前发黑。Maria Lechner 2让孩子去走廊坐一会儿，给了半块面包；Klara把面包屑拢在手心吃完，胃里还是空的，但看Maria Lechner 2的眼神软了些，回到座位时把腰挺直了一点。
  _effects: health -0.030000000000000027 (went to school hungry); condition; relation acquaintance with person-materialized-world-vienna-1912-33; relation acquaintance with person-materialized-world-vienna-1912-33_

  “你看看这角。”Anna Moser把床单拎起来，煤渣簌簌往下掉。Klara伸手去拍，反而把灰按进布纹里。Anna Moser从工钱里数回两枚，只留一赫勒在Klara手心，让重洗那角。晚饭前Klara一直蹲在井台边，换了两盆水，指甲缝里全是黑泥。
  _effects: cash 0.01 (被扣后只剩一赫勒); mood -0.05000000000000002_

  Klara白天帮家里干活，只能夜里复习。煤油灯熏得眼睛发酸，第二天考试时，Klara把会做的题做完，剩下的凭记忆填满。成绩不高，但够升级。Anna Bauer在成绩单上写：尚有潜力。
  _effects: skill; condition; reputation 0.01; relation acquaintance with person-anna-bauer; flag +passed_exam_barely_

  _effects: relation friend with person-phase15-Lena-world-vienna-1912; mood -0.030000000000000027; reputation -0.01_

- **1927.05** `r1-fam.sibling-rivalry::ctx-vienna-reform-1930::milieu-tenement/rivalry.neither` tags: family, siblings, rivalry, apprenticeship, craft, reform, medical, schooling
  Alois Kral看了看兄弟俩的手，说两个人的指节都不够硬，谁也不收。Katharina Bauer求了半天，Alois Kral答应让两人一起干杂活，管饭不给钱。Elisabeth Bauer干了三天就走了，Klara留下来扫地、磨刀，磨到第六个月，Alois Kral才让她碰第一块好木料。
  _effects: cash 1 (同一把刨子); flag +worked_up_from_dogsbody; role; skill; relation acquaintance with person-elisabeth-bauer; relation acquaintance with person-materialized-world-vienna-1912-59_

- **1927.06** `yf18-politics.latin-pamphlet::ctx-vienna-reform-1930::milieu-tenement/pass-it-on` tags: politics, school, pamphlet, censorship, awakening, reform, medical, schooling
  Maria Lechner 3把一本拉丁文课本推到Klara面前，说第三十四页的译文错了。Klara翻到那一页，夹在里面的是一张油印纸，写着面粉配给和工厂检查的数字。放学后，Klara把纸抄了一遍，塞进Emil的历史作业本；那天晚上，Klara第一次觉得自己住的街区有一张看不见的地图。
  _effects: knowledge “配给数字与官方公告并不一致”; flag +yf18_read_pamphlet; relation acquaintance with person-materialized-world-vienna-1912-45; mood 0.030000000000000027; relation acquaintance with person-materialized-world-vienna-1912-12_

- **1928.01** `vienna-life-young-worker/long-shift` tags: adolescence, labour, union, injury
  Klara连续上了十二天夜班，最后一天在机器旁睡着，被工头踢醒。月底多拿了两克朗，咳嗽却一直没好。
  _effects: cash 2 (overtime pay); health -0.050000000000000044 (night shifts); condition; relation acquaintance with person-materialized-world-vienna-1912-14_

- **1928.01** `yf18-firstlook.parish-dance::ctx-vienna-reform-1930::milieu-tenement/friend-cuts-in` tags: first_love, dance, church, courtship, jealousy, reform, medical, schooling
  Josef在第二支曲子前把Lena请走，说Klara答应过要替她看管外套。Klara抱着一件不属于自己的大衣站在圣像下面，看他们在蜡烛光里转圈；回家路上，Klara把舞卡撕成小片，扔进多瑙运河。
  _effects: state_parameter yf18-first-awakening.intensity 61 -> 66; state_parameter yf18-first-awakening.nerve 23 -> 15; state_parameter yf18-first-awakening.talk 15 -> 20; relation friend with person-phase15-Lena-world-vienna-1912; mood -0.040000000000000036_

- **1928.01** `state_tick:yf18-first-awakening/half-year-of-glances/answered-glance` tags: yf18-first-awakening, love, youth, first_love, desire, school, awakening, state
  Klara在教堂门口或学校走廊里又遇见Josef。这一次对方先点了头，Klara准备好的整段话全忘了，只把手里的书换到另一只手上；回家后Klara才发现，自己把书拿倒了。
  _effects: state_parameter yf18-first-awakening.intensity 63 -> 68; state_parameter yf18-first-awakening.nerve 15 -> 23; state_parameter yf18-first-awakening.reciprocated true -> true; mood 0.030000000000000027_

  Klara又学会了一道工序。师傅没有夸，只把下一块料推到面前。
  _effects: state_parameter apprenticeship-contract.progress 30 -> 50; skill; mood 0.020000000000000018_

- **1928.01** `r13-civ.first-aid-course::ctx-vienna-reform-1930::milieu-tenement/certificate-earned` tags: health, first_aid, course, skill, school, reform, medical, schooling
  Alois Kral把一条绷带绕在Klara的小臂上，教怎么打结才不会勒住血管。Klara在同桌的手臂上练了三遍，最后一遍得到了点头。课程结束时，Alois Kral发了一张油印证书，上面盖着红十字的章。
  _effects: flag +earned_first_aid_certificate; role; skill; knowledge “a bandage must be tight enough to hold but loose enough for ”_

  考试周里，Klara把课本摊在桌上，眼睛却一直看着窗外那条电车线。笔记的页边多了几个没有写完的名字，Klara用橡皮擦掉，纸面起了毛。
  _effects: state_parameter yf18-first-awakening.intensity 66 -> 63; state_parameter yf18-first-awakening.talk 20 -> 22_

  教堂年历的订单催得紧，普拉特动物园夜班队把压印机的护板卸了，说这样进纸快一半。Klara看着那块铁板，说手会进去；普拉特动物园夜班队把工钱袋往桌上一扔，问Klara是不是不想出师了。Klara没有碰机器，那天下午的活由Josef Huber接手。
  _effects: state_parameter apprenticeship-contract.progress 50 -> 45; state_parameter apprenticeship-contract.failures 1 -> 2; flag +yf18_refused_unguarded_press; mood 0.020000000000000018; relation acquaintance with person-materialized-world-vienna-1912-44; relation acquaintance with person-materialized-world-vienna-1912-5_

- **1928.06** `vienna-adolescence.workshop-apprentice/apprenticeship-rejected` tags: adolescence, school, work, family, social
  Buchdruckerei Winter & Söhne捏了捏Klara的虎口，又让Klara张开手指，说骨节还没长开，推硬木会伤手。Josef Bauer想再问一句，Buchdruckerei Winter & Söhne已经转身去搬木料。Klara退到门槛外，听见刨子重新响起来，木花从门缝里卷到脚边。
  _effects: mood -0.07999999999999996; reputation -0.05; relation kin with person-josef-bauer_

  德语作文题是《我的家乡》。Klara写了半页多瑙河，剩下半页写一个总在电车上看书的人。Maria Lechner 3没有打分，只在页边写“感情太多，论据太少”，把作文折好放进自己的公文包；放学后，Klara看见Elisabeth Bauer在门口等，忽然不敢走过去。
  _effects: state_parameter yf18-first-awakening.intensity 68 -> 74; state_parameter yf18-first-awakening.talk 22 -> 25; skill; reputation -0.009999999999999998; mood 0.010000000000000009_

  _effects: flag +study_circle_dissolved; relation acquaintance with person-anna-bauer; skill; knowledge “读书会散了，但老师家的地址还留着。”; mood -0.050000000000000044_

  Katharina Winter 2在俱乐部后间放了一张留声机，让Klara和Josef跳一支舞。窗帘没有拉严，对面楼里有人看见了；第二天，Klara的母亲问她昨晚是不是去了“那种地方”，Klara说去听了音乐。
  _effects: state_add ld18-public-desire state:world-vienna-1912:356; mood 0.040000000000000036; flag +danced_in_the_back_room; relation acquaintance with person-materialized-world-vienna-1912-57; flag +public_desire_active_

- **1930.01** `vienna-family-courtship/ask-to-share-a-home` tags: family, courtship, household, social, lifecycle
  Klara在Leopoldstädter Zinshaus, gemeinsamer Gang靠窗的桌边，把一只空咖啡杯转了半圈，说房租两人合付、桌子两人共用。Ferdinand Weiss的手停在糖罐盖上，问了一句“要是过不下去呢”，没有当场答应，只说要想一想；杯里的咖啡凉了，话还悬在桌面上。
  _effects: interaction; relation acquaintance with person-materialized-world-vienna-1912-60_

- **1930.01** `p22-forged-review::ctx-vienna-reform-1930::milieu-tenement/the-dead-critic-signs-again` tags: forged_review, opera, fame, fraud, comic, media, art, scandal
  第二篇署名同一个死人的剧评出现在另一家报纸上，笔迹却不一样。Hermann Winter请来笔迹专家，Anton在咖啡馆里把两张剪报并排摊开；Klara发现自己的墨水在纸背留下同样的蓝点，而这一次，没有人再笑。他把两张纸叠在一起，蓝点重合得像一枚印章。
  _effects: state_add p22-scandal-engine state:world-vienna-1912:406; state_add public-scandal state:world-vienna-1912:407; reputation -0.05; relation rival with person-phase15-Anton-world-vienna-1912; flag +forged_review_exposed; mood -0.040000000000000036; relation acquaintance with person-materialized-world-vienna-1912-49; flag +scandal_engine_active_

  道德协会寄来一封措辞客气的信，请Klara去“谈谈自己的生活方式”。她把信读完，折好，垫在桌脚下；桌子还是晃。第二天，她照常去上班，只是把头发剪短了一截，短到所有人都能看出她不在乎。
  _effects: state_parameter ld18-public-desire.protection 5 -> 0; state_parameter ld18-public-desire.defiance 15 -> 23; mood -0.010000000000000009_

  _effects: state_parameter raising-child.schoolProgress 20 -> 23; mood 0.020000000000000018_

  故事又换了一个版本：有人说Klara是为了钱，有人说是为了爱，还有人说她只是喜欢看别人议论。Klara在咖啡馆听见第三种说法，没有更正，只把杯子往窗边挪了挪；阳光照进来，她第一次觉得这些版本都离自己很远。
  _effects: state_parameter ld18-public-desire.rumorHeat 35 -> 31; state_parameter ld18-public-desire.spectacle 30 -> 32; mood 0.010000000000000009_

  蓝皮册合上时扬起一点灰，Klara 看见 Alois Lechner 把表压在会费铁盒底下，像压一张废纸。街角互助会的窗子只开一条缝，里头的人听完来意，舀了勺汤递出来，汤面上浮着两片菜叶。
  _effects: food; mood -0.040000000000000036; relation acquaintance with person-alois-lechner_

  Klara按传单上的暗号找到印刷所。印刷所的人正在洗版，手上全是墨，说今晚还要再印五百张。Klara帮忙把纸捆上自行车，换回一句“别走正街”。回家时，Klara绕了三座桥，车链子响得像打鼓。
  _effects: cash 1 (a few coins for carrying the paper bundles); skill; flag +delivered_revolution_leaflets; relation acquaintance with person-materialized-world-vienna-1912-68_

  Klara数出八赫勒交给Maria Bauer，看她在蓝皮本上找到自家门牌，把这一周的格子划掉。Maria Bauer说上个月隔壁巷子的人走了，公账出了棺材和一场小弥撒，剩下的钱记在册尾。楼梯口的灯很暗，Klara盯着自己的名字看了两遍才转身。

  Klara把裙子穿去了Ferdinand Weiss表妹的婚礼。席间有人问是谁裁的，Klara说是旧货店；话没说完，Elisabeth Weiss从邻桌站起来，说领口的针脚是自己缝的。音乐还在响，Ferdinand Weiss的叉子停在盘子上，整个大厅忽然安静得能听见冰化开的声音。

  _effects: relation_end partner with person-materialized-world-vienna-1912-60; mood -0.08000000000000002; cash -3 (separate household goods)_

  两个便衣从后门进来，Berta Winter把名册摊在桌上，一页页翻。Emil Weiss 2说名册上只有领汤的人，Berta Winter说“领汤的人也要登记原籍”。地下室的锅被踢翻，豆汤流到门槛。Klara从煤槽爬出去时，听见Emil Weiss 2在里屋咳嗽。

  _effects: cash -6 (雇马车搬家具一趟的工钱（其中4克朗记入债务）); residence; relation acquaintance with person-hermann-kral_

  _effects: employment 店员 @ org:r15-club:evt:world-vienna-1912:008228; skill; flag +hired_on_probation; reputation 0.02; mood 0.040000000000000036_

  _effects: state_add stage-sovereign state:world-vienna-1912:896; cash 2 (游行演出的酬劳); flag +stage_sovereign; reputation 0.04_

  _effects: relation acquaintance with person-materialized-world-vienna-1912-73; flag +demands_estate_accounting_

  _effects: cash -1 (上工前把小的送下楼); condition; flag +shared_childcare_with_neighbours; relation friend with person-materialized-world-vienna-1912-47; knowledge “the neighbours will take turns watching the child”; relation acquaintance with person-materialized-world-vienna-1912-47_

  _effects: relation acquaintance with person-mitzi-lechner; food; mood 0.020000000000000018_

  _effects: cash -1 (请师兄喝一杯啤酒（其中1克朗记入债务）); skill_

  _effects: cash 1 (a market stall gave short weight and then overpaid in change); relation acquaintance with person-materialized-world-vienna-1912-26; flag +market_short_weight; mood 0.020000000000000018; knowledge “市场那台秤的秤砣被摊主用拇指抵过。”; relation acquaintance with person-materialized-world-vienna-1912-26_

  _effects: cash -1 (每天两小时的火车); health -0.04000000000000001 (每天两小时的火车); condition; flag +slept_past_station; relation acquaintance with person-materialized-world-vienna-1912-91; relation acquaintance with person-materialized-world-vienna-1912-91_

- **1942.01** `vienna-life-injury-recovery/wound-infection` tags: health, work, family, injury

  _effects: health -0.12 (wound infection); cash -14 (drainage and medicine); condition_

  _effects: state_add hs18-blackout-rumor state:world-vienna-1912:1599; relation acquaintance with person-hermann-kral; mood -0.010000000000000009; flag +heard_radio_rumor_

  Alois Kral把Klara拉到一边，说银行只是缺现钱，资产还在，别去挤兑。Klara信了他，把存折放回抽屉。两周后，银行关门，玻璃上贴着一张白纸，写着清算人的地址；Luise Lechner把存折翻到最后一页，发现最后一笔利息停在三个月前。

  Klara在Leopoldstädter Zinshaus, gemeinsamer Gang病倒了，肺炎让身体发热、虚弱，接下来的日子只能卧床。

- **1944.10** `office16-rival.removal-motion::ctx-vienna-postwar-1945::milieu-tenement/motion-fails` tags: office, rivalry, politics, removal, power, intrigue, vienna, urban

  _effects: organization_found 小巷电影院 (household_firm); position 放映员 @cinema:person-phase15-Klara-world-vienna-1912-evt:world-vienna-1912:017628; state_add oj18-odd-apprenticeship state:world-vienna-1912:1729; cash 1 (projectionist wages); skill; flag +cut_the_reel; knowledge “第十一本胶片里夹着没送审的新闻片，{projectionist}让我接回正片。”; role; flag +odd_job_apprentice_

- **1945.08** `vienna-legacy-worklife.union-office/union-office.write-off` tags: legacy, work, family, reform, ordinary_life
  窗外有人来问入会，接待的人一时不知道怎么开口，Klara替他说“进来坐”。老成员把账本拿回去，用一块旧布包好，放进抽屉最里面。抽屉关上的声音比平时响。
  _effects: mood -0.020000000000000018; relation acquaintance with person-materialized-world-vienna-1912-96; flag +union_debt_written_off; relation acquaintance with person-materialized-world-vienna-1912-96_

- **1946.01** `office16-admin.quiet-ledger::ctx-vienna-postwar-1945::milieu-tenement/balanced` tags: office, administration, budget, competence, quiet, power, vienna, urban
  Klara用三个月把账本上的零碎支出并成四类，又停掉两份没有人看的简报。年底结账时，Hermann Moser发现不仅没有赤字，还剩下够换一批档案柜的钱；没有剪彩，只有仓库里新柜子的木头味。
  _effects: organization_funds; reputation 0.03999999999999998; skill; mood 0.020000000000000018; flag +balanced_the_books; knowledge “停掉两份简报、合并零碎支出后，年底还剩一笔够换档案柜的钱。”; relation acquaintance with person-materialized-world-vienna-1912-17; relation acquaintance with person-materialized-world-vienna-1912-132_

- **1946.01** `state_tick:hs18-street-witness/memory-drift/holds` tags: hs18-street-witness, witness, memory, history, testimony, rumor, risk, street
  Klara又把那天的顺序在脑子里走了一遍：先是门响，再是靴子，最后才是那声喊。细节还在，只是每讲一次，旁边就多出一点别人替你补上的颜色。
  _effects: state_parameter hs18-street-witness.certainty 81 -> 83_

- **1946.01** `state_tick:ld18-public-desire/the-story-changes-hands/strangers-know-best` tags: ld18-public-desire, desire, public, scandal, reputation, body, rumor, notoriety
  一个陌生女人在电车上叫住Klara，说自己认识她的“那位”，还劝她趁年轻找个正经人家。Klara没有回答，提前一站下了车；走在回家的路上，她发现自己的步子越来越快，像在躲一个并不存在的人。
  _effects: state_parameter ld18-public-desire.rumorHeat 61 -> 69; state_parameter ld18-public-desire.shame 19 -> 23; reputation -0.02999999999999997_

- **1946.01** `state_tick:ml18-reversal/reversal-half-year/the-swing-holds` tags: ml18-reversal, money, luck, reversal, risk, debt, reputation, loss
  半年里没有新的坏消息。Klara把零钱分成三份：房租、还账、留一点在抽屉里。旧债主没有上门，咖啡馆里的人也不再每见一次就提那件赔本的事；日子没有变好，但至少不再往下滑。
  _effects: state_parameter ml18-reversal.swing -39 -> -33; state_parameter ml18-reversal.strain 46 -> 42; cash 1 (small economies and an odd job); mood 0.010000000000000009_

- **1946.01** `state_tick:oj18-odd-apprenticeship/strange-year/blamed-for-the-noise` tags: oj18-odd-apprenticeship, work, odd_job, apprenticeship, night, trust, secrecy, state
  楼上住户投诉夜里有人拖椅子，管事的把巡查簿翻到Klara的那一页，说签字太潦草，看不出巡过几趟。Klara想辩解，却想起那晚确实在楼梯间多站了一刻钟；扣掉的工钱刚好够买一双不漏水的鞋。
  _effects: state_parameter oj18-odd-apprenticeship.trust 30 -> 14; state_parameter oj18-odd-apprenticeship.reliability 50 -> 40; mood -0.010000000000000009; flag +odd_job_scare_

- **1946.01** `agency:attend_meeting` tags: agency, agency:attend_meeting
  Klara走到Leopoldstädter Zinshaus, gemeinsamer Gang门口，看见便衣和拥挤的人群，最终没有进去。
  _effects: reputation -0.010000000000000009_

- **1946.01** `state_reaction:ml18-reversal/a-second-chance-arrives` tags: ml18-reversal, money, luck, reversal, risk, debt, reputation, loss
  一封没有署名的信里夹着一张彩票，号码是Klara上个月随口说过的。信纸是廉价的学生用纸，字迹却写得很慢。Klara把彩票翻过来，背面只写着一句：这次别再押上房租。
  _effects: state_parameter ml18-reversal.swing -33 -> -23; state_parameter ml18-reversal.strain 42 -> 37; mood 0.020000000000000018; flag +reversal_second_chance_
  _causes: evt:world-vienna-1912:018102_

- **1946.02** `vienna-housing-move.flat-viewing/flat-viewing-too-damp` tags: housing, home, rent, family, money
  Klara在单间里站了一刻钟，脚底发潮，墙纸底下透出一股霉味。看门人说刷一层石灰就好，Emil Huber却在门口轻轻咳了一声，提醒这背面没有阳光。Klara把定金收回口袋，谢过看门人，宁可多付三克朗，也不愿把湿气带进夜里。
  _effects: mood -0.020000000000000018; relation acquaintance with person-materialized-world-vienna-1912-41; flag +still_looking_for_room; relation acquaintance with person-materialized-world-vienna-1912-41_

- **1946.08** `r15-wish.found-faction::ctx-vienna-postwar-1945::milieu-tenement/founded` tags: politics, power, wish, organization, office, status, vienna, urban
  Klara和Katharina 2在咖啡馆后间成立了一个小小的政治俱乐部。桌子是借的，章程是手写的，但签名、印章和一间可以锁门的办公室是真的。Rosa Lechner 2没有到场，只送来一句“先看看你们能撑多久”。
  _effects: cash -2 (一间借来的办公室); reputation 0.06; organization_found 权力俱乐部 (party); position 发起人 @org:r15-faction:evt:world-vienna-1912:018450_

- **1946.12** `ld18-wedding.private-rite::ctx-vienna-postwar-1945::milieu-tenement/rings-in-a-borrowed-room` tags: love, wedding, marriage, queer, secret, ritual, commitment, secret_intimacy
  婚礼在借来的房间里举行，证人是洗衣房的Ferdinand Kraus。戒指是从当铺买的，誓词写在洗衣单背面；Ferdinand Kraus念到“无论贫穷还是疾病”时，楼下有人敲门，三个人同时屏住呼吸。门开了，是来收煤气费的。
  _effects: state_add ld18-impossible-attachment state:world-vienna-1912:1801; cash -1 (the pawnshop rings and the gas bill); mood 0.06000000000000005; flag +private_wedding_rite; relation acquaintance with person-materialized-world-vienna-1912-127; flag +impossible_attachment_active_

- **1947.01** `r15-wish.power-price::ctx-vienna-postwar-1945::milieu-tenement/resigned` tags: politics, power, wish, office, betrayal, vienna, urban, postwar
  Klara在任职满两年后递了辞呈。对外说是身体原因，实际是Klara第一次发现，自己拿到的东西和当初想要的不是同一件东西。
  _effects: mood 0.040000000000000036; reputation -0.020000000000000018; position_end 部门主管 @org:r15-faction:evt:world-vienna-1912:006113; relation acquaintance with person-materialized-world-vienna-1912-123_

  _effects: mood -0.06000000000000005_
  _causes: evt:world-vienna-1912:018210_

- **1947.01** `ml18-tax.audit-of-the-windfall::ctx-vienna-postwar-1945::milieu-tenement/hide-the-ledger` tags: tax, legal, money, windfall, bureaucracy, comic, vienna, urban
  Alois Kral把账本塞进钢琴后面的夹层，说等风声过去再拿出来。Maria Weiss第二次来时带了一个记录员和一张搜查令；钢琴被挪开时，琴键响了一串不成调的音。Klara看见黑皮本翻开的那一页上，已经用红笔圈出了三笔利息。
  _effects: cash -10 (the accountant's fee for hiding the ledger); state_add under-investigation state:world-vienna-1912:1800; relation colleague with person-materialized-world-vienna-1912-59; flag +tax_ledger_hidden; knowledge “黑皮本上被红笔圈出的三笔利息都没有报税。”; relation acquaintance with person-materialized-world-vienna-1912-136_

- **1947.01** `state_tick:ld18-impossible-attachment/invented-anniversary/a-form-with-an-empty-line` tags: ld18-impossible-attachment, love, intimacy, queer, unregistered, commitment, secret, desire
  户籍窗口换了一张新表格，配偶一栏不能空着。Klara最后写了“同住人”，办事员抬头看了一眼，说这不作数。回家后，Klara把表格折成很小的一块，塞进针线盒；伴侣问今天顺不顺利，Klara说和平时一样。
  _effects: state_parameter ld18-impossible-attachment.pressure 15 -> 21; state_parameter ld18-impossible-attachment.discretion 55 -> 52; mood -0.020000000000000018_

- **1947.01** `p10-migration-tasks.pawn-belongings/pawn-watch-and-coat` tags: adulthood, migration, housing, family, travel, task, pawn, sale
  他把怀表和两件冬衣递上铁栅栏后的柜台，当铺的人翻过表壳、抖了抖衣领，报出九克朗，写在一张蓝纸当票上。他留下那套木工工具，把当票折成小方块塞进身份证后面，数钱时手指有点抖。
  _effects: cash 9 (当掉怀表与两件冬衣换来的现钱); mood -0.020000000000000018; knowledge “当铺赎当期限六个月，过期死当，蓝纸当票要收好”; relation acquaintance with person-materialized-world-vienna-1912-126_

- **1947.03** `r3-crime.domestic-theft::ctx-vienna-postwar-1945::milieu-tenement::empty-rice-crock/confessed-before-dinner` tags: crime, family, poverty, shame, household, vienna, urban, postwar
  Klara在晚饭前把当票放在Luise Lechner的碗边，说银勺已经赎回来了，钱是从菜钱里省的。Luise Lechner把汤勺放下，问“为什么不跟我说”。Klara说“怕你为难”，Katharina Bauer在隔壁咳了一声，Elisabeth Bauer把收音机的声音调大了。
  _effects: cash -4 (米缸后面藏着一只银勺（其中1克朗记入债务）); flag +confessed_household_theft; relation friend with person-materialized-world-vienna-1912-25; knowledge “the partner would have pawned the spoon too”_

- **1947.04** `vienna-historical-social-movement.public-meeting/back-immediate-action` tags: history, historical_process, social_movement, organization, politics
  有人喊罢工，有人喊谈判，Klara站到前排说先动才有得谈。保守派转身去拿大衣，Elisabeth Bauer追出去又回来，说外头雪深，别把人推到对面去。会议没散，火气没降。
  _effects: reputation 0.050000000000000044; relation acquaintance with person-elisabeth-bauer; mood 0.030000000000000027_

- **1948.01** `r15-wish.faction-price::ctx-vienna-postwar-1945::milieu-tenement/grew` tags: politics, power, wish, organization, intrigue, office, vienna, urban
  俱乐部从十一人变成四十人。Klara仍然坐主席的位置，但每次开会前都要先和Maria Lechner 3对一遍名单。有人开始叫Klara“主席”，也有人只在需要签字时才来。
  _effects: reputation 0.08000000000000002; knowledge “俱乐部扩大后，真正的权力不在主席的椅子上，而在会前对名单的修改。”; flag +faction_grew_

- **1948.01** `state_reaction:wish-power/power-office` tags: wish-power, wish, wish:power, power, status, ambition, state, state_reaction
  Klara坐在那张有权力的桌子后面，签下自己的名字。愿望没有因此变近，但桌上的纸张、印章和等待回话的人，都变得具体了。
  _effects: reputation 0.05999999999999994_
  _causes: evt:world-vienna-1912:019057_

  办公室有人问Klara，为什么总有一个女人在楼下等她。Klara说那是表妹；对方笑了一下，说现在的表妹真体贴。下午，Klara把桌上的相框扣倒，又在没人时翻回来，用袖子擦了擦玻璃上的灰。
  _effects: state_parameter ld18-impossible-attachment.pressure 21 -> 29; state_parameter ld18-impossible-attachment.discretion 52 -> 48; reputation -0.020000000000000018_
  _causes: evt:world-vienna-1912:019237_

  Klara没有等投票，而是让Maria Weiss把委员会过去三年的差旅账目复印了十份。会议开始前，Hermann Stein的动议被一份“程序审查”取代；审查没有找到Klara的问题，却找到了两次重复报销。

  Klara和伴侣把认识的那一天当作纪念日。没有登记，没有证人，只有一块从旧货摊买来的蛋糕和两杯兑了水的酒；Klara在日历上画了一个别人看不懂的记号，第二年又画在同一个格子里。
  _effects: state_parameter ld18-impossible-attachment.devotion 70 -> 74; state_parameter ld18-impossible-attachment.pressure 29 -> 26; mood 0.020000000000000018_

  Klara去了Leopoldstädter Zinshaus, gemeinsamer Gang，坐在后排听完了关于圣马克斯殡葬合作社在Leopoldstädter Zinshaus, gemeinsame的争论；散会时带走一张油印提纲。
  _effects: historical development:initiative-mortuary:person-materialized-world-vienna-1912-64-evt:world-vienna-1912:010594-1942 as participant; knowledge “我参加了圣马克斯殡葬合作社在Leopoldstädter Zinshaus, gemeinsamer Gan。”; mood 0.030000000000000027_

  你们站在当铺招牌下面，风从巷口灌进来。邻居说他婆娘这个月也要买煤，但两克朗还能匀出来。你把表给他看了一眼，说这表不能当，是爹留下的。他点点头，没再问。
  _effects: cash 2.3 (向邻居借来的现钱); mood -0.030000000000000027_

  _effects: organization_funds; reputation 0.040000000000000036; skill; mood 0.020000000000000018; flag +signed_balanced_budget; knowledge “把冬季煤房的款项并成一笔后，省下的钱够修会议室屋顶。”_

  一个市民代表团真的来到Klara门口，带来一份任命书：公共礼仪官，负责节庆、吊唁和接待。职位不大，薪水也不高，但上面有印章，有任期，有一间可以锁门的办公室。Klara把任命书读了三遍，才确认这不是戏票。

- **1950.01** `r15-wish.faction-price::ctx-vienna-postwar-1945::milieu-tenement/split` tags: politics, power, wish, organization, intrigue, office, vienna, urban
  俱乐部在一次表决中分裂。Katharina Lechner带走了将近一半的人，留下的那间办公室突然显得很大。Klara把两把没人坐的椅子搬到墙边，没有扔掉。
  _effects: mood -0.07999999999999996; reputation -0.050000000000000044; relation acquaintance with person-materialized-world-vienna-1912-101_
  _causes: evt:world-vienna-1912:018450_

- **1950.01** `r15-wish.found-faction::ctx-vienna-postwar-1945::milieu-tenement/founded` tags: politics, power, wish, organization, office, status, vienna, urban
  Klara和Emil Huber在咖啡馆后间成立了一个小小的政治俱乐部。桌子是借的，章程是手写的，但签名、印章和一间可以锁门的办公室是真的。Hermann Stein没有到场，只送来一句“先看看你们能撑多久”。
  _effects: cash -2 (一间借来的办公室（其中2克朗记入债务）); reputation 0.05999999999999994; organization_found 权力俱乐部 (party); position 发起人 @org:r15-faction:evt:world-vienna-1912:021001_

- **1950.01** `state_tick:ld18-impossible-attachment/invented-anniversary/the-date-that-is-not-on-paper` tags: ld18-impossible-attachment, love, intimacy, queer, unregistered, commitment, secret, desire
  Klara和伴侣把认识的那一天当作纪念日。没有登记，没有证人，只有一块从旧货摊买来的蛋糕和两杯兑了水的酒；Klara在日历上画了一个别人看不懂的记号，第二年又画在同一个格子里。
  _effects: state_parameter ld18-impossible-attachment.devotion 78 -> 82; state_parameter ld18-impossible-attachment.pressure 31 -> 28; mood 0.020000000000000018_

- **1950.01** `agency:support_process` tags: agency, agency:support_process
  Klara想帮忙，但组织者不信任新来的人，只让其在外围等消息。
  _effects: mood -0.040000000000000036; reputation -0.010000000000000009_

- **1950.01** `ad20-job-interview::ctx-vienna-postwar-1945::milieu-tenement/thanks-but-no` tags: work, interview, employment, everyday, reputation, obligation, vienna, urban
  接手后的俱乐部把Klara的证明信从头看到尾，最后说店里不缺人。Klara站起来时，椅子在地板上刮出一声长响；接手后的俱乐部送到门口，又补了一句“以后有机会”。街上正在下雨，Klara把证明信折成四折，塞进内袋。
  _effects: flag +interview_rejected; cash -1 (tram fare to the interview); knowledge “面试后，店主说店里不缺人。”_

- **1950.09** `vienna-legacy-worklife.hours-ledger/hours-ledger.refuse` tags: legacy, work, family, reform, ordinary_life
  学徒在旁边小声说，拒绝按手印的人名字会被单独抄在一张纸上。Klara没接话，把八克朗换成一克朗的硬币，数给家里时硬币在桌上滚了两圈才停。屋里的煤油灯芯剪得太短，光只够照亮手边的桌面。
  _effects: cash 8 (追回的拖欠计件工资); reputation -0.030000000000000027; relation colleague with person-materialized-world-vienna-1912-59_

- **1950.09** `p9-social-family-conflict-care.sick-parent-night/hospital-deposit` tags: family, care, siblings, inheritance, housing, money
  Klara跑了一趟医院，把Katharina Bauer送进病房：押金三克朗，搪瓷杯和旧毛巾一克朗二十赫勒，头三天的药费三克朗八十赫勒，一共八克朗。护士收走便盆，换上带刻度的玻璃壶。Klara回到空了的床边，把塌下去的椅子扶正，才发现自己一整天只喝了一杯咖啡。
  _effects: cash -8 (住院押金和住院用品（其中1克朗记入债务）); health 0.05999999999999999 (父亲住进医院得到治疗); flag +parent_hospitalized_

- **1951.01** `office16-scandal.public-hearing::ctx-vienna-postwar-1945::milieu-tenement/weathered` tags: office, scandal, media, reputation, politics, power, vienna, urban
  报纸在头版登了Klara和承包商在一张桌子上的照片，配文只有一句“谁签的字”。质询会开了三个小时，Klara把合同、比价单和验收记录一页页摊开；记者第二天没有再写，但把照片留在了档案里。
  _effects: reputation -0.030000000000000027; mood -0.030000000000000027; state_add office-under-siege state:world-vienna-1912:2096; flag +scandal_weathered; knowledge “照片是真的，合同也是真的；报纸没有再追，但档案里留了底片。”; flag +office_under_siege_

- **1951.01** `state_reaction:wish-power/power-office` tags: wish-power, wish, wish:power, power, status, ambition, state, state_reaction
  Klara坐在那张有权力的桌子后面，签下自己的名字。愿望没有因此变近，但桌上的纸张、印章和等待回话的人，都变得具体了。
  _effects: reputation 0.06000000000000005_
  _causes: evt:world-vienna-1912:021852_

- **1951.01** `state_tick:ld18-impossible-attachment/invented-anniversary/a-form-with-an-empty-line` tags: ld18-impossible-attachment, love, intimacy, queer, unregistered, commitment, secret, desire
  户籍窗口换了一张新表格，配偶一栏不能空着。Klara最后写了“同住人”，办事员抬头看了一眼，说这不作数。回家后，Klara把表格折成很小的一块，塞进针线盒；伴侣问今天顺不顺利，Klara说和平时一样。
  _effects: state_parameter ld18-impossible-attachment.pressure 28 -> 34; state_parameter ld18-impossible-attachment.discretion 44 -> 41_

  Klara想帮忙，但组织者不信任新来的人，只让其在外围等消息。
  _effects: reputation -0.010000000000000009_

- **1951.01** `p9-social-family-conflict-care.cousin-bed/give-camp-bed-and-board` tags: family, care, siblings, inheritance, housing, money
  Klara让Lena睡厨房的行军床，讲好每月三克朗饭钱，煤钱另算。白天把铺盖卷起来靠在碗柜边，晚上再铺开；厨房多一个人，面包和煤都下去得快，伙食上短了两克朗。看门人再来问时，Klara把藤箱指过去，说人住下了，灯油钱也记在账上。
  _effects: cash 3 (表亲交来的饭钱); relation kin with person-phase15-Lena-world-vienna-1912; flag +kin_staying_over_

- **1952.01** `r15-wish.faction-price::ctx-vienna-postwar-1945::milieu-tenement/grew` tags: politics, power, wish, organization, intrigue, office, vienna, urban
  俱乐部从十一人变成四十人。Klara仍然坐主席的位置，但每次开会前都要先和Grete Hofer 2对一遍名单。有人开始叫Klara“主席”，也有人只在需要签字时才来。
  _effects: reputation 0.07999999999999996; knowledge “俱乐部扩大后，真正的权力不在主席的椅子上，而在会前对名单的修改。”_
  _causes: evt:world-vienna-1912:021001_

  名单在委员会卡住了。Rosa Weiss的名字被划掉，理由是“没有相关资历”；Klara没有争，只把名单收回公文包。晚上，Rosa Weiss来问结果，Klara说再等一年；Rosa Weiss没有坐，站了一会儿就走了。
  _effects: reputation -0.020000000000000018; relation friend with person-materialized-world-vienna-1912-31; flag +appointment_list_rejected; knowledge “委员会以“没有相关资历”为由划掉了联络员的名字。”_

  _effects: flag +wish:power:wounded_
  _causes: evt:world-vienna-1912:022867_

  户籍窗口换了一张新表格，配偶一栏不能空着。Klara最后写了“同住人”，办事员抬头看了一眼，说这不作数。回家后，Klara把表格折成很小的一块，塞进针线盒；伴侣问今天顺不顺利，Klara说和平时一样。

  第二天的报纸又登了一封匿名信，说Klara把一份合同给了亲戚。信里没有证据，但足够让两个原本答应到场的人临时出差。
  _effects: state_parameter office-under-siege.exposure 30 -> 40; state_parameter office-under-siege.support 60 -> 54; state_parameter office-under-siege.months 0 -> 1; reputation -0.030000000000000027_

- **1952.01** `agency:support_process` tags: agency, agency:support_process
  Klara为圣马克斯殡葬合作社在Leopoldstädter Zinshaus, gemeinsame出了力：整理名单、传递消息或替同伴作证，名字被记进参与记录。
  _effects: historical development:initiative-mortuary:person-materialized-world-vienna-1912-64-evt:world-vienna-1912:010594-1942 as supporter; historical_influence; reputation 0.015000000000000013_

- **1952.01** `initiative:found_process:org:r15-club:evt:world-vienna-1912:015961`
  接手后的俱乐部在Leopoldstädter Zinshaus, gemeinsamer Gang经过数年的招募、会议和互助，把分散的行动整理成公开纲领；这不是预先写好的历史，而是成员自己累积出来的运动。
  _effects: historical process 接手后的俱乐部的持续运动; historical development:initiative-org:r15-club:evt:world-vienna-1912:015961-1952 as organizer; historical development:initiative-org:r15-club:evt:world-vienna-1912:015961-1952 as organizer; knowledge “接手后的俱乐部的持续运动不是一次会议，而是我们这些年积累出来的持续组织。”; reputation 0.040000000000000036_

- **1952.01** `historical_phase`
  历史进程《接手后的俱乐部在Leopoldstädter Zinshaus, gemeinsamer Gang经过数年的招募、会议和互助，把分散的行动整理成公开纲领；这不是》从 emerging 进入 mobilizing。
  _effects: knowledge “我亲历或知晓「接手后的俱乐部在Leopoldstädter Zinshaus, gemeinsamer Gang经过数年”; knowledge “我亲历或知晓「接手后的俱乐部在Leopoldstädter Zinshaus, gemeinsamer Gang经过数年”_

  折叠椅缺了一条腿，Klara站着说话，把粮店赊账的单据举起来念。Luise Kraus在第三排跟着报出另一家的房租，两人一唱一和。大厅后头有人喊先谈罢工，主持人敲纸板叫安静。
  _effects: reputation 0.05500000000000027; skill; relation acquaintance with person:evt:world-vienna-1912:000182:child_

- **1952.05** `r15-wish.power-offer::ctx-vienna-postwar-1945::milieu-tenement/real-appointment` tags: politics, power, wish, office, status, vienna, urban, postwar
  Maria Weiss把一份任命书推到Klara面前。职位不大，但有印章、有预算、有能签字的地方。Klara签下名字时，第一次觉得那件一直想着的事有了一个可以摸到的边。
  _effects: reputation 0.12; position 部门主管 @org:r15-faction:evt:world-vienna-1912:006113_

- **1952.10** `p9-gap-adult-solitude.river-walk/river-walk.far` tags: adulthood, daily, solitude, ordinary, home, walk, river
  Klara顺着河岸一直走，走过铁路桥又走回来，天已经黑透。回来时脚底发热，袜子磨在脚背上，她一路数着路灯走完最后一段巷子，进门先坐下脱了鞋。
  _effects: mood 0.050000000000000044_

  Berta Stein把一篮鸡蛋放在Klara家门口，说只要一个不用打卡的位子。Klara把鸡蛋拎回对方手里，说编制表不是自家的抽屉。两个人站在楼梯间，谁也没有再提这件事；第二天见面，还是点了头。
  _effects: relation acquaintance with person:evt:world-vienna-1912:006022:child; reputation 0.030000000000000027; flag +refused_private_favor_

  Klara想帮忙，但组织者不信任新来的人，只让其在外围等消息。

- **1953.08** `vienna-historical-social-movement.district-list/hold-list-and-watch` tags: history, historical_process, social_movement, organization, politics
  楼梯间的灯泡忽明忽暗，Klara让Anton Bauer记下今晚进出院子的人。名单留在桌上没动，像一件没人认领的活。快到午夜，Anton Bauer只说了一句，明早再核一遍。
  _effects: relation acquaintance with person:evt:world-vienna-1912:005530:child; mood -0.030000000000000027_

- **1953.08** `vienna-women-housework.coal-stove/coal-stove.borrow-fire` tags: gender, housework, home, labour, family
  Klara端着一只旧锅去敲Maria Bauer的门，想借几块烧红的炭。Maria Bauer让她从自家炉膛里夹了一块，又抓了一小把引火的木片。Klara连声道谢，回来把炭放进炉栅，火是有了，屋里却还是冷得能呵出白气。
  _effects: relation acquaintance with person:evt:world-vienna-1912:005537:child; food; mood 0.020000000000000018_

- **1954.01** `r15-wish.power-intrigue::ctx-vienna-postwar-1945::milieu-tenement/outmaneuvered` tags: politics, power, wish, intrigue, office, vienna, urban, postwar
  Hermann Moser在会议上先一步拿到了多数。Klara的提案被改成一段附注，连名字都没有留下。散会后，Klara在走廊里站了一会儿，然后去下一间办公室。
  _effects: reputation -0.050000000000000044; state_add political-suspect state:world-vienna-1912:2287_
  _causes: evt:world-vienna-1912:023621_

- **1954.01** `p23-coup-through-wall::ctx-vienna-postwar-1945::milieu-tenement/it-was-only-a-rehearsal` tags: politics, conspiracy, coup, surveillance, fear, high_drama, vienna, urban
  Klara报了警，警察冲进隔壁时，屋里的人正穿着戏服排练《理查三世》。Anna Bauer 2站在门口，脸色比戏里的暴君还难看。第二天，整条街都知道Klara把演员当成了政变者；Helene Bauer在咖啡馆里学给所有人看。戏服上的假血还没有干。
  _effects: state_add public-scandal state:world-vienna-1912:2324; reputation -0.050000000000000044; state_add p23-dangerous-secret state:world-vienna-1912:2325; relation acquaintance with person:evt:world-vienna-1912:005445:child; flag +false_alarm_

- **1954.01** `p22-enemy-wedding::ctx-vienna-postwar-1945::milieu-tenement/the-feud-signs-the-register` tags: wedding, enemies, marriage, feud, romantic, scandal, debt, power
  两家人的官司打了七年，最后一份和解书要求Klara和Maria Stein在同一个星期天结婚。婚礼上，Maria Weiss把债务清单念成祝词，Luise Lechner在最后一排数着来宾；交换戒指时，两个人都用了力，戒指差点套不进去。散场后，两家把账本合在一张桌上。
  _effects: state_add p22-wild-fortune state:world-vienna-1912:2326; state_add p22-scandal-engine state:world-vienna-1912:2327; cash 3 (the two families pooled their legal costs); flag +enemy_wedding_kept; mood 0.030000000000000027; flag +wild_fortune_active; flag +scandal_engine_active_

- **1954.01** `kernel-social.reconciliation::ctx-vienna-postwar-1945::milieu-tenement/reconciliation.cold-peace` tags: task, task:social, social, reconciliation, family, apology, conflict, vienna
  Emil收下了锅，却没有让Klara进门，说东西留下，人先回去。Klara在门口站了一会儿，把锅盖掀开又盖上；第二天，空锅被放在门槛上，里面压着一张写着“收到了”的纸条。
  _effects: relation kin with person-phase15-Emil-world-vienna-1912; role; flag +cold_peace; knowledge “对方只收下东西，没有让进门，也没有提那笔钱。”; relation acquaintance with person-materialized-world-vienna-1912-148_

- **1954.06** `absurd15-stage.cast-the-king::ctx-vienna-postwar-1945::milieu-tenement/cast-as-king` tags: performance, theatre, power, irony, work, pretend, vienna, urban
  Marie Huber把一顶掉漆的铅皮王冠扣在Klara头上，说不用演得像国王，只要像一个人突然被允许坐下。首演那天，Klara在台上忘了两句台词，观众却以为那是停顿。
  _effects: state_add stage-sovereign state:world-vienna-1912:2403; cash 2 (首演酬劳); role; reputation 0.030000000000000027; flag +stage_sovereign; reputation 0.040000000000000036_

- **1954.09** `ad20-newspaper-interview::ctx-vienna-postwar-1945::milieu-tenement/story-prints-wrong` tags: media, interview, newspaper, everyday, public, reputation, vienna, urban
  Anna Bauer 2把Klara的话记成了另一层意思。报纸出来时，标题写着“她说她从不后悔”；Klara记得自己说的是“后悔也没有用”。Klara把报纸折起来，又打开，最后还是把它塞进了炉子。
  _effects: reputation -0.030000000000000027; flag +interview_misquoted; knowledge “报纸把“后悔也没有用”写成了“从不后悔”。”_

- **1955.01** `pl19-bridge.public-rumor::ctx-vienna-postwar-1945::milieu-tenement/a-room-with-a-list` tags: scandal, protection, network, press, media, rumor, bridge, vienna
  一位官员在散场后拦住Klara，说可以借一间会议室，每周二晚上用，条件是名单由他过目。Klara看见他手里还攥着当天的报纸，头版折得很整齐；那间会议室后来真的开了灯，只是门口多了一把没人坐的椅子。
  _effects: state_add pl19-protected-scandal state:world-vienna-1912:2402; relation friend with person:evt:world-vienna-1912:000787:child; flag +official_room; knowledge “会议室每周二晚上开灯，名单要先给一位官员过目。”; flag +protected_scandal_active_

- **1955.01** `state_reaction:ld18-impossible-attachment/a-question-at-the-office` tags: ld18-impossible-attachment, love, intimacy, queer, unregistered, commitment, secret, desire
  办公室有人问Klara，为什么总有一个女人在楼下等她。Klara说那是表妹；对方笑了一下，说现在的表妹真体贴。下午，Klara把桌上的相框扣倒，又在没人时翻回来，用袖子擦了擦玻璃上的灰。

  Klara和伴侣把认识的那一天当作纪念日。没有登记，没有证人，只有一块从旧货摊买来的蛋糕和两杯兑了水的酒；Klara在日历上画了一个别人看不懂的记号，第二年又画在同一个格子里。

### 139 subject events

- **1912.03** `p22-warm-fathers-letter::ctx-vienna-1912::milieu-tenement/the-recipe-arrives` tags: infancy, family, letter, uncle, warm, melancholy, memory, drama
  信是叔叔死前写的，在邮袋里压了两年才到。Alois Lechner拆开时先笑出声：里面没有遗言，只有一张李子饺子的做法和一个关于祖母的笨笑话。那天晚上，全家真的吃了饺子。
  _effects: state_add p22-family-legend state:world-vienna-1912:19; mood 0.04; knowledge “家里有一封叔叔留下的信，里面写着李子饺子的做法。”_

- **1912.03** `state_reaction:p22-family-legend/someone-asks-again` tags: p22-family-legend, family_legend, family, story, secret, inheritance, promise, memory
  又有人问起Mitzi家的那件旧事。大人先笑了一下，才说“都是很早以前了”；问的人点点头，眼睛却往摇篮那边看，手指在杯沿上停了一会儿，谁也没有接话，窗外有风。
  _effects: state_parameter p22-family-legend.secrecy 20 -> 24; state_parameter p22-family-legend.warmth 70 -> 72; mood 0.01_
  _causes: evt:world-vienna-1912:000109_

- **1912.08** `p22-strange-doctor-prediction::ctx-vienna-1912::milieu-tenement/the-doctor-is-optimistic` tags: infancy, family, doctor, illness, omen, strange, dark, drama
  医生说孩子只是瘦，开了一瓶味道很坏的补药，说“过了春天就好了”。Alois Lechner把这句话记了很多年；每年春天，家里都要说一遍，像在核对一张旧账单，药瓶上还贴着手写的标签。
  _effects: mood 0.029999999999999992; health 0.010000000000000009 (a tonic and a hopeful sentence); state_add p22-child-omen state:world-vienna-1912:20; flag +doctor_optimism_

- **1912.12** `p22-warm-naming-feast::ctx-vienna-1912::milieu-tenement/the-name-is-too-long` tags: infancy, family, feast, name, neighbor, warm, tender, drama
  两个祖母的名字都想要，最后写在纸上连成一串。Hermann Kral念到一半就笑了，说这孩子以后签名字要多蘸一次墨水；Mitzi在摇篮里睡着，拳头举在耳边，蛋糕上只插了一根蜡烛。
  _effects: mood 0.04; relation acquaintance with person-phase15-Mitzi-world-vienna-1912; flag +double_name; knowledge “我的名字里有两个祖母的名字。”; state_add p22-family-legend state:world-vienna-1912:17_

- **1912.12** `p22-comic-name-register::ctx-vienna-1912::milieu-tenement/the-clerk-hears-a-sneeze` tags: infancy, family, bureaucracy, name, official, comic, tender, drama
  登记员把Mitzi打喷嚏的声音当成了名字，低头就写，笔尖还在舌头上蘸了蘸。Alois Lechner抱着孩子，没看见那行字；等到拿着纸回家，才发现孩子的名字里多了一个“嚏”。
  _effects: mood 0.04; knowledge “登记簿上孩子的名字被写成了喷嚏声。”; flag +registered_as_hatschi; state_add p22-family-legend state:world-vienna-1912:18_

- **1913.01** `state_tick:p22-family-legend/the-story-changes-hands/told-at-the-table` tags: p22-family-legend, family_legend, family, story, secret, inheritance, promise, memory
  又过了一年，Mitzi家的故事在饭桌上换了新的讲法：多了一句笑话，少了一处哭声。听的人把汤匙放下，等下一句；讲故事的人却先去看窗子有没有关好，谁也没有先动筷子。
  _effects: state_parameter p22-family-legend.warmth 72 -> 75; state_parameter p22-family-legend.secrecy 24 -> 22; state_parameter p22-family-legend.promise 65 -> 67; mood 0.01_

- **1913.01** `state_tick:p22-child-omen/the-omen-returns/the-coincidence-repeats` tags: p22-child-omen, child_omen, omen, strangeness, attention, protection, fear, superstition
  又过了一年，同一件小事又发生了：钟停在同一个时刻，门口的狗对着空处叫。Mitzi正在学系鞋带，抬头问“又来了吗”；大人说没有，手却先去摸门框，谁也没有回答。
  _effects: state_parameter p22-child-omen.strangeness 40 -> 45; state_parameter p22-child-omen.attention 35 -> 39; state_parameter p22-child-omen.fear 20 -> 23; mood -0.01_

- **1913.02** `statistical_infection`
  Mitzi在Lechner-Wohnung, Leopoldstadt病倒了，痢疾让身体发热、虚弱，接下来的日子只能卧床。

- **1913.02** `vienna-infant-household.croup/doctor-call` tags: infancy, health, family, home
  Alois Lechner打发邻居去请街角的医生。医生提着皮包来，听了听胸口，留下半瓶药水，收走一克朗。Mitzi三天后不再咳得蜷成一团。
  _effects: cash -1 (医生出诊费和药水钱); relation acquaintance with person-hermann-kral_

- **1913.03** `statistical_illness_worsened`
  Mitzi的痢疾没有完全好转，咳嗽和发热又拖了一个月，家里人轮流守着。

  _effects: cash 3 (anonymous coal saved fuel money); mood 0.03; flag +anonymous_gift_coal; state_add p22-family-legend state:world-vienna-1912:43_

  Mitzi熬过了这场痢疾，能坐起来喝一点汤，但身体还很虚。

- **1914.01** `vienna-life-birth-letter-from-the-dead/the-letter-is-real` tags: infancy, family, secret, letter, drama
  邮差把一封边角磨白的信塞进门缝，收信人是一个已经死了三年的名字。Alois Lechner把Mitzi放在膝上，拆开信，里面只有一句：“孩子像不像他？”Anna Moser在门口探头，问是不是寄错了；Alois Lechner把信折回去，说“没有寄错”。
  _effects: flag +letter_from_dead_relative; knowledge “一封写给死去亲人的信问：孩子像不像他。”; mood -0.04_

- **1914.01** `state_tick:p22-family-legend/the-story-changes-hands/told-at-the-table` tags: p22-family-legend, family_legend, family, story, secret, inheritance, promise, memory
  又过了一年，Mitzi家的故事在饭桌上换了新的讲法：多了一句笑话，少了一处哭声。听的人把汤匙放下，等下一句；讲故事的人却先去看窗子有没有关好，谁也没有先动筷子。
  _effects: state_parameter p22-family-legend.warmth 65 -> 68; state_parameter p22-family-legend.secrecy 50 -> 48; state_parameter p22-family-legend.promise 40 -> 42; mood 0.01_

- **1915.02** `vienna-early-childhood.fever-night/home-remedy` tags: childhood, family, home, street
  Mitzi烧得翻来覆去，Alois Lechner把醋倒进温水，用布蘸着擦手心、脚心，又去灶间煮接骨木汁。勺子送到嘴边，Mitzi偏头躲，Alois Lechner就等一会儿再喂。鸡叫时炉火压小，床脚用旧大衣堵住。两天后热度退了，嘴唇起了一层干皮。
  _effects: health 0.03 (土法退烧，热度渐退); mood -0.06_

- **1916.01** `vienna-life-child-family-legend/stolen-inheritance` tags: childhood, family, secret, story
  Alois Lechner说，家里本来有一件东西，后来被“更会说话的人”拿走了。Mitzi问是什么，Alois Lechner只说是一块表，表盖里刻着姓。孩子记住了“表盖里刻着姓”，却没有记住那个姓。
  _effects: knowledge “家里有一块表盖里刻着姓的表，被一个更会说话的人拿走了。”; flag +family_legend_watch_

- **1916.01** `state_reaction:p22-family-legend/someone-asks-again` tags: p22-family-legend, family_legend, family, story, secret, inheritance, promise, memory
  又有人问起Mitzi家的那件旧事。大人先笑了一下，才说“都是很早以前了”；问的人点点头，眼睛却往摇篮那边看，手指在杯沿上停了一会儿，谁也没有接话，窗外有风。
  _effects: state_parameter p22-family-legend.secrecy 48 -> 52; state_parameter p22-family-legend.warmth 68 -> 70; mood 0.009999999999999998_
  _causes: evt:world-vienna-1912:000558_

- **1916.01** `state_tick:p22-child-omen/the-omen-returns/the-coincidence-repeats` tags: p22-child-omen, child_omen, omen, strangeness, attention, protection, fear, superstition
  又过了一年，同一件小事又发生了：钟停在同一个时刻，门口的狗对着空处叫。Mitzi正在学系鞋带，抬头问“又来了吗”；大人说没有，手却先去摸门框，谁也没有回答。
  _effects: state_parameter p22-child-omen.strangeness 45 -> 50; state_parameter p22-child-omen.attention 39 -> 43; state_parameter p22-child-omen.fear 23 -> 26; mood -0.010000000000000002_

- **1916.02** `vienna-life-child-inherited-wish/the-child-wants-a-dog` tags: childhood, family, wish, grief, uncanny, drama
  Mitzi说那句话只是想要一只狗，而且要“会看门的、不咬人的、像外婆家那只一样”。Alois Lechner松了一口气，又有点失落；那天下午，两个人用旧毛线给一只并不存在的狗量了脖围。
  _effects: mood 0.04000000000000001; skill_

- **1916.02** `vienna-life-child-first-friend/imaginary-friend` tags: childhood, friendship, family, comic
  院子里没有别的孩子，Mitzi给墙上的裂纹起了一个名字，每天吃饭时给它留一个位置。家里的人没有纠正，只在摆碗时多放一只空碗；Rosa从门口经过，问Mitzi在等谁。
  _effects: skill; flag +imaginary_friend; relation friend with person-phase15-Rosa-world-vienna-1912_

- **1917.01** `state_tick:p22-family-legend/the-story-changes-hands/told-at-the-table` tags: p22-family-legend, family_legend, family, story, secret, inheritance, promise, memory
  又过了一年，Mitzi家的故事在饭桌上换了新的讲法：多了一句笑话，少了一处哭声。听的人把汤匙放下，等下一句；讲故事的人却先去看窗子有没有关好，谁也没有先动筷子。
  _effects: state_parameter p22-family-legend.warmth 70 -> 73; state_parameter p22-family-legend.secrecy 52 -> 50; state_parameter p22-family-legend.promise 42 -> 44; mood 0.010000000000000002_

- **1917.10** `p9-childhood-household.fever-night/vinegar-compress` tags: childhood, family, household, home, siblings, illness, neighbors
  没有钱请医生，Alois Lechner把醋瓶拎到床边，拿两只碗轮换着兑水。Mitzi烧得嘴唇起皮，Alois Lechner用小勺喂了两口温水，又把冷布敷上去。鸡叫时热度退了一点，Mitzi睁开眼，看见Alois Lechner趴在床沿，手指还攥着湿布。
  _effects: health 0.03 (湿敷后退了烧); mood 0.020000000000000004; relation kin with person-alois-lechner_

- **1918.01** `state_expire:p22-family-legend` tags: p22-family-legend, family_legend, family, story, secret, inheritance, promise, memory
  Mitzi的「家族传说」到期；相关安排结束。
  _effects: state_remove p22-family-legend state:world-vienna-1912:43_

- **1918.01** `p9-gap-childhood-chores.market-line/line-sold-out-potatoes` tags: childhood, household, chores, school, task, daily, market, food
  木窗“咔”地合上，Mitzi的布袋还敞着口。伙计隔着板缝说下星期再来。Mitzi在墙根站了一会儿，把硬币排在掌心数了两遍，走到旁边土豆摊前，挑了几个小的，让摊主当面过秤。家里人晚上把钱和土豆倒在桌上，重新数了一遍。
  _effects: food; mood -0.03; skill; relation acquaintance with person-materialized-world-vienna-1912-12_

- **1919.01** `state_expire:p22-child-omen` tags: p22-child-omen, child_omen, omen, strangeness, attention, protection, fear, superstition
  Mitzi的「孩子身上的兆头」到期；相关安排结束。
  _effects: state_remove p22-child-omen state:world-vienna-1912:20_

- **1919.01** `p9-gap-childhood-chores.mind-sibling/sibling-shelter-missed-river` tags: childhood, household, chores, school, task, daily, sibling, care
  河水那边传来同伴的笑声，Mitzi站在院门口听了听，还是回去把木盆扶正。Maria Lechner睡醒哭了一声，Mitzi把最后半块面包掰碎泡进温水，一勺一勺喂。Alois Lechner回来时，Mitzi正蹲在盆边打瞌睡，手里还攥着喂饭的木勺。
  _effects: mood -0.020000000000000004; relation kin with person:evt:world-vienna-1912:000382:child; relation kin with person-alois-lechner_

- **1919.05** `vienna-childhood-street.washhouse-fever/fever-recover` tags: childhood, street, social, home
  洗衣房里全是蒸汽和碱水味。Mitzi靠在墙边等Alois Lechner，湿衣服堆在脚边。夜里Mitzi开始发烧，Alois Lechner把湿布叠成长条，敷在额头，凉了再换。三天后热度退下去，Mitzi的嘴唇起了一层白皮，Alois Lechner的手指也裂了口子。
  _effects: condition; relation kin with person-alois-lechner_

- **1920.01** `p9-gap-childhood-chores.laundry-help/laundry-three-heller` tags: childhood, household, chores, school, task, daily, laundry, work
  “拽平，别留褶。”Ferdinand Winter在绳子那头喊。Mitzi踮着脚把床单拉直，碱水把指缝泡得发白。收工时天已经黑了，Ferdinand Winter数了三赫勒，又在围裙上擦干手才递过来。Mitzi把钱塞进袜筒，回家路上一直攥着，虎口的裂口沾了汗，火辣辣地疼。
  _effects: cash 0.03 (帮邻居晾床单挣的三赫勒); relation acquaintance with person-ferdinand-winter; relation acquaintance with person-materialized-world-vienna-1912-24_

- **1920.08** `vienna-childhood-friends.courtyard-race/fell-on-wet-stones` tags: childhood, social, school, street
  晒衣绳上的水一滴一滴落在石板上。Mitzi跑到第二圈，鞋底一滑，膝盖先着地。Emil Weiss从屋里端出半盆清水，蹲下冲掉伤口里的沙，又用手帕扎紧，说今天别跑了。
  _effects: relation acquaintance with person-emil-weiss_

- **1921.01** `p9-gap-childhood-chores.mind-sibling/sibling-shelter-missed-river` tags: childhood, household, chores, school, task, daily, sibling, care
  巷口的喊声第二次传来时，Mitzi正用膝盖压住木盆边沿，给Katharina Lechner挡穿堂风。Katharina Lechner在旧布底下翻了个身，Mitzi立刻停住，等呼吸又匀了才敢动。Alois Lechner推门进来，先看见盆里睡着的Katharina Lechner，再看见Mitzi冻红的脚背，把灶上留的汤热了热。
  _effects: mood -0.02; relation kin with person:evt:world-vienna-1912:000689:child; relation kin with person-alois-lechner_

- **1921.09** `vienna-childhood-street.market-errand/errand-ok` tags: childhood, street, social, home
  Mitzi在土豆摊前蹲了很久，挑了一磅没有芽眼的。付完钱，手里只剩几个铜板，又去买了盐。围裙兜太浅，Mitzi用下巴压住布边，走两步就低头看一眼。到家Alois Lechner把土豆倒进盆里，盐包放在灶台边。
  _effects: food; cash -0.15 (市场花销); relation kin with person-alois-lechner_

- **1922.01** `p9-gap-childhood-chores.market-line/line-both-families` tags: childhood, household, chores, school, task, daily, market, food
  队伍拐弯时，Luise Bauer朝Mitzi招手。Mitzi挤过去，替两家把面包领出来，纸包用不同的绳结系住。家里人晚上解开绳结，先掂了掂份量，再问价钱；Mitzi把每一包的面包数、找零和欠的铜板都说了一遍，家里人听完才把两包分别收进柜子。
  _effects: food; relation acquaintance with person-materialized-world-vienna-1912-7; skill; relation acquaintance with person-materialized-world-vienna-1912-7; relation acquaintance with person-materialized-world-vienna-1912-20_

- **1922.11** `vienna-childhood-friends.hidden-book/book-borrowed-and-returned` tags: childhood, social, school, street
  Anna Bauer从讲台抽屉里拿出一本没有封面的书，书脊用线重新缝过。Mitzi答应只在课间看。第三天放学下起雨，书角在书包里洇湿。Mitzi回家用熨斗隔着旧布压了半晚，还书时把淋湿的那页也指给Anna Bauer看。
  _effects: skill; relation acquaintance with person-anna-bauer_

- **1923.01** `p9-gap-childhood-chores.laundry-help/laundry-three-heller` tags: childhood, household, chores, school, task, daily, laundry, work
  床单浸了碱水，沉得拽不动。Mitzi站在木凳上，把湿布一点点递过绳，水顺着肘弯滴进袖口。Maria Kral在绳头数夹子，最后一条挂好，从围裙口袋里摸出三枚赫勒，按进Mitzi掌心。晚上洗手时，虎口那道口子被水一蜇，Mitzi才看见。
  _effects: cash 0.03 (帮邻居晾床单挣的三赫勒); relation acquaintance with person-maria-kral; relation acquaintance with person-materialized-world-vienna-1912-13_

- **1924.01** `state_tick:wish-knowledge/knowledge-pressure/put-away` tags: wish-knowledge, wish, wish:knowledge, knowledge, education, curiosity, state, state_tick
  Mitzi暂时把那件事收进抽屉；日子还有别的东西要应付。
  _effects: mood -0.020000000000000004_

- **1924.02** `vienna-adolescence.workshop-apprentice/apprenticeship-accepted` tags: adolescence, school, work, family, social
  作坊里全是松木味。Buchdruckerei Winter & Söhne把一块方木夹进台钳，让Mitzi推刨子。Mitzi推了十分钟，木花卷到脚背上，刨面总算平了。Buchdruckerei Winter & Söhne用拇指摸了摸，收下Mitzi，从钱盒里数出三克朗，说是头一周的饭钱。Alois Lechner在门口把围裙攥得很紧。
  _effects: cash 3 ({subject}学徒首周的膳食预付款); education apprenticeship; reputation 0.1; relation kin with person-alois-lechner_

- **1924.02** `vienna-youth-threshold.sibling-departure/quarrel-who-leaves` tags: adolescence, school, work, family, transition
  车票只够一个人走，晚饭桌上话越说越大声。Mitzi Lechner说该自己走，Mitzi说自己也快够岁数了。母亲把汤勺往锅沿一磕，屋里静下来，最后Mitzi摔门出去在楼梯上坐了半宿，直到楼上有人喊冷。
  _effects: relation kin with person-mitzi-lechner; mood -0.07_

- **1924.02** `r13-turn.school-leaving::ctx-vienna-reform-1930::milieu-tenement::reform-leaving-age/straight-to-factory` tags: education, school, work, family, transition, reform, medical, schooling
  Maria Lechner 2把Mitzi的名字从升学名单上划掉，说厂里正缺人手。家里没有人争，只把午饭里的半块面包塞给Mitzi。Mitzi进了纺织厂，工牌上写着“临时”，每天站十二个钟头，月底工钱比学徒多两克朗。
  _effects: cash 2 (毕业钟响过以后); health -0.06 (毕业钟响过以后); condition; flag +left_school_for_factory; role; relation acquaintance with person-materialized-world-vienna-1912-33_

- **1924.02** `r3-teen.first-cigarette::ctx-vienna-reform-1930::milieu-tenement/refuses` tags: smoking, friendship, risk, family, school, reform, medical, schooling
  Mitzi把烟推回去，说家里有人咳血。Elisabeth Moser 3把烟叼在自己嘴上，说胆小鬼才拿家里当借口。放学路上两人隔着三步走，谁也没先开口。他把手插进空口袋，摸到上午剩下的一截粉笔，捏成了两段。
  _effects: reputation 0.03; flag +refused_first_cigarette; relation acquaintance with person-materialized-world-vienna-1912-50; relation acquaintance with person-materialized-world-vienna-1912-34_

- **1924.08** `r3-teen.first-friend-group::ctx-vienna-reform-1930::milieu-tenement/split` tags: friendship, roof, belonging, betrayal, class, reform, medical, schooling
  团里有人偷了市场的香肠，被抓住后供出了所有人。Elisabeth Moser 3把Mitzi拉到楼梯拐角，让她把责任推给最小的那个，说这样大家都能脱身。Mitzi没答应，只把口袋里的香肠纸揉成一团。第二天，屋顶上的折叠椅少了一把，Elisabeth Moser 3见了她也不再吹那两声口哨。
  _effects: mood -0.1; flag +friend_group_split; relation rival with person-materialized-world-vienna-1912-50; relation acquaintance with person-materialized-world-vienna-1912-50_

- **1924.08** `state_reaction:wish-knowledge/knowledge-humiliation` tags: wish-knowledge, wish, wish:knowledge, knowledge, education, curiosity, state, state_reaction
  Mitzi把那句轻视记了很久。它没有变成一个决定，但也没有消失。
  _effects: mood -0.1_
  _causes: evt:world-vienna-1912:002489_

- **1925.01** `r13-fam.help-homework::ctx-vienna-reform-1930::milieu-tenement/tears-on-the-page` tags: task, family, school, homework, sibling, literacy, reform, medical
  Emil Lechner把铅笔芯写断了两次，第三遍还是算错，眼泪滴在本子上，把墨迹洇开。Mitzi把本子拿开，说再写一遍，孩子却把椅子往后一推。母亲在灶边喊别吵，Mitzi只好把豆子收进碗里，听见Emil Lechner在床上抽鼻子。
  _effects: mood -0.06; flag +homework_ended_in_tears; relation kin with person:evt:world-vienna-1912:000787:child; condition_

- **1925.01** `r2-leisure.ferris-wheel::ctx-vienna-reform-1930::milieu-tenement::ferris-wheel-reform/tested-the-new-brake` tags: leisure, fairground, ferris_wheel, fear, youth, reform, medical, schooling
  游乐场新装了紧急制动，铁牌上写着“由市议会验讫”。摩天轮升到一半，Helene Kral 2拉了一下闸，座舱猛地停住，又慢慢往下走。Mitzi问这是不是坏了，Helene Kral 2说这是新规矩，每趟都要试一次。Maria Lechner在座位上把栗子撒了一地。
  _effects: mood -0.030000000000000027; condition; flag +tested_new_ferris_brake; relation colleague with person-materialized-world-vienna-1912-12; knowledge “the municipal inspector requires a brake test on every ride”_

- **1925.01** `vienna-adolescence.school-or-brickworks/go-to-brickworks` tags: adolescence, school, work, family, social, injury
  Alois Lechner把算术本合上，说家里等钱，下周一去砖厂。Mitzi没争。放学时Ferdinand Hofer在门口等着，塞来一张纸条，上面抄着六区夜校的地址。Mitzi把纸条折小，塞进鞋垫。砖厂第一周结四克朗，Mitzi搬砖搬到手指发抖，晚上脱鞋，袜底全是红砖末。
  _effects: role; cash 4 ({subject}砖厂首周工钱); health -0.03 (砖厂头几天搬砖); flag +has-evening-school-address; relation acquaintance with person-materialized-world-vienna-1912-34; relation kin with person-alois-lechner_

- **1925.02** `r15-work.master-takes-on::ctx-vienna-reform-1930::milieu-tenement/unpaid-trial` tags: work, craft, apprentice, trade, poverty, reform, medical, schooling
  Josefine Hofer先说试三个月，不给工钱，只管一顿午饭。家里同意了，因为多一张嘴比多一双手更难。Mitzi每天最早到，最晚走，三个月后师傅才把契约拿出来。
  _effects: food; mood -0.03999999999999998; state_add apprenticeship-contract state:world-vienna-1912:142; education apprenticeship; role_

- **1925.08** `r2-leisure.scullery-punishment::ctx-vienna-reform-1930::milieu-tenement::scullery-punishment-reform/reported-to-inspector` tags: domestic_service, abuse, punishment, burn, servant, reform, medical, schooling
  检查员来查佣工合同，Sozialdemokratische Arbeiterpartei Österreichs (SDAP)让Mitzi把手藏在围裙下面。Mitzi没有藏，摊开掌心，那块新疤还没有褪成白色。检查员看了一眼，又看了一眼Ferdinand Stein，把名字写进本子。Sozialdemokratische Arbeiterpartei Österreichs (SDAP)说这是烫衣服不小心，谁家没有这种事。
  _effects: reputation 0.04999999999999999; flag +reported_iron_burn_to_inspector; role; knowledge “the inspector recorded the burn and the household address”; relation colleague with person-materialized-world-vienna-1912-47; relation acquaintance with person-materialized-world-vienna-1912-17; relation acquaintance with person-materialized-world-vienna-1912-43; relation acquaintance with person-materialized-world-vienna-1912-47_

- **1926.01** `state_tick:apprenticeship-contract/workshop-year/learned` tags: apprenticeship-contract, education, craft, apprentice, work, state, state_tick
  Mitzi又学会了一道工序。师傅没有夸，只把下一块料推到面前。
  _effects: state_parameter apprenticeship-contract.progress 8 -> 28; skill; mood 0.020000000000000018_

- **1926.01** `p9-childhood-youth-aspiration.ask-a-mentor/asked-to-come-back-later` tags: adolescence, education, aspiration, school, books, club, exam, task
  Elisabeth Weiss看了看Mitzi抄的字母表，说大小不一，让他回去用直尺画格子重抄一遍，下个月再来。少年把纸折好，在咖啡馆门口站了一会儿，走回印刷厂时绕到排字房窗外看了很久。

- **1926.02** `yf18-body.danube-cabin::ctx-vienna-reform-1930::milieu-tenement/wade-in-first` tags: body, desire, river, swimming, shame, first_love, reform, medical

  邻居听见孩子哭，端来一壶热水和几片退烧药，替Mitzi守了半宿。Anna Lechner的烧在清晨退了，Mitzi给邻居送去半块面包，折合一克朗的吃食；两家门口从此多了一句招呼，也多了一份欠着的人情。
  _effects: health 0.08 (邻居帮助后烧退); relation acquaintance with person-materialized-world-vienna-1912-14; relation acquaintance with person-materialized-world-vienna-1912-14_

- **1930.10** `r1-fam.marriage-contract::ctx-vienna-reform-1930::milieu-tenement::contract-reform/contract.new-law` tags: family, marriage, contract, law, money, reform, medical, schooling
  办事处的墙上贴着新婚姻法的告示，Anton Hofer逐条念给两人听：婚后财产各半，离婚要双方到场。Alois Lechner在长椅上打瞌睡，Josef Huber听到“各半”时看了Mitzi一眼。Mitzi说就按新的办；Anton Hofer把印章盖得很正。
  _effects: cash -2 (婚约的第七条（其中1克朗记入债务）); flag +married_under_new_law; role; relation partner with person-materialized-world-vienna-1912-44; knowledge “新婚姻法规定婚后财产各半。”; relation acquaintance with person-materialized-world-vienna-1912-44_

- **1931.01** `oj18-zoo.escape::ctx-vienna-reform-1930::milieu-tenement/chimp-on-the-prater` tags: zoo, animals, escape, night, blame, work, odd_job, reform
  黑猩猩“卡斯帕”用一根弯钉挑开第二道门，顺着排水沟跑进普拉特。Mitzi提着一桶苹果追到旋转木马后面，看见它坐在售票亭顶上，正把一张张票根往嘴里塞。Friedrich Huber 2赶来时，Mitzi已经用苹果把它引进了送货马车。
  _effects: reputation 0.04999999999999999; state_parameter oj18-odd-apprenticeship.trust 30 -> 38; state_parameter oj18-odd-apprenticeship.strangeKnowledge 20 -> 25; cash -1 (apples used to lure the chimpanzee（其中1克朗记入债务）); relation colleague with person-materialized-world-vienna-1912-70; flag +monkey_returned; knowledge “卡斯帕会用弯钉挑门闩，苹果比网子管用。”; relation acquaintance with person-materialized-world-vienna-1912-70_

- **1931.01** `state_expire:yf18-first-awakening` tags: yf18-first-awakening, love, youth, first_love, desire, school, awakening, state
  Mitzi的「第一次心动」到期；相关安排结束。

- **1931.01** `state_tick:oj18-odd-apprenticeship/strange-year/trusted-with-the-keys` tags: oj18-odd-apprenticeship, work, odd_job, apprenticeship, night, trust, secrecy, state
  两年过去，Mitzi已经能闭着眼说出哪块地板会响、哪盏煤气灯该在几点关。师傅把备用钥匙交下来，没有解释为什么第三把钥匙磨得最亮；Mitzi也没有问，只在巡查簿最后一页画了一个小小的记号。
  _effects: state_parameter oj18-odd-apprenticeship.trust 38 -> 48; state_parameter oj18-odd-apprenticeship.strangeKnowledge 25 -> 35; state_parameter oj18-odd-apprenticeship.nightsWorked 0 -> 24; cash 2 (night-shift wages); skill_

- **1931.01** `p9-social-daily-culture.lending-library/p9-social-daily-culture.lending-library.volksbibliothek` tags: daily, culture, leisure, cafe, music, walk, task, task:knowledge
  Mitzi听Emil Moser报出押金数目，把伸进内袋的手又抽了回来。问清免费阅览处的位置后，Mitzi穿过两条街，在 Volksbibliothek 的阅览室门口登记了姓名，坐在长桌边读了一下午；一个赫勒没花，只是不能把书带回家，天黑前就得还回去。
  _effects: mood 0.030000000000000027; skill; relation acquaintance with person-materialized-world-vienna-1912-38; relation acquaintance with person-materialized-world-vienna-1912-38_

- **1931.04** `state_reaction:oj18-odd-apprenticeship/the-thing-in-the-basement` tags: oj18-odd-apprenticeship, work, odd_job, apprenticeship, night, trust, secrecy, state
  夜班结束前，Mitzi听见地下室传来拖拽声。下去时，门开着，地上只有一道湿拖把的痕迹；第二天，所有人都说那间屋子锁了十年。
  _effects: state_parameter oj18-odd-apprenticeship.strangeKnowledge 35 -> 43; state_parameter oj18-odd-apprenticeship.trust 48 -> 45; mood -0.030000000000000027; knowledge “地下室那扇门后面有东西被搬动过，但巡查簿上没有记录。”_

- **1931.04** `vienna-parish-aid.funeral-society/funeral-society.quit` tags: faith, parish, mutual_aid, poverty, family
  Mitzi说互助会先退了，这笔钱要留给煤和面包。Hermann Kral没争辩，在蓝皮本上把名字划了一道，问清楚是不是全家都退，然后把划掉的那行折进本子夹缝。她说以后想再进，要重新排三个月。门关上，Mitzi听见铅笔在楼梯上轻轻敲了两下栏杆。
  _effects: cash 0.4 (省下本周会费留作煤钱); flag +funeral_society_left_

- **1931.04** `vienna-women-housework.piecework-bundle/piecework.hide-defects` tags: gender, housework, home, labour, family
  篮底那两件领子卷得很紧，Mitzi用围裙角把篮沿擦了擦。Elisabeth Weiss翻了翻面上几件，拿尺子比了比，没再往下。Emil Weiss在走廊里问交了多少，Mitzi含混说了句够买面包。
  _effects: cash 0.7 (交活所得，含未返工的两件); mood -0.040000000000000036; reputation -0.01999999999999999_

- **1931.11** `ad20-police-stop::ctx-vienna-reform-1930::milieu-tenement/taken-in` tags: police, legal, street, everyday, obligation, risk, reform, medical
  Katharina Bauer 2让Mitzi靠墙站好，把口袋里的东西一件件放在地上。钥匙、手帕、半块面包，还有一张写着地址的纸。Katharina Bauer 2把纸对着路灯看了很久，最后说“跟我们走一趟”。
  _effects: cash -2 (a fine after the police stop); reputation -0.03; flag +police_stop_record; mood -0.050000000000000044_

- **1932.01** `p23-lovers-ledger::ctx-vienna-reform-1930::milieu-tenement/the-ledger-burns-first` tags: love, desire, betrayal, ledger, secret, blackmail, high_drama, reform
  Josef Huber比Mitzi早到一步。炉膛里只剩一截没烧透的蓝线，Mitzi从灰里拈出来，认出那是账本装订用的线。Josef Huber说这样对大家都好；Mitzi没有回答，只把灰烬扫进一只信封，收进抽屉最深处。炉子里的火还没有完全灭。
  _effects: state_add p23-dangerous-secret state:world-vienna-1912:525; relation partner with person-materialized-world-vienna-1912-44; knowledge “partner burned the ledger”; mood -0.030000000000000027; flag +ledger_burned_

  _effects: mood -0.040000000000000036_
  _causes: evt:world-vienna-1912:006120_

- **1932.09** `vienna-tenement-neighbors.shared_stove_fire/fire_scald_hand` tags: home, social, family, housing
  Mitzi在合租房走廊的公用灶台上煮土豆，转身取盐时袖子扫翻了铁锅，滚水浇在左手背上。Hermann Kral从隔壁冲出来，扯下自己的围裙裹住烫伤处，又跑下楼叫来住在二楼的接生婆。接生婆用蛋清和旧亚麻布包扎，嘱咐三天不能沾水。
  _effects: relation friend with person-hermann-kral_

  Mitzi在队伍拐进小巷时掉了队，反而找到一家开着门的手风琴作坊。老人正在给簧片调音，让Mitzi试了一个和弦。Katharina Weiss的旗子在街角晃了两下，不见了。Mitzi在作坊里待到雨停，买了一张手写的乐谱。
  _effects: cash -3 (红色封面的旅行指南（其中2克朗记入债务）); mood 0.09999999999999998; flag +found_real_craft_shop; skill; relation acquaintance with person-materialized-world-vienna-1912-1; knowledge “the accordion workshop on the side lane sells handwritten sc”; relation acquaintance with person-materialized-world-vienna-1912-62; relation acquaintance with person-materialized-world-vienna-1912-1; relation acquaintance with person-materialized-world-vienna-1912-30_

- **1933.01** `r14-edu.sibling-exam::ctx-vienna-reform-1930::milieu-tenement/sibling-failed` tags: education, tutored_sibling, family, exam, reform, medical, schooling, home
  Helene Lechner差三分。回家路上，Helene Lechner一句话也没说，进了门就把自己关在屋里。Mitzi把饭菜放在门口，敲了两下，没有回应。半夜，Mitzi听见屋里有人在翻书，翻到天亮。第二天清早，Helene Lechner出来洗脸，眼睛肿着，问Mitzi能不能再教一年。补考费一克朗。
  _effects: cash -1 (弟弟妹妹的准考证（其中1克朗记入债务）); flag +sibling_failed_exam; relation kin with person:evt:world-vienna-1912:000860:child_

- **1933.01** `vienna-family-divorce/partner-moves-to-shared-housing` tags: family, marriage, divorce, housing, lifecycle, separation, household
  Josef Huber把一只箱子和工票搬去合租房，带走一半锅碗。Mitzi留在Bauer-Wohnung, Leopoldstadt，把空出来的床租给一个学徒，用租金补房租；搬家花掉四克朗，Mitzi在户籍上成了分居的人，夜里听见隔壁床板响，总要愣一下。
  _effects: relation_end partner with person-materialized-world-vienna-1912-44; household; cash -4 (moving and separate goods（其中4克朗记入债务）); mood -0.05999999999999994; role_

  汇款单填到一半，Mitzi听见Emil Lechner在门口咳嗽，便把纸币收回口袋，先去煤店买了一筐煤。夜里他给故乡写信，说这个月厂里活少；信写完，他把去年的一张汇款回执翻出来，看了很久。
  _effects: cash 1 (the money stayed with the household for coal); state_parameter mb17-remittance-web.arrears 2 -> 4; state_parameter mb17-remittance-web.obligation 58 -> 64; relation kin with person:evt:world-vienna-1912:000787:child; mood -0.019999999999999796; flag +mb17_remittance_kept_for_coal_

  楼上住户投诉夜里有人拖椅子，管事的把巡查簿翻到Mitzi的那一页，说签字太潦草，看不出巡过几趟。Mitzi想辩解，却想起那晚确实在楼梯间多站了一刻钟；扣掉的工钱刚好够买一双不漏水的鞋。
  _effects: state_parameter oj18-odd-apprenticeship.trust 45 -> 29; state_parameter oj18-odd-apprenticeship.reliability 55 -> 45; mood -0.050000000000000044; flag +odd_job_scare_

  _effects: state_parameter p23-dangerous-secret.exposure 20 -> 28; state_parameter p23-dangerous-secret.leverage 15 -> 11; state_parameter p23-dangerous-secret.loyalty 55 -> 49; mood -0.020000000000000018_

  Mitzi退烧第二天就回工厂，站到下午，眼前一阵阵发黑。回家后半夜又烧起来，她把湿毛巾搭在额头上，听见自己的心跳撞着枕头。第二天早上，Mitzi又去了，因为请假的扣款比药贵。
  _effects: cash 2 (退烧后的第一碗粥); condition; flag +returned_to_work_too_soon_

  Mitzi讲到一半，前排的人开始看表，后排的人陆续往门口走。Gustav Hofer 2站在门边，想拦又不好拦。最后只剩下三排椅子，Mitzi把稿子念完，声音在空屋子里显得太大。
  _effects: reputation -0.03; mood -0.04999999999999993; flag +speech_hall_empty; cash -1 (the hall rental was not refunded（其中1克朗记入债务）); relation acquaintance with person-materialized-world-vienna-1912-73_

  _effects: health 0.10000000000000002 (医生诊治后退烧); cash -3 (支付医生诊费和药粉（其中3克朗记入债务）); mood -0.030000000000000027_

  Mitzi只收下篮里的面包和豆子，把煤袋推回Helene Weiss手里，说房租自己再想办法，请她们给介绍点零工。Helene Weiss把煤放在走廊窗台，说礼拜四后院有人要拆旧木箱，一赫勒一捆，工钱不多但现结。Mitzi记下门牌，答应一早过去。
  _effects: food; flag +seeking_work; relation acquaintance with person-materialized-world-vienna-1912-1_

  _effects: role; flag +denounced_neighbor; cash -1 (paper, ink and a stamp for the counter-letter（其中1克朗记入债务）); knowledge “你写一封，我写一封；信寄出去后两条街会安静几天，再见面只点头。”_

- **1934.07** `p21-mistaken-identity::ctx-vienna-authoritarian-1934::milieu-tenement/the-photograph-is-wrong` tags: mistaken_identity, police, identity, arrest, public, secret, absurd, high_drama
  Gustav Kral把通缉照片和Mitzi的脸并排放在灯下，发现照片上的人左眉有一道疤，而Mitzi没有。Emil Weiss在旁边作证，说那天晚上Mitzi一直在帮自己搬煤；Gustav Kral把银怀表推回来，表盖内侧的名字却不是Mitzi的。灯罩里有一只死蛾。

- **1934.09** `bc18-ghost.death-certificate::ctx-vienna-authoritarian-1934::milieu-tenement/accepts-the-file` tags: bureaucracy, legal, death, identity, mistake, paperwork, absurd, vienna
  户籍官Anton Hofer为了结掉一桩陈年失踪案，在档案上给Mitzi盖了“推定死亡”。三天后，Mitzi收到一张慰问卡和一本作废的配给簿；同一天，追了半年的债主忽然不再上门。旧货商听说后，花两克朗买走一份抄本，说要给一个躲债的朋友看看格式。
  _effects: cash 2 (卖给旧货商的死亡证明抄本); state_add bc18-bureaucratic-ghost state:world-vienna-1912:814; reputation -0.010000000000000009; relation acquaintance with person-materialized-world-vienna-1912-66; flag +declared_dead_while_alive; mood 0.010000000000000009; flag +bureaucratic_ghost; mood -0.010000000000000009_

- **1935.01** `r13-edu.sell-books::ctx-vienna-authoritarian-1934::milieu-tenement/sell-old-textbooks` tags: education, books, sale, cash, study, vienna, urban, crisis
  Mitzi把用过的课本捆成一摞，提到旧书摊。Elisabeth Kral 2一本本翻，把缺页的挑出来，最后给了五克朗。Mitzi接过钱，看见自己写在页边的笔记被Elisabeth Kral 2随手撕掉，想说什么，还是没说，只把撕下的纸片捡了回来。纸片被油墨粘住，展开了。
  _effects: cash 5 (卖掉的课本); mood -0.030000000000000027; relation acquaintance with person-materialized-world-vienna-1912-46_

  _effects: cash -3 (老师替另一个孩子交了学费（其中3克朗记入债务）); relation kin with person-phase15-Rosa-world-vienna-1912; state_add university-student state:world-vienna-1912:901; education higher; education higher; role_

- **1936.01** `p23-revenge-marriage::ctx-vienna-authoritarian-1934::milieu-tenement/the-groom-writes-another-name` tags: love, revenge, marriage, family_enemy, debt, warmth, high_drama, vienna
  Emil Huber在签名栏停住，笔尖落下的不是自己的名字。Elisabeth Hofer抬头看了一眼，又低下头；Mitzi把婚书转过来，看见那三个字排得很整齐，像早就练过。Emil Huber说对不起，Mitzi却笑了，说这倒省得我装。窗外的乐队还在奏一支旧华尔兹。
  _effects: state_add public-scandal state:world-vienna-1912:898; state_add known-affair state:world-vienna-1912:899; relation rival with person-emil-weiss; mood -0.010000000000000009; flag +wrong_name_on_certificate; flag +known_affair_active_

- **1912.03** `vienna-life-birth-letter-from-the-dead/the-neighbour-takes-it` tags: infancy, family, secret, letter, drama
  Katharina Bauer把信从门缝里抽走，说这封信已经转过三家，不该再往这里送。Josef Bauer问上面写了什么；Katharina Bauer把信折成四折塞进自己口袋，说“都是旧账”。那天夜里，Josef Bauer在厨房坐到很晚。
