- **1912.07** `p22-comic-name-register::ctx-vienna-1912::milieu-tenement/the-clerk-hears-a-sneeze` tags: infancy, family, bureaucracy, name, official, comic, tender, drama
  登记员把Josef打喷嚏的声音当成了名字，低头就写，笔尖还在舌头上蘸了蘸。Josef Bauer抱着孩子，没看见那行字；等到拿着纸回家，才发现孩子的名字里多了一个“嚏”。
  _effects: mood 0.04000000000000001; knowledge “登记簿上孩子的名字被写成了喷嚏声。”; flag +registered_as_hatschi; state_add p22-family-legend state:world-vienna-1912:14_

- **1913.01** `vienna-life-infant-first-smile/smile-at-the-lamp` tags: infancy, family, first, warm
  Josef Bauer把Josef抱到窗边，煤汽灯罩上有一圈晃动的光。孩子盯着看了很久，忽然笑出声，口水滴在Josef Bauer的袖口上。屋里的人都停了一下，像是听见了什么了不起的消息。
  _effects: mood 0.04000000000000001; health 0.010000000000000009 (a good day)_

- **1913.01** `state_tick:p22-family-legend/the-story-changes-hands/a-detail-goes-missing` tags: p22-family-legend, family_legend, family, story, secret, inheritance, promise, memory
  又过了一年，故事里最要紧的那件东西不见了：先是名字，后来是地点。Josef问起时，大人说“你记错了”；可柜子最底层那只空盒子，谁也没有扔，锁却换过了，钥匙也不见了。
  _effects: state_parameter p22-family-legend.secrecy 10 -> 14; state_parameter p22-family-legend.danger 5 -> 8; state_parameter p22-family-legend.warmth 65 -> 63; mood -0.01_

- **1913.02** `p22-warm-fathers-letter::ctx-klosterneuburg-rural-1912::milieu-tenement/the-recipe-arrives` tags: infancy, family, letter, uncle, warm, melancholy, memory, drama
  信是叔叔死前写的，在邮袋里压了两年才到。Josef Bauer拆开时先笑出声：里面没有遗言，只有一张李子饺子的做法和一个关于祖母的笨笑话。那天晚上，全家真的吃了饺子。
  _effects: state_add p22-family-legend state:world-vienna-1912:40; mood 0.04000000000000001; knowledge “家里有一封叔叔留下的信，里面写着李子饺子的做法。”_

- **1913.05** `p22-strange-cradle-secret::ctx-klosterneuburg-rural-1912::milieu-tenement/the-ticket-is-redeemed` tags: infancy, family, cradle, secret, inheritance, strange, dark, drama
  Josef Bauer拿着当票去赎，取回一把银勺子，勺柄上刻着一个看不清的字母。回家后把勺子塞进Josef手里，说“用吧”；孩子敲着桌沿，勺子发出很轻的响声，像在回答什么。
  _effects: cash -5 (redeeming the pawn ticket); state_add p22-family-legend state:world-vienna-1912:41; flag +spoon_returned_

- **1914.01** `vienna-life-toddler-wrong-cradle/the-blanket-is-wrong` tags: infancy, family, rumour, identity, comic, drama
  Anna Moser盯着Josef的摇篮看了很久，说这床蓝被子不是这家人的。Josef Bauer把被子翻过来，角落绣着一个陌生的姓；Josef却抓着被角不放，像是认得它。Anna Moser退后一步，说“我什么都没说”。
  _effects: flag +wrong_cradle_blanket; knowledge “摇篮里的蓝被子绣着一个陌生的姓。”; mood -0.030000000000000013_

- **1914.01** `state_tick:p22-family-legend/the-story-changes-hands/a-detail-goes-missing` tags: p22-family-legend, family_legend, family, story, secret, inheritance, promise, memory
  又过了一年，故事里最要紧的那件东西不见了：先是名字，后来是地点。Josef问起时，大人说“你记错了”；可柜子最底层那只空盒子，谁也没有扔，锁却换过了，钥匙也不见了。
  _effects: state_parameter p22-family-legend.secrecy 60 -> 64; state_parameter p22-family-legend.danger 25 -> 28; state_parameter p22-family-legend.warmth 55 -> 53; mood -0.01_

- **1915.01** `vienna-life-child-family-legend/stolen-inheritance` tags: childhood, family, secret, story
  Josef Bauer说，家里本来有一件东西，后来被“更会说话的人”拿走了。Josef问是什么，Josef Bauer只说是一块表，表盖里刻着姓。孩子记住了“表盖里刻着姓”，却没有记住那个姓。
  _effects: knowledge “家里有一块表盖里刻着姓的表，被一个更会说话的人拿走了。”; flag +family_legend_watch_

- **1915.01** `state_reaction:p22-family-legend/someone-asks-again` tags: p22-family-legend, family_legend, family, story, secret, inheritance, promise, memory
  又有人问起Josef家的那件旧事。大人先笑了一下，才说“都是很早以前了”；问的人点点头，眼睛却往摇篮那边看，手指在杯沿上停了一会儿，谁也没有接话，窗外有风。
  _effects: state_parameter p22-family-legend.secrecy 64 -> 68; state_parameter p22-family-legend.warmth 53 -> 55; mood 0.01_
  _causes: evt:world-vienna-1912:000403_

- **1915.02** `p22-warm-night-move::ctx-klosterneuburg-rural-1912::milieu-tenement/the-cradle-goes-first` tags: childhood, family, move, neighbor, tenement, warm, bittersweet, drama
  搬家定在夜里，怕白天遇见旧房东。Emil Weiss在前面打灯，Josef Bauer抱着摇篮，Josef抱着一只枕头跟在后面。新屋有一扇能看见院子的窗，孩子把枕头放在窗台上，说这是给月亮留的。
  _effects: mood 0.05; relation acquaintance with person-phase15-Josef-world-vienna-1912; cash -3 (moving at night); flag +moved_at_night; state_add p22-family-legend state:world-vienna-1912:48_

- **1915.02** `p22-comic-mistaken-child::ctx-klosterneuburg-rural-1912::milieu-tenement/the-real-boy-laughs` tags: childhood, family, market, neighbor, mistaken_identity, comic, tender, drama
  真正的孩子从摊子底下爬出来，笑得直不起腰，说奶奶每个月都要认错一次。Josef跟着笑，两个孩子在樱桃核堆里玩了很久，脚下咯吱响，直到各自的家长来拎人。
  _effects: mood 0.049999999999999996; relation acquaintance with person-phase15-Josef-world-vienna-1912; flag +hide_and_seek_

- **1915.02** `p22-strange-grandmother-voice::ctx-klosterneuburg-rural-1912::milieu-tenement/the-child-is-playing` tags: childhood, family, grandmother, memory, omen, strange, eerie, drama
  Josef Bauer认定孩子在学大人说话，笑着拍手。Josef愣了一下，又把那句话说得更响；Anna Bauer也跟着学，三个人的声音在厨房里撞来撞去，最后谁也说不清原句是什么，一起笑了。
  _effects: mood 0.020000000000000004; relation kin with person-phase15-Josef-world-vienna-1912; flag +saying_is_a_game_

- **1916.01** `vienna-life-child-first-friend/stolen-marble` tags: childhood, friendship, family, comic
  Klara把Josef的玻璃弹珠藏进鞋里，被当场看见。Klara说只是“替你拿着”，Josef没有揭穿，也没有再拿出第二颗。两个人蹲在墙根，把一只蚂蚁看了很久，谁也没有先回家。
  _effects: relation friend with person-phase15-Klara-world-vienna-1912; skill; mood -0.009999999999999995_

- **1916.01** `state_tick:p22-family-legend/the-story-changes-hands/told-at-the-table` tags: p22-family-legend, family_legend, family, story, secret, inheritance, promise, memory
  又过了一年，Josef家的故事在饭桌上换了新的讲法：多了一句笑话，少了一处哭声。听的人把汤匙放下，等下一句；讲故事的人却先去看窗子有没有关好，谁也没有先动筷子。
  _effects: state_parameter p22-family-legend.warmth 67 -> 70; state_parameter p22-family-legend.secrecy 39 -> 37; state_parameter p22-family-legend.promise 55 -> 57; mood 0.009999999999999995_

- **1916.02** `vienna-life-child-inherited-wish/the-dead-grandmothers-wish` tags: childhood, family, wish, grief, uncanny, drama
  Josef在饭桌上忽然说：“等我长大了，要把那间屋子买回来。”Josef Bauer的勺子停在半空，因为这句话是去世多年的外婆常说的，连“那间屋子”都指得一模一样。Josef说完就低头喝汤，好像只是随口。
  _effects: flag +inherited_wish_house; knowledge “孩子说要把“那间屋子”买回来，和去世的外婆说过的一模一样。”; mood -0.010000000000000009_

- **1916.02** `state_reaction:p22-family-legend/someone-asks-again` tags: p22-family-legend, family_legend, family, story, secret, inheritance, promise, memory
  又有人问起Josef家的那件旧事。大人先笑了一下，才说“都是很早以前了”；问的人点点头，眼睛却往摇篮那边看，手指在杯沿上停了一会儿，谁也没有接话，窗外有风。
  _effects: state_parameter p22-family-legend.secrecy 35 -> 39; state_parameter p22-family-legend.warmth 65 -> 67; mood 0.009999999999999995_
  _causes: evt:world-vienna-1912:000552_

- **1916.10** `p9-childhood-household.half-bed/chalk-line` tags: childhood, family, household, home, siblings, illness, neighbors, injury
  “过线了。”Josef用粉笔敲了敲床板。Elisabeth Bauer不服，翻身时故意把腿伸过来。Josef顶了一下，Elisabeth Bauer又顶回来。被子被蹬到床脚，谁也没去捡，天亮时两个人都缩着肩膀，眼圈发青。
  _effects: health -0.030000000000000027 (夜里推搡没睡踏实); relation kin with person-elisabeth-bauer_

- **1918.01** `p9-gap-childhood-chores.mind-sibling/sibling-carried-afternoon` tags: childhood, household, chores, school, task, daily, sibling, care
  Ferdinand Bauer哭得打嗝，手指一直朝井台方向指。Josef蹲下把Ferdinand Bauer驮上背，沿着墙根走，数砖缝给Ferdinand Bauer听：一、二、三……数到第四圈，背上的呼吸才匀了。天黑时Josef的胳膊麻得抬不过肩，Katharina Bauer回来接过孩子，Josef扶着门框甩了好一会儿手。
  _effects: health -0.030000000000000027 (背弟妹走了一下午，胳膊酸痛); skill; relation kin with person:evt:world-vienna-1912:000513:child; relation acquaintance with person-materialized-world-vienna-1912-4_

- **1918.02** `state_reaction:p22-family-legend/someone-asks-again` tags: p22-family-legend, family_legend, family, story, secret, inheritance, promise, memory
  又有人问起Josef家的那件旧事。大人先笑了一下，才说“都是很早以前了”；问的人点点头，眼睛却往摇篮那边看，手指在杯沿上停了一会儿，谁也没有接话，窗外有风。
  _effects: state_parameter p22-family-legend.secrecy 37 -> 41; state_parameter p22-family-legend.warmth 70 -> 72; mood 0.009999999999999995_
  _causes: evt:world-vienna-1912:000887_

- **1918.03** `vienna-childhood-street.market-errand/errand-lost-coin` tags: childhood, street, social, home
  菜摊前的地面是湿的。Josef弯腰捡土豆时，一枚铜板从指缝里滑出去，顺着水沟格栅掉了下去。Josef把剩下的钱摊在柜台上，让摊主帮忙数，还是少了三十克鲁泽。晚上Katharina Bauer把钱一枚枚排开，问了三遍怎么少的，Josef每答一次，声音就低一点。
  _effects: cash -0.3 (铜板掉进下水道); relation kin with person-katharina-bauer; mood -0.06_

- **1918.07** `p22-drama-inheritance-papers::ctx-klosterneuburg-rural-1912::milieu-tenement/the-seal-is-wrong` tags: childhood, family, inheritance, papers, secret, official, dramatic, dark
  Josef把墨水瓶碰倒在继承文书上。Josef Bauer一边擦一边看见，纸角的火漆印是去年的，文书却写着十年前的日期。公证人伸手去够那张纸，慢了一步，脸一下子白了。
  _effects: knowledge “继承文书上的火漆印比文书日期新。”; flag +wrong_seal; mood -0.030000000000000027; cash -2 (ink and a ruined envelope); state_add p22-family-legend state:world-vienna-1912:80; relation acquaintance with person-materialized-world-vienna-1912-22_

- **1918.09** `iw18-friend.chair-by-the-stove::ctx-klosterneuburg-rural-1912::milieu-tenement/a-guest-named-by-the-child` tags: childhood, play, imaginary_friend, solitude, family, wonder, rural, agriculture
  Josef从碗橱里多拿了一只缺口的盘子，摆在灶边那把没人坐的椅子上，还认真地告诉屋里的人：今天有客人。盘子里放着一小块土豆和一片面包边。Josef Bauer问客人叫什么，Josef说了一个从没听过的名字。
  _effects: state_add iw18-imaginary-friend state:world-vienna-1912:79; food; mood 0.04000000000000001; flag +invisible_guest_named; flag +imaginary_friend_active_

- **1919.01** `state_tick:iw18-imaginary-friend/friend-year/still-there` tags: iw18-imaginary-friend, childhood, play, imaginary_friend, solitude, imagination, secret, state
  又过了一年，Josef还给那把空椅子留着位置。椅面上有一道被裤子磨亮的痕迹，大人说那是旧漆；Josef知道那是朋友坐出来的。冬天煤少，Josef把最暖的位置让给椅子，自己坐在床沿。
  _effects: state_parameter iw18-imaginary-friend.closeness 60 -> 63; state_parameter iw18-imaginary-friend.secrecy 25 -> 27; mood 0.020000000000000004_

- **1919.01** `state_tick:p22-family-legend/the-story-changes-hands/told-at-the-table` tags: p22-family-legend, family_legend, family, story, secret, inheritance, promise, memory
  又过了一年，Josef家的故事在饭桌上换了新的讲法：多了一句笑话，少了一处哭声。听的人把汤匙放下，等下一句；讲故事的人却先去看窗子有没有关好，谁也没有先动筷子。
  _effects: state_parameter p22-family-legend.warmth 30 -> 33; state_parameter p22-family-legend.secrecy 75 -> 73; state_parameter p22-family-legend.promise 65 -> 67; mood 0.009999999999999995_

- **1919.01** `p9-gap-childhood-chores.market-line/line-half-flour` tags: childhood, household, chores, school, task, daily, market, food
  队伍从面包房门口拐进巷子，Josef把布袋夹在腋下，学着前面人的样子，用胳膊肘顶住后面人的篮子。窗口的伙计舀面粉时，Josef踮脚看着秤，直到半袋面倒进袋口。挤出人群时鞋跟被踩掉，Josef单脚跳了两步，在泥里摸回鞋，索性赤脚走回家。
  _effects: food; health -0.020000000000000018 (赤脚走石板路磨伤脚底); skill; relation acquaintance with person-materialized-world-vienna-1912-13; relation acquaintance with person-materialized-world-vienna-1912-24_

- **1919.02** `kernel-social.first-meeting::ctx-klosterneuburg-rural-1912::milieu-tenement/first-meeting.coal-basket` tags: social, friendship, first_meeting, childhood, neighbor, everyday, rural, agriculture
  在合住的出租公寓的楼梯上，Josef抱着一筐煤，Emil从楼上下来，伸手托住筐底。两人把煤抬到四楼，在窗台上分了一块黑面包；此后上学、提水都结伴，谁家晚归就替谁留门。煤灰落在鞋面上，他们用同一块破布擦手。
  _effects: role; flag +childhood_friend; knowledge “那个总在楼梯上帮忙的孩子住在同一栋楼的顶层。”_

- **1919.03** `iw18-secret.cellar-door::ctx-klosterneuburg-rural-1912::milieu-tenement/saw-the-bundle` tags: childhood, secret, family, cellar, knowledge, fear, rural, agriculture
  地窖门虚掩着，Josef下去找腌菜坛，看见Josef Bauer蹲在煤堆后面，把一包用油纸裹好的东西塞进墙缝。Josef Bauer听见脚步，回头时脸上还带着灰；Josef说自己只是来拿坛子，声音比平时高。
  _effects: state_add iw18-child-secret state:world-vienna-1912:90; knowledge “大人在酒窖的墙缝里藏了一包用油纸裹着的东西。”; relation kin with person-josef-bauer; mood -0.020000000000000004; flag +saw_cellar_bundle; flag +child_secret_active_

- **1919.09** `iw18-friend.sugar-cube::ctx-klosterneuburg-rural-1912::milieu-tenement/the-friend-refuses` tags: childhood, play, imaginary_friend, food, family, comic, rural, agriculture
  Josef把方糖推到空椅子前，等了一会儿，又收回来，说朋友今天不想吃甜的。Josef Bauer问是不是朋友不喜欢糖，Josef认真点头，还补充说朋友只喜欢硬面包的边。第二天，碟子里果然只剩下一圈面包边。
  _effects: state_parameter iw18-imaginary-friend.boldness 40 -> 45; state_parameter iw18-imaginary-friend.closeness 63 -> 61; food; flag +friend_has_tastes_

- **1920.01** `iw18-friend.takes-the-blame::ctx-klosterneuburg-rural-1912::milieu-tenement/the-sibling-knows` tags: childhood, imaginary_friend, blame, family, secret, small_cruelty, rural, agriculture
  Klara从门后探出头，说看见是Josef自己碰倒的。Josef赶紧把Klara拉到楼梯间，答应把攒了半年的彩色玻璃珠分一半。Klara想了一会儿，说还要替自己写一页大字；两个人在楼梯上坐了很久，牛奶在厨房里慢慢渗进砖缝。
  _effects: state_parameter iw18-imaginary-friend.secrecy 27 -> 32; relation kin with person-phase15-Klara-world-vienna-1912; cash -1 (milk lost on the kitchen floor); flag +sibling_keeps_friend_secret; mood -0.020000000000000004_

- **1920.01** `state_tick:iw18-imaginary-friend/friend-year/still-there` tags: iw18-imaginary-friend, childhood, play, imaginary_friend, solitude, imagination, secret, state
  又过了一年，Josef还给那把空椅子留着位置。椅面上有一道被裤子磨亮的痕迹，大人说那是旧漆；Josef知道那是朋友坐出来的。冬天煤少，Josef把最暖的位置让给椅子，自己坐在床沿。
  _effects: state_parameter iw18-imaginary-friend.closeness 61 -> 64; state_parameter iw18-imaginary-friend.secrecy 32 -> 34; mood 0.020000000000000004_

- **1920.01** `state_exit:p22-family-legend` tags: p22-family-legend, family_legend, family, story, secret, inheritance, promise, memory
  Josef不再处于「家族传说」；条件已经改变。
  _effects: state_remove p22-family-legend state:world-vienna-1912:80_

- **1920.01** `state_tick:iw18-child-secret/secret-year/held` tags: iw18-child-secret, childhood, secret, knowledge, guilt, family, fear, state
  这一年Josef把秘密藏得更深。走过地窖门口时，Josef会故意哼歌；饭桌上有人提起那件事，Josef就把汤勺在碗底转三圈。秘密没有变大，却像一颗卡在鞋里的石子，走每一步都知道它在。
  _effects: state_parameter iw18-child-secret.weight 50 -> 54; state_parameter iw18-child-secret.tellUrge 45 -> 48; mood -0.010000000000000002_

- **1920.01** `p9-gap-childhood-chores.mind-sibling/sibling-falls-at-well` tags: childhood, household, chores, school, task, daily, sibling, care
  土豆滚进墙角的煤灰里，Josef弯腰去追，再抬头时Ferdinand Bauer已经坐在井台边，裤腿湿了一片，哭声一抽一抽。Josef Bauer把孩子抱进灶间，Josef跟在后面，手指抠着门框。Katharina Bauer回来没问经过，只把Josef的碗收走一半，说“看个人都看不住”。
  _effects: mood -0.06; relation kin with person-katharina-bauer_

- **1920.02** `iw18-secret.adult-asks::ctx-klosterneuburg-rural-1912::milieu-tenement/the-child-refuses` tags: childhood, secret, family, pressure, fear, rural, agriculture, small_town
  Josef说“我看见了”，声音不高，却把厨房里的水壶声压了下去。Josef Bauer的手抬起来又放下，最后只把门打开，让孩子出去。Josef走到楼梯口才发现自己忘了拿外套；那天晚上，饭桌上多摆了一副碗筷，没有人坐。
  _effects: state_parameter iw18-child-secret.weight 55 -> 50; state_parameter iw18-child-secret.tellUrge 35 -> 45; state_parameter iw18-child-secret.discovered false -> true; relation kin with person-josef-bauer; mood -0.06_

- **1920.12** `kernel-child.punishment::ctx-klosterneuburg-rural-1912::milieu-tenement/edu-punish-belt` tags: childhood, family, punishment, neighbor, conflict, rural, agriculture, small_town
  Josef打碎了厨房的水罐，碎片扫进簸箕时还在响。Josef Bauer解下皮带，让Josef转过身。三下之后，Josef Bauer的手停在半空，最后把皮带扔回椅背，自己去院里提水。Josef站着没动，听见水桶碰井沿的声音。
  _effects: health -0.040000000000000036 (beaten with a belt); relation kin with person-josef-bauer; flag +beaten_for_broken_jar; mood -0.10000000000000002_

- **1921.01** `iw18-secret.sibling-bargain::ctx-klosterneuburg-rural-1912::milieu-tenement/a-marble-for-silence` tags: childhood, secret, sibling, bargain, family, small_cruelty, rural, agriculture
  Emil从柜子后面摸出那张揉皱的糖纸，说只要分一颗彩色玻璃珠，就不把地窖的事说出去。Josef蹲在床边数珠子，数到第三颗时又收回去一颗。交易在楼梯间完成，两个人都没有看对方的眼睛。
  _effects: state_parameter iw18-child-secret.weight 54 -> 57; state_parameter iw18-child-secret.tellUrge 48 -> 50; mood -0.019999999999999997; flag +sibling_bought_silence_

- **1921.01** `state_expire:iw18-imaginary-friend` tags: iw18-imaginary-friend, childhood, play, imaginary_friend, solitude, imagination, secret, state
  Josef的「一个只有孩子看得见的朋友」到期；相关安排结束。
  _effects: state_remove iw18-imaginary-friend state:world-vienna-1912:79; flag -imaginary_friend_active_

- **1921.01** `state_tick:iw18-child-secret/secret-year/slipped` tags: iw18-child-secret, childhood, secret, knowledge, guilt, family, fear, state
  话是在洗碗时滑出来的。Josef说“那天我看见了”，说完立刻把水龙头开到最大。大人没有听清，问了一句“看见什么”；Josef摇头，说水太凉。那天夜里，Josef第一次希望自己当时没有下楼。
  _effects: state_parameter iw18-child-secret.tellUrge 50 -> 42; state_parameter iw18-child-secret.weight 57 -> 51; state_parameter iw18-child-secret.discovered true -> true; mood 0.01999999999999999_

- **1921.01** `p9-gap-childhood-chores.market-line/line-half-flour` tags: childhood, household, chores, school, task, daily, market, food
  面粉袋压在胸口，越抱越沉。Josef前面的大婶一直往后靠，Josef就把膝盖顶住对方的篮子，硬留出半步。买到半袋面粉后，Josef的鞋跟留在人堆里，找回来时鞋面全是泥。回家路上布袋换了两次肩，赤脚踩过石板时脚底发麻。
  _effects: food; health -0.020000000000000018 (赤脚走石板路磨伤脚底); skill; relation acquaintance with person-materialized-world-vienna-1912-21_

- **1921.04** `r1-child.grandfather-watch::ctx-klosterneuburg-rural-1912::milieu-tenement/pawned-for-medicine` tags: inheritance, grandfather, watch, grief, family, rural, agriculture, small_town
  祖父住院时，Josef Bauer把怀表送进了当铺，换回药和煤。Josef在抽屉里找到当票，上面的日期是祖父生日。赎当的期限是三个月，Josef Bauer每天数一遍钱，数到第二个月就不再数了。
  _effects: cash 4 (祖父的怀表); condition; flag +watch_pawned_for_medicine; relation kin with person-josef-bauer; knowledge “the watch is in pawn and the ticket expires in three months”_

- **1921.07** `r2-rural.child-farm-labor::ctx-klosterneuburg-rural-1912::milieu-tenement/sent-to-pick-grapes` tags: rural, child_labour, harvest, school, injury, agriculture, small_town, peace
  Josef Bauer把一只小篮子挂在Josef脖子上，说捡满一篮就回家。葡萄行里的刺划破手指，Josef用嘴吸了吸，继续捡。Elisabeth Winter过秤时多给了半个铜板，说这孩子手快。
  _effects: cash 1 (八岁的手和镰刀); health -0.050000000000000044 (八岁的手和镰刀); flag +child_harvest_labor; skill; relation acquaintance with person-materialized-world-vienna-1912-27; relation acquaintance with person-materialized-world-vienna-1912-27; relation acquaintance with person-materialized-world-vienna-1912-34_

- **1921.08** `iw18-school.ink-pot::ctx-klosterneuburg-rural-1912::milieu-tenement/the-teacher-says-nothing` tags: childhood, school, cruelty, shame, teacher, ink, rural, agriculture
  Maria Lechner 2从讲台抽屉里拿出一块湿布，把桌上的墨迹慢慢擦掉，又把Josef的抄写本翻到新的一页，说“重抄”。整个教室只有布擦过木头的沙沙声；Josef低头写字时，第一次明白有些大人不需要发火也能让人害怕。
  _effects: mood -0.01999999999999999; relation acquaintance with person-materialized-world-vienna-1912-33; skill; flag +ink_wiped_in_silence; relation acquaintance with person-materialized-world-vienna-1912-33_

  巡警说街上卖火柴要牌照，把Josef的盒子收走了。Josef跟了两条街，Anna Hofer在门口喊他回来，塞给他一把客人落下的火柴，说别在巡警眼皮底下卖。Josef把空盒子留在窗台上，里面放了一根没卖出去的火柴。
  _effects: cash 1 (咖啡馆门外的火柴); reputation -0.05; flag +matches_confiscated; relation friend with person-materialized-world-vienna-1912-29; relation acquaintance with person-materialized-world-vienna-1912-29_

- **1922.01** `p9-gap-childhood-chores.laundry-help/laundry-three-heller` tags: childhood, household, chores, school, task, daily, laundry, work
  “拽平，别留褶。”Luise Lechner在绳子那头喊。Josef踮着脚把床单拉直，碱水把指缝泡得发白。收工时天已经黑了，Luise Lechner数了三赫勒，又在围裙上擦干手才递过来。Josef把钱塞进袜筒，回家路上一直攥着，虎口的裂口沾了汗，火辣辣地疼。
  _effects: cash 0.03 (帮邻居晾床单挣的三赫勒); health -0.030000000000000027 (碱水泡手，虎口裂开); relation acquaintance with person-materialized-world-vienna-1912-25; relation acquaintance with person-materialized-world-vienna-1912-25; relation acquaintance with person-materialized-world-vienna-1912-20_

- **1922.09** `vienna-childhood-street.cigarette-card/card-trade` tags: childhood, street, social, home
  “三张换一张。”Klara把皇帝像在Josef眼前晃了晃。Josef数出三张攒了半年的烟画，一张张递过去。皇帝像到手还没捂热，Klara已经推开杂货铺的门，用同一张卡换了糖。Josef把卡举到光下看，觉得皇帝的脸有点歪。
  _effects: mood 0.04000000000000001; relation friend with person-phase15-Klara-world-vienna-1912_

- **1923.01** `p9-gap-childhood-chores.market-line/line-sold-out-potatoes` tags: childhood, household, chores, school, task, daily, market, food
  “卖完了。”三个字从窗口里传出来，Josef后面的队伍立刻散了一半。Josef没走，趴在窗台上看伙计扫面屑，直到人家挥手赶人。旁边摊子的土豆带着泥，Josef用围裙兜住，一枚一枚数铜板，回家报账时连找零都说清了。
  _effects: food; mood -0.03; skill; relation acquaintance with person-materialized-world-vienna-1912-43_

  _effects: condition; flag +left_school_after_exam_failure; role; relation colleague with person-materialized-world-vienna-1912-45; education apprenticeship; relation acquaintance with person-materialized-world-vienna-1912-45_

  Anna Bauer的箱子摊在床板上，里面是两件衬衫、一双旧靴和母亲塞的一小袋面粉。Josef把攒了半年的零钱数到第三遍，一共一克朗二十赫勒，全塞进箱角的袜子里。Anna Bauer看见了，没说话，只把箱子扣上，铜扣响了一声。
  _effects: cash -1.2 (把攒下的零钱塞给离家的兄姐); mood -0.03_

  Josef Bauer把Josef生父的旧大衣拿去当铺，说家里不缺这件碍事的衣服。Josef追到巷口，把当票抢回来，撕成两半。Katharina Bauer站在两人中间，最后谁也没有帮。
  _effects: mood -0.10000000000000003; condition; flag +conflict_over_dead_fathers_coat; relation rival with person-josef-bauer; relation rival with person-katharina-bauer_

- **1925.01** `p9-childhood-apprenticeship.cover-for-sibling/delivery-paid` tags: adolescence, work, apprenticeship, labour, training, family, task, task:family
  Josef背着床单筐走过两条街，肩膀被带子磨出一道红印。第二户人家的女佣数了件数，把工钱用纸包好。Josef回家时Emil还躺着，Josef把纸包塞到枕头底下，没叫醒人。晚饭时Emil自己坐起来端碗，Josef才觉得肩膀没那么沉。
  _effects: cash 1.6 (替妹妹送床单的计件工钱); health -0.030000000000000027 (背着湿床单走了几个街区); food; relation acquaintance with person-materialized-world-vienna-1912-47_

- **1925.02** `yf18-firstlook.tram-glove::ctx-vienna-reform-1930::milieu-tenement/return-it-at-the-gate` tags: first_love, love, tram, glove, youth, courtship, reform, medical
  下一站，Josef追下车，把那只手套递还给Elisabeth Bauer。Elisabeth Bauer说了一声谢谢，手指在毛线手套里缩了一下；Josef回到车上才发现自己坐过了两站，只好沿着环城路走回家。
  _effects: state_add yf18-first-awakening state:world-vienna-1912:140; mood 0.03; flag +yf18_first_awakening_active_

  Ferdinand Hofer把半年前的作业本找出来，纸边已经发黄。Josef坐在最后一排，铅笔短得握不住，Luise Stein 2从桌下递来一截新的。放学后，Ferdinand Hofer留下他补算术，黑板上的粉笔字擦了又写。

  Josef在厨房里对着镜子练了七遍开场白，第二天却在离Elisabeth Bauer三步远的地方拐进面包店。Josef买了一袋并不需要的盐，回家路上把开场白又想了一遍；这一次，连标点都更熟练了。

  “开春再来。”Franz Hofer说完就转身去接纸车。Josef站在门槛外，把这句话在心里复述了一遍，弯腰捡起地上的纸边。两个赫勒落在掌心时还带着围裙的体温。回家那条街上有三家亮着灯的作坊，Josef一家一家看过去，把位置记住。
  _effects: cash 0.2 (替作坊捡纸边得到的两个赫勒); mood -0.03999999999999998; flag +seeking_work; relation acquaintance with person-materialized-world-vienna-1912-3_

  教堂音乐会的独唱轮到Josef，第一句拉丁文还稳，唱到“荣耀”那个高音时，嗓子忽然劈了一下。Josef看见Josef Bauer在第三排低下头，Ferdinand Hofer在风琴旁边打手势让继续；Josef用假声把剩下的两小节唱完，声音细得像一根线。

  作坊里全是松木味。Buchdruckerei Winter & Söhne把一块方木夹进台钳，让Josef推刨子。Josef推了十分钟，木花卷到脚背上，刨面总算平了。Buchdruckerei Winter & Söhne用拇指摸了摸，收下Josef，从钱盒里数出三克朗，说是头一周的饭钱。Katharina Bauer在门口把围裙攥得很紧。
  _effects: cash 3 ({subject}学徒首周的膳食预付款); education apprenticeship; reputation 0.1; relation kin with person-katharina-bauer_

  _effects: cash -0.1 (工人青年会月费); skill; relation friend with person-phase15-Mitzi-world-vienna-1912; flag +workers_youth_member; mood 0.03999999999999998_

  考试周里，Josef把课本摊在桌上，眼睛却一直看着窗外那条电车线。笔记的页边多了几个没有写完的名字，Josef用橡皮擦掉，纸面起了毛。

  试用日的白围裙浆得发硬。Berta Winter让Josef先擦餐厅的十二把椅子，再用手背试炉门温度；Wiener Psychoanalytische Vereinigung戴着戒指的手指在椅背上一划，举到窗边看。Josef说上一家做了两年，Wiener Psychoanalytische Vereinigung问为什么离开，Berta Winter在门后轻轻咳了一声。

- **1928.04** `vienna-adolescence.brothers-torn-jacket/sell-the-coat` tags: adolescence, school, work, family, social
  巷口的风把外套下摆吹起来。旧货贩子闻了闻领子，说给两克朗。Josef把钱卷进袜筒，回家前在楼梯口站了一会儿。Katharina Bauer发现衣钩空了，问了三遍，Josef都说没看见，脚趾在鞋里把硬币越踩越紧。
  _effects: cash 2 (卖掉哥哥旧外套); relation kin with person-katharina-bauer; mood -0.03999999999999998_

- **1929.01** `r13-edu.boarding-school::ctx-vienna-reform-1930::milieu-tenement/admitted-to-dormitory` tags: education, boarding_school, residence, family, scholarship, reform, medical, schooling
  Josef把铁皮箱绑在自行车后座，骑了三十里到寄宿学校。舍监检查行李，把家里的腌菜罐没收，说会招老鼠。Josef在熄灯后偷偷把罐子从垃圾堆捡回来，藏在床底，第一顿饭就着腌菜吃完了两碗粥。第一个星期Josef把腌菜分给同屋，换回三张邮票和一支不漏水的钢笔。
  _effects: flag +boarding_school_admitted; education secondary_

- **1930.01** `p23-lovers-ledger::ctx-vienna-reform-1930::milieu-tenement/the-ledger-in-the-safe` tags: love, desire, betrayal, ledger, secret, blackmail, high_drama, reform
  Josef没有把账本寄出去，而是租了银行最小的一只保险箱。钥匙挂在Josef的脖子上，账本上的名字却开始出现在Josef的梦里。Maria Lechner 3问起那本旧册子，Josef说已经烧了；说这话时，Josef的手一直按着胸口。钥匙很小，锁孔却很深。
  _effects: state_add p23-dangerous-secret state:world-vienna-1912:405; knowledge “the ledger is in a bank safe”; mood -0.010000000000000009; flag +ledger_hidden; relation acquaintance with person-materialized-world-vienna-1912-68_

  _effects: state_parameter p23-dangerous-secret.exposure 30 -> 38; state_parameter p23-dangerous-secret.leverage 75 -> 71; state_parameter p23-dangerous-secret.loyalty 45 -> 39; mood -0.020000000000000018_

- **1930.01** `task-vienna-activist.join-union/join-union-foreman-warning` tags: politics, activism, union, party, leaflet, meeting, task, task:politics
  入会的事第二天就传到车间。工头在过道里拦住他，说厂里不反对工人交朋友，但下个月的排班要看表现。他回到机器旁，发现自己的工具柜被挪到了最靠门的位置，冷风直往领口灌。
  _effects: role; mood -0.06000000000000005; flag +foreman_watching_union_activity_

- **1930.02** `vienna-tenement-neighbors.lost_room_key/key_stolen_by_stranger` tags: home, social, family, housing
  Josef记得出门时钥匙还在，回来却没了。Josef Huber说下午看见一个穿灰外套的男人在楼道里转悠，手里晃着一把铜钥匙。两人下楼查看，房门虚掩，抽屉被拉开，攒着买煤的六克朗不见了。Josef Huber陪着Josef去街角警察岗亭报了案，警察记下灰外套，说会留意。
  _effects: cash -6 (被盗的买煤钱); relation friend with person-materialized-world-vienna-1912-44; mood -0.09999999999999998; relation acquaintance with person-materialized-world-vienna-1912-44_

  有人抱怨守夜人太多，踩坏了楼梯木板。楼里的人围着看，谁也没先走开。
  _effects: relation acquaintance with person-materialized-world-vienna-1912-43; reputation -0.030000000000000002; mood -0.05999999999999994_

- **1930.02** `r3-crime.crime-scene::ctx-vienna-reform-1930::milieu-tenement/kept-the-crowd-back` tags: crime, police, evidence, street, mistake, reform, medical, schooling
  Josef把围观的人挡在粉笔线外，又用一只木箱扣住那半枚鞋印。Anton Hofer来的时候，先看了看地上的血，再看了看Josef，说“你倒懂得不少”。Mitzi Lechner在人群里喊“我家孩子还没回来”，Helene Kral 2让她先登记。
  _effects: reputation 0.06; flag +preserved_the_crime_scene; role; relation friend with person-materialized-world-vienna-1912-66; knowledge “the footprint was covered before the crowd arrived”; relation acquaintance with person-materialized-world-vienna-1912-66; relation acquaintance with person-materialized-world-vienna-1912-12_

- **1930.03** `vienna-tenement-neighbors.sick_child_doorstep/borrows_coal_risk` tags: home, social, family, housing, injury
  Josef把自家煤箱里仅剩的四块煤分了两块给Grete Moser，自己家那一夜没生火，裹着大衣睡的。孩子烧退了，Grete Moser第二天还煤时多塞了一把粗盐。Josef当天下午就开始流清鼻涕，第三天咳得直不起腰。
  _effects: health -0.07999999999999996 (受凉咳嗽); food; relation friend with person-materialized-world-vienna-1912-24_

  _effects: food; relation acquaintance with person-materialized-world-vienna-1912-52; mood 0.050000000000000044_

- **1931.01** `p23-wedding-dress::ctx-vienna-reform-1930::milieu-tenement/she-wears-it-to-another-wedding` tags: love, desire, obsession, stalker, wedding, fear, high_drama, reform
  Josef把裙子穿去了Maria Lechner 3表妹的婚礼。席间有人问是谁裁的，Josef说是旧货店；话没说完，Ferdinand Weiss从邻桌站起来，说领口的针脚是自己缝的。音乐还在响，Maria Lechner 3的叉子停在盘子上，整个大厅忽然安静得能听见冰化开的声音。
  _effects: state_add public-scandal state:world-vienna-1912:451; state_add known-affair state:world-vienna-1912:452; relation rival with person-materialized-world-vienna-1912-60; reputation -0.04; mood -0.040000000000000036; flag +dress_worn; relation acquaintance with person-materialized-world-vienna-1912-60; flag +known_affair_active_

- **1931.01** `p23-bigamist-collision::ctx-vienna-reform-1930::milieu-tenement/the-second-household-knew` tags: love, bigamy, double_life, collision, black_comedy, warmth, high_drama, reform
  Maria Bauer在晚饭后把一叠信放在桌上，都是Maria Lechner 3写给Josef的，被Maria Bauer从信箱里截了下来。Maria Bauer说从第一年就知道，只是想看看Josef能演到什么时候。Josef伸手去拿信，Maria Bauer先一步把信收进围裙口袋，说饭还没吃完。桌上的汤已经凉了。
  _effects: state_add p23-dangerous-secret state:world-vienna-1912:453; relation rival with person-materialized-world-vienna-1912-10; state_add public-scandal state:world-vienna-1912:454; mood -0.050000000000000044; flag +second_knew; relation acquaintance with person-materialized-world-vienna-1912-10; relation acquaintance with person-materialized-world-vienna-1912-35; flag -known_affair_active_

  Helene Winter把收据本合上，说这条街上等着租的人排到了巷口。Josef把准备好的账目念到一半，Helene Winter已经转身去敲下一家的门。第二天，门缝里塞进来一张纸条，欠租的数目后面多了一个月。
  _effects: cash -8 (房东的收租日); condition; flag +rent_increase_refused; relation rival with person-helene-winter_

  _effects: food; skill; relation acquaintance with person-materialized-world-vienna-1912-41; mood 0.020000000000000018; relation acquaintance with person-materialized-world-vienna-1912-41_

  _effects: cash -12 (疗养院车票和住宿押金); health 0.12 (疗养院松林空气和正规休养); relation friend with person-helene-winter_

  一个曾经替Josef传话的人搬走了，带走了半条街的信任。第二天，信箱里多了一张没有署名的明信片，上面只写着“我还记得”；Josef把明信片翻过来，背面是空的。
  _effects: state_parameter p22-scandal-engine.heat 82 -> 89; state_parameter p22-scandal-engine.reach 68 -> 73; state_parameter p22-scandal-engine.leverage 39 -> 35; state_parameter p22-scandal-engine.shame 61 -> 67; mood -0.020000000000000018_

- **1933.01** `state_tick:ad20-public-attention/the-attention-cycle/warms` tags: ad20-public-attention, public, attention, reputation, media, everyday, risk, opportunity
  Josef在街角被人认出来，对方没有要求什么，只说“上次那件事做得不错”。这句客气话被旁边的人听见，下午便有两个陌生人朝Josef点头。关注没有减少，但空气松了一点。
  _effects: state_parameter ad20-public-attention.attention 60 -> 56; state_parameter ad20-public-attention.sympathy 55 -> 61; state_parameter ad20-public-attention.opportunity 40 -> 44; mood 0.020000000000000018_

- **1933.01** `r13-civ.recover-at-home::ctx-vienna-reform-1930::milieu-tenement/back-to-work-too-soon` tags: health, recovery, care, household, food, reform, medical, schooling
  Josef退烧第二天就回工厂，站到下午，眼前一阵阵发黑。回家后半夜又烧起来，他把湿毛巾搭在额头上，听见自己的心跳撞着枕头。第二天早上，Josef又去了，因为请假的扣款比药贵。
  _effects: cash 2 (退烧后的第一碗粥); health -0.05 (退烧后的第一碗粥); condition; flag +returned_to_work_too_soon_

- **1933.04** `major-crisis.rent-increase/tenant-meeting` tags: crisis, housing, rent, eviction, family, poverty
  巷口洗衣房的老板娘把几张椅子摆开，叫来半条街的租户。Josef把涨租单摊在桌上，逐条念出上面的数字；Friedrich Berg站在门边，既没走，也没替房东说话。大家决定先集体迟交一个月，签名纸传到Josef手里时，纸上已经有一块油渍。
  _effects: cash -2 (paper, ink and tram fare for the tenants meeting); role; flag +rent_strike; relation acquaintance with person:evt:world-vienna-1912:000206:child; reputation 0.03_

- **1933.06** `bc18-inherit.forty-seven-franz::ctx-vienna-authoritarian-1934::milieu-tenement/sell-the-horses` tags: inheritance, mistaken_identity, debt, funeral, work, absurd, family, vienna
  Josef把灵车和两匹马卖给了城西的殡仪馆，拿到的钱正好抵掉欠账，只剩下一盒黑手套和半瓶擦铜油。Katharina Weiss 3说这笔遗产算是结清了；Josef却总觉得，自己像替一个陌生人做了一场没有尸体的买卖。
  _effects: cash 3 (卖掉灵车和马匹的余款); reputation -0.02; relation acquaintance with person-materialized-world-vienna-1912-77; condition; flag +sold_inherited_hearse; mood -0.010000000000000009; relation acquaintance with person-materialized-world-vienna-1912-77_

- **1933.06** `vienna-tenement-neighbors.shared_stove_fire/fire_no_damage` tags: home, social, family, housing
  Josef刚把锅端下灶，火苗就蹿了起来，Hermann Winter 2一脚踩灭地上的油布，顺手抄起沙箱里的沙子盖住灶口。两人都没受伤，但Hermann Winter 2的鞋底烧穿一个洞，那是唯一一双出门的鞋。
  _effects: relation friend with person-materialized-world-vienna-1912-52; mood -0.050000000000000044_

- **1933.06** `state_reaction:public-scandal/gossip` tags: public-scandal, scandal, reputation, social, state, state_reaction, home, family
  Josef走进咖啡馆时，邻桌的声音低了一拍。没有人当面说什么，但每个人都知道该说什么。
  _effects: mood -0.030000000000000027; reputation -0.03_
  _causes: evt:world-vienna-1912:007457_

- **1933.10** `vienna-historical-social-movement.relief-network/open-the-books-again` tags: history, historical_process, social_movement, organization, politics
  墙面粗灰，Josef用图钉钉上分发清单，纸边被穿堂风吹得直抖。老人说自家也在等煤，Luise Stein把围巾往肩上紧了紧，没接话。有人围上来看，有人转身回屋。
  _effects: relation acquaintance with person-materialized-world-vienna-1912-20; reputation 0.03; skill_

  Josef发现最近没有人再提那件事。报纸换了头版，邻居也换了话题；本该轻松，Josef却在买菜时反复整理衣领，好像还在等一个不会来的目光。
  _effects: state_parameter ad20-public-attention.attention 56 -> 48; state_parameter ad20-public-attention.sympathy 61 -> 58; state_parameter ad20-public-attention.opportunity 44 -> 42; mood -0.010000000000000009_

- **1934.01** `r13-fam.ask-neighbour-help::ctx-vienna-authoritarian-1934::milieu-tenement/borrowed-coal` tags: task, social, neighbor, help, poverty, vienna, urban, crisis
  Josef把空煤筐拎到隔壁门口，说只借一筐，等手头松了就还。Luise Winter看了看筐底的锈，进屋铲了半筐，又从灶边拿了两块引火的柴。Josef道谢时，Luise Winter说这条走廊里借东西要记着，不是记在纸上。
  _effects: food; condition; flag +borrowed_coal_from_neighbour; relation friend with person-materialized-world-vienna-1912-26; relation acquaintance with person-materialized-world-vienna-1912-26_

- **1934.05** `vienna-parish-aid.sick-call/sick-call.with-priest` tags: faith, parish, mutual_aid, poverty, family
  Josef觉得一个人去不合适，绕到教堂请了修女一同上楼。修女看过病人，留下药包和一小瓶圣水，说会从教区账上出钱请医生来看一趟。Luise Stein 2在楼下等着，说这样妥当，病人家里没男人，多个见证人心里安稳。
  _effects: relation acquaintance with person-materialized-world-vienna-1912-53; mood 0.040000000000000036; flag +parish_sick_visitor_

- **1934.07** `vienna-tenement-neighbors.lost_room_key/key_stolen_by_stranger` tags: home, social, family, housing
  Josef记得出门时钥匙还在，回来却没了。Ferdinand Stein说下午看见一个穿灰外套的男人在楼道里转悠，手里晃着一把铜钥匙。两人下楼查看，房门虚掩，抽屉被拉开，攒着买煤的六克朗不见了。Ferdinand Stein陪着Josef去街角警察岗亭报了案，警察记下灰外套，说会留意。
  _effects: cash -6 (被盗的买煤钱); relation friend with person-materialized-world-vienna-1912-47; mood -0.040000000000000036_

- **1934.09** `vienna-legacy-worklife.sick-fund/sick-fund.fill-form` tags: legacy, work, family, reform, ordinary_life
  表格有四栏，第四栏要写“工种及所属行会”。Josef问工友平时怎么称呼自己的活，工友靠在机器旁边说“就是上油”。办事员的印章是木柄的，按下去时咔的一声。
  _effects: skill; relation acquaintance with person-materialized-world-vienna-1912-6_

- **1935.01** `vienna-tenement-neighbors.laundry_line_dispute/compromise_split_line` tags: home, social, family, housing
  Josef把洗好的床单搭上院子里的公用晾衣绳，占了整条。Hermann Winter 2抱着一盆湿衣服下来，说自家孩子明天要穿干净衬衫上学。两人在绳子两头扯了一阵，最后Josef把床单对折，腾出一半，Hermann Winter 2用木夹子把衬衫一件件排上去。谁也没道歉，但第二天见面都点了头。
  _effects: relation acquaintance with person-materialized-world-vienna-1912-52; mood 0.020000000000000018_
  _causes: evt:world-vienna-1912:007457_

  _effects: mood -0.030000000000000027_

  _effects: cash 8 (追回的拖欠计件工资); reputation -0.029999999999999995_

  院子里孩子们从湿石板之间跳过去，把晾衣绳碰得一晃。Josef Bauer说到一半，被自家孩子喊回去添柴。Josef乘机把最后一只木夹子夹好，端起木盆，盆底的水滴了一路。

  _effects: cash -3 (牙医拔牙费用); health 0.05999999999999999 (拔除病牙解除疼痛); mood -0.020000000000000018_

  _effects: cash -0.4 (一周丧葬互助会会费); relation acquaintance with person-materialized-world-vienna-1912-43; flag +funeral_society_member_

  孩子把一张陌生人的照片贴进家庭相册，说这是“爸爸以前最好的朋友”。Josef认出那是十年前打过官司的Elisabeth Huber，正要发火，孩子却说照片背面写着一行字：“等我回来吃饭。”第二天，Elisabeth Huber真的提着一只蛋糕站在门口。孩子把最大的一块留给Josef，Elisabeth Huber没有反对。
  _effects: relation friend with person-materialized-world-vienna-1912-14; cash 1 (the enemy brought a cake and a small gift); flag +enemy_brought_cake; mood 0.050000000000000044_

- **1937.10** `vienna-medical-treatment.night-fever/sweat-it-out` tags: health, medical, family, money

  _effects: condition; relation friend with person-emil-weiss_

  _effects: role; cash -3 (new permits and stamps); condition_

- **1938.01** `ad20-school-meeting::ctx-vienna-authoritarian-1934::milieu-tenement/teacher-blames` tags: family, school, child, meeting, everyday, public, vienna, urban
  Grete Huber 3说Helene Bauer这学期退步，是因为家里晚上太吵。Josef想说自己夜里在做计件活，话到嘴边又咽回去；Helene Bauer站在走廊尽头，书包带子断了一边。回家的路上，Josef把书包带打了个结，谁也没有提老师的话。

- **1938.01** `state_tick:mb17-remittance-web/remittance-year/posted-in-time` tags: mb17-remittance-web, migration, remittance, household, obligation, money, diaspora, state
  Josef把工钱摊在桌上，先数出房租和煤钱，再把剩下的换成一张汇款单。邮局职员在单子上敲了章，说“两周能到”；回家的路上，他买了一张最便宜的明信片，写了两行字又划掉一行。
  _effects: cash -2 (the money order and postage); state_parameter mb17-remittance-web.supportSent 4 -> 6; state_parameter mb17-remittance-web.arrears 2 -> 1; state_parameter mb17-remittance-web.obligation 55 -> 52; mood 0.010000000000000009_

- **1938.01** `state_tick:raising-child/school-year/struggles` tags: raising-child, family, child, care, school, household, state, state_tick
  Josef被叫到学校，老师说Helene Bauer这学期总在课上看窗外。回家的路上，Josef没有骂，只把买好的铅笔又数了一遍；晚饭时，两个人都吃得很少。
  _effects: state_parameter raising-child.schoolProgress 36 -> 31; state_parameter raising-child.caregiverBurden 47 -> 52; cash -2 (extra lessons and replacement pencils); mood -0.030000000000000027_

- **1938.01** `p9-work-career.clerk-post/p9-work-career.clerk-post.hired` tags: work, career, craft, clerk, education, task, office, application
  Josef在保险公司写字间的小桌上抄完一页德文书信，又算出利息表上最后一行，速记只漏了两个词。Elisabeth Hofer把卷子翻过来看背面，说手是稳的。月底他领到七十克朗，扣掉十五克朗保证金，签字画押，坐进靠窗那把椅子，桌上有一块吸墨纸和一支借来的笔。
  _effects: cash 55 (首月工钱扣除保证金后实发); skill; employment 保险写字间试用办事员 @ cinema:person-luise-kral-evt:world-vienna-1912:003176; relation colleague with person-materialized-world-vienna-1912-5_

- **1938.01** `state_reaction:mb17-newcomer/workplace-footing` tags: mb17-newcomer, migration, newcomer, language, network, housing, belonging, state
  工头让Josef把一箱零件搬到后间，又顺口教了他两个德语词。午休时，一个老工人把多余的汤推过来；Josef没有推回去，只把碗底最后一点喝干净，把空碗放回对方手边。
  _effects: state_parameter mb17-newcomer.localNetwork 11 -> 14; state_parameter mb17-newcomer.language 22 -> 23; mood 0.010000000000000009_
  _causes: evt:world-vienna-1912:011161_

- **1938.01** `state_reaction:raising-child/school-note` tags: raising-child, family, child, care, school, household, state, state_reaction
  老师托人带来一张纸条，说Helene Bauer这周主动擦了黑板。Josef把纸条压在桌玻璃下面；那张玻璃下面已经有三张纸条了。
  _effects: state_parameter raising-child.schoolProgress 31 -> 34; mood 0.020000000000000018_
  _causes: evt:world-vienna-1912:011189_

- **1938.06** `vienna-tenement-neighbors.lost_room_key/key_found_by_neighbor` tags: home, social, family, housing
  Josef傍晚从面粉厂下工回来，发现挂在腰带上的房门钥匙不见了，在楼梯间来回找了四趟。Hermann Kral在二楼拐角的煤堆旁捡到那把铜钥匙，钥匙上缠着红线，一眼就认出来。Hermann Kral敲开Josef的门，把钥匙拍在桌上，说自己眼神好使。
  _effects: relation friend with person-hermann-kral; mood 0.050000000000000044_

- **1938.09** `vienna-legacy-worklife.sick-fund/sick-fund.keep-quiet` tags: legacy, work, family, reform, ordinary_life
  止咳药水是褐色的，瓶口塞着软木塞，摇一摇会起沫。Josef看着工友把表折成小块，折了三折才塞进帽檐。墙上的规章还贴在那里，纸角被风掀起，没人去按。
  _effects: mood -0.050000000000000044_

- **1938.11** `major-crisis.rent-increase/tenant-meeting` tags: crisis, housing, rent, eviction, family, poverty
  巷口洗衣房的老板娘把几张椅子摆开，叫来半条街的租户。Josef把涨租单摊在桌上，逐条念出上面的数字；Helene Lechner站在门边，既没走，也没替房东说话。大家决定先集体迟交一个月，签名纸传到Josef手里时，纸上已经有一块油渍。
  _effects: cash -2 (paper, ink and tram fare for the tenants meeting); relation acquaintance with person:evt:world-vienna-1912:000860:child; reputation 0.03_

- **1939.01** `r14-edu.sibling-favour::ctx-vienna-authoritarian-1934::milieu-tenement/sibling-fell-ill` tags: education, tutored_sibling, family, reunion, vienna, urban, crisis, war
  Anna Bauer病倒的消息是邻居带来的。Josef赶到时，屋里只有一张床和一只药罐，孩子坐在门槛上。Josef留下来照顾了半个月，把自己的夜校报名费垫进了药钱。Anna Bauer退烧那天，叫了Josef一声小名。药钱六克朗，是Josef垫的。
  _effects: cash -6 (多年后的一封家信); flag +cared_for_sick_sibling_
  _causes: evt:world-vienna-1912:006500_

- **1939.01** `mb17-xenophobia.stairwell-notice::ctx-vienna-authoritarian-1934::milieu-printshop/the-neighbor-hides-the-family` tags: migration, xenophobia, occupation, housing, neighbor, harm, vienna, urban
  Anna Bauer在夜里敲开门，把Josef一家领到地窖后面的空屋。那里没有窗，但有一盏煤油灯和半袋土豆；Anna Bauer说，巡逻队只在白天来，晚上别开灯。Josef把孩子的鞋放在门边，第一次觉得这栋楼里还有人站在自己这边。
  _effects: state_parameter mb17-newcomer.localNetwork 14 -> 24; state_parameter mb17-newcomer.housingStrain 63 -> 58; relation acquaintance with person-anna-bauer; mood 0.030000000000000027; flag +mb17_hidden_by_neighbor; relation acquaintance with person-materialized-world-vienna-1912-78_

- **1939.01** `state_reaction:mb17-newcomer/workplace-footing` tags: mb17-newcomer, migration, newcomer, language, network, housing, belonging, state
  工头让Josef把一箱零件搬到后间，又顺口教了他两个德语词。午休时，一个老工人把多余的汤推过来；Josef没有推回去，只把碗底最后一点喝干净，把空碗放回对方手边。
  _effects: state_parameter mb17-newcomer.localNetwork 24 -> 27; state_parameter mb17-newcomer.language 23 -> 24; mood 0.010000000000000009_
  _causes: evt:world-vienna-1912:011504_

- **1939.01** `p9-social-daily-culture.cafe-newspaper/p9-social-daily-culture.cafe-newspaper.talk-tram` tags: daily, culture, leisure, cafe, music, walk, task, task:daily
  Josef把报纸推到一边，和Alois Lechner聊起电车票价要涨的传闻。两人拿过糖罐当算盘，在桌布上比划通勤开销，两杯咖啡四十赫勒，争论得忘了时间，直到窗外的煤气灯亮起来。Josef起身时发现大衣下摆沾了咖啡渍，晚饭的点早过了，却约好下回还坐这张桌子。
  _effects: cash -0.4 (两杯咖啡的价钱); relation acquaintance with person-alois-lechner; mood 0.040000000000000036_

- **1939.01** `state_reaction:mb17-remittance-web/border-closes` tags: mb17-remittance-web, migration, remittance, household, obligation, money, diaspora, state
  边境又收紧了，往故乡的列车要换乘三次。邮局说汇款还能走，但信可能压在路上；Josef还是把两张单子一起递进窗口，像把一句话交给一个不保证送达的人。
  _effects: state_parameter mb17-remittance-web.arrears 1 -> 3; mood -0.040000000000000036_
  _causes: evt:world-vienna-1912:011988_

- **1939.05** `vienna-housing-move.rent-notice/rent-notice-pay` tags: housing, home, rent, family, money
  Josef把通知念给Alois Lechner听，Alois Lechner摇头说是楼梯间那几块新玻璃的账。Josef去楼下杂货铺换开一张五克朗的纸币，回屋一枚一枚摆开数，煤箱底多留了二十赫勒。
  _effects: cash -2 (按月多付两克朗房租); food; mood -0.030000000000000027_

- **1939.08** `vienna-historical-social-movement.district-list/hold-list-and-watch` tags: history, historical_process, social_movement, organization, politics
  Otto Hofer把会费账本推回去，说外头那个组织答应给煤，只要名单。Josef把账本合上，用一块旧布包好塞进缝纫机底下，说先看看谁在往外递话。两人都没再提这事。
  _effects: relation acquaintance with person-materialized-world-vienna-1912-13; mood -0.030000000000000027; flag +kept_district_list_private_

- **1940.01** `hist15-occ.seed-occupation::ctx-vienna-authoritarian-1934::milieu-tenement/the-old-clerk-stays` tags: occupation, war, curfew, history, place, institution, vienna, urban
  新来的军官站在市政厅门口，旧办事员还在窗后盖章。Josef排队领一张新的通行证，窗口里的人低声说：“名字照旧写，住址别改。”队伍里没有人说话，只有纸张翻动的声音。
  _effects: state_add occupied-city-regime state:world-vienna-1912:1244; mood -0.030000000000000027; knowledge “旧办事员仍在窗后盖章，只要名字和住址不改，通行证还能办。”_

- **1940.01** `hist15-occ.requisition-notice::ctx-vienna-authoritarian-1934::milieu-factory/hide-the-last-sack` tags: occupation, requisition, shortage, institution, history, family, vienna, urban
  Josef把最后一袋黑麦埋进煤堆下面，夜里听见巡逻队从窗前走过。第二天面包房照常开门，但货架上少了三排面包；Josef知道那不是因为城里没有粮。
  _effects: food; mood 0.020000000000000018; flag +hidden_grain_

- **1940.01** `state_tick:mb17-newcomer/first-year/still-a-guest` tags: mb17-newcomer, migration, newcomer, language, network, housing, belonging, state
  Josef又搬了一次房，箱子里多了一把缺齿的梳子和一叠没寄出的信。新住处离电车线更远，邻居说德语很快；他只能点头，等对方走后再把门关上，把今天听错的词一个一个记在旧票据背面。
  _effects: state_parameter mb17-newcomer.language 24 -> 25; state_parameter mb17-newcomer.localNetwork 27 -> 25; state_parameter mb17-newcomer.housingStrain 58 -> 62; cash -1 (the move and a new rent deposit)_

- **1940.01** `state_tick:mb17-remittance-web/remittance-year/month-behind` tags: mb17-remittance-web, migration, remittance, household, obligation, money, diaspora, state
  这个月厂里少发了工钱。Josef把汇款单填到一半又放下，先给房东送去一部分；夜里他给故乡写信，说“下个月一定补上”，写完把信纸折了三折，像怕它自己会开口。
  _effects: cash -1 (the rent was paid first); state_parameter mb17-remittance-web.arrears 5 -> 6; state_parameter mb17-remittance-web.obligation 52 -> 56_

- **1940.01** `state_tick:wish-craft/craft-pressure/press-on` tags: wish-craft, wish, wish:craft, craft, work, mastery, state, state_tick
  Josef又把那件没实现的事翻出来想了一遍；这一次没有告诉任何人。
  _effects: mood 0.020000000000000018_

- **1940.01** `p9-social-relationship-initiative.introduce-two-people/let-them-talk` tags: courtship, friendship, family, social, initiative, housing, task, task:social
  星期天下午窗边三把椅子，介绍只用了两句，随后就任两人自己聊。老友讲印厂的油墨味，缝纫女工讲机针怎么断。有人只管叫侍者续咖啡，三杯一共九十赫勒，坐在旁边听了一下午。
  _effects: cash -0.9 (三杯咖啡和给侍者的小费); relation friend with person:evt:world-vienna-1912:000787:child; relation acquaintance with person-materialized-world-vienna-1912-83; skill; mood 0.050000000000000044; relation acquaintance with person-materialized-world-vienna-1912-83_

- **1940.02** `state_reaction:mb17-remittance-web/border-closes` tags: mb17-remittance-web, migration, remittance, household, obligation, money, diaspora, state
  边境又收紧了，往故乡的列车要换乘三次。邮局说汇款还能走，但信可能压在路上；Josef还是把两张单子一起递进窗口，像把一句话交给一个不保证送达的人。
  _effects: state_parameter mb17-remittance-web.arrears 3 -> 5_
  _causes: evt:world-vienna-1912:012519_

- **1940.03** `vienna-courtship-courtyard.family-table/leave-before-dessert` tags: adulthood, courtship, partner, social, family, partnership
  问到后来，Josef Bauer提起别家有个更稳当的差事。Elisabeth Lechner 2把餐巾往桌上一放，说吃好了，先走。两人在楼梯间站住，Elisabeth Lechner 2说不是Josef的错，可下次再来还得一样坐这张桌子；Josef心里松了一点，也知道这顿饭没吃完，事情并没有过去。
  _effects: relation partner with person-materialized-world-vienna-1912-116; relation kin with person-josef-bauer; mood 0.030000000000000027; relation acquaintance with person-materialized-world-vienna-1912-116_

- **1940.10** `p9-gap-adult-solitude.misdelivered-letter/misdelivered-letter.upstairs` tags: adulthood, daily, solitude, ordinary, home, letter
  Josef拿着信上了四层楼，敲了两户都不是，门牌是七号还是十七号已看不清。第三户的老太太说那人去年就搬去法沃里滕了。她把信交回门房，门房在登记簿上写下一个赫勒的墨水钱。
  _effects: mood 0.030000000000000027; reputation 0.02_

- **1940.12** `lc17-care.offers-to-care::ctx-vienna-authoritarian-1934::milieu-tenement/hires-the-neighbour` tags: care, family, aging, health, household, duty, vienna, urban
  Josef算了一夜账，第二天去敲楼下退休护士的门。看护每天下午来两小时，工钱按周付；Josef Bauer嫌贵，Josef说“总比我辞工强”。第一周结束时，药盒上的时间第一次没有写错。
  _effects: state_add lc17-caregiver state:world-vienna-1912:1359; cash -3 (the first week of paid care); relation kin with person-josef-bauer; flag +paid_for_parent_care_

- **1941.01** `mb17-language.school-counter::ctx-vienna-authoritarian-1934::milieu-printshop/child-corrects-the-teacher` tags: migration, language, school, child, humiliation, belonging, vienna, urban
  老师问Helene Bauer会不会拼自己的姓，Helene Bauer用维也纳话答了一句，办公室里的人都笑了。Josef没听懂，只看见Helene Bauer抬起下巴；出门后，Helene Bauer小声把刚才的话翻译了一遍，说老师把“sch”念错了。
  _effects: state_parameter mb17-newcomer.language 25 -> 28; state_parameter mb17-newcomer.localNetwork 25 -> 27; state_parameter raising-child.childConfidence 51 -> 57; state_parameter raising-child.schoolProgress 34 -> 38; mood 0.040000000000000036; flag +mb17_child_translated_for_parent_

- **1941.01** `state_reaction:raising-child/school-note` tags: raising-child, family, child, care, school, household, state, state_reaction
  老师托人带来一张纸条，说Helene Bauer这周主动擦了黑板。Josef把纸条压在桌玻璃下面；那张玻璃下面已经有三张纸条了。
  _effects: state_parameter raising-child.schoolProgress 38 -> 41; mood 0.020000000000000018_
  _causes: evt:world-vienna-1912:013284_

- **1941.01** `state_tick:lc17-caregiver/care-year/carried` tags: lc17-caregiver, care, family, illness, elder, duty, household, state
  Josef又撑过一年。药盒按星期分好，账本上多了一栏“看护”，工钱少了一些，但还没有断；Josef Bauer今年记得住Josef的名字，偶尔也记得住昨天吃了什么。
  _effects: state_parameter lc17-caregiver.burden 20 -> 16; state_parameter lc17-caregiver.resentment 5 -> 3; mood 0.010000000000000009_

- **1941.01** `state_tick:raising-child/school-year/learns` tags: raising-child, family, child, care, school, household, state, state_tick
  Josef把Helene Bauer的旧课本包上书皮，又在本子上签了名。Helene Bauer今年学会了自己系鞋带，也学会了在饭桌上说“不”；Josef听着，觉得屋子比去年更满。
  _effects: state_parameter raising-child.schoolProgress 41 -> 49; state_parameter raising-child.caregiverBurden 52 -> 49; mood 0.020000000000000018_

- **1941.01** `r13-fam.propose-cohabitation::ctx-vienna-authoritarian-1934::milieu-tenement/keys-on-table` tags: task, family, cohabitation, household, courtship, vienna, urban, crisis
  Josef把两把钥匙放在桌上，一把是自家门，一把是今天刚配的。Elisabeth Lechner 2没有立刻拿，先问房东知不知道，又问邻居会不会说闲话。Josef说已经跟门房讲过，只说是表亲来住；Elisabeth Lechner 2把钥匙收进围裙口袋，当晚就提来一只藤箱。
  _effects: cash -5 (两把钥匙放在一张桌上); flag +moved_in_together; role; relation partner with person-materialized-world-vienna-1912-116; knowledge “the porter was told the partner is a cousin”; household_

- **1941.07** `p9-social-family-conflict-care.sick-parent-night/shifts-with-sibling` tags: family, care, siblings, inheritance, housing, money, injury
  Josef和Klara把值班时辰写在门后的粉笔板上：Klara守前半夜，Josef守后半夜，医生说静养二十一天。煤油灯芯拧小，药粉分成小包摆在床头，便盆换水时尽量不碰出响。二十一天熬下来，Josef的眼皮肿了，走路发飘，但Josef Bauer每次起身总有人扶。
  _effects: health -0.03 (连续夜里守夜睡眠不足); relation kin with person-phase15-Klara-world-vienna-1912; flag +night_care_duty; relation acquaintance with person-materialized-world-vienna-1912-23_

- **1941.09** `ad20-funeral::ctx-vienna-authoritarian-1934::milieu-tenement/a-place-to-stand` tags: funeral, death, grief, everyday, debt, public, vienna, urban
  Josef站在人群最后一排，手里没有花，只带了一把伞。Hermann Moser回头看见，往旁边挪了半步，让出一块干地。仪式很长，伞一直没打开；散场时，Hermann Moser说“下次别站那么远”，Josef点了点头。
  _effects: mood 0.020000000000000018; relation acquaintance with person-materialized-world-vienna-1912-17; flag +funeral_stand; food; relation acquaintance with person-materialized-world-vienna-1912-17_

- **1941.10** `vienna-medical-treatment.sanatorium/home-rest` tags: health, medical, family, money
  炉子封小了，屋里凉得快，Josef把大衣盖在被子上。安娜收碗时看见窗台上一排药瓶，没说什么，第二天多带了半块黑面包。楼下的管道工敲了一下午铁皮，咳嗽声混在里面听不真切。
  _effects: health 0.03 (在家休养但条件有限); food; cash -0.5 (房东要求的清洁费); relation friend with person-materialized-world-vienna-1912-43_

- **1942.01** `mb17-belonging.sunday-walk::ctx-vienna-authoritarian-1934::milieu-factory/the-child-speaks-like-a-local` tags: migration, belonging, ordinary, family, park, quiet, vienna, urban
  Helene Bauer跑去买烤栗子，回来时已经和摊主聊上了。摊主问Josef：“你女儿在这里生的？”Josef摇头，Helene Bauer却抢着说“我在这里上学”。Josef听见那一口维也纳话，忽然分不清自己是高兴还是失落。
  _effects: state_parameter mb17-newcomer.language 28 -> 29; state_parameter mb17-newcomer.localNetwork 27 -> 30; mood 0.040000000000000036; flag +mb17_child_at_home_here_

- **1942.01** `state_reaction:mb17-remittance-web/family-emergency` tags: mb17-remittance-web, migration, remittance, household, obligation, money, diaspora, state
  故乡来了一封电报，说屋顶塌了一角，老人暂时住到邻居家。Josef当晚就去邮局，把本来留着交学费的钱先汇了出去；回家后，他把墙上的缴费单取下来，折好放进抽屉。
  _effects: state_parameter mb17-remittance-web.obligation 56 -> 66; state_parameter mb17-remittance-web.arrears 6 -> 7; cash -3 (an emergency money order sent home); mood -0.040000000000000036_
  _causes: evt:world-vienna-1912:014300_

- **1942.01** `state_tick:mb17-newcomer/first-year/still-a-guest` tags: mb17-newcomer, migration, newcomer, language, network, housing, belonging, state
  Josef又搬了一次房，箱子里多了一把缺齿的梳子和一叠没寄出的信。新住处离电车线更远，邻居说德语很快；他只能点头，等对方走后再把门关上，把今天听错的词一个一个记在旧票据背面。
  _effects: state_parameter mb17-newcomer.language 29 -> 30; state_parameter mb17-newcomer.localNetwork 30 -> 28; state_parameter mb17-newcomer.housingStrain 62 -> 66; cash -1 (the move and a new rent deposit); mood -0.030000000000000027_

- **1942.01** `state_tick:mb17-remittance-web/remittance-year/posted-in-time` tags: mb17-remittance-web, migration, remittance, household, obligation, money, diaspora, state
  Josef把工钱摊在桌上，先数出房租和煤钱，再把剩下的换成一张汇款单。邮局职员在单子上敲了章，说“两周能到”；回家的路上，他买了一张最便宜的明信片，写了两行字又划掉一行。
  _effects: cash -2 (the money order and postage); state_parameter mb17-remittance-web.supportSent 6 -> 8; state_parameter mb17-remittance-web.arrears 7 -> 6; state_parameter mb17-remittance-web.obligation 66 -> 63; mood 0.010000000000000009_

- **1942.01** `state_tick:lc17-caregiver/care-year/carried` tags: lc17-caregiver, care, family, illness, elder, duty, household, state
  Josef又撑过一年。药盒按星期分好，账本上多了一栏“看护”，工钱少了一些，但还没有断；Josef Bauer今年记得住Josef的名字，偶尔也记得住昨天吃了什么。
  _effects: state_parameter lc17-caregiver.burden 16 -> 12; state_parameter lc17-caregiver.resentment 3 -> 1; mood 0.010000000000000009_

- **1942.01** `r13-fam.take-over-family-shop::ctx-vienna-authoritarian-1934::milieu-tenement::shop-crisis/shop-closed` tags: task, commerce, shop, family_firm, succession, debt, vienna, urban
  Josef把最后一批货折价卖给街口的贩子，招牌摘下来靠在门后。Josef Bauer坐在空柜台后面，说这铺子开了三十七年，第一次白天上门板。Josef没有接话，只把钥匙放在Josef Bauer手边；两人听见街上的失业队伍从门口走过去。
  _effects: cash 4 (把家里的招牌重新钉上); condition; flag +closed_family_shop; role; relation kin with person-josef-bauer; knowledge “the shop traded for thirty-seven years before closing”_

- **1942.01** `state_reaction:mb17-remittance-web/border-closes` tags: mb17-remittance-web, migration, remittance, household, obligation, money, diaspora, state
  边境又收紧了，往故乡的列车要换乘三次。邮局说汇款还能走，但信可能压在路上；Josef还是把两张单子一起递进窗口，像把一句话交给一个不保证送达的人。
  _effects: state_parameter mb17-remittance-web.arrears 6 -> 8; mood -0.040000000000000036_
  _causes: evt:world-vienna-1912:014735_

- **1942.01** `state_reaction:mb17-newcomer/workplace-footing` tags: mb17-newcomer, migration, newcomer, language, network, housing, belonging, state
  工头让Josef把一箱零件搬到后间，又顺口教了他两个德语词。午休时，一个老工人把多余的汤推过来；Josef没有推回去，只把碗底最后一点喝干净，把空碗放回对方手边。
  _effects: state_parameter mb17-newcomer.localNetwork 28 -> 31; state_parameter mb17-newcomer.language 30 -> 31; mood 0.010000000000000009_
  _causes: evt:world-vienna-1912:014747_

- **1942.03** `major-crisis.rent-increase/pay-the-increase` tags: crisis, housing, rent, eviction, family, poverty
  房东没有露面，只让看门人Elisabeth Lechner 2把补款单塞进门缝：每月要多十四克朗，先交十八克朗（本月十四加四克朗手续费）才肯谈续租。Josef把准备买冬煤的钱数了两遍，还是把钱交了，收据上写的是“自愿捐助”。Elisabeth Lechner 2低声说，不签的人已经有两家搬走了，楼下的煤价牌又换了数字。
  _effects: cash -18 (advance payment demanded with the rent increase); relation acquaintance with person-materialized-world-vienna-1912-116; flag +rent_increase_accepted_

- **1942.06** `vienna-courtship-courtyard.breakup/stay-for-the-rent` tags: adulthood, courtship, partner, social, family, partnership
  东西还到一半，两人算了算房租、煤钱和两个人的工钱凑在一起才够。最后钥匙没收回去，外套也留下；这个月能少花一克朗，话说到“以后”两个字就断了，谁都不往下接，炉子今晚还是得两个人一起生。
  _effects: mood -0.050000000000000044; relation partner with person-materialized-world-vienna-1912-83; cash 1 (合租省下一份房租); relation acquaintance with person-materialized-world-vienna-1912-56_

- **1942.07** `vienna-market-household.friday-fish/friday-fish.salt` tags: home, market, food, social
  腌鲱鱼切成两段，一段今天吃，一段留着。黑面包硬得要用刀锯。Emil Weiss来借盐，眼睛落在鱼上，说可以用半棵卷心菜换一块。Josef切了一指宽递过去。
  _effects: cash -2 (腌鲱鱼和黑面包); food_

- **1942.11** `state_reaction:ml18-sudden-fortune/a-relative-remembers` tags: ml18-sudden-fortune, money, windfall, sudden_fortune, household, luck, inheritance, lottery
  一个多年没走动的亲戚提着一盒点心上门，先问Josef的身体，再问那笔钱是怎么来的。临走时他说起小时候替Josef家垫过一冬天的煤钱，数字记不清了，但意思很清楚。
  _effects: state_parameter ml18-sudden-fortune.claims 0 -> 1; flag +windfall_claim_by_relative; mood -0.020000000000000018_
  _causes: evt:world-vienna-1912:014980_

- **1943.01** `vienna-life-injury-recovery/wound-infection` tags: health, work, family
  Josef的伤口在几天后肿起来，手腕发热。医生说要切开引流，费用比上次更高；Josef看着账单，先问能不能分两次付。
  _effects: cash -14 (drainage and medicine); condition_
  _causes: evt:world-vienna-1912:014200_

- **1943.01** `state_reaction:mb17-remittance-web/border-closes` tags: mb17-remittance-web, migration, remittance, household, obligation, money, diaspora, state
  边境又收紧了，往故乡的列车要换乘三次。邮局说汇款还能走，但信可能压在路上；Josef还是把两张单子一起递进窗口，像把一句话交给一个不保证送达的人。
  _effects: state_parameter mb17-remittance-web.arrears 8 -> 10; mood -0.040000000000000036_
  _causes: evt:world-vienna-1912:014947_

- **1943.01** `vienna-family-same-sex-male/leave-vienna` tags: family, intimacy, same_sex, persecution, lifecycle, partnership, migration
  两张车票分开买，Josef在月台这头，Gustav Moser在那头。火车开动后两人才在车厢连接处站到一起，谁也没有说话，只把行李往同一张座位底下推。
  _effects: relation partner with person-materialized-world-vienna-1912-83; move place-klosterneuburg-aunt-house; move place-klosterneuburg-aunt-house; cash -12 (train tickets and lodging)_

- **1943.01** `state_expire:mb17-newcomer` tags: mb17-newcomer, migration, newcomer, language, network, housing, belonging, state
  Josef的「初到维也纳的人」到期；相关安排结束。
  _effects: state_remove mb17-newcomer state:world-vienna-1912:1067; flag -mb17_newcomer_active_

- **1943.01** `state_tick:lc17-caregiver/care-year/worn-thin` tags: lc17-caregiver, care, family, illness, elder, duty, household, state
  Josef Bauer今年摔了两次，第二次把胯骨摔裂了。Josef在医院走廊的硬椅上睡了四夜，回家以后发现厂里已经把位置给了别人；药费单、住院单和一张停工的证明一起压在桌玻璃下面，边角每天都被翻卷一点。
  _effects: state_parameter lc17-caregiver.burden 12 -> 20; state_parameter lc17-caregiver.resentment 1 -> 7; cash -2 (medicine and hospital fees); mood -0.01000000000000012_

- **1943.01** `state_tick:ml18-sudden-fortune/fortune-year/money-keeps-working` tags: ml18-sudden-fortune, money, windfall, sudden_fortune, household, luck, inheritance, lottery
  一年过去，钱没有变多，却也没有一下花完。Josef把一部分存进邮局储蓄所，一部分借给楼下的鞋匠；鞋匠每月送来一双补好的鞋，利息就夹在鞋垫下面。街口的杂货商开始把好面粉留到Josef来的时候。
  _effects: cash 2 (interest and small repayments on the windfall); state_parameter ml18-sudden-fortune.claims 1 -> 2; mood 0.010000000000000009_

- **1943.01** `p9-social-daily-culture.sunday-hill/p9-social-daily-culture.sunday-hill.reach-summit` tags: daily, culture, leisure, cafe, music, walk, task, task:daily
  Josef和Elisabeth Lechner 2按原计划沿葡萄园间的土路往上走，鞋子沾满黄泥，到山顶时风把汗湿的衬衫吹得冰凉。下山在第一个酒棚坐下，两人合点了一壶新酒和一块面包，一共一克朗二十赫勒；Josef喝到第二杯时脸颊发烫，把剩下的半块面包包进手帕带回家，第二天腿酸，却睡得很沉。
  _effects: cash -1.2 (卡伦山酒棚的新酒和面包); food; health 0.019999999999999997 (星期天登山远足); relation friend with person-materialized-world-vienna-1912-116; mood 0.06000000000000005_

- **1943.01** `vienna-life-wwii-homefront/shelter` tags: war, homefront, shortage
  警报响时Josef躲进地下室，和十几户人挤在长凳上。头顶传来闷响，灰尘从梁上落下。警报解除后，街上多了一堆碎瓦。
  _effects: relation acquaintance with person-phase15-Emil-world-vienna-1912_

- **1943.03** `vienna-medical-treatment.night-fever/corner-doctor` tags: health, medical, family, money
  药粉含在嘴里发苦，Josef就着糖浆咽下去，喉头一阵凉。医生拎包出门时，楼梯间的煤气灯刚好灭了，Anton Huber 2摸黑送他到拐角，回来时手里攥着找回的几枚赫勒。
  _effects: cash -4 (医生出诊费和药费); health 0.05 (药粉和糖浆缓解症状); relation friend with person-materialized-world-vienna-1912-120; relation acquaintance with person-materialized-world-vienna-1912-120_

- **1943.05** `vienna-tenement-neighbors.lost_room_key/key_lost_landlady` tags: home, social, family, housing
  Josef把钥匙丢在了回家路上的有轨电车轨道旁，天黑也没找着。房东太太说换锁芯要一克朗五十赫勒，从下月房租里扣。Elisabeth Lechner 2借给Josef一把旧挂锁，但锁鼻和门扣对不上，只能用铁丝拧着凑合。那夜Josef睡不踏实，总觉得有人在拧门。
  _effects: cash -1.5 (换锁芯费用); relation acquaintance with person-materialized-world-vienna-1912-116; mood -0.06000000000000005_

- **1943.12** `vienna-church-community.tenement-prayer/rent-prayer-argument` tags: faith, social, family, home
  有人抱怨守夜人太多，踩坏了楼梯木板。楼里的人围着看，谁也没先走开。
  _effects: relation acquaintance with person-materialized-world-vienna-1912-121; reputation -0.029999999999999995; mood -0.010000000000000009; relation acquaintance with person-materialized-world-vienna-1912-121_

- **1944.01** `state_tick:mb17-remittance-web/remittance-year/month-behind` tags: mb17-remittance-web, migration, remittance, household, obligation, money, diaspora, state
  这个月厂里少发了工钱。Josef把汇款单填到一半又放下，先给房东送去一部分；夜里他给故乡写信，说“下个月一定补上”，写完把信纸折了三折，像怕它自己会开口。
  _effects: cash -1 (the rent was paid first); state_parameter mb17-remittance-web.arrears 10 -> 11; state_parameter mb17-remittance-web.obligation 63 -> 67_

  Josef又撑过一年。药盒按星期分好，账本上多了一栏“看护”，工钱少了一些，但还没有断；Josef Bauer今年记得住Josef的名字，偶尔也记得住昨天吃了什么。
  _effects: state_parameter lc17-caregiver.burden 20 -> 16; state_parameter lc17-caregiver.resentment 7 -> 5; health -0.010000000000000002 (a year of broken nights); mood 0.010000000000000009_

- **1944.01** `state_tick:raising-child/school-year/struggles` tags: raising-child, family, child, care, school, household, state, state_tick
  Josef被叫到学校，老师说Helene Bauer这学期总在课上看窗外。回家的路上，Josef没有骂，只把买好的铅笔又数了一遍；晚饭时，两个人都吃得很少。
  _effects: state_parameter raising-child.schoolProgress 49 -> 44; state_parameter raising-child.caregiverBurden 49 -> 54; cash -2 (extra lessons and replacement pencils); mood -0.020000000000000018_

- **1944.01** `state_tick:wish-craft/craft-pressure/press-on` tags: wish-craft, wish, wish:craft, craft, work, mastery, state, state_tick
  Josef又把那件没实现的事翻出来想了一遍；这一次没有告诉任何人。
  _effects: mood 0.020000000000000018_

- **1944.01** `p9-social-relationship-initiative.apology-at-bakery/semmel-no-words` tags: courtship, friendship, family, social, initiative, housing, task, task:social
  在柜台前买了两只 Semmel，递过一只，自己咬另一只，旧账一个字没提。对方接过面包，两人并肩站在店门口，把那点热气吃完。钱的事还横在中间，但对方说星期六照旧来喝咖啡，别又躲着。
  _effects: cash -0.14 (两只 Semmel); food; mood 0.030000000000000027; relation acquaintance with person-materialized-world-vienna-1912-101_

- **1944.04** `p9-social-tenement-neighbors.gramophone-wall/gramophone-wall.knock-and-bargain` tags: housing, neighbors, tenement, daily, money, reputation
  Josef上楼敲了Otto Kral的门，说唱针刮在墙上，夜班的人睡不安稳。Otto Kral把留声机从墙边挪到窗下，垫了一块毛毡，答应十点以后不再放。下楼时进行曲还在响，但闷了些，Josef能听见自家水壶的响声了。
  _effects: relation acquaintance with person-materialized-world-vienna-1912-86; health 0.03 (夜里终于能早些入睡)_

- **1944.06** `vienna-medical-treatment.toothache/drops-and-endure` tags: health, medical, family, money
  夜里疼醒时，Josef摸黑往棉球上又滴药水，手抖得洒在枕头上。Grete Lechner 3第二天一早来敲门，看见肿脸就说别硬撑了，可Josef只是摆摆手，说再熬一天也许就好了。
  _effects: cash -0.6 (牙痛药水); condition_

- **1944.11** `vienna-social-movement.recruitment/read-and-decline` tags: development, social_movement, organization, politics
  Josef把传单读了两遍，记下上面的地址，却没有去。第二天照常上工；午休时听见两个工友低声谈论同一场集会。
  _effects: mood -0.010000000000000009_

  _effects: role; food; cash -2 (replacement documents); relation acquaintance with person-materialized-world-vienna-1912-86_

  房租催到门口，Josef把已写的章节卖给一家小报，换了八克朗，够付一个月房租和两顿热饭。长篇锁进抽屉，钥匙搁在盐罐后面，Elisabeth Lechner 2问起时他只说“先不提了”，第二天照常去上工。

  Josef在受伤后的第三天去了诊所。医生清洗伤口、换药，收下诊费，嘱咐不要急着回机器旁。Josef把药方折好，回家路上买了纱布。
  _effects: health 0.05 (wound treatment); cash -8 (clinic and bandages)_
  _causes: evt:world-vienna-1912:016914_

  _effects: cash -1 (the rent was paid first); state_parameter mb17-remittance-web.arrears 12 -> 12; state_parameter mb17-remittance-web.obligation 67 -> 71_

- **1946.01** `p9-work-career.client-list/p9-work-career.client-list.referral` tags: work, career, craft, clerk, education, task, clients, reputation
  Josef把街角咖啡馆松动的八把椅子逐一套榫、重粘、上蜡，收了四克朗。老板让他下次过来喝咖啡时捎上小册子，当天就把楼下杂货铺和公寓看门人介绍给他。小册子上多了三行字，每个名字旁边写着“由某某介绍”。
  _effects: cash 4 (修咖啡馆八把椅子的工钱); reputation 0.05; relation acquaintance with person-phase15-Klara-world-vienna-1912; skill_

- **1946.03** `vienna-medical-treatment.night-fever/corner-doctor` tags: health, medical, family, money
  施特劳斯医生用酒精棉擦手，把药粉倒进小纸袋，折口时指甲掐出印子。他说夜里别再喝醋水，那东西烧胃。Emil Weiss把炉火拨旺，在灶上坐了一壶水，答应第二天替Josef去铺子里告假。
  _effects: cash -4 (医生出诊费和药费); health 0.05 (药粉和糖浆缓解症状); relation friend with person-emil-weiss_

- **1946.07** `vienna-tenement-neighbors.shared_stove_fire/fire_burns_curtain` tags: home, social, family, housing
  Josef在公用灶台上热猪油，油星子溅到旁边晾着的床单上，火苗顺着床单爬上木隔板。Grete Lechner 3拎起水桶泼过去，火灭了，但床单烧掉半幅，隔板熏黑一片。房东扣了那户人家两克朗押金，那家的女人在楼道里哭了一下午。
  _effects: cash -2 (赔给房东的隔板熏黑损失); relation acquaintance with person-materialized-world-vienna-1912-95_

- **1946.09** `p9-social-family-conflict-care.sick-parent-night/hire-neighbor-daycare` tags: family, care, siblings, inheritance, housing, money
  Josef请楼下的Karl Kraus白天来照看Katharina Bauer，先付一周六克朗工钱，讲好每天另管两顿饭，煤钱也从这六克里出。Karl Kraus上午换床单，下午喂药，临走把便盆提到楼道口。Josef下工回来先摸煤箱，再在练习本上记下当天的饭钱和煤钱，看见Katharina Bauer的嘴唇不再干裂。
  _effects: cash -6 (支付邻居照看工钱和煤钱); food; relation acquaintance with person-karl-kraus; health 0.04 (白天有人按时喂药和换洗)_

- **1946.12** `p9-social-courtship-friendship.opened-letter/opened-letter-cold-reply` tags: courtship, friendship, social, adulthood, reputation, leopoldstadt
  Josef信了听来的话，把回信写得又短又冷，只问对方家里到底嫌哪一样，写完封了口，第二天一早塞进街角的邮筒。此后走廊里碰见Berta 2，两边都只点头，话绕开信的事走；Josef与Gustav Hofer之间那点热乎气，也被这四行字压凉了。
  _effects: relation acquaintance with person:evt:world-vienna-1912:002744:child_

- **1947.01** `state_reaction:mb17-remittance-web/border-closes` tags: mb17-remittance-web, migration, remittance, household, obligation, money, diaspora, state
  边境又收紧了，往故乡的列车要换乘三次。邮局说汇款还能走，但信可能压在路上；Josef还是把两张单子一起递进窗口，像把一句话交给一个不保证送达的人。
  _effects: state_parameter mb17-remittance-web.arrears 12 -> 12_
  _causes: evt:world-vienna-1912:018246_

- **1947.01** `state_reaction:raising-child/child-death-in-the-household` tags: raising-child, family, child, care, school, household, state, state_reaction
  孩子的名字从学校的名册上被划掉以后，家里的碗还多摆了一副。Josef没有把它收起来，直到有一天发现碗沿积了灰，才拿去洗。
  _effects: state_parameter raising-child.childConfidence 57 -> 27; state_parameter raising-child.caregiverBurden 54 -> 69; flag +lost_child_
  _causes: evt:world-vienna-1912:018335_

- **1947.01** `state_tick:raising-child/school-year/struggles` tags: raising-child, family, child, care, school, household, state, state_tick
  Josef被叫到学校，老师说Helene Bauer这学期总在课上看窗外。回家的路上，Josef没有骂，只把买好的铅笔又数了一遍；晚饭时，两个人都吃得很少。
  _effects: state_parameter raising-child.schoolProgress 44 -> 39; state_parameter raising-child.caregiverBurden 69 -> 74; cash -2 (extra lessons and replacement pencils)_

- **1947.01** `task-vienna-family-care.reconcile-siblings/soup-and-grave` tags: family, care, courtship, siblings, inheritance, housing, task, task:family
  饭桌摆在圣器室旁边，椅子不够，两人挨着坐。Emil说到欠账时声音低下去，Josef没有接话，只把盐罐递过去；出门时天已经擦黑，墓地的铁门还开着。
  _effects: cash -0.5 (墓前的蜡烛); mood 0.050000000000000044_

- **1947.04** `vienna-market-household.friday-fish/friday-fish.salt` tags: home, market, food, social
  腌鲱鱼切成两段，一段今天吃，一段留着。黑面包硬得要用刀锯。Klara来借盐，眼睛落在鱼上，说可以用半棵卷心菜换一块。Josef切了一指宽递过去。
  _effects: cash -2 (腌鲱鱼和黑面包); food; relation acquaintance with person-phase15-Klara-world-vienna-1912_

- **1948.01** `vienna-tenement-neighbors.laundry_line_dispute/neighbor_yanks_clothes` tags: home, social, family, housing
  Grete Lechner 3嫌Josef的床单滴水滴到Grete Lechner 3家衬衫上，一把扯下床单扔在院子的石板上。Josef下楼捡起来时，床单角沾了泥，重洗又要费肥皂。两人在院子当中对喊，楼上有人推开窗看热闹。Josef把床单重新搭上绳子时，故意把水拧在Grete Lechner 3那侧。
  _effects: relation rival with person-materialized-world-vienna-1912-95; cash -0.15 (重洗床单的肥皂钱)_
  _causes: evt:world-vienna-1912:018443_

- **1948.01** `vienna-postwar-housing-1950s/private-sublet` tags: adulthood, postwar, housing, family, household
  Josef从报纸小广告上找到一间转租屋，付了三十先令“钥匙钱”。墙皮一碰就掉，冬天窗框漏风；Katharina Bauer 2说这一带都这样，先住下再说。
  _effects: residence; cash -30 (key money and rent in advance); condition; relation acquaintance with person-materialized-world-vienna-1912-35_

- **1948.01** `vienna-family-same-sex-male/private-arrangement` tags: family, intimacy, same_sex, persecution, lifecycle, partnership
  Josef和Gustav Hofer在城边租了一间小屋，对外只说是同事合住。房租四克朗，两人不同时出门，信件用假名，夜里把窗帘拉严；Gustav Hofer在门后放了一根木棍，Josef把两人的工票分开放，连咳嗽都压着声。
  _effects: relation partner with person:evt:world-vienna-1912:002744:child; mood 0.040000000000000036; cash -4 (separate room rent)_

- **1948.01** `major-crisis.press-accident/press-kills` tags: crisis, work, accident, disability, death, printshop, health
  同一台机器在夜班又卡住了。Josef伸手去拽纸边，滚筒突然合拢，把人和半截工作服一起拖向齿轮箱。Ferdinand Weiss拉下总闸时，Josef的胸口已经塌下去一块。医生蹲在油污的地上写死亡证明，工厂当天下午就换了新的防护栏，工头说订单不能停。
  _effects: death (crushed chest in a printing press)_

### 131 subject events

- **1912.02** `vienna-life-birth-letter-from-the-dead/the-letter-is-real` tags: infancy, family, secret, letter, drama
  邮差把一封边角磨白的信塞进门缝，收信人是一个已经死了三年的名字。Alois Lechner把Rosa放在膝上，拆开信，里面只有一句：“孩子像不像他？”Emil Weiss在门口探头，问是不是寄错了；Alois Lechner把信折回去，说“没有寄错”。
  _effects: flag +letter_from_dead_relative; knowledge “一封写给死去亲人的信问：孩子像不像他。”; mood -0.04_

- **1912.03** `p22-comic-christening::ctx-vienna-1912::milieu-tenement/the-wrong-name` tags: infancy, family, christening, church, neighbor, comic, tender, drama
  神父念的是另一张纸条上的名字。Alois Lechner小声纠正，Rosa正好吐出一口奶，落在登记簿边上；神父用袖子擦了擦，说这样也算盖过章了，只是纸角留下一小片湿痕。
  _effects: mood -0.010000000000000002; knowledge “洗礼时神父差点念错孩子的名字。”; flag +wrong_christening_name; cash -1 (a christening candle)_

- **1912.12** `p22-warm-naming-feast::ctx-vienna-1912::milieu-tenement/the-name-is-too-long` tags: infancy, family, feast, name, neighbor, warm, tender, drama
  两个祖母的名字都想要，最后写在纸上连成一串。Emil Weiss念到一半就笑了，说这孩子以后签名字要多蘸一次墨水；Rosa在摇篮里睡着，拳头举在耳边，蛋糕上只插了一根蜡烛。
  _effects: mood 0.04; relation acquaintance with person-phase15-Rosa-world-vienna-1912; flag +double_name; knowledge “我的名字里有两个祖母的名字。”; state_add p22-family-legend state:world-vienna-1912:21_

- **1912.12** `state_reaction:p22-family-legend/someone-asks-again` tags: p22-family-legend, family_legend, family, story, secret, inheritance, promise, memory
  又有人问起Rosa家的那件旧事。大人先笑了一下，才说“都是很早以前了”；问的人点点头，眼睛却往摇篮那边看，手指在杯沿上停了一会儿，谁也没有接话，窗外有风。
  _effects: state_parameter p22-family-legend.secrecy 15 -> 19; state_parameter p22-family-legend.warmth 75 -> 77; mood 0.01_
  _causes: evt:world-vienna-1912:000115_

- **1913.01** `vienna-life-toddler-wrong-cradle/the-swap-story-spreads` tags: infancy, family, rumour, identity, comic, drama
  三天后，整条楼道都在说这家的孩子“抱错了”。Alois Lechner抱着Rosa去敲Hermann Kral的门，Hermann Kral不开，只在门里说“我也是听来的”。Rosa在母亲怀里睡着了，口水把她的领口洇出一小片深色。
  _effects: flag +cradle_swap_rumour; relation rival with person-hermann-kral; mood -0.05_

- **1913.01** `state_tick:p22-family-legend/the-story-changes-hands/told-at-the-table` tags: p22-family-legend, family_legend, family, story, secret, inheritance, promise, memory
  又过了一年，Rosa家的故事在饭桌上换了新的讲法：多了一句笑话，少了一处哭声。听的人把汤匙放下，等下一句；讲故事的人却先去看窗子有没有关好，谁也没有先动筷子。
  _effects: state_parameter p22-family-legend.warmth 77 -> 80; state_parameter p22-family-legend.secrecy 19 -> 17; state_parameter p22-family-legend.promise 60 -> 62; mood 0.01_

- **1913.10** `iw18-infancy.first-word::ctx-vienna-1912::milieu-tenement/the-dog-gets-the-first-word` tags: infancy, first_word, memory, family, wonder, eerie, vienna, urban
  Rosa追着炉边的狗爬了半间屋，忽然拍着地板喊出狗的名字。狗吓得钻到床下，Alois Lechner笑得把水壶都放下了；邻居在门外问出了什么事。那天晚上，家里给狗多留了半块面包。
  _effects: mood 0.04; food; flag +first_word_dog_name_

- **1914.01** `vienna-life-toddler-first-words/first-word-nonsense` tags: infancy, family, first, comic
  Rosa说了一长串谁也听不懂的话，最后用力点头，像是在宣布一件已经决定的事。Alois Lechner认真听完，也点了点头；孩子满意地爬走了，留下大人们互相看着，忍不住笑。
  _effects: mood 0.04; skill_

- **1914.02** `vienna-life-toddler-accident/safe-in-the-doorway` tags: infancy, accident, family, danger
  Alois Lechner在最后一刻抓住了Rosa的后领。孩子悬在两级台阶之间，鞋底蹭着地，手里还攥着那只线轴。Alois Lechner把孩子放回地上，蹲下来看了很久，才说：“再跑，我就把门关上。”
  _effects: mood -0.020000000000000004; skill_

- **1916.01** `vienna-life-child-family-legend/stolen-inheritance` tags: childhood, family, secret, story
  Alois Lechner说，家里本来有一件东西，后来被“更会说话的人”拿走了。Rosa问是什么，Alois Lechner只说是一块表，表盖里刻着姓。孩子记住了“表盖里刻着姓”，却没有记住那个姓。
  _effects: knowledge “家里有一块表盖里刻着姓的表，被一个更会说话的人拿走了。”; flag +family_legend_watch_

- **1916.02** `vienna-life-child-inherited-wish/the-dead-grandmothers-wish` tags: childhood, family, wish, grief, uncanny, drama
  Rosa在饭桌上忽然说：“等我长大了，要把那间屋子买回来。”Alois Lechner的勺子停在半空，因为这句话是去世多年的外婆常说的，连“那间屋子”都指得一模一样。Rosa说完就低头喝汤，好像只是随口。
  _effects: flag +inherited_wish_house; knowledge “孩子说要把“那间屋子”买回来，和去世的外婆说过的一模一样。”; mood -0.009999999999999995_

- **1916.04** `vienna-early-childhood.fever-night/home-remedy` tags: childhood, family, home, street
  Rosa烧得翻来覆去，Alois Lechner把醋倒进温水，用布蘸着擦手心、脚心，又去灶间煮接骨木汁。勺子送到嘴边，Rosa偏头躲，Alois Lechner就等一会儿再喂。鸡叫时炉火压小，床脚用旧大衣堵住。两天后热度退了，嘴唇起了一层干皮。
  _effects: health 0.03 (土法退烧，热度渐退); mood -0.06_

- **1917.01** `vienna-life-child-first-friend/shared-toy` tags: childhood, friendship, family, comic
  Mitzi在楼道里滚一只铁环，Rosa站在门口看。Mitzi把铁环推过来，说“你推一次，我推一次”。两个人从楼梯口推到院子里，铁环倒下的声音把午睡的大人吵醒了一排。
  _effects: skill; mood 0.049999999999999996_

- **1917.03** `p9-childhood-household.fever-night/hospital-deposit` tags: childhood, family, household, home, siblings, illness, neighbors
  医院走廊里全是湿鞋印。Alois Lechner把Rosa放在长椅上，自己去窗口交钱，六克朗换来一张薄薄的收据。护士把Rosa领进观察室，白灯照得人睁不开眼。Rosa抓着Alois Lechner的袖口不放，直到药水推进胳膊才松手。
  _effects: cash -6 (医院急诊押金和电车票); health 0.08 (在医院接受处理); mood -0.04_

- **1920.07** `vienna-childhood-friends.courtyard-race/fell-on-wet-stones` tags: childhood, social, school, street
  晒衣绳上的水一滴一滴落在石板上。Rosa跑到第二圈，鞋底一滑，膝盖先着地。Maria Kral从屋里端出半盆清水，蹲下冲掉伤口里的沙，又用手帕扎紧，说今天别跑了。
  _effects: relation acquaintance with person-maria-kral_

- **1921.01** `p9-gap-childhood-chores.market-line/line-both-families` tags: childhood, household, chores, school, task, daily, market, food
  Josefine Hofer把Rosa拽到队伍前头，往自己身前塞，说“孩子占不了多大地儿”。窗口一次递出两包面包，Rosa把邻居那包夹在胳肢窝，自己这包抱在胸前。到Leopoldstädter Zinshaus, gemeinsamer Gang门口，Rosa蹲下把两包并排，按Josefine Hofer说的份量分开，连找零的铜板也分成两小堆。
  _effects: food; relation acquaintance with person-josefine-hofer; skill; relation acquaintance with person-materialized-world-vienna-1912-25_

- **1921.02** `vienna-childhood-friends.hidden-book/book-confiscated` tags: childhood, social, school, street
  Rosa把书压在算术本底下，只露出书脊。Ferdinand Hofer走过来，抽走算术本，书也跟着滑出来。折角那页画着一艘帆船。Ferdinand Hofer合上书，说放学后擦黑板，擦一星期；书被锁进讲台抽屉，没往校长室送。
  _effects: mood -0.05; relation acquaintance with person-materialized-world-vienna-1912-34; relation acquaintance with person-materialized-world-vienna-1912-34_

- **1921.12** `vienna-childhood-street.market-errand/errand-bullied` tags: childhood, street, social, home
  Rosa被推到拱廊柱子边，围裙带子绕了两圈，系在铁环上。Rosa一挣，肩膀撞上石柱，整条胳膊麻了一下。Anton提着菜篮过来，把篮子往地上一放，从怀里摸出削土豆的小刀，挑开绳结，没跟那两个孩子说一句话。
  _effects: relation friend with person-phase15-Anton-world-vienna-1912; mood -0.05; condition_

- **1922.01** `p9-gap-childhood-chores.mind-sibling/sibling-carried-afternoon` tags: childhood, household, chores, school, task, daily, sibling, care
  Anna Lechner哭得打嗝，手指一直朝井台方向指。Rosa蹲下把Anna Lechner驮上背，沿着墙根走，数砖缝给Anna Lechner听：一、二、三……数到第四圈，背上的呼吸才匀了。天黑时Rosa的胳膊麻得抬不过肩，Alois Lechner回来接过孩子，Rosa扶着门框甩了好一会儿手。
  _effects: skill; relation kin with person:evt:world-vienna-1912:001041:child_

- **1922.01** `vienna-childhood-friends.courtyard-race/won-the-race-lost-a-button` tags: childhood, social, school, street
  Rosa跑到第二圈时鞋带松了，索性踩着鞋后跟跑。最后一圈，Rosa从床单底下钻过去，先拍到墙。Friedrich Hofer喘着气，把两颗弹珠拍进Rosa手心。Rosa低头才发现外套少了一颗扣子；窗口的Friedrich Hofer扔下一根别针，说先别着。
  _effects: mood 0.05; relation acquaintance with person-friedrich-hofer_

- **1923.01** `p9-gap-childhood-chores.market-line/line-sold-out-potatoes` tags: childhood, household, chores, school, task, daily, market, food
  “卖完了。”三个字从窗口里传出来，Rosa后面的队伍立刻散了一半。Rosa没走，趴在窗台上看伙计扫面屑，直到人家挥手赶人。旁边摊子的土豆带着泥，Rosa用围裙兜住，一枚一枚数铜板，回家报账时连找零都说清了。
  _effects: food; mood -0.03; skill; relation acquaintance with person-materialized-world-vienna-1912-16_

- **1924.02** `vienna-youth-threshold.sibling-departure/take-over-bed-and-ledger` tags: adolescence, school, work, family, transition
  Mitzi Lechner走后，靠墙那张床空了出来，Rosa把自己的铺盖搬过去，也接过那本记着欠账的旧账本：面包店三克朗、煤铺一克朗八十赫勒。晚上Rosa坐在床沿一笔笔抄清，第一次知道家里的钱是怎么缺的。
  _effects: relation kin with person-mitzi-lechner; skill; mood -0.05; cash -0.5 (替兄姐补齐欠煤铺的零头)_

- **1924.02** `vienna-adolescence.workshop-apprentice/apprenticeship-accepted` tags: adolescence, school, work, family, social
  Buchdruckerei Winter & Söhne先看Rosa的手，又让Rosa推两下刨子试试。Rosa用力过猛，刨刀啃进木头，Buchdruckerei Winter & Söhne皱眉，让再来一遍。第二遍木花薄而匀。Buchdruckerei Winter & Söhne说“留下”，当面点了三克朗给Alois Lechner，算头一周饭钱，又指了指墙上的钟，说迟到一刻钟扣半天。
  _effects: cash 3 ({subject}学徒首周的膳食预付款); education apprenticeship; reputation 0.1; relation kin with person-alois-lechner_

- **1924.04** `r3-teen.first-friend-group::ctx-vienna-reform-1930::milieu-tenement/admitted` tags: friendship, roof, belonging, betrayal, class, reform, medical, schooling
  屋顶上的暗号是两声口哨。Rosa吹对了一声，第二声卡在喉咙里，Elisabeth Moser 3笑着替她补上。四个人分吃一袋偷来的栗子，壳扔到楼下，砸在Josefine Hofer的雨棚上。
  _effects: mood 0.08000000000000002; flag +joined_roof_group; role; relation friend with person-materialized-world-vienna-1912-50; relation acquaintance with person-materialized-world-vienna-1912-50_

- **1924.06** `vienna-adolescence.brothers-torn-jacket/sew-it-yourself` tags: adolescence, school, work, family, social
  Rosa在厨房找到一截粗线，颜色和外套差了一截。缝到一半，线打结，Rosa用牙咬断，重新来。Alois Lechner坐在对面，没问外套的事，只把汤碗推过来，说“先吃”。Rosa把最后一针打完，汤面已经结了一层薄皮。
  _effects: skill; food; relation kin with person-alois-lechner_

- **1925.01** `r13-fam.help-homework::ctx-vienna-reform-1930::milieu-tenement/sums-finished` tags: task, family, school, homework, sibling, literacy, reform, medical
  Rosa把煤油灯往桌子中间挪，让Maria Lechner看清算术本上的格子。孩子把七加八数了三遍，Rosa没有直接说答案，只把豆子一颗颗排在桌上。最后一页写完时，灯芯已经烧黑，Rosa才发现自己的作业还空着半页。
  _effects: flag +helped_sibling_with_homework; role; skill; relation kin with person:evt:world-vienna-1912:000382:child; skill_

- **1925.02** `r15-work.master-takes-on::ctx-vienna-reform-1930::milieu-tenement/unpaid-trial` tags: work, craft, apprentice, trade, poverty, reform, medical, schooling
  Franz Hofer先说试三个月，不给工钱，只管一顿午饭。家里同意了，因为多一张嘴比多一双手更难。Rosa每天最早到，最晚走，三个月后师傅才把契约拿出来。
  _effects: food; mood -0.04000000000000001; state_add apprenticeship-contract state:world-vienna-1912:143; relation acquaintance with person-materialized-world-vienna-1912-43; education apprenticeship; role_

- **1925.02** `r3-teen.sexual-rumor::ctx-vienna-reform-1930::milieu-tenement::reform-school-paint-order/confronted` tags: rumor, shame, school, reputation, institution, reform, medical, schooling
  Rosa在操场边堵住Elisabeth Moser 3，问他从哪听来的。他先说是别人说的，后来承认是自己编的，因为Rosa在课堂上答对了他答错的问题。Ferdinand Hofer让他们各写一份说明，两份都锁进抽屉。
  _effects: reputation 0.04000000000000001; flag +confronted_rumor_source; relation rival with person-materialized-world-vienna-1912-50; knowledge “the classmate invented the rumour out of envy”_

- **1925.09** `yf18-firstlook.tram-glove::ctx-vienna-reform-1930::milieu-tenement/keep-it-in-the-satchel` tags: first_love, love, tram, glove, youth, courtship, reform, medical
  Rosa把手套塞进书包最里层，回家后放在枕头下面。第二天上学，Rosa每隔一会儿就摸一下书包带，像在确认一件不能给家里看见的东西；手套上还留着电车木座的气味。
  _effects: state_add yf18-first-awakening state:world-vienna-1912:181; flag +yf18_kept_glove; mood 0.020000000000000004; flag +yf18_first_awakening_active_

- **1926.01** `state_tick:apprenticeship-contract/workshop-year/learned` tags: apprenticeship-contract, education, craft, apprentice, work, state, state_tick
  Rosa又学会了一道工序。师傅没有夸，只把下一块料推到面前。
  _effects: state_parameter apprenticeship-contract.progress 8 -> 28; skill; mood 0.01999999999999999_

- **1926.01** `state_tick:yf18-first-awakening/half-year-of-glances/answered-glance` tags: yf18-first-awakening, love, youth, first_love, desire, school, awakening, state
  Rosa在教堂门口或学校走廊里又遇见Mitzi。这一次对方先点了头，Rosa准备好的整段话全忘了，只把手里的书换到另一只手上；回家后Rosa才发现，自己把书拿倒了。
  _effects: state_parameter yf18-first-awakening.intensity 65 -> 70; state_parameter yf18-first-awakening.nerve 0 -> 8; state_parameter yf18-first-awakening.reciprocated false -> true; mood 0.03_

- **1926.01** `p9-childhood-youth-aspiration.join-a-club/worked-for-dues` tags: adolescence, education, aspiration, school, books, club, exam, task
  Rosa翻遍口袋只有几枚赫勒，便跟管事的说愿替大家抄歌本抵会费。他坐在窗边抄了一下午，手指被墨水染黑，换来的借书权让他捧回一本讲齿轮的旧书，书页里夹着上一个人留下的铅笔字。
  _effects: skill; skill; relation friend with person-materialized-world-vienna-1912-49; flag +workers_youth_work_trade; mood 0.03; relation acquaintance with person-materialized-world-vienna-1912-49_

- **1926.02** `yf18-friend.two-tickets::ctx-vienna-reform-1930::milieu-tenement/confront-at-the-cloakroom` tags: first_love, friendship, betrayal, dance, tickets, reform, medical, schooling
  Rosa在衣帽间堵住Klara，问那两张票怎么到了Josef手里。Klara说Rosa从来没敢开口，自己只是替Rosa把话说了；Rosa把票根揉成一团，塞进Klara的西装口袋，转身时撞翻了侍者的托盘。
  _effects: state_parameter yf18-first-awakening.intensity 60 -> 65; state_parameter yf18-first-awakening.talk 0 -> 10; state_parameter yf18-first-awakening.nerve 5 -> 0; relation friend with person-phase15-Klara-world-vienna-1912; relation friend with person-phase15-Josef-world-vienna-1912; reputation -0.020000000000000004; mood -0.05_

- **1926.02** `yf18-performance.choir-solo::ctx-vienna-reform-1930::milieu-tenement/laugh-and-bow` tags: performance, choir, humiliation, school, voice, reform, medical, schooling
  Rosa听见自己的声音劈开，索性停下来，对着教堂的穹顶笑了一声。Ferdinand Hofer在风琴后面瞪眼，Rosa却把那句拉丁文降了八度重新唱了一遍，声音厚得像另一个人；台下有人先笑，随后跟着鼓掌，Lena在最后一排把手套举起来挥了挥。
  _effects: reputation 0.030000000000000013; relation acquaintance with person-materialized-world-vienna-1912-34; flag +yf18_laughed_at_solo; mood 0.04000000000000001_

- **1926.02** `yf18-runaway.bundle::ctx-vienna-reform-1930::milieu-tenement/unpack-and-boil-water` tags: runaway, escape, family, plan, night_train, reform, medical, schooling
  Rosa把包袱重新打开，衬衫上的褶子已经压平了。Rosa把洗礼证明放回抽屉，去厨房烧了一壶水；Alois Lechner醒来时，桌上多了一杯茶，Rosa说今天不想去上学。
  _effects: mood -0.03; relation kin with person-alois-lechner; flag +yf18_unpacked_

- **1926.12** `r2-leisure.servant-trial::ctx-vienna-reform-1930::milieu-tenement/rejected-at-the-back-door` tags: domestic_service, servant, hiring, household, reference, reform, medical, schooling
  Freie Gewerkschaft der graphischen Arbeiter Wiens把介绍信折回信封，说上一家太太的名字她没听过。Otto Hofer领Rosa从后门出去，塞给她一块冷面包，说下回别把指甲修得这样尖。Rosa在电车站把面包吃完，手指上还留着擦铜油的味道。
  _effects: mood -0.1; flag +rejected_servant_trial; relation colleague with person-materialized-world-vienna-1912-13; knowledge “an unfamiliar reference can end a trial before lunch”; relation acquaintance with person-materialized-world-vienna-1912-13_

- **1927.01** `yf18-dare.bridge-rail::ctx-vienna-reform-1930::milieu-tenement/climb-back-down` tags: dare, bridge, river, friendship, risk, reform, medical, schooling
  Rosa的手已经搭上栏杆，听见桥下有驳船拉笛，忽然把手收回来。Elisabeth Bauer骂了一句，Emil却把围巾解下来递给Rosa；三个人在桥头站了一会儿，谁也没有再提这件事。
  _effects: state_parameter yf18-first-awakening.intensity 70 -> 73; state_parameter yf18-first-awakening.nerve 8 -> 3; relation friend with person-phase15-Emil-world-vienna-1912; mood -0.009999999999999995_

  Rosa在教堂门口或学校走廊里又遇见Mitzi。这一次对方先点了头，Rosa准备好的整段话全忘了，只把手里的书换到另一只手上；回家后Rosa才发现，自己把书拿倒了。
  _effects: state_parameter yf18-first-awakening.intensity 81 -> 86; state_parameter yf18-first-awakening.nerve 13 -> 21; state_parameter yf18-first-awakening.reciprocated true -> true; mood 0.03_

  Rosa接了一单远活，回来时天已经黑透。第二天的算术课，Rosa在课堂上睡着了，Maria Lechner 3用粉笔头敲了敲课桌。放学后Maria Lechner 3把Rosa留下，说再缺两次课就要叫家长；Rosa摸着口袋里那几枚铜板，没有说是去送货了。
  _effects: cash 1 (放学后的四个钟头); reputation -0.05; flag +work13_part_time_made_late_for_school; relation rival with person-materialized-world-vienna-1912-45; relation acquaintance with person-materialized-world-vienna-1912-32; relation acquaintance with person-materialized-world-vienna-1912-45_
