  毕业钟敲过三下，Ferdinand Hofer把成绩单按在讲台上，说Rosa还能再读一年，只要家里肯出四克朗。家里人在走廊里把学徒契约展开，纸角还带着面粉。Rosa签了名，第二天就站到刨床前，手指上多了第一道木刺。
  _effects: cash -2 (毕业钟响过以后); flag +left_school_for_apprenticeship; education apprenticeship_

- **1927.02** `yf18-firstlook.parish-dance::ctx-vienna-reform-1930::milieu-tenement/the-second-dance` tags: first_love, dance, church, courtship, jealousy, reform, medical, schooling
  舞卡上第一支曲子被Emil写走了。Rosa在教会礼堂的木地板上踩错了两步，Emil说没关系，把手放回Rosa背后；第三支曲子响起时，Elisabeth Bauer在门口冲Rosa比了个手势，Rosa假装没看见。
  _effects: state_parameter yf18-first-awakening.intensity 73 -> 81; state_parameter yf18-first-awakening.nerve 3 -> 13; state_parameter yf18-first-awakening.reciprocated true -> true; relation friend with person-phase15-Emil-world-vienna-1912; mood 0.04_

  Rosa在学校的煤炉边把油印纸烧了，灰烬和煤渣混在一起。Ferdinand Hofer第二天问第三十四页读得怎么样，Rosa说译文看不出来；Ferdinand Hofer点点头，把课本收回去，从此没有再借给Rosa任何书。
  _effects: mood -0.019999999999999997; relation acquaintance with person-materialized-world-vienna-1912-34; flag +yf18_burned_pamphlet_

  德语作文题是《我的家乡》。Rosa写了半页多瑙河，剩下半页写一个总在电车上看书的人。Ferdinand Hofer没有打分，只在页边写“感情太多，论据太少”，把作文折好放进自己的公文包；放学后，Rosa看见Klara在门口等，忽然不敢走过去。
  _effects: state_parameter yf18-first-awakening.intensity 86 -> 92; state_parameter yf18-first-awakening.talk 10 -> 13; skill; reputation -0.009999999999999995; mood 0.010000000000000009_

  Buchdruckerei Winter & Söhne先看Rosa的手，又让Rosa推两下刨子试试。Rosa用力过猛，刨刀啃进木头，Buchdruckerei Winter & Söhne皱眉，让再来一遍。第二遍木花薄而匀。Buchdruckerei Winter & Söhne说“留下”，当面点了三克朗给Alois Lechner，算头一周饭钱，又指了指墙上的钟，说迟到一刻钟扣半天。
  _effects: cash 3 ({subject}学徒首周的膳食预付款); education apprenticeship; reputation 0.1; relation kin with person-alois-lechner_

- **1928.02** `r13-turn.exam-failure::ctx-vienna-reform-1930::milieu-tenement::reform-open-secondary/name-missing` tags: education, exam, failure, school, work, reform, medical, schooling
  补考那天，Rosa在考场门口等到钟响，监考说名单上没有名字。Maria Lechner 3跑去教务处，回来时手里只有一张转学建议。Rosa去了印刷厂当学徒，师傅说铅字比字母好认，至少不会骗人。
  _effects: condition; flag +left_school_after_exam_failure; relation colleague with person-materialized-world-vienna-1912-45; education apprenticeship_

- **1928.02** `ld18-letter.schoolbook::ctx-vienna-reform-1930::milieu-tenement/read-aloud-in-class` tags: love, letter, school, secret, queer, courtship, secret_intimacy, reform
  Maria Lechner 3在课堂上翻到那封信，没有念出名字，只把句子读了一遍。教室里先是安静，随后有人笑。Klara低下头，说那不是自己写的；Rosa看着课本上的拉丁文，一个字也认不出来。
  _effects: state_add ld18-public-desire state:world-vienna-1912:357; reputation -0.06; relation friend with person-phase15-Klara-world-vienna-1912; mood -0.07; flag +letter_read_aloud; flag +public_desire_active_

  布告上的录取名额用黑框围起来，下面是各科分数线。Rosa在德语一行来来回回看，那一分写在括号里。Maria Lechner 3把预科班的说明纸裁了一半递给他，边角还带着裁纸的毛。
  _effects: mood -0.08000000000000002; knowledge “技术学院差一分可先读一年预科再考”; relation acquaintance with person-materialized-world-vienna-1912-24; relation acquaintance with person-materialized-world-vienna-1912-35; relation acquaintance with person-materialized-world-vienna-1912-24_

- **1928.12** `state_reaction:yf18-first-awakening/exam-week-distraction` tags: yf18-first-awakening, love, youth, first_love, desire, school, awakening, state
  考试周里，Rosa把课本摊在桌上，眼睛却一直看着窗外那条电车线。笔记的页边多了几个没有写完的名字，Rosa用橡皮擦掉，纸面起了毛。

- **1929.01** `state_tick:yf18-first-awakening/half-year-of-glances/answered-glance` tags: yf18-first-awakening, love, youth, first_love, desire, school, awakening, state
  Rosa在教堂门口或学校走廊里又遇见Mitzi。这一次对方先点了头，Rosa准备好的整段话全忘了，只把手里的书换到另一只手上；回家后Rosa才发现，自己把书拿倒了。
  _effects: state_parameter yf18-first-awakening.intensity 94 -> 99; state_parameter yf18-first-awakening.nerve 29 -> 37; state_parameter yf18-first-awakening.reciprocated true -> true; mood 0.030000000000000027_

  故事又换了一个版本：有人说Rosa是为了钱，有人说是为了爱，还有人说她只是喜欢看别人议论。Rosa在咖啡馆听见第三种说法，没有更正，只把杯子往窗边挪了挪；阳光照进来，她第一次觉得这些版本都离自己很远。
  _effects: state_parameter ld18-public-desire.rumorHeat 45 -> 41; state_parameter ld18-public-desire.spectacle 30 -> 32; mood 0.010000000000000009_

  Rosa每晚给Katharina Lechner讲题，自己的作业只能留到深夜。期中考试，Rosa的名次掉了十几位，Anna Bauer在家长会上点名。回家后，Rosa看见Katharina Lechner趴在桌上睡着了，算术本还摊在胳膊下面，就没有叫醒。
  _effects: mood -0.06; flag +neglected_own_studies_

- **1930.01** `p9-social-daily-culture.lending-library/p9-social-daily-culture.lending-library.volksbibliothek` tags: daily, culture, leisure, cafe, music, walk, task, task:knowledge
  Rosa听Helene Kral 2报出押金数目，把伸进内袋的手又抽了回来。问清免费阅览处的位置后，Rosa穿过两条街，在 Volksbibliothek 的阅览室门口登记了姓名，坐在长桌边读了一下午；一个赫勒没花，只是不能把书带回家，天黑前就得还回去。
  _effects: mood 0.030000000000000027; skill; relation acquaintance with person-materialized-world-vienna-1912-12_

- **1930.02** `bc18-enemy.accidental-help::ctx-vienna-reform-1930::milieu-tenement/wrong-coal-delivery` tags: enemy, mistake, work, reputation, absurd, luck, social, reform
  Emil Moser 2把二十袋“劣质煤”送到Rosa门口，货到付款，想看Rosa拿不出钱。卸货时，煤块黑得发亮，是上好的无烟煤；Rosa付了钱，转手卖给面包房，净赚五克朗。Emil Moser 2来打听煤的去向，Rosa说已经烧了，还谢谢他记得自己怕冷。
  _effects: cash 5 (转卖无烟煤的差价); relation rival with person-materialized-world-vienna-1912-65; condition; flag +enemy_coal_was_anthracite; mood 0.02999999999999997; relation acquaintance with person-materialized-world-vienna-1912-65_

- **1930.02** `vienna-legacy-worklife.sick-fund/sick-fund.advance-from-fund` tags: legacy, work, family, reform, ordinary_life
  互助金的铁盒上了两把锁，钥匙一把在Rosa手里，一把在邻街的面包师那里。取钱时面包师说“月底前要补回来”。药买回来了，雇主那边的人说手续“走流程”，说完就去忙别的。
  _effects: cash -2 (从互助金垫付药费); health 0.05999999999999999 (及时买到药); relation acquaintance with person-materialized-world-vienna-1912-17; relation acquaintance with person-materialized-world-vienna-1912-26; relation acquaintance with person-materialized-world-vienna-1912-17_

- **1930.02** `vienna-medical-treatment.sanatorium/ignore-diagnosis` tags: health, medical, family, money, injury
  抽屉一推就卡住，Rosa用膝盖顶了一下才合严。上工路上经过药房，橱窗里摆着润肺糖浆的广告画，她别过头去。车间主任点名时，她应声比平时慢了一拍。
  _effects: health -0.08000000000000002 (肺病未治继续劳累); condition; mood -0.03999999999999998_

- **1930.02** `ad20-family-dinner::ctx-vienna-reform-1930::milieu-tenement/the-fourth-bowl` tags: family, dinner, everyday, obligation, public, grief, reform, medical
  Friedrich Huber来得比约定早，手里提着一小袋栗子。Rosa多摆了一副碗筷，又把汤里的肉往那只碗里拨了两块。饭吃到一半，Friedrich Huber忽然说“你们家吃饭真安静”，Rosa才发现自己一直在听楼上的脚步。
  _effects: food; mood 0.02999999999999997; relation acquaintance with person-materialized-world-vienna-1912-23; flag +family_dinner_guest; relation acquaintance with person-materialized-world-vienna-1912-23; relation acquaintance with person-materialized-world-vienna-1912-6_

  Elisabeth Moser用铁尺量了梁的间距，当场让房东把阁楼的隔墙拆掉。房东赔着笑，等检查员下楼就把门锁了；Rosa站在楼道上，听见里面开始砸墙，灰从门缝里飘出来。
  _effects: cash -6 (阁楼加出来的那间房（其中6克朗记入债务）); condition; flag +ordered_structural_repair; role; knowledge “the inspector measures beams but cannot stay to watch them”_

- **1930.05** `vienna-medical-treatment.toothache/barber-pull` tags: health, medical, family, money
  Ferdinand Weiss在门口替Rosa捏着帽子，听见里面一声闷哼。弗朗茨把牙扔进搪瓷盘，说瞧这牙根都黑了。收钱时他多要了二十赫勒，算明矾水的钱，说下次别再拖成这样。
  _effects: cash -1.2 (理发师拔牙收费（其中1.2克朗记入债务）); health 0.03 (病牙被拔除但手法粗糙); mood -0.01999999999999999; relation acquaintance with person-materialized-world-vienna-1912-60_

- **1930.06** `ad20-public-speech::ctx-vienna-reform-1930::milieu-tenement/speech-lands` tags: speech, public, reputation, everyday, fame, scandal, reform, medical
  礼堂里坐了一半人，前排还有几个空位。Rosa讲到第三段时，后排有人开始点头；讲到第五段，连门口卖票的也转过身来。散场后，Hermann Kral握着Rosa的手说“下次还请你”，手心里全是汗。
  _effects: state_add ad20-public-attention state:world-vienna-1912:460; reputation 0.04000000000000001; skill; relation acquaintance with person-hermann-kral; flag +public_speech_landed; mood 0.04000000000000001; flag +public_attention_active_

- **1930.09** `vienna-courtship-courtyard.letter-answer/mother-opened-letter` tags: adulthood, courtship, partner, social, family
  回信没有到Rosa手里。Anna Bauer的母亲先拆了它，连信封上的邮戳一起摊在洗衣房的桌上，说有人写字来缠自家孩子。话传到院子里时已经添了油，Friedrich Bauer隔着栏杆把原话学了一遍，说得很难听；Anna Bauer再见到Rosa时，眼睛先往母亲那边看。Rosa站在水桶边，知道这封信已经被拆成了两家的脸面。
  _effects: reputation -0.06; mood -0.08000000000000002; relation acquaintance with person-anna-bauer; relation acquaintance with person-materialized-world-vienna-1912-4_

- **1931.01** `vienna-life-injury-recovery/wound-infection` tags: health, work, family
  Rosa的伤口在几天后肿起来，手腕发热。医生说要切开引流，费用比上次更高；Rosa看着账单，先问能不能分两次付。
  _effects: cash -14 (drainage and medicine（其中14克朗记入债务）); condition_
  _causes: evt:world-vienna-1912:005010_

  Rosa在街角被人认出来，对方没有要求什么，只说“上次那件事做得不错”。这句客气话被旁边的人听见，下午便有两个陌生人朝Rosa点头。关注没有减少，但空气松了一点。

- **1931.11** `vienna-medical-treatment.night-fever/hospital-night` tags: health, medical, family, money

  _effects: mood -0.09999999999999998; condition; flag +could_not_find_the_grave; relation friend with person-materialized-world-vienna-1912-3; knowledge “the old tree by the grave was cut down last year”_

  Rosa在公证处坐了很久，最后说自己没有力气再经营一间铺子。Helene Weiss接过钥匙，脸上先是惊讶，随后是一种被托付的沉重。半年后，那间印刷所改了招牌，Helene Weiss在门口看见Rosa，只点了点头，没有提请客吃饭的事。

  画室的门一关，时间就又变成Emil Weiss的。Rosa坐在同一把椅子上，听见窗外电车经过，数到第七次才被允许动一下；回家路上，Rosa发现自己的围巾上沾着一股松节油味，洗了两次也没掉。
  _effects: state_parameter fa17-muse.dependence 41 -> 47; state_parameter fa17-muse.inspiration 52 -> 54; state_parameter fa17-muse.credit 8 -> 6; mood -0.030000000000000027_

  Rosa等到夜里，从后院的柴门溜出去上工。车间里没有人问，机器照常转，只有换班的人往旁边让了半步。回家时白线还在，Rosa跨过去，鞋底带回一层湿泥。
  _effects: cash 3 (门框上的石灰线); reputation -0.1; flag +broke_quarantine; knowledge “the workshop does not ask why a quarantined worker returned”_

- **1933.09** `vienna-medical-treatment.night-fever/corner-doctor` tags: health, medical, family, money
  药粉含在嘴里发苦，Rosa就着糖浆咽下去，喉头一阵凉。医生拎包出门时，楼梯间的煤气灯刚好灭了，Helene Weiss摸黑送他到拐角，回来时手里攥着找回的几枚赫勒。
  _effects: cash -4 (医生出诊费和药费); health 0.049999999999999996 (药粉和糖浆缓解症状); relation friend with person-materialized-world-vienna-1912-1_

- **1933.10** `vienna-life-overcrowded-flat/take-in-a-lodger` tags: housing, home, health, family, injury
  Rosa把厨房角落隔出一块，租给一个在附近上工的学徒。每月多四克朗，但煤、水和厕所都要排队；夜里有人咳嗽，Rosa睡不踏实，第二天眼下发青，孩子也在床上翻来覆去，四克朗换来一屋子醒着的人。
  _effects: cash 4 (lodger rent); health -0.04 (overcrowded housing); condition_

- **1934.01** `vienna-family-same-sex-female/public-suspicion` tags: family, intimacy, same_sex, housing, lifecycle, partnership
  楼里的邻居开始议论Rosa和Anna Lechner 2。有人把门牌记下来，房东来敲门，说这栋楼不接受“这种住法”；Anna Lechner 2把箱子拖出来又推回床下，搬家花掉五克朗，两人那一夜都没睡稳。Rosa在院子里被人盯着看，门上的名字也被人用手指划过。
  _effects: relation partner with person-materialized-world-vienna-1912-88; reputation -0.12; mood -0.09999999999999998; cash -5 (moving and lost rent（其中5克朗记入债务）); role; relation acquaintance with person-materialized-world-vienna-1912-88_

  Rosa看见Emil Weiss在楼梯口歇了三回，便把煤筐接过来，一筐一筐抬上三楼。最后一趟，Rosa的腰在门框上撞了一下，Emil Weiss从柜里摸出一小瓶李子酒，硬倒了一盅。
  _effects: food; health -0.03 (把煤筐抬上三楼); flag +carried_neighbour_coal; role; relation friend with person-emil-weiss_

- **1934.09** `vienna-legacy-worklife.hours-ledger/hours-ledger.read-for-others` tags: legacy, work, family, reform, ordinary_life
  围过来的工友有七八个，有人把耳朵凑到Rosa嘴边，怕听漏一个数字。念到“星期六下午停工”时，人群里有人笑了一声，又马上收住。墙上的表被风掀起一角，Rosa用拇指按住，继续往下念。
  _effects: skill; reputation 0.04000000000000001_

- **1934.09** `vienna-housing-move.flat-viewing/flat-viewing-deposit` tags: housing, home, rent, family, money, household, injury
  窗子推开只有一线天光，墙纸边角翘起，Rosa用指甲一刮便掉下一层湿粉。看门人揣好定金，说这间是朝北的，冬天火炉要添得勤些。Emil Weiss站在楼梯口没进屋，只替Rosa看着箱子。
  _effects: cash -4 (当场付下定金和钥匙钱（其中4克朗记入债务）); residence; health -0.03 (在新屋里待久了关节发凉)_

  Katharina Lechner没有等到毕业，跟着同乡去了铁路工地上工。走的那天，Rosa把攒下的两克朗塞进Katharina Lechner的口袋，Katharina Lechner没有推辞。火车开走后，Rosa在站台上站了很久，手里还拿着那本没讲完的算术书。
  _effects: cash 2 (弟弟妹妹的准考证); flag +sibling_left_school; relation kin with person:evt:world-vienna-1912:000689:child_
  _causes: evt:world-vienna-1912:004716_

- **1935.01** `vienna-life-injury-recovery/clinic-treatment` tags: health, work, family
  Rosa在受伤后的第三天去了诊所。医生清洗伤口、换药，收下诊费，嘱咐不要急着回机器旁。Rosa把药方折好，回家路上买了纱布。
  _effects: health 0.05 (wound treatment); cash -8 (clinic and bandages（其中6克朗记入债务）); condition_
  _causes: evt:world-vienna-1912:007476_

- **1935.01** `vienna-life-depression/short-time` tags: adulthood, depression, work, injury
  Rosa改成每周上三天班，工钱按日结算。家里的面包从每天一条变成隔天一条，Rosa开始把午饭省下来带回家。
  _effects: cash -3 (short-time work（其中3克朗记入债务）); health -0.04000000000000001 (reduced food); condition_

- **1935.01** `vienna-family-divorce/partner-moves-to-shared-housing` tags: family, marriage, divorce, housing, lifecycle, separation, household
  Anna Lechner 2把一只箱子和工票搬去合租房，带走一半锅碗。Rosa留在Bauer-Wohnung, Leopoldstadt，把空出来的床租给一个学徒，用租金补房租；搬家花掉四克朗，Rosa在户籍上成了分居的人，夜里听见隔壁床板响，总要愣一下。
  _effects: relation_end partner with person-materialized-world-vienna-1912-88; household; cash -4 (moving and separate goods（其中4克朗记入债务）); mood -0.05999999999999994; role_

- **1935.01** `state_tick:fa17-muse/the-sitting-year/set-a-boundary` tags: fa17-muse, muse, art, desire, inspiration, dependence, vulnerability, beauty
  又一年过去，Rosa学会了在进画室前先问清楚这次要坐多久、画完给谁看。Emil Weiss嫌Rosa变得难安排，却也不得不把每次画像的时间写在纸上；那张纸后来被Rosa收进了自己的抽屉。
  _effects: state_parameter fa17-muse.autonomy 55 -> 60; state_parameter fa17-muse.credit 4 -> 8; state_parameter fa17-muse.dependence 53 -> 49; mood 0.030000000000000027_

  Emil Weiss把两人叫到厨房，把借条和账本摊在桌上，说今天要么还钱，要么写个期限。Katharina Lechner先还四克朗，余下的按月在面包房门口交；Rosa把借条折好，放进饼干盒。两人没有握手，但一起把账本翻到了新的一页。
  _effects: cash 4 (first instalment of the family debt); relation kin with person:evt:world-vienna-1912:000689:child; role; flag +family_debt_settled; knowledge “余款每月在面包房门口交，借条放在饼干盒里。”_

- **1935.02** `vienna-housing-move.rent-notice/rent-notice-neighbors-together` tags: housing, home, rent, family, money
  Rosa把Luise Stein和楼里另两户人家叫到楼道，把那张纸摆在窗台上传看。看门人在楼梯拐角听着，说房东礼拜三来收钱。几家人合计好，到那天一起等在门厅，先把克朗递过去，再把修补的事一件件摆出来。Rosa把纸折进围裙口袋，觉得人凑齐了话也硬气些。
  _effects: relation acquaintance with person-materialized-world-vienna-1912-20; mood 0.040000000000000036; flag +tenant_collective_

- **1935.05** `vienna-medical-treatment.night-fever/hospital-night` tags: health, medical, family, money
  护士把冰袋按在Rosa额上，水顺着鬓角流进衣领。Luise Winter坐在床脚，数着窗外渐渐亮起的铺面门板，说回去给炉子添块煤。隔壁床的老头整夜在喊一个女人的名字。
  _effects: cash -2 (医院急诊押金（其中2克朗记入债务）); health 0.08 (急诊观察和冰敷退烧); relation friend with person-materialized-world-vienna-1912-26_

- **1935.10** `vienna-tenement-neighbors.shared_stove_fire/fire_scald_hand` tags: home, social, family, housing
  Rosa在合租房走廊的公用灶台上煮土豆，转身取盐时袖子扫翻了铁锅，滚水浇在左手背上。Josef Bauer从隔壁冲出来，扯下自己的围裙裹住烫伤处，又跑下楼叫来住在二楼的接生婆。接生婆用蛋清和旧亚麻布包扎，嘱咐三天不能沾水。
  _effects: relation friend with person-josef-bauer_

- **1936.01** `r15-edu.tuition-crisis::ctx-vienna-authoritarian-1934::milieu-factory/leave-for-good` tags: education, university, debt, poverty, student, vienna, urban, crisis
  Rosa在最后一学期退了学，因为家里再也寄不出钱。Anna Bauer帮Rosa把宿舍里的书装箱，箱底压着没写完的论文。去车站买票时，Rosa看见同学抱着书从图书馆出来，便转身走进旁边的巷子，等他们走远。
  _effects: mood -0.12; flag +left_university_for_money; state_transition university-student -> university-dropout; education secondary_

  _effects: state_parameter raising-child.schoolProgress 52 -> 60; state_parameter raising-child.caregiverBurden 36 -> 33; mood 0.020000000000000018_

  边境又收紧了，往故乡的列车要换乘三次。邮局说汇款还能走，但信可能压在路上；Rosa还是把两张单子一起递进窗口，像把一句话交给一个不保证送达的人。
  _effects: state_parameter mb17-remittance-web.arrears 6 -> 8; mood -0.040000000000000036; flag +mb17_border_slow_letters_

  Rosa把钱包塞进内袋，走了两条街才敢回头看。三克朗够买一周的煤，彩票也许什么都不是；但那天晚上，Rosa把彩票压在盐罐底下，像压着一张还没有兑现的明天。

  _effects: death (暴力); flag -university_dropout; flag -lucky_idiot_

### 224 subject events

- **1912.03** `vienna-life-birth-letter-from-the-dead/the-letter-is-real` tags: infancy, family, secret, letter, drama
  邮差把一封边角磨白的信塞进门缝，收信人是一个已经死了三年的名字。Josef Bauer把Emil放在膝上，拆开信，里面只有一句：“孩子像不像他？”Katharina Bauer在门口探头，问是不是寄错了；Josef Bauer把信折回去，说“没有寄错”。
  _effects: flag +letter_from_dead_relative; knowledge “一封写给死去亲人的信问：孩子像不像他。”; mood -0.04000000000000001_

- **1912.07** `vienna-infant-household.laundry-day/scalded-hand` tags: infancy, health, family, home, injury
  Josef Bauer提热水壶往木盆里兑水时壶底脱了焊，滚水溅在左手背上。她咬着牙把壶搁下，Emil被响声惊得大哭。手背起了水泡，接下来三天她只能用一只手洗尿布。
  _effects: health -0.07999999999999996 (左手被开水烫伤); mood -0.04999999999999999_

- **1912.09** `p22-comic-christening::ctx-vienna-1912::milieu-tenement/the-wrong-name` tags: infancy, family, christening, church, neighbor, comic, tender, drama
  神父念的是另一张纸条上的名字。Josef Bauer小声纠正，Emil正好吐出一口奶，落在登记簿边上；神父用袖子擦了擦，说这样也算盖过章了，只是纸角留下一小片湿痕。
  _effects: mood -0.010000000000000009; knowledge “洗礼时神父差点念错孩子的名字。”; flag +wrong_christening_name; cash -1 (a christening candle)_

- **1913.01** `vienna-life-infant-first-smile/smile-at-the-lamp` tags: infancy, family, first, warm
  Josef Bauer把Emil抱到窗边，煤汽灯罩上有一圈晃动的光。孩子盯着看了很久，忽然笑出声，口水滴在Josef Bauer的袖口上。屋里的人都停了一下，像是听见了什么了不起的消息。
  _effects: mood 0.04000000000000001; health 0.010000000000000009 (a good day)_

- **1913.02** `vienna-life-toddler-wrong-cradle/the-neighbour-is-mistaken` tags: infancy, family, rumour, identity, comic, drama
  Josef Bauer把被子摊在桌上，指着上面的补丁说这是外婆的针脚。Anna Moser看了半天，承认自己认错了；Emil在旁边把一只木勺敲得咚咚响，像在给这场争论判案。
  _effects: relation acquaintance with person-anna-moser; mood 0.020000000000000004; skill_

- **1913.12** `iw18-infancy.first-word::ctx-klosterneuburg-rural-1912::milieu-tenement/the-dead-grandmothers-name` tags: infancy, first_word, memory, family, wonder, eerie, rural, agriculture
  Emil扶着桌沿站起来，指着门口的空处，清清楚楚叫出一个名字。屋里没人教过这个词；Josef Bauer手里的铝勺停在半空。那是去年冬天埋掉的老人的名字，窗外正好有送煤的车经过，铁轮压过石缝。
  _effects: mood -0.03; knowledge “孩子说出的第一个词，是去年死去的祖母的名字。”; flag +first_word_dead_name_

- **1914.01** `vienna-life-toddler-first-words/first-word-nonsense` tags: infancy, family, first, comic
  Emil说了一长串谁也听不懂的话，最后用力点头，像是在宣布一件已经决定的事。Josef Bauer认真听完，也点了点头；孩子满意地爬走了，留下大人们互相看着，忍不住笑。
  _effects: mood 0.04000000000000001; skill_

- **1915.01** `vienna-life-child-family-legend/lost-relative` tags: childhood, family, secret, story
  Josef Bauer在收拾箱子时翻出一张没有署名的照片，说那是家里一个“去了很远地方”的人。Emil问还回不回来，Josef Bauer把照片翻过去，说先把名字记住，别在饭桌上问。
  _effects: knowledge “家里有一张没有署名的照片，照片上的人去了很远的地方。”; flag +family_legend_photo; mood -0.01_

- **1915.02** `p22-warm-night-move::ctx-klosterneuburg-rural-1912::milieu-tenement/the-cart-loses-a-wheel` tags: childhood, family, move, neighbor, tenement, warm, bittersweet, drama
  手推车在石子路上掉了一只轮子，锅碗滚了一地，邻居的独轮车吱呀作响。Hermann Kral从家里推来独轮车，两个孩子坐在车斗里笑；Josef Bauer一边捡勺子，一边也忍不住笑。
  _effects: cash -2 (a broken cart wheel); mood 0.03; relation acquaintance with person-josef-bauer; flag +cart_wheel_

- **1915.02** `p22-comic-mistaken-child::ctx-klosterneuburg-rural-1912::milieu-tenement/the-cherries-under-the-stall` tags: childhood, family, market, neighbor, mistaken_identity, comic, tender, drama
  市场尽头，一个老太太抓住Emil的手腕，说“你可回来了”。Emil低头看见摊子底下露出两只鞋；真正的孩子正躲在那里吃樱桃，嘴边全是红的，手里还攥着老太太的篮子。
  _effects: mood 0.04; relation acquaintance with person-phase15-Emil-world-vienna-1912; flag +mistaken_for_grandson; cash 1 (a handful of cherries)_

- **1915.02** `p22-drama-fire::ctx-klosterneuburg-rural-1912::milieu-tenement/the-neighbour-catches-the-child` tags: childhood, family, fire, accident, neighbor, survival, dramatic, danger
  油灯倒了，窗帘先冒烟，随后窜起火。Anna Moser冲进来，用湿毯子裹住Emil，自己的手背烧起一串水泡，后来留了疤。孩子的头发全是烟味，人还醒着，邻居的手一直抖，孩子记得。
  _effects: health -0.07999999999999996 (smoke and a tenement fire); condition; health -0.050000000000000044 (burns from the rescue); relation acquaintance with person-phase15-Emil-world-vienna-1912; cash -5 (burnt curtains and a new blanket); flag +fire_survived; mood -0.06_

  Josef Bauer认定孩子在学大人说话，笑着拍手。Emil愣了一下，又把那句话说得更响；Klara也跟着学，三个人的声音在厨房里撞来撞去，最后谁也说不清原句是什么，一起笑了。
  _effects: mood 0.020000000000000018; relation kin with person-phase15-Emil-world-vienna-1912; flag +saying_is_a_game_

- **1916.01** `vienna-life-child-inherited-wish/the-dead-grandmothers-wish` tags: childhood, family, wish, grief, uncanny, drama
  Emil在饭桌上忽然说：“等我长大了，要把那间屋子买回来。”Josef Bauer的勺子停在半空，因为这句话是去世多年的外婆常说的，连“那间屋子”都指得一模一样。Emil说完就低头喝汤，好像只是随口。
  _effects: flag +inherited_wish_house; knowledge “孩子说要把“那间屋子”买回来，和去世的外婆说过的一模一样。”; mood -0.010000000000000009_

  Josef在楼道里滚一只铁环，Emil站在门口看。Josef把铁环推过来，说“你推一次，我推一次”。两个人从楼梯口推到院子里，铁环倒下的声音把午睡的大人吵醒了一排。
  _effects: relation friend with person-phase15-Josef-world-vienna-1912; skill; mood 0.05_

  Emil把墨水瓶碰倒在继承文书上。Josef Bauer一边擦一边看见，纸角的火漆印是去年的，文书却写着十年前的日期。公证人伸手去够那张纸，慢了一步，脸一下子白了。
  _effects: knowledge “继承文书上的火漆印比文书日期新。”; flag +wrong_seal; mood -0.02999999999999997; cash -2 (ink and a ruined envelope); state_add p22-family-legend state:world-vienna-1912:55; relation acquaintance with person-materialized-world-vienna-1912-9_

  面粉袋压在胸口，越抱越沉。Emil前面的大婶一直往后靠，Emil就把膝盖顶住对方的篮子，硬留出半步。买到半袋面粉后，Emil的鞋跟留在人堆里，找回来时鞋面全是泥。回家路上布袋换了两次肩，赤脚踩过石板时脚底发麻。

  Josef Bauer什么也没问，拿出两只杯子，给空椅子那边也倒了一点咖啡代用品。Emil先是高兴，后来又有些不好意思，把杯子往朋友那边推了推。Josef Bauer说“客人先请”，Emil便一本正经地替朋友道谢，声音比平时轻。
  _effects: state_parameter iw18-imaginary-friend.closeness 53 -> 59; state_parameter iw18-imaginary-friend.secrecy 47 -> 44; mood 0.03_

- **1920.01** `state_reaction:p22-family-legend/someone-asks-again` tags: p22-family-legend, family_legend, family, story, secret, inheritance, promise, memory
  又有人问起Emil家的那件旧事。大人先笑了一下，才说“都是很早以前了”；问的人点点头，眼睛却往摇篮那边看，手指在杯沿上停了一会儿，谁也没有接话，窗外有风。
  _effects: state_parameter p22-family-legend.secrecy 81 -> 85; state_parameter p22-family-legend.warmth 37 -> 39; mood 0.010000000000000009_
  _causes: evt:world-vienna-1912:001272_

- **1920.01** `state_exit:p22-family-legend` tags: p22-family-legend, family_legend, family, story, secret, inheritance, promise, memory
  Emil不再处于「家族传说」；条件已经改变。
  _effects: state_remove p22-family-legend state:world-vienna-1912:55_

- **1920.01** `state_tick:iw18-imaginary-friend/friend-year/fading` tags: iw18-imaginary-friend, childhood, play, imaginary_friend, solitude, imagination, secret, state
  Emil这一年很少再和那把椅子说话。学校里有了新的同桌，放学后要在楼梯间跳格子；有一天Emil把书包扔在椅面上，才想起自己已经三天没有给朋友留位置。椅面凉得很快，像一块普通木头。
  _effects: state_parameter iw18-imaginary-friend.closeness 59 -> 51; state_parameter iw18-imaginary-friend.boldness 30 -> 28; mood -0.010000000000000009_

- **1920.01** `p9-gap-childhood-chores.mind-sibling/sibling-carried-afternoon` tags: childhood, household, chores, school, task, daily, sibling, care
  Ferdinand Bauer哭得打嗝，手指一直朝井台方向指。Emil蹲下把Ferdinand Bauer驮上背，沿着墙根走，数砖缝给Ferdinand Bauer听：一、二、三……数到第四圈，背上的呼吸才匀了。天黑时Emil的胳膊麻得抬不过肩，Katharina Bauer回来接过孩子，Emil扶着门框甩了好一会儿手。
  _effects: health -0.030000000000000027 (背弟妹走了一下午，胳膊酸痛); skill; relation kin with person:evt:world-vienna-1912:000513:child_

- **1920.01** `kernel-child.first-errand::ctx-klosterneuburg-rural-1912::milieu-tenement/edu-errand-buys-sweets` tags: childhood, family, market, money, errand, rural, agriculture, small_town
  Emil路过糖果铺，用买盐的钱买了两颗糖。盐只买了半包，Josef Bauer一尝就尝出来。Josef Bauer让Emil把糖纸交出来，摊在桌上，说这下全家都要少放盐。Emil把没吃的那颗推回去，糖纸粘在掌心。
  _effects: food; cash -1 (money spent on sweets); relation kin with person-josef-bauer; flag +spent_errand_money_

- **1920.07** `iw18-play.kite-on-roof::ctx-klosterneuburg-rural-1912::milieu-tenement/climbs-and-gets-it` tags: childhood, play, kite, roof, neighbor, comic, rural, agriculture
  风筝挂在修鞋铺的屋檐上，尾巴缠着铁皮烟囱。Emil踩着煤箱爬上矮墙，Josef在下面扶着箱子，不停地说“慢一点”。Emil把风筝扯下来时撕了一个角，落地时膝盖先着地，手里还举着那截竹骨。
  _effects: health -0.010000000000000009 (a scrape while retrieving the kite); reputation 0.02; relation friend with person-phase15-Josef-world-vienna-1912; mood 0.03999999999999998; flag +kite_from_roof_

- **1920.11** `r1-child.match-seller::ctx-klosterneuburg-rural-1912::milieu-tenement/licence-confiscation` tags: street_selling, matches, cafe, police, poverty, rural, agriculture, small_town
  巡警说街上卖火柴要牌照，把Emil的盒子收走了。Emil跟了两条街，Elisabeth Hofer在门口喊他回来，塞给他一把客人落下的火柴，说别在巡警眼皮底下卖。Emil把空盒子留在窗台上，里面放了一根没卖出去的火柴。
  _effects: cash 1 (咖啡馆门外的火柴); reputation -0.05; flag +matches_confiscated; relation friend with person-materialized-world-vienna-1912-5; relation acquaintance with person-materialized-world-vienna-1912-5; relation acquaintance with person-materialized-world-vienna-1912-16_

- **1921.01** `state_expire:iw18-imaginary-friend` tags: iw18-imaginary-friend, childhood, play, imaginary_friend, solitude, imagination, secret, state
  Emil的「一个只有孩子看得见的朋友」到期；相关安排结束。
  _effects: state_remove iw18-imaginary-friend state:world-vienna-1912:78; flag -imaginary_friend_active_

- **1921.01** `p9-gap-childhood-chores.market-line/line-half-flour` tags: childhood, household, chores, school, task, daily, market, food
  面粉袋压在胸口，越抱越沉。Emil前面的大婶一直往后靠，Emil就把膝盖顶住对方的篮子，硬留出半步。买到半袋面粉后，Emil的鞋跟留在人堆里，找回来时鞋面全是泥。回家路上布袋换了两次肩，赤脚踩过石板时脚底发麻。
  _effects: food; health -0.020000000000000018 (赤脚走石板路磨伤脚底); skill; relation acquaintance with person-materialized-world-vienna-1912-32_

- **1921.09** `kernel-child.secret::ctx-klosterneuburg-rural-1912::milieu-tenement/edu-secret-tells-neighbor` tags: childhood, family, secret, poverty, knowledge, rural, agriculture, small_town
  Emil把当票拿给Emil Weiss看，问银表能赎回来吗。第二天，整栋楼都知道Josef Bauer当了表。Josef Bauer回家时没有发火，只把柜子里的空盒子翻过来给Emil看，说表早就不是我们的了。母亲把空盒子擦了一遍，放回柜子。
  _effects: relation acquaintance with person-emil-weiss; relation kin with person-josef-bauer; reputation -0.03; knowledge “母亲说银表早就不是家里的东西了。”; flag +told_pawn_secret_

- **1921.10** `r2-rural.child-farm-labor::ctx-klosterneuburg-rural-1912::milieu-tenement/kept-in-school` tags: rural, child_labour, harvest, school, injury, agriculture, small_town, peace
  Maria Lechner 2来家里说Emil的算术跟得上，不该缺课。Katharina Bauer把学费的事说了一遍，Maria Lechner 2说可以免一半。Emil坐在门槛上，一手攥着课本，一手攥着没剥完的豆子。
  _effects: flag +kept_child_in_school; skill; relation friend with person-materialized-world-vienna-1912-33; relation rival with person-katharina-bauer; education primary; relation acquaintance with person-materialized-world-vienna-1912-35; relation acquaintance with person-materialized-world-vienna-1912-33_

- **1921.11** `iw18-school.ink-pot::ctx-klosterneuburg-rural-1912::milieu-tenement/the-classmate-confesses` tags: childhood, school, cruelty, shame, teacher, ink, rural, agriculture
  Klara站起来，说瓶子是自己碰的，因为想看清Emil本子上的画。Ferdinand Hofer让两个人都站到讲台前，罚抄同一页字；抄到一半，Klara用胳膊肘碰了碰Emil，把一块橡皮推过来。Emil没有接，嘴角却松了一点。
  _effects: relation acquaintance with person-phase15-Klara-world-vienna-1912; relation acquaintance with person-materialized-world-vienna-1912-34; mood 0.030000000000000027; flag +ink_pot_confessed; relation acquaintance with person-materialized-world-vienna-1912-34_

- **1922.01** `p9-gap-childhood-chores.laundry-help/laundry-bread-with-friend` tags: childhood, household, chores, school, task, daily, laundry, work
  两个孩子在院子两头跑，一个递，一个夹，床单在风里鼓起来。最后一条挂好，Friedrich Huber从厨房拿出面包，先给了自家孩子，再掰一块给Emil。Emil把面包塞进围裙口袋，回家后放在家里人的碗边，说自己在外面吃过了。
  _effects: food; relation acquaintance with person-materialized-world-vienna-1912-23; skill; relation acquaintance with person-materialized-world-vienna-1912-23_

- **1922.08** `vienna-childhood-friends.hidden-book/book-confiscated` tags: childhood, social, school, street
  Emil把书压在算术本底下，只露出书脊。Ferdinand Hofer走过来，抽走算术本，书也跟着滑出来。折角那页画着一艘帆船。Ferdinand Hofer合上书，说放学后擦黑板，擦一星期；书被锁进讲台抽屉，没往校长室送。
  _effects: mood -0.04999999999999999; relation acquaintance with person-materialized-world-vienna-1912-34_

- **1922.11** `vienna-childhood-street.cigarette-card/card-stolen` tags: childhood, street, social, home
  巷口有人跑过来，肩膀撞在Emil的胳膊上。烟画卡脱手，被风卷到路中间。Emil追了两步，鞋底在湿石板上打滑，手掌先着地。Josef从后面追上去，在拐角抓住那男孩的袖子，布条撕下来一截，人还是跑了。
  _effects: mood -0.08000000000000002; relation friend with person-phase15-Josef-world-vienna-1912; condition_

- **1923.01** `vienna-life-school-day/hungry-in-class` tags: childhood, school, hunger, injury
  Emil早上只喝了一碗清汤，第二节课时眼前发黑。Ferdinand Hofer让孩子去走廊坐一会儿，给了半块面包；Emil把面包屑拢在手心吃完，胃里还是空的，但看Ferdinand Hofer的眼神软了些，回到座位时把腰挺直了一点。
  _effects: health -0.030000000000000027 (went to school hungry); condition; relation acquaintance with person-materialized-world-vienna-1912-34_

- **1923.01** `p9-gap-childhood-chores.market-line/line-sold-out-potatoes` tags: childhood, household, chores, school, task, daily, market, food
  “卖完了。”三个字从窗口里传出来，Emil后面的队伍立刻散了一半。Emil没走，趴在窗台上看伙计扫面屑，直到人家挥手赶人。旁边摊子的土豆带着泥，Emil用围裙兜住，一枚一枚数铜板，回家报账时连找零都说清了。
  _effects: food; mood -0.03; skill_

- **1923.06** `r2-faith.parish-school-fee::ctx-klosterneuburg-rural-1912::milieu-tenement/expelled-for-arrears` tags: faith, school, charity, education, fees, rural, agriculture, small_town
  欠了兩個月学费，Ferdinand Hofer把Emil的座位调到最后一排，说书本先留下。Friedrich Hofer 2在门口拦住Emil，说学校不是救济院。Emil回家没有说，第二天照常出门，在火车站坐到放学时间。Josef Bauer后来在书包里发现了一张空白的退学单。
  _effects: mood -0.14; condition; flag +expelled_from_parish_school_for_arrears; relation rival with person-materialized-world-vienna-1912-22; knowledge “two months of unpaid fees can cost a child his seat”; relation acquaintance with person-materialized-world-vienna-1912-22_

- **1924.01** `statistical_infection`
  Emil在Haus der Tante in Klosterneuburg病倒了，肺炎让身体发热、虚弱，接下来的日子只能卧床。

- **1924.01** `state_tick:wish-love/love-pressure/press-on` tags: wish-love, wish, wish:love, love, family, intimacy, state, state_tick
  Emil又把那件没实现的事翻出来想了一遍；这一次没有告诉任何人。
  _effects: mood 0.020000000000000004; flag +wish:love:pursuing_

- **1924.01** `kernel-edu.prepare-for-exam::ctx-klosterneuburg-rural-1912::milieu-tenement/edu-prepare-barely` tags: task, adolescence, school, exam, study, rural, agriculture, small_town
  Emil白天帮家里干活，只能夜里复习。煤油灯熏得眼睛发酸，第二天考试时，Emil把会做的题做完，剩下的凭记忆填满。成绩不高，但够升级。Maria Lechner 3在成绩单上写：尚有潜力。
  _effects: skill; condition; reputation 0.010000000000000002; relation acquaintance with person-materialized-world-vienna-1912-45; flag +passed_exam_barely; relation acquaintance with person-materialized-world-vienna-1912-45_

- **1924.02** `r13-turn.exam-failure::ctx-klosterneuburg-rural-1912::milieu-tenement/retake-fee` tags: education, exam, failure, school, work, rural, agriculture, small_town
  成绩单上差两分。Maria Lechner 2说可以补考，但要先交一克朗卷子费，还得让Katharina Bauer来签字。Katharina Bauer在办公室门口把帽子捏变了形，说家里拿不出这份钱。Emil把错题抄到烟盒背面，第二天照样去上课，只是不再举手。
  _effects: cash -1 (落榜名单上的第三个名字); mood -0.15; condition; flag +failed_entrance_exam; skill; relation guardian with person-katharina-bauer_

- **1924.03** `statistical_recovery`
  Emil从肺炎中慢慢恢复，能下床喝水，但身体还虚弱。

- **1924.12** `vienna-youth-threshold.school-leaving/stay-home-siblings` tags: adolescence, school, work, family, transition
  家里最小的两个还在出疹子，母亲天不亮就得去洗衣房。Emil于是留在家照看弟妹，把离校证明压在镜子后面。街口的杂货铺说等秋天招工再来讲，Ferdinand Hofer临走时叮嘱别把算术丢下，可水要烧，粥要搅，一天就这么过去了。
  _effects: cash -0.8 (留在家中多烧的煤和额外口粮); relation acquaintance with person-materialized-world-vienna-1912-34; mood -0.039999999999999994_

  Ferdinand Bauer把铅笔芯写断了两次，第三遍还是算错，眼泪滴在本子上，把墨迹洇开。Emil把本子拿开，说再写一遍，孩子却把椅子往后一推。母亲在灶边喊别吵，Emil只好把豆子收进碗里，听见Ferdinand Bauer在床上抽鼻子。
  _effects: mood -0.06; flag +homework_ended_in_tears; relation kin with person:evt:world-vienna-1912:000513:child; condition_

  下一站，Emil追下车，把那只手套递还给Klara。Klara说了一声谢谢，手指在毛线手套里缩了一下；Emil回到车上才发现自己坐过了两站，只好沿着环城路走回家。
  _effects: state_add yf18-first-awakening state:world-vienna-1912:138; mood 0.03; flag +yf18_first_awakening_active_

  Hermann Berg把学徒契约摊在桌上，指给Emil看期限、工钱和出师考试。Emil签了名，第二天站到刨床前，手指上多了第一道木刺。
  _effects: relation employer with person-materialized-world-vienna-1912-48; state_add apprenticeship-contract state:world-vienna-1912:139; relation acquaintance with person-materialized-world-vienna-1912-48; education apprenticeship; role_

  Mitzi Moser收了Elisabeth Bauer。Emil在门口站到天黑，回家把父亲的工具箱打开，照着记忆里的样子练。Katharina Bauer进来，说别跟你兄弟争，争来的东西不长久；Emil没有抬头，刨花落在脚背上，像一层薄雪。
  _effects: mood -0.1; flag +lost_apprenticeship_to_sibling; role; skill; relation rival with person-elisabeth-bauer_

- **1925.05** `statistical_infection`
  Emil在Leopoldstädter Zinshaus, gemeinsamer Gang病倒了，肺炎让身体发热、虚弱，接下来的日子只能卧床。

  Emil的肺炎没有完全好转，咳嗽和发热又拖了一个月，家里人轮流守着。

- **1925.08** `vienna-adolescence.stolen-roll/caught-and-returned` tags: adolescence, school, work, family, social
  Emil的手刚碰到面包皮，身后就有人清了清嗓子。Karl Kraus站在晾衣绳下，手里还捏着夹子，什么也没问，只把手伸过来。Emil把面包放回筐里，手背挨了两下，面包筐的盖子被Karl Kraus合上，发出一声闷响。
  _effects: reputation -0.06; relation acquaintance with person-karl-kraus; mood -0.10000000000000003_

  Emil的肺炎没有完全好转，咳嗽和发热又拖了一个月，家里人轮流守着。

  Emil熬过了这场肺炎，能坐起来喝一点汤，但身体还很虚。

  Emil在衣帽间堵住Josef，问那两张票怎么到了Mitzi手里。Josef说Emil从来没敢开口，自己只是替Emil把话说了；Emil把票根揉成一团，塞进Josef的西装口袋，转身时撞翻了侍者的托盘。

  _causes: evt:world-vienna-1912:002874_

  Emil在教堂门口或学校走廊里又遇见Klara。这一次对方先点了头，Emil准备好的整段话全忘了，只把手里的书换到另一只手上；回家后Emil才发现，自己把书拿倒了。

  Emil又学会了一道工序。师傅没有夸，只把下一块料推到面前。
  _effects: state_parameter apprenticeship-contract.progress 10 -> 30; skill; mood 0.020000000000000018_

- **1926.01** `r14-edu.tutoring-sibling::ctx-vienna-reform-1930::milieu-tenement/own-marks-slipped` tags: education, tutored_sibling, family, tutoring, reform, medical, schooling, home
  Emil每晚给Ferdinand Bauer讲题，自己的作业只能留到深夜。期中考试，Emil的名次掉了十几位，Ferdinand Hofer在家长会上点名。回家后，Emil看见Ferdinand Bauer趴在桌上睡着了，算术本还摊在胳膊下面，就没有叫醒。
  _effects: mood -0.06; flag +neglected_own_studies_

- **1926.02** `yf18-runaway.bundle::ctx-vienna-reform-1930::milieu-tenement/hide-the-bundle` tags: runaway, escape, family, plan, night_train, reform, medical, schooling
  Emil把两件衬衫、一双袜子和小时候的洗礼证明卷进一块旧桌布，塞到床底下。Josef Bauer在厨房里数硬币，数了三遍都没有抬头；Emil躺在黑暗里，听见夜车从远处经过，铁轨的声音一直传到枕头下面。
  _effects: state_add yf18-runaway-plan state:world-vienna-1912:176; flag +yf18_bundle_hidden; mood 0.020000000000000018; flag +yf18_runaway_plan_active_

  Emil在天亮前从后门溜走，只带了一个包袱和一双鞋。她沿着电车轨道走到工厂区，门房问她会不会认字，她说会写自己的名字。Anna Bauer后来托人带来一句话：太太把你的工钱扣了。
  _effects: flag +left_domestic_service_for_factory; relation friend with person-anna-bauer; move place-wien-printshop-winter; employment Arbeiterin @ org-winter-print_

  外套腋下裂了一道口子，里布翻出来。Emil把袖口套在桌角上撑开，穿针时试了三次才引上线。针脚一针长一针短，像蜈蚣。Katharina Bauer端着汤进来，站在桌边看了一会儿，把碗往Emil那边推了推，自己只留了半碗。
  _effects: skill; food; relation kin with person-katharina-bauer_

  _effects: mood -0.07; relation colleague with person-materialized-world-vienna-1912-59; relation acquaintance with person-materialized-world-vienna-1912-13; relation acquaintance with person-materialized-world-vienna-1912-59_

- **1927.04** `vienna-youth-threshold.night-class/give-up-for-overtime` tags: adolescence, school, work, family, transition, injury
  工头说这个月赶印一批账簿，晚上加班每小时多给二十赫勒。Emil站在教室门口听完课程表，转身回厂里搬纸。Anna Bauer追出来说座位可以保留两周，Emil点点头，手已经摸上了作坊的门把，夜里机器的声音把课本的事盖了过去。
  _effects: cash 1.6 (夜间加班多挣的工钱); skill; mood -0.03999999999999998; health -0.040000000000000036 (连续夜间加班)_

- **1927.12** `vienna-apprentice-printshop.lead-type-drop/lead-type-drop.repaired` tags: work, apprentice, factory, social, injury
  Katharina Winter 2把散落的铅字按字母补回字盘，Emil赶在午休前重新排完价目表。Buchdruckerei Winter & Söhne用指甲划过一行，说“排得还直”，没扣钱，只让把过道扫干净。
  _effects: skill; health -0.010000000000000009 (蹲着捡铅字与扫过道); relation colleague with person-materialized-world-vienna-1912-57; relation acquaintance with person-materialized-world-vienna-1912-57_

  _effects: role; cash -1 (union dues); relation acquaintance with person-materialized-world-vienna-1912-14; relation acquaintance with person-materialized-world-vienna-1912-14_

  Emil又把那件没实现的事翻出来想了一遍；这一次没有告诉任何人。

- **1928.01** `p9-childhood-apprenticeship.cover-for-sibling/forewoman-catches` tags: adolescence, work, apprenticeship, labour, training, family, task, task:family
  Alois Kral把Emil叫到巷口，问Josef叫什么、住在哪间屋。Emil答到一半就停住了。Alois Kral说这半天的活不算数，说冒名顶替坏了规矩。Emil回到Leopoldstädter Zinshaus, gemeinsamer Gang时，Josef正扶着床沿站着，听完只说“别去了”，说完又低下头，像是自己连累了Emil。
  _effects: mood -0.07; relation kin with person-phase15-Josef-world-vienna-1912; health -0.020000000000000018 (白跑一趟，路上没吃东西)_

- **1928.04** `vienna-adolescence.workshop-apprentice/apprenticeship-accepted` tags: adolescence, school, work, family, social
  作坊里全是松木味。Buchdruckerei Winter & Söhne把一块方木夹进台钳，让Emil推刨子。Emil推了十分钟，木花卷到脚背上，刨面总算平了。Buchdruckerei Winter & Söhne用拇指摸了摸，收下Emil，从钱盒里数出三克朗，说是头一周的饭钱。Josef Bauer在门口把围裙攥得很紧。
  _effects: cash 3 ({subject}学徒首周的膳食预付款); education apprenticeship; reputation 0.1; relation kin with person-josef-bauer_

- **1929.01** `state_transition:apprenticeship-contract/journeyman` tags: apprenticeship-contract, education, craft, apprentice, work, state, state_tick, journeyman
  Emil从「学徒契约」转入另一段处境：passed the journeyman test
  _effects: state_transition apprenticeship-contract -> journeyman; education apprenticeship; role; reputation 0.05; role_

- **1929.01** `p9-childhood-apprenticeship.learn-the-task/small-ad-set` tags: adolescence, work, apprenticeship, labour, training, family, task, task:work
  Marie Huber先让Emil排价目表，自己站在背后看。Emil把字距一行行校齐，锁版时手心全是汗。Marie Huber没夸，只把一张小广告挪到案头，说“你来”。Emil排完最后一行，作坊里两个伙计围过来看，Marie Huber把版举到窗光下，点了点头。
  _effects: skill; reputation 0.039999999999999994; mood 0.04999999999999999; relation colleague with person-marie-huber_

- **1930.01** `major-family.proposal-refused/proposal-refused.wait-for-the-brother` tags: family, courtship, refusal, major, lifecycle
  Grete Hofer 2把话说得很慢：母亲还在，弟弟的学徒期还有三年。Emil点头，把准备好的结婚证词折起来放回口袋，那是借教堂的旧纸写的。两人在电车站分开，约好明年复活节再谈。Grete Hofer 2上车前替母亲拢了拢披肩，没有回头看。
  _effects: relation friend with person-materialized-world-vienna-1912-69; flag +engagement_postponed; cash -1 (tram fare to the meeting); relation acquaintance with person-materialized-world-vienna-1912-69_

- **1930.01** `task-vienna-bourgeois-youth.family-business-or-study/one-year-trial` tags: bourgeois, education, culture, salon, university, family, task, task:education
  Emil和Katharina Bauer谈成一年试用：先管账，若明年考试通过再回学校。Maria Lechner 3听说后只说了句“时间不等人”，但答应借他几本教材。他把教材摞在账房柜台上。
  _effects: relation kin with person-katharina-bauer; relation acquaintance with person-materialized-world-vienna-1912-45; skill; mood 0.030000000000000027_

- **1930.02** `major-education-night-technical-school/night-school-certificate` tags: education, night_school, work, exhaustion, major
  夜校在第七区的旧校舍里，十二个工人挤在一张长桌前。Katharina Winter 2用粉笔画出齿轮的剖面，又让大家传看一只烧坏的保险盒。Emil把图纸带回家，在厨房桌上描到半夜。结业考试通过后，Emil拿到一张机械制图的证书，学费一共九克朗。
  _effects: education secondary; skill; cash -9 (evening-school fees); role; flag +night_school_certificate; relation acquaintance with person-materialized-world-vienna-1912-57_

- **1930.02** `p9-work-printshop.printed-secret/tell-foreman` tags: work, printshop, printing, compositor, craft, workshop, secret, handbill
  Emil把传单的事原样说给Friedrich Huber 2听，Friedrich Huber 2说照印，钱照收，但把原稿锁进自己的抽屉，钥匙揣在背心口袋里。Berta Winter若来问，两人就说排的是戏单。
  _effects: relation colleague with person-materialized-world-vienna-1912-70; cash 1.5 (传单活分到的工钱); mood -0.03999999999999998; relation acquaintance with person-materialized-world-vienna-1912-70; relation acquaintance with person-materialized-world-vienna-1912-46; relation acquaintance with person-materialized-world-vienna-1912-30_

- **1930.02** `vienna-family-courtship/wait-for-a-better-year` tags: family, courtship, household, social, lifecycle
  Emil把话咽回去，只说等明年工钱稳一点再谈。Grete Hofer 2没有追问，两人把桌上的面包分完，各自去上工；出门时Emil回头看了一眼，Grete Hofer 2正把掉在桌上的面包屑拢进掌心，谁也没有把那个话头重新拾起来。
  _effects: relation acquaintance with person-materialized-world-vienna-1912-69_

- **1930.05** `vienna-printshop-daily.union-pamphlet/pamphlet-print-secretly` tags: work, factory, labour, social
  Elisabeth Huber趁夜班把一版工会呼吁加薪的传单塞进Emil的排字盘里，说是替工友印的，谁都不会知道。Emil把那张纸压到排版最底层，机器照样轰隆转。
  _effects: flag +printed_union_pamphlet; relation colleague with person-materialized-world-vienna-1912-14; mood -0.030000000000000027_

- **1930.09** `p9-social-family-conflict-care.sick-parent-night/shifts-with-sibling` tags: family, care, siblings, inheritance, housing, money, injury
  Emil和Klara把值班时辰写在门后的粉笔板上：Klara守前半夜，Emil守后半夜，医生说静养二十一天。煤油灯芯拧小，药粉分成小包摆在床头，便盆换水时尽量不碰出响。二十一天熬下来，Emil的眼皮肿了，走路发飘，但Josef Bauer每次起身总有人扶。
  _effects: health -0.050000000000000044 (连续夜里守夜睡眠不足); flag +night_care_duty_

  Emil没有把账本寄出去，而是租了银行最小的一只保险箱。钥匙挂在Emil的脖子上，账本上的名字却开始出现在Emil的梦里。Katharina Winter 2问起那本旧册子，Emil说已经烧了；说这话时，Emil的手一直按着胸口。钥匙很小，锁孔却很深。
  _effects: state_add p23-dangerous-secret state:world-vienna-1912:449; knowledge “the ledger is in a bank safe”; mood -0.010000000000000009; flag +ledger_hidden_

  _effects: state_parameter p23-dangerous-secret.exposure 30 -> 38; state_parameter p23-dangerous-secret.leverage 75 -> 71; state_parameter p23-dangerous-secret.loyalty 45 -> 39; mood -0.020000000000000018_

  _causes: evt:world-vienna-1912:005534_

  Katharina Winter 2在Leopoldstädter Zinshaus, gemeinsamer Gang生下一个孩子，取名Maria Bauer。Emil在厨房和走廊之间来回，接生的人让烧水、拿布、付六克朗；孩子天亮后睡着了，Emil从此在户籍表上多了“父亲”一栏，手背上还留着烫水壶的红印。
  _effects: birth female; health -0.07999999999999996 (childbirth); cash -6 (midwife fee); role; state_add raising-child state:world-vienna-1912:450_

  Emil站在书架前抽出一本厚小说，就着门口的光读了半章，Marie Huber在柜台后抬起眼皮看了一眼，没说话。读到要紧处，Emil摸出铅笔头把页码记在车票背面，把书插回原处，说下次带够押金再来；白读半章，管理员的眼神却凉了一点。
  _effects: skill; mood 0.020000000000000018; relation acquaintance with person-marie-huber_

  Emil把自己的新鞋留在家里，向Maria Bauer借了一双旧舞鞋。鞋跟矮半寸，鞋面有一道补过的痕。Maria Bauer说这双跳过三场婚礼，跟脚。Emil在舞池里转了两圈，果然不疼；Ferdinand Stein却盯着那双旧鞋看了很久，说鞋跟像上一季的。
  _effects: mood 0.06000000000000005; condition; flag +wore_borrowed_shoes_to_ball; relation friend with person-materialized-world-vienna-1912-10; relation rival with person-materialized-world-vienna-1912-47; knowledge “the borrowed shoes have danced through three weddings”; relation acquaintance with person-materialized-world-vienna-1912-2; relation acquaintance with person-materialized-world-vienna-1912-47_

  _effects: skill; relation colleague with person-alois-lechner; relation acquaintance with person-materialized-world-vienna-1912-6; relation acquaintance with person-materialized-world-vienna-1912-73_

  Ferdinand Bauer没有等到毕业，跟着同乡去了铁路工地上工。走的那天，Emil把攒下的两克朗塞进Ferdinand Bauer的口袋，Ferdinand Bauer没有推辞。火车开走后，Emil在站台上站了很久，手里还拿着那本没讲完的算术书。

  _effects: cash -15 (失去零用后自付开销); relation kin with person-josef-bauer; flag +self_supporting_student; skill_

- **1932.12** `p9-gap-adult-solitude.stopped-clock/stopped-clock.church-bell` tags: adulthood, daily, solitude, ordinary, home, clock, time
  Emil没有再修钟，把上工的时间交给楼下教堂的钟声。风从多瑙河那边刮过来时，钟声就散，窗户一关更听不见。有一回她误了钟点，到车间时工头已经在门口站着，扣了半个克朗。
  _effects: cash -0.5 (迟到被扣工钱（其中0.5克朗记入债务）); mood -0.050000000000000044_

- **1933.01** `vienna-family-divorce/partner-moves-to-shared-housing` tags: family, marriage, divorce, housing, lifecycle, separation, household
  Katharina Winter 2把一只箱子和工票搬去合租房，带走一半锅碗。Emil留在Leopoldstädter Zinshaus, gemeinsamer Gang，把空出来的床租给一个学徒，用租金补房租；搬家花掉四克朗，Emil在户籍上成了分居的人，夜里听见隔壁床板响，总要愣一下。
  _effects: relation_end partner with person-materialized-world-vienna-1912-57; household; cash -4 (moving and separate goods（其中4克朗记入债务）); mood -0.06; role_

  Emil反复回想最后一次见面。对方说过的话被拆成很多小块，每一块都能扎人。

  _causes: evt:world-vienna-1912:006757_

  Emil在Leopoldstädter Zinshaus, gemeinsamer Gang靠窗的桌边，把一只空咖啡杯转了半圈，说房租两人合付、桌子两人共用。Rosa的手停在糖罐盖上，问了一句“要是过不下去呢”，没有当场答应，只说要想一想；杯里的咖啡凉了，话还悬在桌面上。

  有人被问起Emil时，只说自己那晚在家煮汤。Emil后来听说这件事，把汤勺在锅里多搅了两圈。

  厂门口的砖墙被太阳晒得发烫。Ferdinand Stein在旁听着，插了一句这后生看着手稳。工头把烟灰弹进阴沟，说试半天，午饭他管。Emil点头时，肚子先响了一声。
  _effects: flag +trial_work_pending; relation acquaintance with person-materialized-world-vienna-1912-6; mood 0.050000000000000044_

- **1933.07** `vienna-adult-social.sunday-table/sunday-table.argument` tags: adulthood, social, work, family, partnership
  星期天中午，Emil和Franz Kral在出租屋的厨房桌边分面包，话题从房租转到Emil母亲上个月的药钱。Franz Kral把刀放下，说这个家不能总先填那边的窟窿。面包凉了，汤也没再热。
  _effects: relation partner with person-materialized-world-vienna-1912-87; mood -0.050000000000000044_

- **1933.10** `vienna-family-same-sex-male/private-arrangement` tags: family, intimacy, same_sex, persecution, lifecycle, partnership
  Emil和Franz Kral在城边租了一间小屋，对外只说是同事合住。房租四克朗，两人不同时出门，信件用假名，夜里把窗帘拉严；Franz Kral在门后放了一根木棍，Emil把两人的工票分开放，连咳嗽都压着声。
  _effects: relation partner with person-materialized-world-vienna-1912-87; mood 0.040000000000000036; cash -4 (separate room rent); relation acquaintance with person-materialized-world-vienna-1912-87_

  Buchdruckerei Winter & Söhne的工头翻完工票，问Emil上一个东家是谁。Emil说厂子关了，工头又问有没有辞工证明；没有证明，名册就不能写。后面的人挤上来，Emil被推到台阶下面。中午的钟响了，Emil站在街对面，看新来的人把名字一个个写上去。
  _effects: cash -2 (carfare and waiting); reputation -0.03; flag +no_work_references; role; mood -0.06999999999999995; relation acquaintance with person-materialized-world-vienna-1912-67; relation acquaintance with person-materialized-world-vienna-1912-67_

  浆糊是隔夜的，刷上去时带着股酸味。Emil把纸四角按平，有人凑近念出“一九〇一年，欠三十四克朗”。办公室里的椅子腿不平，坐在上面的人一动就晃，可没人起身。
  _effects: reputation 0.05; relation acquaintance with person-anna-bauer; flag +union_ledger_public_

  厂里把一张短工名单贴在厂门口。Emil的名字在第三行，旁边写着“下周等通知”。等到月底，通知没有来，工票也停了。
  _effects: unemployment (laid off in the depression); cash -5 (lost wages); condition_

- **1935.01** `wc17-commission.masterwork::ctx-vienna-authoritarian-1934::milieu-tenement/masterpiece-accepted` tags: craft, commission, masterwork, workshop, reputation, patronage, vienna, urban
  主顾在契约上按了印，要求一把带黄铜镶边的胡桃木书柜。Emil带着Rosa做了四十天，刨花堆到窗台；交货那天，主顾把尾款数了两遍，又在工匠名册上添了Emil的名字。
  _effects: organization_found 师傅作坊 (household_firm); position 师傅 @workshop:person-phase15-Emil-world-vienna-1912-evt:world-vienna-1912:008222; state_add wc17-master-craftsperson state:world-vienna-1912:812; cash 4 (masterwork commission fee); skill; reputation 0.08; relation acquaintance with person-materialized-world-vienna-1912-66; flag +masterwork_accepted; knowledge “主顾把尾款数了两遍，并把我的名字写进了工匠名册。”; relation acquaintance with person-materialized-world-vienna-1912-66; role; flag +master_craftsperson_

  又过了两年，秘密没有自己长脚。Emil把知道的人一一数过，发现只剩两个还住在这条街上；其中一个已经老得记不清门牌，另一个每次见面都先移开眼睛。Emil把剩下的线索重新收好，放回原处。
  _effects: state_parameter p23-dangerous-secret.exposure 49 -> 45; state_parameter p23-dangerous-secret.leverage 63 -> 68; state_parameter p23-dangerous-secret.loyalty 35 -> 38; mood 0.010000000000000009_

  Berta Winter端来一碗兑了水的牛奶，顺手把Emil家的炉子捅旺。Emil靠在床头，听Berta Winter讲楼下谁也被传染了，谁家把病人送去了医院。人走后，屋里还留着一点煤烟和奶味。
  _effects: cash -1 (退烧后的第一碗粥); flag +recovered_with_neighbor_help; relation friend with person-materialized-world-vienna-1912-30_

  登记桌后面，Maria Kral把表格推过来，说这一家人缺一个本地担保人。Emil Huber抱着一只藤箱，箱角用绳子捆了三道。Emil写下自己的名字和门牌号，笔尖顿了一下；Maria Kral盖章时没有抬头，只说“出了事找你”。
  _effects: flag +guaranteed_refugee_family; knowledge “担保人要替被担保的人负责。”; reputation -0.010000000000000009_

- **1935.10** `vienna-market-household.friday-fish/friday-fish.salt` tags: home, market, food, social
  Emil把腌鲱鱼放在黑面包上，咸味够配一大杯水。Katharina Bauer拿着盐罐过来，看见鱼就说拿卷心菜换。换完以后，两家灶上都多了一样东西。
  _effects: cash -2 (腌鲱鱼和黑面包); food; relation acquaintance with person-katharina-bauer_

  旅馆老板Emil Weiss把一张订单摊在刨床上：四十张床架，月底交货。Emil算了一遍工时，按现在的做法只能保本；第二天，Emil把Grete Kraus的午饭时间缩短，又把工钱改成按件计。Grete Kraus没有反对，只在木料上多画了一道线。
  _effects: state_transition wc17-master-craftsperson -> wc17-sweatshop-owner; cash 4 (hotel order deposit); relation employer with person:evt:world-vienna-1912:001003:child; reputation -0.01999999999999999; flag +took_the_big_order; mood -0.010000000000000009; knowledge “四十张床架的利润来自缩短午饭时间和按件压价。”; role; flag -master_craftsperson; role; flag +sweatshop_owner_

  铅笔头短得捏不住，Emil把它夹在两指之间，在表格背面写下两个名字。被漏掉的人一个叫扬，一个叫弗兰茨，都是每天清早从火车站走来的临时工。工头看了一眼背面，没说话，把表贴回墙上。
  _effects: skill; relation colleague with person-materialized-world-vienna-1912-93; reputation 0.04000000000000001; relation acquaintance with person-materialized-world-vienna-1912-93_

  _effects: reputation 0.01999999999999999; skill; relation rival with person-materialized-world-vienna-1912-24; flag +blocked_permit; knowledge “扩建申请的排水图与消防通道不符合现行规定。”; relation acquaintance with person-materialized-world-vienna-1912-24_

  午休的哨子刚响，Katharina Moser夹着烟走到门口。Emil报上姓名、住址和干过的活，声音有点发紧。工头嗯了一声，说明早六点来，管一顿午饭，干得好再谈工钱。
  _effects: relation acquaintance with person-materialized-world-vienna-1912-79; mood 0.050000000000000044; relation acquaintance with person-materialized-world-vienna-1912-79; relation acquaintance with person-materialized-world-vienna-1912-7_

- **1938.01** `state_reaction:wc17-sweatshop-owner/blood-on-the-cloth` tags: wc17-sweatshop-owner, work, factory, ownership, profit, guilt, labor, sweatshop
  一个学徒的手指被卷进皮带，血滴在麻布上。Emil让人把她送去诊所，又吩咐把染红的布裁掉；晚上算账时，赔偿金和那批布的钱写在同一页。
  _effects: state_parameter wc17-sweatshop-owner.guilt 20 -> 30; state_parameter wc17-sweatshop-owner.profit 47 -> 45; cash -2 (worker compensation)_
  _causes: evt:world-vienna-1912:011159_

- **1938.05** `p9-social-tenement-neighbors.stove-and-line/stove-and-line.first-come-order` tags: housing, neighbors, tenement, daily, money, reputation
  Emil把四只洗衣盆按到厨房的先后排成一列，用粉笔在门后写下各家的时辰：六点到七点半、七点半到九点。Hermann Kral起初嘟囔，看到自家名字排在第二格，也就把水壶提了回去。晾衣绳按同样次序分两段，谁家的床单先上谁先占。
  _effects: mood 0.040000000000000036; relation acquaintance with person-hermann-kral; relation acquaintance with person-materialized-world-vienna-1912-91_

- **1938.06** `vienna-housing-move.rent-notice/rent-notice-demand-repair` tags: housing, home, rent, family, money
  Emil没有立刻答话，先去敲Berta Winter 2的门。两人一起数落墙上的水渍和门框里灌进来的风，楼梯间的窗户修好了，自家墙皮还在剥落。Berta Winter 2提议一起把话带给看门人：先修好漏水的墙角，涨租的事再谈。Emil把纸重新折好，请他把话带到门外。
  _effects: relation acquaintance with person-materialized-world-vienna-1912-56; mood 0.030000000000000027; flag +rent_dispute_

- **1939.01** `state_tick:wc17-sweatshop-owner/ledger-year/ledger-balances` tags: wc17-sweatshop-owner, work, factory, ownership, profit, guilt, labor, sweatshop
  账本合上时，Emil又多出三克朗：少算的工时、扣下的学徒午饭、一批没报税的麻布。车间里没人抬头，机器声盖住了算盘声。
  _effects: state_parameter wc17-sweatshop-owner.profit 45 -> 50; state_parameter wc17-sweatshop-owner.loyalty 35 -> 33; state_parameter wc17-sweatshop-owner.guilt 30 -> 32; cash 2 (workshop profit)_

- **1939.01** `p9-social-relationship-initiative.introduce-two-people/leave-them-alone` tags: courtship, friendship, family, social, initiative, housing, task, task:social
  介绍完就找了个由头先走，说家里炉子还烧着，账只结了自己的那一杯。晚上回到楼里，缝纫女工在楼梯上碰见，只说那人挺有意思，没再多讲。老友第二天在印厂什么也没提，这事就搁在那里。
  _effects: cash -0.3 (自己那一杯咖啡); relation friend with person-materialized-world-vienna-1912-93; relation acquaintance with person:evt:world-vienna-1912:000823:child; mood 0.010000000000000009_

- **1939.10** `vienna-parish-aid.funeral-society/funeral-society.pay` tags: faith, parish, mutual_aid, poverty, family
  Emil数出八赫勒交给Berta Winter 2，看她在蓝皮本上找到自家门牌，把这一周的格子划掉。Berta Winter 2说上个月隔壁巷子的人走了，公账出了棺材和一场小弥撒，剩下的钱记在册尾。楼梯口的灯很暗，Emil盯着自己的名字看了两遍才转身。
  _effects: cash -0.4 (一周丧葬互助会会费); relation acquaintance with person-materialized-world-vienna-1912-56; flag +funeral_society_member_

- **1940.01** `state_tick:wc17-sweatshop-owner/ledger-year/inspection-week` tags: wc17-sweatshop-owner, work, factory, ownership, profit, guilt, labor, sweatshop
  检查员在楼梯口闻到了煤烟和汗味，数了数窗边的床位。Emil把罚单折进账本，转身催工头把剩下的活赶完；这一批货的利润刚好抵掉罚款。
  _effects: state_parameter wc17-sweatshop-owner.inspectionRisk 75 -> 90; state_parameter wc17-sweatshop-owner.profit 50 -> 47; cash -1 (inspection fine); mood -0.040000000000000036_

  Emil暂时把那件事收进抽屉；日子还有别的东西要应付。
  _effects: mood -0.020000000000000018_

- **1940.01** `p9-history-public-action.organize-relief/backyard-stove` tags: politics, union, party, activism, public_action, relief, leaflet, task

  _effects: cash -3 (买柴火和盐); food; mood -0.030000000000000027; relation acquaintance with person-materialized-world-vienna-1912-35_

  _effects: state_parameter wc17-sweatshop-owner.loyalty 33 -> 25; state_parameter wc17-sweatshop-owner.profit 47 -> 44; state_parameter wc17-sweatshop-owner.inspectionRisk 90 -> 100_

- **1940.04** `vienna-market-household.friday-fish/friday-fish.share` tags: home, market, food, social
  鱼还滴着水，Emil在走廊里分成两份。一份拿进自家，一份搁在Katharina Moser的门口。傍晚Katharina Moser来还盘子，盘子里是一小袋面粉。
  _effects: cash -6 (买鱼); food; relation acquaintance with person-materialized-world-vienna-1912-79_

- **1940.07** `p9-gap-adult-solitude.misdelivered-letter/misdelivered-letter.opened` tags: adulthood, daily, solitude, ordinary, home, letter
  信封没封牢，Emil在灯下把它掀开，里面是维也纳联合银行的催款单，欠了三十四克朗。她认出那名字是楼上常穿黑裙的女人，便把纸原样叠回去，用米汤把封口粘好，放到门房窗台上。
  _effects: mood -0.010000000000000009; flag +knows_upstairs_debt_

- **1940.08** `vienna-housing-move.rent-notice/rent-notice-pay` tags: housing, home, rent, family, money
  Emil把通知念给Berta Winter 2听，Berta Winter 2摇头说是楼梯间那几块新玻璃的账。Emil去楼下杂货铺换开一张五克朗的纸币，回屋一枚一枚摆开数，煤箱底多留了二十赫勒。
  _effects: cash -2 (按月多付两克朗房租); food; mood -0.030000000000000027_

- **1941.01** `state_tick:wc17-sweatshop-owner/ledger-year/inspection-week` tags: wc17-sweatshop-owner, work, factory, ownership, profit, guilt, labor, sweatshop
  检查员在楼梯口闻到了煤烟和汗味，数了数窗边的床位。Emil把罚单折进账本，转身催工头把剩下的活赶完；这一批货的利润刚好抵掉罚款。

- **1941.01** `p9-social-relationship-initiative.introduce-two-people/overpraise` tags: courtship, friendship, family, social, initiative, housing, task, task:social
  介绍时把两边都夸过了头，说老友是印厂里最靠得住的人，说女工的手艺整条街都找不出第二个。话太多，两人反倒拘谨起来，各自看窗外。咖啡凉了，坐了不到一个钟头就散了，出门时谁也没提再见面。
  _effects: cash -0.9 (三杯咖啡和点心); relation friend with person-materialized-world-vienna-1912-93; relation acquaintance with person:evt:world-vienna-1912:001948:child_

- **1941.01** `p9-gap-adult-unemployed.dock-morning/dock-picked-coal` tags: adulthood, unemployment, casual_labour, poverty, daily, dock, injury
  多瑙河码头的雾还没散，工头捏着名单念了Emil的名字。煤灰落在汗湿的脖颈上，Alois Weiss在铁门边看着，说这活儿一天顶三天，可肩膀得疼上一个礼拜。
  _effects: cash 4.2 (卸煤半日的工钱); health -0.050000000000000044 (扛煤筐磨破肩膀)_

- **1941.11** `vienna-tenement-neighbors.laundry_line_dispute/neighbor_yanks_clothes` tags: home, social, family, housing
  Katharina Weiss 3嫌Emil的床单滴水滴到Katharina Weiss 3家衬衫上，一把扯下床单扔在院子的石板上。Emil下楼捡起来时，床单角沾了泥，重洗又要费肥皂。两人在院子当中对喊，楼上有人推开窗看热闹。Emil把床单重新搭上绳子时，故意把水拧在Katharina Weiss 3那侧。
  _effects: relation rival with person-materialized-world-vienna-1912-77; cash -0.15 (重洗床单的肥皂钱); relation acquaintance with person-materialized-world-vienna-1912-77_

  _effects: cash -0.5 (给表亲车费); food; relation kin with person-karl-kraus; mood -0.030000000000000027_

  Emil把旧账本摊在汤锅旁边，翻到药费那一页，说今天得当场算清才肯坐下。Anna Bauer的勺子停在半空，最后也把本子拉过去，一行行对起数来。汤在炉子上咕嘟着，谁也没先盛；等账算到最后一个赫勒，Emil在页脚写下“结清”，两人才同时伸手去够勺子。
  _effects: relation kin with person-anna-bauer; flag +medicine_bill_settled_

  _effects: role; food; cash -2 (replacement documents)_

  _effects: cash -6 (支付邻居照看工钱和煤钱); food; health 0.04 (白天有人按时喂药和换洗)_

- **1912.06** `vienna-life-infant-first-smile/colic-night` tags: infancy, family, first, warm, injury
  夜里Lena哭了很久，Friedrich Hofer抱着孩子在屋里来回走，数着地板缝。天快亮时孩子终于打了个嗝，安静下来；Friedrich Hofer坐在床沿，发现自己还保持着走路的姿势。
  _effects: mood -0.029999999999999992; health -0.010000000000000009 (lost sleep)_

  _effects: state_add p22-child-omen state:world-vienna-1912:16; mood -0.04; knowledge “医生写下又划掉的诊断里，露出一个“冬”字。”_

- **1913.01** `vienna-life-toddler-first-words/first-word-nonsense` tags: infancy, family, first, comic
  Lena说了一长串谁也听不懂的话，最后用力点头，像是在宣布一件已经决定的事。Josefine Hofer认真听完，也点了点头；孩子满意地爬走了，留下大人们互相看着，忍不住笑。
  _effects: mood 0.04; skill_

- **1913.01** `state_tick:p22-family-legend/the-story-changes-hands/told-at-the-table` tags: p22-family-legend, family_legend, family, story, secret, inheritance, promise, memory
  又过了一年，Lena家的故事在饭桌上换了新的讲法：多了一句笑话，少了一处哭声。听的人把汤匙放下，等下一句；讲故事的人却先去看窗子有没有关好，谁也没有先动筷子。
  _effects: state_parameter p22-family-legend.warmth 77 -> 80; state_parameter p22-family-legend.secrecy 19 -> 17; state_parameter p22-family-legend.promise 60 -> 62; mood 0.01_

- **1913.01** `state_tick:p22-child-omen/the-omen-returns/the-coincidence-repeats` tags: p22-child-omen, child_omen, omen, strangeness, attention, protection, fear, superstition
  又过了一年，同一件小事又发生了：钟停在同一个时刻，门口的狗对着空处叫。Lena正在学系鞋带，抬头问“又来了吗”；大人说没有，手却先去摸门框，谁也没有回答。
  _effects: state_parameter p22-child-omen.strangeness 60 -> 65; state_parameter p22-child-omen.attention 65 -> 69; state_parameter p22-child-omen.fear 55 -> 58; mood -0.01_

- **1913.11** `vienna-life-toddler-wrong-cradle/the-blanket-is-wrong` tags: infancy, family, rumour, identity, comic, drama
  Hermann Kral盯着Lena的摇篮看了很久，说这床蓝被子不是这家人的。Josefine Hofer把被子翻过来，角落绣着一个陌生的姓；Lena却抓着被角不放，像是认得它。Hermann Kral退后一步，说“我什么都没说”。
  _effects: flag +wrong_cradle_blanket; knowledge “摇篮里的蓝被子绣着一个陌生的姓。”; mood -0.03_

- **1914.01** `vienna-life-birth-letter-from-the-dead/the-letter-is-a-bill` tags: infancy, family, secret, letter, drama
  信里没有问候，只有一张旧账单：棺材、墓地、一趟从边境回来的车费。Josefine Hofer把账单翻过来，背面用铅笔写着“别让孩子知道”。Lena伸手去抓纸角，Josefine Hofer没有让。
  _effects: cash -4 (paid an old funeral debt); flag +family_debt_letter; mood -0.03_

- **1914.02** `vienna-life-toddler-accident/scalded-hand` tags: infancy, accident, family, danger, injury
  厨房的锅盖没有盖严，Lena伸手去够桌上的勺子，蒸汽先扑到手腕上。Josefine Hofer把孩子抱到水缸边，一瓢一瓢地浇冷水；孩子哭得打嗝，锅里的汤还在冒泡。
  _effects: health -0.07999999999999996 (scalded hand); condition; cash -1 (bandages and ointment); mood -0.04999999999999999_

- **1915.02** `vienna-life-child-family-legend/happy-legend` tags: childhood, family, secret, story
  Josefine Hofer讲起自己小时候在乡下追一只白鹅，追到河边，鹅没追到，倒捡回一篮鸭蛋。Lena笑得从凳子上滑下来，Josefine Hofer也跟着笑，说这个家不是只有欠账和葬礼。
  _effects: mood 0.04; mood 0.01999999999999999_

- **1915.02** `vienna-early-childhood.stairwell-fall/arm-bruise` tags: childhood, family, home, street, injury
  楼梯间的灯罩空着，第三级踏板比别处高。Lena一脚踩空，滚到平台，右小臂磕在铁扶手的弯头上。Josefine Hofer听见哭声跑出来，把Lena抱回屋，用冷水毛巾敷那块青紫，再用旧布条缠了两圈。夜里Lena哭醒两次。
  _effects: health -0.040000000000000036 (从楼梯滚下，小臂挫伤); mood -0.05000000000000002_

- **1915.02** `state_reaction:p22-family-legend/someone-asks-again` tags: p22-family-legend, family_legend, family, story, secret, inheritance, promise, memory
  又有人问起Lena家的那件旧事。大人先笑了一下，才说“都是很早以前了”；问的人点点头，眼睛却往摇篮那边看，手指在杯沿上停了一会儿，谁也没有接话，窗外有风。
  _effects: state_parameter p22-family-legend.secrecy 17 -> 21; state_parameter p22-family-legend.warmth 80 -> 82; mood 0.010000000000000002_
  _causes: evt:world-vienna-1912:000414_

- **1916.01** `vienna-life-child-inherited-wish/the-caregiver-cries` tags: childhood, family, wish, grief, uncanny, drama
  Josefine Hofer没有回答，起身去洗碗，水开得很大。Lena跟到厨房，拽了拽衣角，说“你别哭，我不说了”。Josefine Hofer蹲下来，把孩子抱得很紧，说“你说，你继续说”。
  _effects: mood -0.04999999999999999; flag +inherited_wish_grief_

- **1916.01** `state_tick:p22-family-legend/the-story-changes-hands/told-at-the-table` tags: p22-family-legend, family_legend, family, story, secret, inheritance, promise, memory
  又过了一年，Lena家的故事在饭桌上换了新的讲法：多了一句笑话，少了一处哭声。听的人把汤匙放下，等下一句；讲故事的人却先去看窗子有没有关好，谁也没有先动筷子。
  _effects: state_parameter p22-family-legend.warmth 82 -> 85; state_parameter p22-family-legend.secrecy 21 -> 19; state_parameter p22-family-legend.promise 62 -> 64; mood 0.010000000000000002_

  又过了一年，同一件小事又发生了：钟停在同一个时刻，门口的狗对着空处叫。Lena正在学系鞋带，抬头问“又来了吗”；大人说没有，手却先去摸门框，谁也没有回答。
  _effects: state_parameter p22-child-omen.strangeness 65 -> 70; state_parameter p22-child-omen.attention 69 -> 73; state_parameter p22-child-omen.fear 58 -> 61; mood -0.010000000000000002_

- **1916.11** `vienna-life-child-first-friend/shared-toy` tags: childhood, friendship, family, comic
  Anton在楼道里滚一只铁环，Lena站在门口看。Anton把铁环推过来，说“你推一次，我推一次”。两个人从楼梯口推到院子里，铁环倒下的声音把午睡的大人吵醒了一排。
  _effects: skill; mood 0.05000000000000001_

- **1917.01** `state_expire:p22-family-legend` tags: p22-family-legend, family_legend, family, story, secret, inheritance, promise, memory
  Lena的「家族传说」到期；相关安排结束。
  _effects: state_remove p22-family-legend state:world-vienna-1912:15_

- **1917.03** `iw18-home.first-memory::ctx-klosterneuburg-rural-1912::milieu-tenement/the-first-snow` tags: childhood, first_memory, fire, family, wonder, fear, rural, agriculture
  Lena清早醒来，窗台上积了一层白，玻璃上的冰花像羽毛。Josefine Hofer还没生火，屋里冷得能看见呼吸；Lena把手指按在窗上，留下五个小洞，又隔着洞看楼下的送煤车。那天是Lena记得的第一个冬天。
  _effects: mood 0.04000000000000001; flag +first_memory_snow_

- **1917.10** `kernel-child.household-rules::ctx-klosterneuburg-rural-1912::milieu-tenement/edu-rules-waits-turn` tags: childhood, family, food, rules, everyday, rural, agriculture, small_town
  Josefine Hofer把汤锅端上桌，先给做工的人盛，再给孩子。Lena伸手去够锅边的面包，被轻轻敲了一下手背。Josefine Hofer说，等大家都坐下。Lena把手收回膝上，数着勺子碰碗的声音，等轮到自己。
  _effects: skill; relation kin with person-josefine-hofer; flag +learned_table_order_

- **1917.12** `p22-drama-inheritance-papers::ctx-klosterneuburg-rural-1912::milieu-tenement/the-seal-is-wrong` tags: childhood, family, inheritance, papers, secret, official, dramatic, dark
  Lena把墨水瓶碰倒在继承文书上。Josefine Hofer一边擦一边看见，纸角的火漆印是去年的，文书却写着十年前的日期。公证人伸手去够那张纸，慢了一步，脸一下子白了。
  _effects: knowledge “继承文书上的火漆印比文书日期新。”; flag +wrong_seal; mood -0.02999999999999997; cash -2 (ink and a ruined envelope); state_add p22-family-legend state:world-vienna-1912:70; relation acquaintance with person-materialized-world-vienna-1912-22_

- **1918.01** `state_tick:p22-family-legend/the-story-changes-hands/a-detail-goes-missing` tags: p22-family-legend, family_legend, family, story, secret, inheritance, promise, memory
  又过了一年，故事里最要紧的那件东西不见了：先是名字，后来是地点。Lena问起时，大人说“你记错了”；可柜子最底层那只空盒子，谁也没有扔，锁却换过了，钥匙也不见了。
  _effects: state_parameter p22-family-legend.secrecy 75 -> 79; state_parameter p22-family-legend.danger 60 -> 63; state_parameter p22-family-legend.warmth 30 -> 28; mood -0.010000000000000009_

- **1918.01** `p9-gap-childhood-chores.market-line/line-half-flour` tags: childhood, household, chores, school, task, daily, market, food
  队伍从面包房门口拐进巷子，Lena把布袋夹在腋下，学着前面人的样子，用胳膊肘顶住后面人的篮子。窗口的伙计舀面粉时，Lena踮脚看着秤，直到半袋面倒进袋口。挤出人群时鞋跟被踩掉，Lena单脚跳了两步，在泥里摸回鞋，索性赤脚走回家。
  _effects: food; health -0.020000000000000004 (赤脚走石板路磨伤脚底); skill; relation acquaintance with person-materialized-world-vienna-1912-24_

- **1918.02** `iw18-play.kite-on-roof::ctx-klosterneuburg-rural-1912::milieu-tenement/climbs-and-gets-it` tags: childhood, play, kite, roof, neighbor, comic, rural, agriculture
  风筝挂在修鞋铺的屋檐上，尾巴缠着铁皮烟囱。Lena踩着煤箱爬上矮墙，Josef在下面扶着箱子，不停地说“慢一点”。Lena把风筝扯下来时撕了一个角，落地时膝盖先着地，手里还举着那截竹骨。
  _effects: health -0.009999999999999995 (a scrape while retrieving the kite); reputation 0.02; relation friend with person-phase15-Josef-world-vienna-1912; mood 0.04000000000000001; flag +kite_from_roof_

- **1919.01** `state_expire:p22-child-omen` tags: p22-child-omen, child_omen, omen, strangeness, attention, protection, fear, superstition
  Lena的「孩子身上的兆头」到期；相关安排结束。
  _effects: state_remove p22-child-omen state:world-vienna-1912:16_

- **1919.01** `p9-gap-childhood-chores.laundry-help/laundry-bread-with-friend` tags: childhood, household, chores, school, task, daily, laundry, work
  两个孩子在院子两头跑，一个递，一个夹，床单在风里鼓起来。最后一条挂好，Anna Moser从厨房拿出面包，先给了自家孩子，再掰一块给Lena。Lena把面包塞进围裙口袋，回家后放在家里人的碗边，说自己在外面吃过了。
  _effects: food; relation acquaintance with person-anna-moser; skill; relation acquaintance with person-materialized-world-vienna-1912-17_

- **1920.01** `state_exit:p22-family-legend` tags: p22-family-legend, family_legend, family, story, secret, inheritance, promise, memory
  Lena不再处于「家族传说」；条件已经改变。
  _effects: state_remove p22-family-legend state:world-vienna-1912:70_

- **1920.01** `p9-gap-childhood-chores.market-line/line-both-families` tags: childhood, household, chores, school, task, daily, market, food
  Josefine Hofer把Lena拽到队伍前头，往自己身前塞，说“孩子占不了多大地儿”。窗口一次递出两包面包，Lena把邻居那包夹在胳肢窝，自己这包抱在胸前。到Leopoldstädter Zinshaus, gemeinsamer Gang门口，Lena蹲下把两包并排，按Josefine Hofer说的份量分开，连找零的铜板也分成两小堆。
  _effects: food; relation acquaintance with person-josefine-hofer; skill_

- **1921.01** `p9-gap-childhood-chores.laundry-help/laundry-three-heller` tags: childhood, household, chores, school, task, daily, laundry, work
  “拽平，别留褶。”Elisabeth Huber在绳子那头喊。Lena踮着脚把床单拉直，碱水把指缝泡得发白。收工时天已经黑了，Elisabeth Huber数了三赫勒，又在围裙上擦干手才递过来。Lena把钱塞进袜筒，回家路上一直攥着，虎口的裂口沾了汗，火辣辣地疼。
  _effects: cash 0.03 (帮邻居晾床单挣的三赫勒); relation acquaintance with person-materialized-world-vienna-1912-14; relation acquaintance with person-materialized-world-vienna-1912-14; relation acquaintance with person-materialized-world-vienna-1912-12_

- **1922.01** `p9-gap-childhood-chores.market-line/line-half-flour` tags: childhood, household, chores, school, task, daily, market, food
  面粉袋压在胸口，越抱越沉。Lena前面的大婶一直往后靠，Lena就把膝盖顶住对方的篮子，硬留出半步。买到半袋面粉后，Lena的鞋跟留在人堆里，找回来时鞋面全是泥。回家路上布袋换了两次肩，赤脚踩过石板时脚底发麻。
  _effects: food; skill; relation acquaintance with person-materialized-world-vienna-1912-2; relation acquaintance with person-materialized-world-vienna-1912-21_

- **1923.01** `p9-gap-childhood-chores.laundry-help/laundry-bread-with-friend` tags: childhood, household, chores, school, task, daily, laundry, work
  Emil Weiss 2家的孩子站在绳子另一头，Lena递夹子时故意慢半拍，两个人把床单拉得没有一道褶。收工后Emil Weiss 2端出半包隔夜面包，掰开时掉了一桌屑。Lena把分到的那块用围裙兜住，回家路上闻着麦味，硬是没咬。
  _effects: food; relation acquaintance with person-materialized-world-vienna-1912-16; skill; relation acquaintance with person-materialized-world-vienna-1912-16; relation acquaintance with person-materialized-world-vienna-1912-32_

- **1924.02** `p9-childhood-adolescence.courtyard-gang/gang-prater-locked-out` tags: adolescence, friendship, identity, work, school, secret, church
  Lena跟着Mitzi穿过普拉特大街，看人放完焰火才想起回家。院门已经上了闩，母亲的灯灭了，Lena在楼梯间坐到后半夜，膝盖冻得发僵。
  _effects: relation friend with person-phase15-Mitzi-world-vienna-1912; mood -0.01999999999999999_

- **1924.04** `r13-turn.exam-failure::ctx-vienna-reform-1930::milieu-tenement::reform-open-secondary/name-missing` tags: education, exam, failure, school, work, reform, medical, schooling
  补考那天，Lena在考场门口等到钟响，监考说名单上没有名字。Anna Bauer跑去教务处，回来时手里只有一张转学建议。Lena去了印刷厂当学徒，师傅说铅字比字母好认，至少不会骗人。
  _effects: condition; flag +left_school_after_exam_failure; role; relation colleague with person-anna-bauer; education apprenticeship_

- **1925.01** `r13-edu.exam-eve::ctx-vienna-reform-1930::milieu-tenement/memorized-wrong-step` tags: education, exam, study, poverty, adolescence, reform, medical, schooling
  Lena把老师讲的例题步骤全背下来，却没弄懂为什么移项。卷子发下来，第一道大题就卡住，Lena在草稿纸上画满小人，交卷前只写出一个答案。回家后Lena把那张卷子折成纸飞机，扔出窗外，又下楼捡了回来。纸飞机摔在煤堆上。
  _effects: mood -0.08; skill; relation acquaintance with person-materialized-world-vienna-1912-34_

- **1925.08** `r13-turn.school-leaving::ctx-vienna-reform-1930::milieu-tenement::reform-leaving-age/straight-to-factory` tags: education, school, work, family, transition, reform, medical, schooling
  Maria Lechner 2把Lena的名字从升学名单上划掉，说厂里正缺人手。家里没有人争，只把午饭里的半块面包塞给Lena。Lena进了纺织厂，工牌上写着“临时”，每天站十二个钟头，月底工钱比学徒多两克朗。
  _effects: cash 2 (毕业钟响过以后); condition; flag +left_school_for_factory; role; relation acquaintance with person-materialized-world-vienna-1912-33_

- **1925.08** `r1-fam.schooling-decision::ctx-vienna-reform-1930::milieu-tenement/school.apprenticeship` tags: family, education, apprenticeship, poverty, future, reform, medical, schooling
  Emil Weiss 2把学徒合同摊在桌上，说三年管饭，出师后一天两克朗。Maria Lechner 2说Lena的算术是班里最好的，再读两年可以考师范。Josefine Hofer把合同推回去，说家里等不起两年；Lena看着窗外，操场上有人在踢一只破球。
  _effects: cash 2 (学徒合同还是课本); flag +left_school_for_apprenticeship; relation colleague with person-materialized-world-vienna-1912-16; knowledge “老师认定这个学生能考上师范。”; education apprenticeship_

- **1926.01** `r13-civ.first-aid-course::ctx-vienna-reform-1930::milieu-tenement/certificate-earned` tags: health, first_aid, course, skill, school, reform, medical, schooling
  Maria Bauer把一条绷带绕在Lena的小臂上，教怎么打结才不会勒住血管。Lena在同桌的手臂上练了三遍，最后一遍得到了点头。课程结束时，Maria Bauer发了一张油印证书，上面盖着红十字的章。
  _effects: flag +earned_first_aid_certificate; role; skill; knowledge “a bandage must be tight enough to hold but loose enough for ”; relation acquaintance with person-materialized-world-vienna-1912-10_

- **1926.02** `yf18-runaway.bundle::ctx-vienna-reform-1930::milieu-tenement/hide-the-bundle` tags: runaway, escape, family, plan, night_train, reform, medical, schooling
  Lena把两件衬衫、一双袜子和小时候的洗礼证明卷进一块旧桌布，塞到床底下。Josefine Hofer在厨房里数硬币，数了三遍都没有抬头；Lena躺在黑暗里，听见夜车从远处经过，铁轨的声音一直传到枕头下面。
  _effects: state_add yf18-runaway-plan state:world-vienna-1912:178; flag +yf18_bundle_hidden; mood 0.020000000000000004; flag +yf18_runaway_plan_active_

- **1926.02** `yf18-firstlook.atlas-margin::ctx-vienna-reform-1930::milieu-tenement/write-back-in-pencil` tags: first_love, school, atlas, note, secrecy, reform, medical, schooling
  地理课上，Lena在地图册的空白处看见一行铅笔字：“多瑙河从维也纳流走，不从这儿。”Lena认出是Rosa的笔迹，趁Ferdinand Hofer转身，在下面添了一句：“那你往哪儿流？”下课铃响时，Lena把地图册塞进Rosa的课桌。
  _effects: state_add yf18-first-awakening state:world-vienna-1912:179; relation friend with person-phase15-Rosa-world-vienna-1912; flag +yf18_atlas_note; flag +yf18_first_awakening_active_

- **1926.04** `vienna-youth-threshold.night-class/enroll-night-class` tags: adolescence, school, work, family, transition
  Ferdinand Hofer在黑板上写下课程表和书本费：一学期三克朗，另加六十赫勒杂费。Lena下工后啃着冷土豆赶到教室，坐到公共长桌最靠煤气灯的一头。手指上还留着白天的油污，握笔时在本子上蹭出灰印，但座位号已经登记在册。
  _effects: cash -3.6 (夜校学费与书本杂费); education secondary; skill_

- **1926.04** `r15-work.master-takes-on::ctx-vienna-reform-1930::milieu-tenement/signed-contract` tags: work, craft, apprentice, trade, poverty, reform, medical, schooling
  Luise Bauer把学徒契约摊在桌上，指给Lena看期限、工钱和出师考试。Lena签了名，第二天站到刨床前，手指上多了第一道木刺。
  _effects: relation employer with person-materialized-world-vienna-1912-7; state_add apprenticeship-contract state:world-vienna-1912:233; relation acquaintance with person-materialized-world-vienna-1912-7; education apprenticeship_

- **1926.10** `yf18-dare.bridge-rail::ctx-vienna-reform-1930::milieu-tenement/climb-back-down` tags: dare, bridge, river, friendship, risk, reform, medical, schooling
  Lena的手已经搭上栏杆，听见桥下有驳船拉笛，忽然把手收回来。Anton骂了一句，Luise Kraus却把围巾解下来递给Lena；三个人在桥头站了一会儿，谁也没有再提这件事。

  Lena在学校的煤炉边把油印纸烧了，灰烬和煤渣混在一起。Ferdinand Hofer第二天问第三十四页读得怎么样，Lena说译文看不出来；Ferdinand Hofer点点头，把课本收回去，从此没有再借给Lena任何书。
  _effects: mood -0.020000000000000004; relation acquaintance with person-materialized-world-vienna-1912-34; flag +yf18_burned_pamphlet_

  _effects: state_parameter yf18-first-awakening.intensity 63 -> 55; state_parameter yf18-first-awakening.secret true -> true; flag +yf18_gave_tickets_away; mood -0.02_

  _effects: state_add ld18-public-desire state:world-vienna-1912:292; reputation -0.06; relation friend with person-phase15-Rosa-world-vienna-1912; mood -0.07; flag +letter_read_aloud; flag +public_desire_active_

  _effects: cash -3 (行李放在楼梯口); mood 0.04; flag +moved_to_lodging_near_work; relation colleague with person-materialized-world-vienna-1912-16; move place-wien-printshop-winter; relation acquaintance with person-materialized-world-vienna-1912-52_

  _effects: mood -0.07_

  德语作文题是《我的家乡》。Lena写了半页多瑙河，剩下半页写一个总在电车上看书的人。Maria Lechner 3没有打分，只在页边写“感情太多，论据太少”，把作文折好放进自己的公文包；放学后，Lena看见Mitzi在门口等，忽然不敢走过去。
  _effects: state_parameter yf18-first-awakening.intensity 69 -> 75; state_parameter yf18-first-awakening.talk 3 -> 6; skill; reputation -0.010000000000000002; mood 0.010000000000000002; relation acquaintance with person-materialized-world-vienna-1912-45_

  _effects: state_parameter ld18-public-desire.rumorHeat 41 -> 53; state_parameter ld18-public-desire.protection 0 -> 0; cash -2 (the first payment to a blackmailer); mood -0.039999999999999994_

  Helene Lechner 2说煤店的账拖不过去，向Lena借五克朗。Lena从工钱袋里抽出钱，Helene Lechner 2在烟盒背面写了借条，说发薪日一定还；两人把烟盒压在厨房的盐罐下面。那天晚上，Lena家的炉子只烧了半筐煤。
  _effects: cash -5 (lent five kronen for the neighbour’s coal bill); relation acquaintance with person-materialized-world-vienna-1912-37; role; flag +loan_outstanding; knowledge “借条写在烟盒背面，压在厨房的盐罐下面。”; relation acquaintance with person-materialized-world-vienna-1912-37_

  Lena下楼倒水，在楼梯口看见院子里站着一对夫妻，男人的围裙上还沾着面粉，女的攥着一张工钱条。她没插话，提着壶又上楼，关门时听见那女人说“一星期八克朗怎么够”。
  _effects: mood -0.03; skill_

- **1930.02** `vienna-courtship-courtyard.sunday-walk/part-at-arch` tags: adulthood, courtship, partner, social, family
  走到桥洞前，Mitzi说该往回走了，家里炉子还没生。Lena站在原地看着Mitzi拐上台阶，围巾的一角被风掀起来；下周的事谁也没有提，两人就此分开，口袋里只剩一颗凉透的栗子。
  _effects: mood -0.05; relation acquaintance with person-phase15-Mitzi-world-vienna-1912; flag +uncertain_courtship_

- **1930.02** `vienna-medical-treatment.night-fever/hospital-night` tags: health, medical, family, money
  电车在环城大道上哐当响，Hermann Winter 2攥着Lena的胳膊，怕人从座位上滑下去。急诊厅的煤气灯发黄，长椅上全是咳嗽声，押金窗口的铜盘递出两克朗，换来一张硬纸号牌。
  _effects: cash -2 (医院急诊押金); health 0.08 (急诊观察和冰敷退烧); relation friend with person-materialized-world-vienna-1912-52_

- **1930.02** `vienna-women-housework.coal-stove/coal-stove.cold-day` tags: gender, housework, home, labour, family
  孩子放学回来搓着手说冷，Lena把仅有的半捧煤末压进炉底，火苗细得像灯芯。她没接Katharina Bauer递来的煤块，只说等明天煤店开门。屋里哈气成雾，晚饭的土豆是凉的。
  _effects: mood -0.04000000000000001; relation acquaintance with person-katharina-bauer_

- **1930.05** `vienna-housing-move.rent-notice/rent-notice-neighbors-together` tags: housing, home, rent, family, money
  Lena把Josef Bauer和楼里另两户人家叫到楼道，把那张纸摆在窗台上传看。看门人在楼梯拐角听着，说房东礼拜三来收钱。几家人合计好，到那天一起等在门厅，先把克朗递过去，再把修补的事一件件摆出来。Lena把纸折进围裙口袋，觉得人凑齐了话也硬气些。
  _effects: relation acquaintance with person-josef-bauer; mood 0.04000000000000001; flag +tenant_collective_

- **1930.10** `p9-social-courtship-friendship.returned-sugar-box/sugar-box-go-in-person` tags: courtship, friendship, social, adulthood, reputation, leopoldstadt
  Lena亲自去Emil Hofer家，把话说清：糖盒既退回来，以后不再托人登门。Emil Hofer的母亲站在门里，说这都是为自家孩子打算，语气不重也不轻。Lena把帽子戴正，没有纠缠，下楼时一步一步数着台阶；门房和邻居都看在眼里，这一回至少把话说在了明处。
  _effects: relation acquaintance with person-materialized-world-vienna-1912-76; reputation 0.03; relation acquaintance with person-materialized-world-vienna-1912-76_

  死去的评论家在最贵的报纸上给一部没人看过的歌剧写了四颗星。Lena把剪报贴在排练厅门口，第二天票房排到街角；Grete Moser问是谁写的，Lena说是一个死人，死人不会否认。三个月后，歌剧院把这部戏列进了秋季剧目。
  _effects: state_add p22-wild-fortune state:world-vienna-1912:458; state_add p22-scandal-engine state:world-vienna-1912:459; cash 5 (a share of the revived opera receipts); reputation 0.04; relation acquaintance with person-materialized-world-vienna-1912-24; flag +forged_review_believed; mood 0.03; relation acquaintance with person-materialized-world-vienna-1912-15; flag +wild_fortune_active; flag +scandal_engine_active_

  Lena买了两张最便宜的楼座票，一共四克朗。Luise Kral家里起初不准，说星期天要回家吃饭，后来松了口。幕间两人分了一块带来的面包，音乐厅的暖气把外套烘得发烫。
  _effects: cash -4 (两张楼座票); mood 0.09; relation friend with person-luise-kral; skill_

- **1931.10** `vienna-tenement-neighbors.laundry_line_dispute/subject_yields_line` tags: home, social, family, housing
  Lena看Maria Lechner 3盆里那几件小衬衫，把自家床单卷起来抱回屋，说等下午再晾。Maria Lechner 3愣了一下，闷头把衣服挂好，收工回来时在Lena门口放了一小包用旧报纸裹的洗衣碱。Lena打开报纸，碱面里还掺着几粒粗盐。
  _effects: relation friend with person-materialized-world-vienna-1912-45; food_

- **1931.11** `vienna-adult-social.burial-fund/burial-fund.skipped` tags: adulthood, social, work, family

  _effects: mood -0.05; relation colleague with person-phase15-Anton-world-vienna-1912_

  一个曾经替Lena传话的人搬走了，带走了半条街的信任。第二天，信箱里多了一张没有署名的明信片，上面只写着“我还记得”；Lena把明信片翻过来，背面是空的。
  _effects: state_parameter p22-scandal-engine.heat 45 -> 52; state_parameter p22-scandal-engine.reach 60 -> 65; state_parameter p22-scandal-engine.leverage 50 -> 46; state_parameter p22-scandal-engine.shame 40 -> 46; mood -0.020000000000000004_

- **1932.01** `r13-civ.clinic-first-visit::ctx-vienna-reform-1930::milieu-tenement/stamp-on-credit` tags: health, clinic, queue, diagnosis, institution, reform, medical, schooling
  Maria Kral把挂号纸推回来，说没有疾病保险的印花就不能看门诊。Lena数出几枚硬币，手在柜台上停住，后面的人绕过她往前挤，把窗口堵得更紧。Maria Kral最后把印花本翻到最后一页，说先记在账上，月底一起收。
  _effects: cash -2 (诊所走廊的第三张长凳); flag +clinic_fee_recorded_as_debt; relation acquaintance with person-maria-kral; knowledge “the outpatient fee can be recorded against next month’s wage”; relation acquaintance with person-materialized-world-vienna-1912-38_

- **1932.02** `hs18-radio.crystal::ctx-vienna-reform-1930::milieu-tenement/charge-pennies` tags: radio, neighbor, comic, daily, media, shortage, reform, medical
  Lena把矿石收音机放在窗台上，邻居们自带小板凳，挤得屋里没有下脚处。有人往桌上放两枚铜币，有人带半块糖。Elisabeth Weiss说这比电影院便宜；话音刚落，天线碰到铁栏，声音又没了。
  _effects: cash 2 (neighbors paid a few coins to listen); relation friend with person-materialized-world-vienna-1912-32; reputation 0.020000000000000004; skill_

- **1932.07** `vienna-women-housework.wash-day/wash-day.send-out` tags: gender, housework, home, labour, family
  洗衣妇把工服从报纸里抖出来，翻开领口看了看油垢，点头收下。Lena转身回屋，铜板在围裙口袋里磕出轻响。Anna Berg还在楼梯上嘟囔，说这钱不如买半斤猪油。
  _effects: cash -0.5 (请洗衣妇代洗工服（其中0.5克朗记入债务）); mood 0.03; relation acquaintance with person:evt:world-vienna-1912:000209:child_

- **1932.11** `vienna-church-community.saint-vincent-box/box-request-granted` tags: faith, social, family, home
  Lena在圣文生会门口登记，领到一张面包券和一小袋土豆。
  _effects: food; relation acquaintance with person-alois-lechner; mood 0.03_

- **1932.11** `vienna-parish-aid.rent-box/rent-box.food-only` tags: faith, parish, mutual_aid, poverty, family
  Lena只收下篮里的面包和豆子，把煤袋推回Hermann Kral手里，说房租自己再想办法，请她们给介绍点零工。Hermann Kral把煤放在走廊窗台，说礼拜四后院有人要拆旧木箱，一赫勒一捆，工钱不多但现结。Lena记下门牌，答应一早过去。
  _effects: food; flag +seeking_work; relation acquaintance with person-hermann-kral_

- **1933.01** `vienna-family-childbirth/daughter-born` tags: family, childbirth, death, lifecycle, birth, state, state:raising-child, raising-child
  Lena在Leopoldstädter Zinshaus, gemeinsamer Gang生下一个女婴，取名Rosa Stein。接生的人收六克朗，Ferdinand Stein在走廊烧水，邻居借来一只摇篮；Lena起身时眼前发黑，三天后腰还像断了一样，孩子哭声响亮，把屋里的冷气都顶了回去。
  _effects: birth female; cash -6 (midwife fee（其中6克朗记入债务）); relation acquaintance with person-materialized-world-vienna-1912-29; state_add raising-child state:world-vienna-1912:612_

- **1933.01** `r13-fam.reconcile-parent::ctx-vienna-reform-1930::milieu-tenement/rebuffed` tags: task, family, parent, reconciliation, estrangement, reform, medical, schooling
  Josefine Hofer让Lena把面包提回去，说这些年没有Lena也过来了。Lena站在门口，看见墙上还挂着自己小时候的照片，玻璃裂了一道。Josefine Hofer顺着她的目光看过去，却没有把照片摘下来。
  _effects: mood -0.16; condition; flag +parent_refused_reconciliation; relation rival with person-josefine-hofer_

- **1933.05** `vienna-family-divorce/reconcile-for-now` tags: family, marriage, divorce, housing, lifecycle
  Lena和Ferdinand Stein在楼道里吵到半夜，最后因为下个月的房租又坐回同一张桌。谁都没有提和好，只把账本重新摊开；Ferdinand Stein把煤钱推过来，Lena接过去数了两遍，手指碰到一起又分开，屋里的灯亮到很晚。
  _effects: mood -0.030000000000000027_

- **1933.07** `p9-social-tenement-neighbors.evicted-neighbours/evicted-neighbours.only-hot-water` tags: housing, neighbors, tenement, daily, money, reputation
  Lena没有开门，只从门缝递出一壶热水，说自家也挤不下。走廊里的箱子到半夜还堆着，孩子的哭声隔着门板一阵一阵。Lena坐在桌边没有点灯，第二天在楼梯间遇见时避开了目光。
  _effects: mood -0.060000000000000026; relation acquaintance with person-materialized-world-vienna-1912-44; relation acquaintance with person-materialized-world-vienna-1912-44_

- **1934.01** `vienna-family-childbirth/difficult-birth` tags: family, childbirth, death, lifecycle, birth, state, state:raising-child, raising-child
  Lena难产，接生的人守了两天。孩子最后生下来，取名Anna Stein；Lena一直发烧，医院又收走二十克朗，Ferdinand Stein把钟表送进当铺。Lena落下产后虚弱的毛病，抱孩子时手臂发抖，站久了眼前就发黑。
  _effects: birth female; condition; cash -20 (midwife and hospital（其中20克朗记入债务）); state_add raising-child state:world-vienna-1912:708_

- **1934.01** `pc20-apprentice.second-workshop::ctx-vienna-authoritarian-1934::milieu-tenement/reopen-bench` tags: craft, apprentice, failure, workshop, second_chance, tools, patronage, vienna
  Katharina Hofer 2把工作台的钥匙放在Lena手心里，说工具可以借，名声不能借。Lena擦了一整夜的刨子，第二天在门口挂出小木牌；第一位客人不是Elisabeth Hofer，而是一个要修椅背的邻居。
  _effects: organization_found 重新开张的作坊 (household_firm); position 师傅 @workshop:person-phase15-Lena-world-vienna-1912-evt:world-vienna-1912:007465; state_add pc20-second-chance state:world-vienna-1912:709; relation colleague with person-materialized-world-vienna-1912-81; cash -2 (tools, rent and a new sign（其中2克朗记入债务）); flag +reopened_workshop_after_failure; knowledge “工作台的钥匙还能用，工具可以借，名声不能借。”; relation acquaintance with person-materialized-world-vienna-1912-81; relation acquaintance with person-materialized-world-vienna-1912-5; flag +second_chance_open_
