- **1934.01** `state_tick:raising-child/school-year/struggles` tags: raising-child, family, child, care, school, household, state, state_tick
  Lena被叫到学校，老师说Rosa Stein这学期总在课上看窗外。回家的路上，Lena没有骂，只把买好的铅笔又数了一遍；晚饭时，两个人都吃得很少。
  _effects: state_parameter raising-child.schoolProgress 20 -> 15; state_parameter raising-child.caregiverBurden 45 -> 50; cash -2 (extra lessons and replacement pencils（其中2克朗记入债务）); mood -0.030000000000000027_

- **1934.01** `r14-work.negotiate-loan::ctx-vienna-authoritarian-1934::milieu-tenement/signed-for-eighteen` tags: debt, loan, money, institution, risk, vienna, urban, crisis
  放贷人把借据推过来，第二行用更小的字写着月息。Lena念到“逾期以家具作抵”时，Ferdinand Hofer把钢笔帽拧上，说这条街上没有第二家肯借钱给一个刚被停用的人。Gustav Moser在门口咳了一声，算是见证。Lena签了名，十八克朗当场点清。
  _effects: cash 18 (借据上的第二行); condition; flag +took_loan; relation acquaintance with person-materialized-world-vienna-1912-34; knowledge “the loan is secured against household furniture”; relation acquaintance with person-materialized-world-vienna-1912-83_

- **1934.01** `ml18-lottery.same-numbers::ctx-vienna-authoritarian-1934::milieu-tenement/the-number-wins-without-them` tags: lottery, luck, money, family, regret, windfall, vienna, urban
  Lena用了十几年同一组号码——父亲当年送奶路线的门牌号。这个月房租涨了，Lena少买了一张；开奖那天，那组号码中了头奖。Ferdinand Stein把报纸摊在桌上，Lena看了很久，最后去厨房把水壶坐上，水开了才发现自己没有拿杯子。
  _effects: cash -2 (the drink and the extra trip to the newsagent（其中2克朗记入债务）); state_add ml18-reversal state:world-vienna-1912:813; flag +missed_the_numbers; knowledge “父亲送奶路线的门牌号中了头奖，而这一期我没有买。”; mood -0.09999999999999998; flag +fortune_reversed; mood -0.010000000000000009_

- **1934.05** `vienna-illness-household.mother-falls/stay-home-care` tags: health, family, home
  Lena跟工头请了三天假，在灶上熬粥，给Josefine Hofer换药。工钱少了一截，母亲的腿却慢慢消了肿；夜里Lena把椅子拼在床边，听见母亲呼吸平稳才合眼。
  _effects: cash -4 (请假三天损失的工钱（其中4克朗记入债务）); health 0.1 (在家照护后腿肿消退); relation kin with person-josefine-hofer_

- **1934.12** `r2-leisure.mourning-dress::ctx-vienna-authoritarian-1934::milieu-tenement/mourning-dress-wrong-shade` tags: leisure, dressmaker, mourning, grief, craft, vienna, urban, crisis
  Lena把裙子举到窗边，发现黑绸在日光下泛出一点蓝。Katharina Bauer说这像舞厅的颜色，不像丧服。Lena说染坊只剩这一匹，Katharina Bauer把脸转过去，说那就再染一遍。Gustav Huber抱着裙子往染坊跑，裙摆拖过煤渣。
  _effects: cash -5 (黑绸裙的最后一针（其中5克朗记入债务）); mood -0.09999999999999998; condition; flag +mourning_dress_wrong_shade; relation rival with person-katharina-bauer; relation colleague with person-materialized-world-vienna-1912-15_

- **1935.01** `vienna-family-childbirth/stillbirth` tags: family, childbirth, death, lifecycle
  孩子生下来没有哭声。接生的人把布盖上去，Ferdinand Stein在楼梯间坐了很久；Lena在床上躺了三天，家里没有办葬礼，只去市政办事处登记，付了六克朗。登记表上母亲一栏写着Lena的名字；此后邻居提起Lena，只说“那个没了孩子的母亲”，柜子里还留着没拆的襁褓。
  _effects: cash -6 (midwife and burial（其中6克朗记入债务）); role_

- **1935.01** `state_tick:raising-child/school-year/learns` tags: raising-child, family, child, care, school, household, state, state_tick
  Lena把Anna Stein的旧课本包上书皮，又在本子上签了名。Anna Stein今年学会了自己系鞋带，也学会了在饭桌上说“不”；Lena听着，觉得屋子比去年更满。
  _effects: state_parameter raising-child.schoolProgress 20 -> 28; state_parameter raising-child.caregiverBurden 45 -> 42; mood 0.020000000000000018_

  Lena把答应过的事又做了一遍：按时出现，把欠下的小钱还掉，没有在旧话上添新的解释。对方没有鼓掌，只把杯子往Lena这边推了推；这一次的沉默比上次轻。
  _effects: state_parameter pc20-second-chance.credibility 55 -> 61; state_parameter pc20-second-chance.suspicion 30 -> 25; state_parameter pc20-second-chance.hope 65 -> 69; mood 0.020000000000000018_

- **1935.01** `agency:attend_meeting` tags: agency, agency:attend_meeting
  Lena去了Arbeiter-Zeitung im Vorwärts-Haus，坐在后排听完了关于维也纳社会民主党在工人街区、工会和报刊网络中持续活动，争取普选、劳动保护与社会救济的争论；散会时带走一张油印提纲。
  _effects: historical development:seed-vienna-social-democracy-1912 as participant; knowledge “我参加了维也纳社会民主党在工人街区、工会和报刊网络中持续活动，争取普选、劳动保护与社会救济。。”; mood 0.030000000000000027_

- **1935.03** `vienna-social-movement.recruitment/read-and-decline` tags: development, social_movement, organization, politics
  Lena把传单读了两遍，记下上面的地址，却没有去。第二天照常上工；午休时听见两个工友低声谈论同一场集会。
  _effects: mood -0.010000000000000009_

- **1935.08** `ml18-business.bottle-cap-patent::ctx-vienna-authoritarian-1934::milieu-tenement/sell-the-patent-outright` tags: business, patent, money, windfall, invention, comic, vienna, urban
  破产拍卖会上，Lena用四克朗买下一张没人举牌的瓶盖专利，只因为图纸画得好看。三个月后，重新开张的作坊的矿泉水厂找上门：那圈软木垫正好能解决他们的漏气问题。Katharina Bauer 2核对完专利号，重新开张的作坊当场开出八十克朗的支票。
  _effects: cash 80 (sale of the bottle-cap patent); state_add ml18-sudden-fortune state:world-vienna-1912:897; reputation 0.06000000000000001; flag +sold_patent; mood 0.09999999999999998; relation acquaintance with person-materialized-world-vienna-1912-35; flag +sudden_fortune; reputation 0.020000000000000004; mood 0.030000000000000027_

  到期日过了三天，Ferdinand Hofer带着法院的人上楼，把缝纫机和两只木箱抬到走廊里登记。Elisabeth Moser把一张粉色的纸贴在镜子上，说七天内可以赎。Maria Bauer把门开了一条缝，又轻轻合上。Lena蹲在地上捡散落的线轴，线缠住了手指。
  _effects: cash -20 (到期日那天（其中20克朗记入债务）); condition; flag +loan_default; relation rival with person-materialized-world-vienna-1912-34; knowledge “the household has seven days to redeem the seized furniture”; relation acquaintance with person-materialized-world-vienna-1912-6_

  _effects: relation acquaintance with person-materialized-world-vienna-1912-83; mood -0.050000000000000044; flag +refused_secret_meeting_

- **1936.02** `state_reaction:ml18-sudden-fortune/the-papers-hear-about-it` tags: ml18-sudden-fortune, money, windfall, sudden_fortune, household, luck, inheritance, lottery
  报纸的社会版把Lena家的门牌号和那笔钱的数目印在一起，标题是“一笔不该有的好运”。第二天门口多了两个陌生人，一个说是远亲，一个说代表一家孤儿院。
  _effects: state_parameter ml18-sudden-fortune.public true -> true; state_parameter ml18-sudden-fortune.claims 1 -> 3; reputation -0.01999999999999999; flag +windfall_went_public_
  _causes: evt:world-vienna-1912:009590_

- **1936.09** `bc18-fame.wrong-bust::ctx-vienna-authoritarian-1934::milieu-tenement/widow-corrects` tags: fame, mistaken_identity, museum, public, invention, absurd, status, humiliation
  揭幕到一半，穿黑纱的Helene Kral 2从第二排站起来，说那根火柴是她丈夫一八九三年在布尔诺登记的，专利号还刻在家里一只锡盒上。Gustav Hofer 2请她进办公室核对文件，Lena被留在展厅里，面对一队已经买票的小学生。第二天，铭牌换成了“无名热心市民”，Lena的名字被刮掉，只剩一道白痕。
  _effects: reputation -0.04; state_add bc18-ridiculous-fame state:world-vienna-1912:985; cash -1 (退掉车马费后的车钱（其中1克朗记入债务）); relation rival with person-materialized-world-vienna-1912-12; condition; flag +plaque_scraped_clean; mood -0.050000000000000044; relation acquaintance with person-materialized-world-vienna-1912-73; flag +ridiculously_famous; reputation 0.020000000000000004; mood 0.020000000000000018_

- **1936.09** `state_reaction:bc18-ridiculous-fame/reprinted-wrong` tags: bc18-ridiculous-fame, fame, reputation, mistaken_identity, promotion, media, absurd, public
  报纸的周末副刊又把Lena的旧照片翻出来，配上一个更整齐的故事：发明、失败、谦逊的晚年。Lena写信去更正，编辑回了一张便条，说读者喜欢现在这个版本。
  _effects: state_parameter bc18-ridiculous-fame.notoriety 22 -> 28; state_parameter bc18-ridiculous-fame.absurdity 45 -> 50; state_parameter bc18-ridiculous-fame.legitimacy 5 -> 1; flag +story_reprinted_wrong; mood -0.010000000000000009_
  _causes: evt:world-vienna-1912:009867_

- **1937.01** `r14-work.criminal-offer::ctx-vienna-authoritarian-1934::milieu-tenement/refused-the-back-door` tags: crime, criminal_offer, risk, money, temptation, vienna, urban, crisis
  Elisabeth Huber把条件说完，Lena把手插回口袋，说这门生意找别人。Elisabeth Huber没有生气，只把烟头踩灭，说这条街上不缺愿意干的人。Lena走出巷子，才发现后背的衬衫已经湿透。Grete Hofer 2在街角等着，问谈得怎么样。
  _effects: cash -1 (仓库后门的提议（其中1克朗记入债务）); mood 0.020000000000000018; flag +refused_criminal_offer; relation rival with person-materialized-world-vienna-1912-14; relation colleague with person-materialized-world-vienna-1912-69; knowledge “the fixer keeps a list of men who owe him money”; relation acquaintance with person-materialized-world-vienna-1912-69_
  _causes: evt:world-vienna-1912:008768_

- **1937.01** `state_reaction:pc20-second-chance/public-doubt` tags: pc20-second-chance, second_chance, reconciliation, return, failure, trust, debt, hope
  旧事被人在街上重新提起，Lena的名字和另一件事绑在一起。有人替Lena辩解，也有人只笑了一声；Lena回家后把当天的收据一张张摊开，像在给自己准备证词。
  _effects: state_parameter pc20-second-chance.suspicion 20 -> 28; state_parameter pc20-second-chance.credibility 67 -> 63; mood -0.020000000000000018_
  _causes: evt:world-vienna-1912:009843_

- **1937.01** `vienna-family-childbirth/difficult-birth` tags: family, childbirth, death, lifecycle, birth, state, state:raising-child, raising-child
  Lena难产，接生的人守了两天。孩子最后生下来，取名Luise Stein；Lena一直发烧，医院又收走二十克朗，Ferdinand Stein把钟表送进当铺。Lena落下产后虚弱的毛病，抱孩子时手臂发抖，站久了眼前就发黑。
  _effects: birth female; cash -20 (midwife and hospital（其中20克朗记入债务）); state_add raising-child state:world-vienna-1912:984_

- **1937.01** `curse15-betrayal.signature::ctx-vienna-authoritarian-1934::milieu-tenement/the-files-went-first` tags: betrayal, office, power, politics, curse, institution, vienna, urban
  Alois Bauer先把Lena抽屉里的往来信件搬空，再在会议上说Lena从来没有保存过记录。没有人能证明那些信存在过，除了Lena自己。散会后，有人让Lena回家休息几天，工资照发。Lena知道这不是照顾，是让Lena别再回来。
  _effects: reputation -0.06; relation rival with person-materialized-world-vienna-1912-80; knowledge “我经手的信件在会议前被人搬空了。”; condition; flag +records_removed; relation acquaintance with person-materialized-world-vienna-1912-80_

- **1937.01** `state_expire:failed-apprentice` tags: failed-apprentice, craft, work, failure, stigma, state, state_tick, state:failed-apprentice
  Lena的「学徒契约终止」到期；相关安排结束。
  _effects: state_remove failed-apprentice state:world-vienna-1912:376_

- **1937.01** `state_tick:raising-child/school-year/struggles` tags: raising-child, family, child, care, school, household, state, state_tick
  Lena被叫到学校，老师说Rosa Stein这学期总在课上看窗外。回家的路上，Lena没有骂，只把买好的铅笔又数了一遍；晚饭时，两个人都吃得很少。
  _effects: state_parameter raising-child.schoolProgress 15 -> 10; state_parameter raising-child.caregiverBurden 50 -> 55; cash -2 (extra lessons and replacement pencils); mood -0.030000000000000027_

- **1937.01** `state_tick:pc20-second-chance/kept-word/small-relapse` tags: pc20-second-chance, second_chance, reconciliation, return, failure, trust, debt, hope
  Lena晚了半个钟头，进门时先解释路上的电车，又解释口袋里的收据。对方没有追问，只把已经凉掉的汤端回厨房；Lena听见锅盖碰在灶台上，知道这次不是天气的问题。
  _effects: state_parameter pc20-second-chance.credibility 63 -> 56; state_parameter pc20-second-chance.suspicion 28 -> 36; state_parameter pc20-second-chance.debt 40 -> 43; mood -0.030000000000000027_

- **1937.01** `state_tick:ml18-sudden-fortune/fortune-year/money-slips-away` tags: ml18-sudden-fortune, money, windfall, sudden_fortune, household, luck, inheritance, lottery
  钱在一年里薄了一层：屋顶要修，姐夫来借，彩票站的老主顾说有个稳赚的买卖。Lena把账本翻到最后一页，发现写下的支出比记得的多出四克朗；窗外有人在敲铁皮，声音像在催账。
  _effects: state_parameter ml18-sudden-fortune.principal 65 -> 60; state_parameter ml18-sudden-fortune.spent 5 -> 10; cash -1 (the account does not balance（其中1克朗记入债务）); mood -0.020000000000000018_

- **1937.01** `state_tick:bc18-ridiculous-fame/ridiculous-fame-year/the-story-holds-another-year` tags: bc18-ridiculous-fame, fame, reputation, mistaken_identity, promotion, media, absurd, public
  又一年过去，Lena仍在被介绍成那个人物。邀请函从市郊的园艺会、女子学校和退伍军人酒馆寄来；Lena学会在签名后加一句模糊的谦辞，听起来像有许多往事不便细说。每一次应付都带来一点小钱，也让真正的解释更不合时宜。
  _effects: state_parameter bc18-ridiculous-fame.notoriety 28 -> 35; state_parameter bc18-ridiculous-fame.invitations 0 -> 1; cash 1 (一场小型演讲的车马费); reputation 0.010000000000000002; mood 0.020000000000000018_

- **1937.01** `kernel-family.care-sick-parent::ctx-vienna-authoritarian-1934::milieu-tenement::care-sick-parent-war/care-sick-parent.requisitioned` tags: task, task:family, family, care, health, parent, work, vienna
  医院要腾床位给伤员，Josefine Hofer被送回家，带回来的只有半瓶药和一张复诊条。Lena把厨房的长凳拼成床，Katharina Bauer教他用温水擦身；夜里空袭灯罩只留一条缝，药瓶放在伸手够得到的地方。
  _effects: household; cash -4 (medicine and extra coal for the sickroom（其中3克朗记入债务）); role; flag +hospital_requisitioned; knowledge “医院只收伤员，复诊条上的日期已经作废。”_

- **1937.04** `ad20-neighbor-dispute::ctx-vienna-authoritarian-1934::milieu-tenement/the-line-moves` tags: neighbor, dispute, home, everyday, public, obligation, vienna, urban
  隔壁把晾衣绳往Lena这边挪了一尺，水滴正好落在窗台上。Lena拿粉笔在墙上画了一道线；Franz Hofer看见了，又把绳子挪回去半尺。两个人都觉得自己让了步，晚饭时却都把窗户关得很紧。
  _effects: relation acquaintance with person-materialized-world-vienna-1912-43; mood -0.020000000000000018; flag +laundry_line_dispute; relation acquaintance with person-materialized-world-vienna-1912-43_

- **1938.01** `vienna-life-anschluss-1938/resists` tags: politics, persecution, 1938
  Lena在登记桌旁说了句“这里以前不是这样”。Elisabeth Hofer记下地址，当晚两个穿制服的人来敲门，把Lena带到警局，第二天放回来时脸上有伤。
  _effects: condition; role; cash -5 (fine and lost work)_

- **1938.01** `vienna-family-childbirth/difficult-birth` tags: family, childbirth, death, lifecycle, birth, state, state:raising-child, raising-child
  Lena难产，接生的人守了两天。孩子最后生下来，取名Anna Stein；Lena一直发烧，医院又收走二十克朗，Ferdinand Stein把钟表送进当铺。Lena落下产后虚弱的毛病，抱孩子时手臂发抖，站久了眼前就发黑。
  _effects: birth female; cash -20 (midwife and hospital); state_add raising-child state:world-vienna-1912:1069_

- **1938.01** `p22-child-matchmaker::ctx-vienna-authoritarian-1934::milieu-tenement/the-enemy-brings-a-cake` tags: child, matchmaker, family_album, enemy, warm, ironic, reconciliation, scandal
  孩子把一张陌生人的照片贴进家庭相册，说这是“爸爸以前最好的朋友”。Lena认出那是十年前打过官司的Marie Huber，正要发火，孩子却说照片背面写着一行字：“等我回来吃饭。”第二天，Marie Huber真的提着一只蛋糕站在门口。孩子把最大的一块留给Lena，Marie Huber没有反对。
  _effects: relation friend with person-marie-huber; relation kin with person:evt:world-vienna-1912:002058:child; cash 1 (the enemy brought a cake and a small gift); flag +enemy_brought_cake; mood 0.050000000000000044_

- **1938.01** `state_reaction:pc20-second-chance/someone-vouches` tags: pc20-second-chance, second_chance, reconciliation, return, failure, trust, debt, hope
  一个不算亲近的人替Lena说了句话：不是保证Lena已经变好，只说那天Lena确实在场、确实没有躲。话传回来时，Lena正在补一只旧口袋，针脚第一次没有歪。
  _effects: state_parameter pc20-second-chance.credibility 56 -> 63; state_parameter pc20-second-chance.hope 73 -> 78; mood 0.020000000000000018_

- **1938.01** `r13-fam.foster-child::ctx-vienna-authoritarian-1934::milieu-tenement/child-taken-in` tags: task, care, foster, child, household, orphan, vienna, urban
  Luise Winter把Berta Bauer领到门口，说孩子的母亲去了外地做工，几个月没寄钱回来。Lena看见孩子脚上那双鞋小了一指，脚趾顶得发白，便让他们进屋。当晚，Lena把自家孩子的旧鞋找出来，又往锅里多加了一勺面糊；Berta Bauer坐在灶边，一直没有开口。
  _effects: cash -3 (门口多出来的一双小鞋); flag +fostered_a_child; role; relation guardian with person:evt:world-vienna-1912:006026:child; knowledge “the child’s mother left with an evacuation train”; household; relation acquaintance with person-materialized-world-vienna-1912-26_

- **1938.04** `vienna-parish-aid.funeral-society/funeral-society.pay` tags: faith, parish, mutual_aid, poverty, family
  Lena数出八赫勒交给Berta Winter，看她在蓝皮本上找到自家门牌，把这一周的格子划掉。Berta Winter说上个月隔壁巷子的人走了，公账出了棺材和一场小弥撒，剩下的钱记在册尾。楼梯口的灯很暗，Lena盯着自己的名字看了两遍才转身。
  _effects: cash -0.4 (一周丧葬互助会会费); relation acquaintance with person-materialized-world-vienna-1912-30; flag +funeral_society_member_

  任期在十二月三十一日结束。Lena把印章擦干净，钥匙串上只剩自己家的那把；Berta Winter来接手续，两个人把最后一份移交清单对了两遍。走出大门时，门房照旧叫了一声“先生”，没有加头衔。
  _effects: position_end 师傅 @workshop:person-phase15-Lena-world-vienna-1912-evt:world-vienna-1912:007465; reputation 0.020000000000000004; mood -0.040000000000000036; condition; flag +left_office_in_order; knowledge “移交清单上最后一项是印章，钥匙串上只剩自己家的那把。”; relation acquaintance with person-materialized-world-vienna-1912-30_

- **1939.01** `vienna-family-childbirth/son-born` tags: family, childbirth, death, lifecycle, birth, injury, state, state:raising-child
  Lena在Leopoldstädter Zinshaus, gemeinsamer Gang生下一个男婴，取名Otto Stein。工友凑的钱抵了一部分，家里还是从糖罐里数出六克朗给接生的人；Ferdinand Stein把消息带到工厂，回来时把唯一的摇篮重新刷了一遍，Lena躺在床上出了一身虚汗。
  _effects: birth male; health -0.08 (childbirth); cash -6 (midwife fee); state_add raising-child state:world-vienna-1912:1156_

  _effects: relation kin with person-materialized-world-vienna-1912-60; cash -1 (路上给车夫买烟叶的道谢钱（其中1克朗记入债务）); relation acquaintance with person-materialized-world-vienna-1912-60; relation acquaintance with person-materialized-world-vienna-1912-95_

  _effects: relation acquaintance with person-katharina-bauer; health 0.03 (夜里终于能早些入睡)_

- **1947.09** `p9-gap-adult-solitude.leaking-shoe/leaking-shoe.stitched` tags: adulthood, daily, solitude, ordinary, home, repair, shoe, poverty
  Lena用鞋蜡把裂缝填上，又拿粗针和麻线把鞋底缝了一圈。针脚歪，鞋底一高一低，走路时右脚落地总比左脚重。第二天上工回来，脚后跟磨出一个水泡，可鞋没再渗水。
  _effects: skill_

- **1948.05** `vienna-courtship-courtyard.sunday-walk/part-at-arch` tags: adulthood, courtship, partner, social, family
  走到桥洞前，Berta Bauer说该往回走了，家里炉子还没生。Lena站在原地看着Berta Bauer拐上台阶，围巾的一角被风掀起来；下周的事谁也没有提，两人就此分开，口袋里只剩一颗凉透的栗子。
  _effects: mood -0.029999999999999916; relation acquaintance with person:evt:world-vienna-1912:005487:child_

- **1948.07** `p9-social-courtship-friendship.opened-letter/opened-letter-church-steps` tags: courtship, friendship, social, adulthood, reputation, leopoldstadt
  Lena请同住的Josef Lechner跑一趟，把Katharina Huber约到教堂前的台阶上。石台阶还带着晨露，两人各占一级，把原信从头念完；念到“工钱”那处，Katharina Huber说那是家里人的意思，不是自己的话。Lena把信纸折回原样，至少这一回没有隔着别人传话。
  _effects: relation acquaintance with person:evt:world-vienna-1912:005413:child_

- **1948.08** `vienna-legacy-worklife.sick-fund/sick-fund.keep-quiet` tags: legacy, work, family, reform, ordinary_life, injury
  止咳药水是褐色的，瓶口塞着软木塞，摇一摇会起沫。Lena看着工友把表折成小块，折了三折才塞进帽檐。墙上的规章还贴在那里，纸角被风掀起，没人去按。
  _effects: mood -0.050000000000000044; health -0.040000000000000036 (没有正规病假，只能靠药水撑着); relation colleague with person-anna-bauer_

- **1948.10** `vienna-market-household.market-credit/market-credit.pay-later` tags: home, market, food, social
  Lena捏着三个克朗硬币站在玛丽亚的菜摊前，土豆和洋葱已经装进布袋。周五结清时，玛丽亚用粉笔在小黑板上划掉名字，Alois Stein点点头。
  _effects: cash -3 (偿还市场赊账（其中3克朗记入债务）); food_

- **1949.01** `p9-work-jobsearch.leave-bad-employer/leave-bad-employer.quit-unpaid` tags: work, job_search, negotiation, wages, employment, task, bad_employer, resignation
  钳子砸在铁案上，火星子溅到靴面。Lena解下围裙说今天不干了。屋顶无线电修理铺从抽屉里数出几张票子推过来，说剩下的月底。Anna Bauer追到巷口，从内衣袋里摸出三克朗塞给他。
  _effects: unemployment (当场辞掉欠薪的作坊); cash 3 (向工友借来的应急钱); relation friend with person-anna-bauer_

- **1949.01** `p9-social-family-conflict-care.sick-parent-night/hospital-deposit` tags: family, care, siblings, inheritance, housing, money
  Lena跑了一趟医院，把Josefine Hofer送进病房：押金三克朗，搪瓷杯和旧毛巾一克朗二十赫勒，头三天的药费三克朗八十赫勒，一共八克朗。护士收走便盆，换上带刻度的玻璃壶。Lena回到空了的床边，把塌下去的椅子扶正，才发现自己一整天只喝了一杯咖啡。
  _effects: cash -8 (住院押金和住院用品（其中8克朗记入债务）); flag +parent_hospitalized_

- **1949.04** `p9-social-courtship-friendship.friend-intercession/intercession-concert-ticket` tags: courtship, friendship, social, adulthood, reputation, leopoldstadt
  Lena不再让Emil Lechner传话，托其带去一张星期天音乐会的站票，花了一克朗二十赫勒，位置在柱子后头。票上不写多余的字，只说若不方便，票留着也无妨。Emil Lechner把票揣进衬衫内袋，Lena则把找回来的铜板一枚枚放进铁盒，等一个回音。
  _effects: cash -1.2 (音乐会站票一张（其中1.2克朗记入债务）); relation acquaintance with person-materialized-world-vienna-1912-151; relation acquaintance with person-materialized-world-vienna-1912-151_

- **1949.05** `p9-gap-adult-ordinary-day.market-closing/market-closing.share-spinach` tags: adulthood, daily, leisure, neighborhood, ordinary, market, food, bargain
  两人同时伸手，又同时缩回去。Otto Hofer说自己本来想买回去做馅，Lena说那正好一人一半。摊主用草绳把菜捆好，嘴里嘟囔着收摊了收摊了，把剩下的零钱扒拉进木匣。
  _effects: cash -0.04 (分买最后一把菠菜（其中0.04克朗记入债务）); food_

- **1949.11** `statistical_infection`
  Lena在Leopoldstädter Zinshaus, gemeinsamer Gang病倒了，肺炎让身体发热、虚弱，接下来的日子只能卧床。

- **1949.12** `statistical_illness_worsened`
  Lena的肺炎没有完全好转，咳嗽和发热又拖了一个月，家里人轮流守着。

- **1950.01** `vienna-life-injury-recovery/home-care` tags: health, work, family
  Lena没有去诊所，只用热水和旧布洗了伤口。第二天伤口周围发红，家里人把仅剩的干净床单撕成条。

  _effects: mood -0.050000000000000044; reputation -0.009999999999999995_

  _effects: flag +has_work_lead; relation acquaintance with person-materialized-world-vienna-1912-41; relation acquaintance with person-materialized-world-vienna-1912-41_

  Lena只把外套的里衬拆了一半，就搁在椅背上。冬天天短，她每天回来都看见那件敞着口子的外套，想着明天接着弄，明天又总是有别的事。到开春外套还搭在那儿，呢面上落了一层灰。
  _effects: flag +unfinished_coat_

  Luise Kraus听Lena把房租和药费的数目报完，从抽屉里数出二十克朗，推到桌上，同时拿出一张纸，要写还款日期。他说每月十五号来一趟，把账报一遍。Lena签字时手有点抖，把纸角压得很平，说好先从月底还三克朗。
  _effects: cash 20 (舅舅借出的钱); relation kin with person:evt:world-vienna-1912:000182:child; flag +owes_uncle_

  午休的哨子刚响，Emil夹着烟走到门口。Lena报上姓名、住址和干过的活，声音有点发紧。工头嗯了一声，说明早六点来，管一顿午饭，干得好再谈工钱。

  围过来的工友有七八个，有人把耳朵凑到Lena嘴边，怕听漏一个数字。念到“星期六下午停工”时，人群里有人笑了一声，又马上收住。墙上的表被风掀起一角，Lena用拇指按住，继续往下念。
  _effects: skill; relation colleague with person-materialized-world-vienna-1912-69; reputation 0.04000000000000001_

  Lena拿着信上了四层楼，敲了两户都不是，门牌是七号还是十七号已看不清。第三户的老太太说那人去年就搬去法沃里滕了。她把信交回门房，门房在登记簿上写下一个赫勒的墨水钱。
  _effects: mood 0.030000000000000027; reputation 0.01999999999999999_

  椅子叠到一半，Grete Huber 4压低声音说今晚有三个人没投票就走了。Lena把最后一块瓜子壳扫进簸箕，说记住那三张脸，比上台多说一句有用。灯熄时大厅只剩木头发出的轻响。
  _effects: skill; relation acquaintance with person-materialized-world-vienna-1912-175; mood -0.020000000000000018_

  那孩子一边跑一边吹口哨，狗就跟着口哨声跑。Lena追到街角时，罗盘钟正敲整点。她扶着墙喘，低头看见鞋底磨穿的洞，算着补鞋匠那里补一只要收六十赫勒。
  _effects: cash -0.6 (补鞋底的开销)_

  开口时只提了手推车，钱的事咽回去。对方爽快地把车钥匙样的铁钩递过来，说用完了搁回院里就行。十克朗改去当铺想办法，一件旧外套和一只怀表抵了十二克朗，赎期三个月，票据折好收在证件夹里。
  _effects: cash 12 (当铺抵当旧外套和怀表换来的钱); relation friend with person-materialized-world-vienna-1912-175; mood -0.020000000000000018_

### 161 subject events

- **1912.02** `p22-warm-fathers-letter::ctx-vienna-1912::milieu-tenement/the-recipe-arrives` tags: infancy, family, letter, uncle, warm, melancholy, memory, drama
  信是叔叔死前写的，在邮袋里压了两年才到。Friedrich Hofer拆开时先笑出声：里面没有遗言，只有一张李子饺子的做法和一个关于祖母的笨笑话。那天晚上，全家真的吃了饺子。
  _effects: state_add p22-family-legend state:world-vienna-1912:10; mood 0.04; knowledge “家里有一封叔叔留下的信，里面写着李子饺子的做法。”_

- **1912.02** `state_reaction:p22-family-legend/someone-asks-again` tags: p22-family-legend, family_legend, family, story, secret, inheritance, promise, memory
  又有人问起Anton家的那件旧事。大人先笑了一下，才说“都是很早以前了”；问的人点点头，眼睛却往摇篮那边看，手指在杯沿上停了一会儿，谁也没有接话，窗外有风。
  _effects: state_parameter p22-family-legend.secrecy 20 -> 24; state_parameter p22-family-legend.warmth 70 -> 72; mood 0.01_

- **1912.08** `p22-comic-christening::ctx-vienna-1912::milieu-tenement/the-priest-sneezes` tags: infancy, family, christening, church, neighbor, comic, tender, drama
  神父念到孩子的名字时打了个大喷嚏，蜡烛灭了，Anton却笑出了声。Emil Weiss赶紧去点蜡烛，手一抖，火苗又灭了；教堂里第一次有人笑，神父也笑了，说这是今天最响的一声阿门。
  _effects: mood 0.04; relation acquaintance with person-phase15-Anton-world-vienna-1912; flag +christening_sneeze; state_add p22-family-legend state:world-vienna-1912:11_

- **1912.08** `state_reaction:p22-family-legend/someone-asks-again` tags: p22-family-legend, family_legend, family, story, secret, inheritance, promise, memory
  又有人问起Anton家的那件旧事。大人先笑了一下，才说“都是很早以前了”；问的人点点头，眼睛却往摇篮那边看，手指在杯沿上停了一会儿，谁也没有接话，窗外有风。

- **1913.01** `vienna-life-infant-first-smile/neighbour-warning` tags: infancy, family, first, warm
  隔壁的女人探头进来，说孩子笑得太早，老人讲这不是好兆头。Josefine Hofer没有接话，只把Anton往怀里拢了拢；孩子却伸手去抓那女人围裙上的扣子，抓得不肯松。
  _effects: mood -0.009999999999999995; flag +early_smile_omen_

- **1913.01** `state_tick:p22-family-legend/the-story-changes-hands/a-detail-goes-missing` tags: p22-family-legend, family_legend, family, story, secret, inheritance, promise, memory
  又过了一年，故事里最要紧的那件东西不见了：先是名字，后来是地点。Anton问起时，大人说“你记错了”；可柜子最底层那只空盒子，谁也没有扔，锁却换过了，钥匙也不见了。
  _effects: state_parameter p22-family-legend.secrecy 14 -> 18; state_parameter p22-family-legend.danger 5 -> 8; state_parameter p22-family-legend.warmth 72 -> 70; mood -0.01_

- **1913.02** `vienna-life-toddler-wrong-cradle/the-blanket-is-wrong` tags: infancy, family, rumour, identity, comic, drama
  Josefine Hofer盯着Anton的摇篮看了很久，说这床蓝被子不是这家人的。Friedrich Hofer把被子翻过来，角落绣着一个陌生的姓；Anton却抓着被角不放，像是认得它。Josefine Hofer退后一步，说“我什么都没说”。
  _effects: flag +wrong_cradle_blanket; knowledge “摇篮里的蓝被子绣着一个陌生的姓。”; mood -0.030000000000000006_

- **1913.03** `vienna-life-toddler-first-words/first-word-door` tags: infancy, family, first, comic
  门外有人上楼，Anton忽然指着门说：“爸爸。”Josefine Hofer看了看空门口，没有纠正。那天晚上，孩子把家里每个人的名字都叫了一遍，叫错两个，却叫得很认真。
  _effects: skill; mood 0.01999999999999999; flag +first_word_door_

- **1913.07** `iw18-infancy.first-word::ctx-vienna-1912::milieu-tenement/a-word-for-bread` tags: infancy, first_word, memory, family, wonder, eerie, vienna, urban
  Anton指着桌上的面包说出一个短词，声音轻得像在试音。Josefine Hofer把面包掰下一角，泡进热水里喂给孩子；Anton吃完又指了一次，这次说得更清楚。厨房里的人都笑了，连灶上的水汽也像在往上冒。
  _effects: food; mood 0.03; flag +first_word_bread_

- **1914.02** `vienna-life-toddler-accident/scalded-hand` tags: infancy, accident, family, danger, injury
  厨房的锅盖没有盖严，Anton伸手去够桌上的勺子，蒸汽先扑到手腕上。Josefine Hofer把孩子抱到水缸边，一瓢一瓢地浇冷水；孩子哭得打嗝，锅里的汤还在冒泡。
  _effects: health -0.07999999999999996 (scalded hand); condition; cash -1 (bandages and ointment); mood -0.04999999999999999_

- **1914.02** `state_reaction:p22-family-legend/someone-asks-again` tags: p22-family-legend, family_legend, family, story, secret, inheritance, promise, memory

- **1915.01** `statistical_infection`
  Anton在Leopoldstädter Zinshaus, gemeinsamer Gang病倒了，肺炎让身体发热、虚弱，接下来的日子只能卧床。

- **1915.01** `vienna-life-child-family-legend/lost-relative` tags: childhood, family, secret, story
  Josefine Hofer在收拾箱子时翻出一张没有署名的照片，说那是家里一个“去了很远地方”的人。Anton问还回不回来，Josefine Hofer把照片翻过去，说先把名字记住，别在饭桌上问。
  _effects: knowledge “家里有一张没有署名的照片，照片上的人去了很远的地方。”; flag +family_legend_photo; mood -0.01_

- **1915.03** `statistical_illness_worsened`
  Anton的肺炎没有完全好转，咳嗽和发热又拖了一个月，家里人轮流守着。

- **1915.06** `statistical_illness_worsened`
  Anton的肺炎没有完全好转，咳嗽和发热又拖了一个月，家里人轮流守着。

- **1915.07** `statistical_recovery`
  Anton熬过了这场肺炎，能坐起来喝一点汤，但身体还很虚。

- **1916.01** `vienna-life-child-first-friend/shared-toy` tags: childhood, friendship, family, comic
  Lena在楼道里滚一只铁环，Anton站在门口看。Lena把铁环推过来，说“你推一次，我推一次”。两个人从楼梯口推到院子里，铁环倒下的声音把午睡的大人吵醒了一排。
  _effects: relation friend with person-phase15-Lena-world-vienna-1912; skill; mood 0.05_

- **1916.01** `state_reaction:p22-family-legend/someone-asks-again` tags: p22-family-legend, family_legend, family, story, secret, inheritance, promise, memory
  又有人问起Anton家的那件旧事。大人先笑了一下，才说“都是很早以前了”；问的人点点头，眼睛却往摇篮那边看，手指在杯沿上停了一会儿，谁也没有接话，窗外有风。

- **1916.01** `state_tick:p22-family-legend/the-story-changes-hands/told-at-the-table` tags: p22-family-legend, family_legend, family, story, secret, inheritance, promise, memory
  又过了一年，Anton家的故事在饭桌上换了新的讲法：多了一句笑话，少了一处哭声。听的人把汤匙放下，等下一句；讲故事的人却先去看窗子有没有关好，谁也没有先动筷子。
  _effects: state_parameter p22-family-legend.warmth 74 -> 77; state_parameter p22-family-legend.secrecy 26 -> 24; state_parameter p22-family-legend.promise 40 -> 42; mood 0.009999999999999995_

- **1917.01** `vienna-life-child-inherited-wish/the-caregiver-cries` tags: childhood, family, wish, grief, uncanny, drama
  Friedrich Hofer没有回答，起身去洗碗，水开得很大。Anton跟到厨房，拽了拽衣角，说“你别哭，我不说了”。Friedrich Hofer蹲下来，把孩子抱得很紧，说“你说，你继续说”。
  _effects: mood -0.04999999999999999; flag +inherited_wish_grief_

- **1918.01** `state_expire:p22-family-legend` tags: p22-family-legend, family_legend, family, story, secret, inheritance, promise, memory
  Anton的「家族传说」到期；相关安排结束。
  _effects: state_remove p22-family-legend state:world-vienna-1912:11_

- **1918.01** `p9-gap-childhood-chores.laundry-help/laundry-bread-with-friend` tags: childhood, household, chores, school, task, daily, laundry, work
  Hedwig Stein家的孩子站在绳子另一头，Anton递夹子时故意慢半拍，两个人把床单拉得没有一道褶。收工后Hedwig Stein端出半包隔夜面包，掰开时掉了一桌屑。Anton把分到的那块用围裙兜住，回家路上闻着麦味，硬是没咬。
  _effects: food; relation acquaintance with person-hedwig-stein; skill_

- **1920.01** `p9-gap-childhood-chores.laundry-help/laundry-dragged-docked-pay` tags: childhood, household, chores, school, task, daily, laundry, work
  “你看看这角。”Katharina Bauer把床单拎起来，煤渣簌簌往下掉。Anton伸手去拍，反而把灰按进布纹里。Katharina Bauer从工钱里数回两枚，只留一赫勒在Anton手心，让重洗那角。晚饭前Anton一直蹲在井台边，换了两盆水，指甲缝里全是黑泥。
  _effects: cash 0.01 (被扣后只剩一赫勒); mood -0.05; relation acquaintance with person-katharina-bauer; relation acquaintance with person-materialized-world-vienna-1912-20_

- **1921.04** `vienna-childhood-friends.courtyard-race/fell-on-wet-stones` tags: childhood, social, school, street, injury
  Anton想从床单底下钻过去，脚下一滑，膝盖磕在石板棱上。Emil Weiss听见哭声出来，先看了看伤口，又回去端水。手帕扎得很紧，Anton试着站起来，被Emil Weiss按回台阶上。
  _effects: health -0.040000000000000036 (膝盖在湿石板上磕破); relation acquaintance with person-emil-weiss_

- **1922.01** `p9-gap-childhood-chores.laundry-help/laundry-bread-with-friend` tags: childhood, household, chores, school, task, daily, laundry, work
  两个孩子在院子两头跑，一个递，一个夹，床单在风里鼓起来。最后一条挂好，Maria Bauer从厨房拿出面包，先给了自家孩子，再掰一块给Anton。Anton把面包塞进围裙口袋，回家后放在家里人的碗边，说自己在外面吃过了。
  _effects: food; relation acquaintance with person-materialized-world-vienna-1912-10; skill; relation acquaintance with person-materialized-world-vienna-1912-10; relation acquaintance with person-materialized-world-vienna-1912-32_

- **1922.02** `vienna-childhood-street.cigarette-card/card-trade` tags: childhood, street, social, home
  烟草店门口的石阶上摆着一排烟画。Anton把三张皱巴巴的卡摊开，Friedrich Berg挑了挑，从口袋里摸出一张皇帝像。交换完，Friedrich Berg转身进了杂货铺，把皇帝像往柜台上一拍，换了一块糖。Anton站在门外，手里还留着卡片的油墨味。
  _effects: mood 0.04000000000000001; relation friend with person:evt:world-vienna-1912:000206:child_

- **1924.09** `vienna-adolescence.workshop-apprentice/apprenticeship-accepted` tags: adolescence, school, work, family, social
  Buchdruckerei Winter & Söhne先看Anton的手，又让Anton推两下刨子试试。Anton用力过猛，刨刀啃进木头，Buchdruckerei Winter & Söhne皱眉，让再来一遍。第二遍木花薄而匀。Buchdruckerei Winter & Söhne说“留下”，当面点了三克朗给Josefine Hofer，算头一周饭钱，又指了指墙上的钟，说迟到一刻钟扣半天。
  _effects: cash 3 ({subject}学徒首周的膳食预付款); education apprenticeship; reputation 0.1; relation kin with person-josefine-hofer_

- **1925.01** `p9-childhood-apprenticeship.cover-for-sibling/forewoman-catches` tags: adolescence, work, apprenticeship, labour, training, family, task, task:family
  Luise Winter把Anton叫到巷口，问Lena叫什么、住在哪间屋。Anton答到一半就停住了。Luise Winter说这半天的活不算数，说冒名顶替坏了规矩。Anton回到Leopoldstädter Zinshaus, gemeinsamer Gang时，Lena正扶着床沿站着，听完只说“别去了”，说完又低下头，像是自己连累了Anton。
  _effects: mood -0.07; relation kin with person-phase15-Lena-world-vienna-1912; health -0.020000000000000018 (白跑一趟，路上没吃东西); relation acquaintance with person-materialized-world-vienna-1912-26_

- **1925.02** `r14-edu.leaving-school::ctx-vienna-reform-1930::milieu-tenement/kept-home-for-kin` tags: education, school_dropout, child_labour, poverty, reform, medical, schooling, home
  Josefine Hofer病倒在床，Anton留在家里看弟妹、煮稀粥、去井边打水。Ferdinand Hofer托人带来一包旧课本，Anton在灶火边翻了两页，弟弟哭了，只好合上。一个月后，学校把Anton的名字从名册上划掉，墨迹很淡。
  _effects: mood -0.06; flag +left_school_for_family; role; relation acquaintance with person-materialized-world-vienna-1912-34_

- **1925.08** `yf18-firstlook.tram-glove::ctx-vienna-reform-1930::milieu-tenement/keep-it-in-the-satchel` tags: first_love, love, tram, glove, youth, courtship, reform, medical
  Anton把手套塞进书包最里层，回家后放在枕头下面。第二天上学，Anton每隔一会儿就摸一下书包带，像在确认一件不能给家里看见的东西；手套上还留着电车木座的气味。
  _effects: state_add yf18-first-awakening state:world-vienna-1912:175; flag +yf18_kept_glove; mood 0.020000000000000004; flag +yf18_first_awakening_active_

- **1926.01** `state_tick:yf18-first-awakening/half-year-of-glances/answered-glance` tags: yf18-first-awakening, love, youth, first_love, desire, school, awakening, state
  Anton在教堂门口或学校走廊里又遇见Mitzi。这一次对方先点了头，Anton准备好的整段话全忘了，只把手里的书换到另一只手上；回家后Anton才发现，自己把书拿倒了。
  _effects: state_parameter yf18-first-awakening.intensity 60 -> 65; state_parameter yf18-first-awakening.nerve 5 -> 13; state_parameter yf18-first-awakening.reciprocated false -> true; mood 0.03_

  Maria Bauer看了看Anton抄的字母表，说大小不一，让他回去用直尺画格子重抄一遍，下个月再来。少年把纸折好，在咖啡馆门口站了一会儿，走回印刷厂时绕到排字房窗外看了很久。
  _effects: mood -0.03; skill; flag +mentor_pending_retry; relation acquaintance with person-materialized-world-vienna-1912-10; relation acquaintance with person-materialized-world-vienna-1912-45_

- **1926.09** `kernel-edu.friendship::ctx-vienna-reform-1930::milieu-tenement/edu-friend-forbidden` tags: adolescence, school, friendship, books, family, reform, medical, schooling
  Josefine Hofer说Emil家里没有正经职业，不许再来往。Anton嘴上答应，放学还是绕路去Emil家。被发现后，Josefine Hofer把借书证撕了。Emil在门口等了两天，第三天没有再来。Anton把撕碎的借书证捡起来，夹进书里。
  _effects: relation kin with person-josefine-hofer; flag +forbidden_friendship; mood -0.07_

  Anton的手已经搭上栏杆，听见桥下有驳船拉笛，忽然把手收回来。Josef骂了一句，Elisabeth Bauer却把围巾解下来递给Anton；三个人在桥头站了一会儿，谁也没有再提这件事。
  _effects: state_parameter yf18-first-awakening.intensity 71 -> 74; state_parameter yf18-first-awakening.nerve 11 -> 6; mood -0.010000000000000009_

  Anton去了一个下午，看那些旧歌本里夹着褪色的圣像卡，工厂来的几个年轻人在最后一排用手指敲长凳打拍子。第二个星期天Anton没再去，在院子里帮母亲晾被单。
  _effects: relation acquaintance with person-elisabeth-bauer; mood 0.010000000000000009; skill_

- **1928.01** `yf18-firstlook.essay-exam::ctx-vienna-reform-1930::milieu-tenement/read-aloud-as-a-warning` tags: first_love, exam, essay, school, humiliation, reform, medical, schooling
  Ferdinand Hofer在班上念了Anton的作文，念到“她的手套放在木座上”时停了一下，说这就是考试里最危险的东西：把私事当成论据。全班笑了，Anton盯着桌面上的墨水圈，听见Klara在最后一排合上了书。
  _effects: state_parameter yf18-first-awakening.talk 0 -> 20; state_parameter yf18-first-awakening.intensity 70 -> 74; state_parameter yf18-first-awakening.nerve 21 -> 11; reputation -0.04; relation friend with person-phase15-Klara-world-vienna-1912; mood -0.04999999999999999_

  考试周里，Anton把课本摊在桌上，眼睛却一直看着窗外那条电车线。笔记的页边多了几个没有写完的名字，Anton用橡皮擦掉，纸面起了毛。
  _effects: state_parameter yf18-first-awakening.intensity 74 -> 71; state_parameter yf18-first-awakening.talk 20 -> 22_

- **1928.01** `state_tick:yf18-first-awakening/half-year-of-glances/rehearsed-conversation` tags: yf18-first-awakening, love, youth, first_love, desire, school, awakening, state
  Anton在厨房里对着镜子练了七遍开场白，第二天却在离Mitzi三步远的地方拐进面包店。Anton买了一袋并不需要的盐，回家路上把开场白又想了一遍；这一次，连标点都更熟练了。
  _effects: state_parameter yf18-first-awakening.intensity 74 -> 80; state_parameter yf18-first-awakening.talk 22 -> 25; mood 0.010000000000000009_

- **1928.01** `agency:seek_work` tags: agency, agency:seek_work
  Anton去了Buchdruckerei Winter & Söhne问工，得到的回答都是“下个月再来”。
  _effects: mood -0.04000000000000001; reputation -0.010000000000000002_

- **1928.04** `yf18-dare.prater-slide::ctx-vienna-reform-1930::milieu-tenement/ride-in-the-dark` tags: dare, prater, fairground, comic, friendship, reform, medical, schooling
  普拉特的木滑道在闭园后还亮着一盏值班灯。Anton和Lena从围栏底下钻进去，用一块麻袋垫着滑下来，木槽里的蜡味呛得人直笑。滑到底时，Berta Winter 2提着灯站在出口，问他们要不要再买一张票。
  _effects: mood 0.05; cash -1 (a late ticket paid to the watchman); reputation -0.010000000000000002; relation friend with person-phase15-Lena-world-vienna-1912; flag +yf18_prater_stunt; relation acquaintance with person-materialized-world-vienna-1912-56_

  Anton把写给Klara的信折成四折，夹在拉丁文课本第三百页。第二天，书还回来时，页边多了一行铅笔字：“星期四，图书馆关门前。”两个人在书架之间站得很近，谁都没有先提那封信。
  _effects: state_add ld18-impossible-attachment state:world-vienna-1912:355; relation friend with person-phase15-Klara-world-vienna-1912; mood 0.04999999999999999; flag +letter_in_the_schoolbook; flag +impossible_attachment_active_

  舞卡上第一支曲子被Anna Berg写走了。Anton在教会礼堂的木地板上踩错了两步，Anna Berg说没关系，把手放回Anton背后；第三支曲子响起时，Josef在门口冲Anton比了个手势，Anton假装没看见。
  _effects: state_parameter yf18-first-awakening.intensity 80 -> 88; state_parameter yf18-first-awakening.nerve 6 -> 16; state_parameter yf18-first-awakening.reciprocated true -> true; mood 0.04000000000000001_

  Anton在教堂门口或学校走廊里又遇见Mitzi。这一次对方先点了头，Anton准备好的整段话全忘了，只把手里的书换到另一只手上；回家后Anton才发现，自己把书拿倒了。
  _effects: state_parameter yf18-first-awakening.intensity 88 -> 93; state_parameter yf18-first-awakening.nerve 16 -> 24; state_parameter yf18-first-awakening.reciprocated true -> true; mood 0.03_

  户籍窗口换了一张新表格，配偶一栏不能空着。Anton最后写了“同住人”，办事员抬头看了一眼，说这不作数。回家后，Anton把表格折成很小的一块，塞进针线盒；伴侣问今天顺不顺利，Anton说和平时一样。

  _effects: cash -4 (借来的琴弓); flag +took_violin_lessons; skill; relation colleague with person-materialized-world-vienna-1912-33; knowledge “the teacher marks the three notes to practise each week”; relation acquaintance with person-materialized-world-vienna-1912-33_

  Anton在Leopoldstädter Zinshaus, gemeinsamer Gang靠窗的桌边，把一只空咖啡杯转了半圈，说房租两人合付、桌子两人共用。Elisabeth Huber的手停在糖罐盖上，问了一句“要是过不下去呢”，没有当场答应，只说要想一想；杯里的咖啡凉了，话还悬在桌面上。
  _effects: interaction; relation acquaintance with person-materialized-world-vienna-1912-14_

  办公室有人问Anton，为什么总有一个女人在楼下等她。Anton说那是表妹；对方笑了一下，说现在的表妹真体贴。下午，Anton把桌上的相框扣倒，又在没人时翻回来，用袖子擦了擦玻璃上的灰。
  _effects: state_parameter ld18-impossible-attachment.pressure 21 -> 29; state_parameter ld18-impossible-attachment.discretion 52 -> 48; reputation -0.02_

  Anna Hofer回话说，对方家里已经应了另一门亲，只是还没下聘。Anton把准备好的小礼物——一块香皂和一双线手套——留在柜台上，没有托人送去；第二天上工时，Luise Lechner从对面走过，两人都装作在看橱窗。

- **1930.02** `vienna-social-movement.recruitment/join-and-carry` tags: development, social_movement, organization, politics
  Anton在楼梯下面接到一张折成四折的传单。纸上没有署名，只写着下一次集会的地址。Anton去了，在昏暗的屋里报上姓名，成为Die Fackel的一员，并答应负责自己所在街区的联络。
  _effects: organization_membership; role; relation acquaintance with person-materialized-world-vienna-1912-14_

- **1930.02** `vienna-courtship-courtyard.sunday-walk/separate-coffee` tags: adulthood, courtship, partner, social, family
  两人拐进街角的咖啡馆，站到锌皮柜台前。Otto Hofer点了牛奶咖啡，Anton要了黑咖啡，十二赫勒一杯。侍者把两杯推过来时，两人几乎同时把钱币按在柜台上，各付各的，谁也没说什么；那一声铜板响，把客气摆得比咖啡还清楚。
  _effects: cash -0.12 (自己那杯黑咖啡); mood -0.03_

- **1930.04** `p9-gap-adult-solitude.window-argument/window-argument.peek` tags: adulthood, daily, solitude, ordinary, home, neighbor, overheard
  Anton下楼倒水，在楼梯口看见院子里站着一对夫妻，男人的围裙上还沾着面粉，女的攥着一张工钱条。她没插话，提着壶又上楼，关门时听见那女人说“一星期八克朗怎么够”。
  _effects: mood -0.03; skill_

- **1930.12** `vienna-legacy-worklife.sick-fund/sick-fund.keep-quiet` tags: legacy, work, family, reform, ordinary_life, injury
  止咳药水是褐色的，瓶口塞着软木塞，摇一摇会起沫。Anton看着工友把表折成小块，折了三折才塞进帽檐。墙上的规章还贴在那里，纸角被风掀起，没人去按。
  _effects: mood -0.05000000000000002; health -0.04 (没有正规病假，只能靠药水撑着); relation colleague with person-marie-huber; relation acquaintance with person-materialized-world-vienna-1912-62; relation acquaintance with person-materialized-world-vienna-1912-73_

- **1931.01** `interaction_response` tags: interaction, partnership, household
  Elisabeth Huber在几天后找到Anton，说可以试试。两人开始合付房租，也把各自欠的账摊在桌上。

- **1931.01** `major-work-first-employment/hired-regular` tags: work, employment, unemployment, major
  清晨六点，印刷厂门口已经站了十几个人。Buchdruckerei Winter & Söhne的工头让Anton把手摊开，看了看指节，又问能不能读一页纸。Anton读完，工头在名册上写了名字：明天六点，带工票和一双干净的手。第一周洗墨辊，月底工资三十克朗，柜上先预支四克朗。
  _effects: employment Druckerhelfer @ org-winter-print; role; cash 4 (first wage advance); relation colleague with person-materialized-world-vienna-1912-69; flag +first_regular_job; relation acquaintance with person-materialized-world-vienna-1912-69_

  孩子出生后取名Anton Bauer，Elisabeth Huber一直出血。Anton跑去医院，医生来晚了，只留下药和十八克朗的账单；Elisabeth Huber落下产后虚弱的毛病，Anton抱着孩子坐在床边，直到邻居把汤送来，才发现自己的鞋穿反了。
  _effects: birth male; cash -18 (doctor and medicine); condition; state_add raising-child state:world-vienna-1912:448_

  _effects: state_parameter ld18-impossible-attachment.devotion 45 -> 49; state_parameter ld18-impossible-attachment.pressure 35 -> 32; mood 0.01999999999999999_

  _effects: mood -0.16; condition; flag +parental_consent_refused; relation rival with person-josefine-hofer_

  Anton的肺结核没有完全好转，咳嗽和发热又拖了一个月，家里人轮流守着。

  Anton没有立刻答话，先去敲Hermann Kral的门。两人一起数落墙上的水渍和门框里灌进来的风，楼梯间的窗户修好了，自家墙皮还在剥落。Hermann Kral提议一起把话带给看门人：先修好漏水的墙角，涨租的事再谈。Anton把纸重新折好，请他把话带到门外。

- **1933.01** `state_tick:ld18-impossible-attachment/invented-anniversary/a-form-with-an-empty-line` tags: ld18-impossible-attachment, love, intimacy, queer, unregistered, commitment, secret, desire
  户籍窗口换了一张新表格，配偶一栏不能空着。Anton最后写了“同住人”，办事员抬头看了一眼，说这不作数。回家后，Anton把表格折成很小的一块，塞进针线盒；伴侣问今天顺不顺利，Anton说和平时一样。
  _effects: state_parameter ld18-impossible-attachment.pressure 47 -> 53; state_parameter ld18-impossible-attachment.discretion 45 -> 42; mood -0.020000000000000018_

- **1933.01** `r13-work.unemployment-benefit::ctx-vienna-reform-1930::milieu-tenement/benefit-approved` tags: work, unemployment, benefit, institution, poverty, reform, medical, schooling
  Helene Kral 2把Anton的工龄册翻到最后一页，又对照了厂里寄来的解雇单，说可以领十二周。钱不多，每周够买面包和煤。Anton在表格上按了手印，Helene Kral 2把一张薄薄的领取券推过来，说下周同一时间来，别迟到。
  _effects: cash 6 (补助金窗口的第二张表); flag +work13_unemployment_benefit_approved; knowledge “the benefit runs for twelve weeks and must be collected in p”; relation acquaintance with person-materialized-world-vienna-1912-12_

- **1933.04** `vienna-tenement-neighbors.lost_room_key/key_lost_landlady` tags: home, social, family, housing
  Anton把钥匙丢在了回家路上的有轨电车轨道旁，天黑也没找着。房东太太说换锁芯要一克朗五十赫勒，从下月房租里扣。Emil Weiss借给Anton一把旧挂锁，但锁鼻和门扣对不上，只能用铁丝拧着凑合。那夜Anton睡不踏实，总觉得有人在拧门。
  _effects: cash -1.5 (换锁芯费用（其中1.5克朗记入债务）); relation acquaintance with person-emil-weiss; mood -0.05999999999999994_

- **1934.01** `p23-wedding-dress::ctx-vienna-authoritarian-1934::milieu-tenement/she-wears-it-to-another-wedding` tags: love, desire, obsession, stalker, wedding, fear, high_drama, vienna
  Anton把裙子穿去了Luise Kral表妹的婚礼。席间有人问是谁裁的，Anton说是旧货店；话没说完，Hermann Kral从邻桌站起来，说领口的针脚是自己缝的。音乐还在响，Luise Kral的叉子停在盘子上，整个大厅忽然安静得能听见冰化开的声音。
  _effects: state_add public-scandal state:world-vienna-1912:703; state_add known-affair state:world-vienna-1912:704; relation rival with person-hermann-kral; reputation -0.04; mood -0.03999999999999998; flag +dress_worn; flag +known_affair_active_

- **1934.01** `vienna-family-fatherhood/child-born-at-home` tags: family, childbirth, death, lifecycle, birth, injury, state, state:raising-child
  Luise Kral在Wohnhaus Victor Adler, Gumpendorfer Straße 54 / Blümelgasse 1生下一个孩子，取名Elisabeth Bauer。Anton在厨房和走廊之间来回，接生的人让烧水、拿布、付六克朗；孩子天亮后睡着了，Anton从此在户籍表上多了“父亲”一栏，手背上还留着烫水壶的红印。
  _effects: birth female; health -0.060000000000000005 (childbirth); cash -6 (midwife fee（其中6克朗记入债务）); role; state_add raising-child state:world-vienna-1912:705_

- **1934.01** `p23-bigamist-collision::ctx-vienna-authoritarian-1934::milieu-tenement/the-two-wives-meet-at-the-station` tags: love, bigamy, double_life, collision, black_comedy, warmth, high_drama, vienna
  Luise Kral和Emil Weiss在车站的失物招领处遇上，手里各拿着一张写着同一地址的纸条。Anton赶到时，两个人已经坐在长椅上聊了很久，连Anton小时候怕打雷的事都说了。Berta Winter在旁边等着做笔录，最后只问了一句：地址到底是谁的。
  _effects: state_add public-scandal state:world-vienna-1912:706; state_add known-affair state:world-vienna-1912:707; relation_end partner with person-luise-kral; relation rival with person-emil-weiss; mood -0.08000000000000002; flag +two_lives_collided; relation acquaintance with person-materialized-world-vienna-1912-30; flag -known_affair_active; flag +known_affair_active_

- **1934.01** `state_tick:ld18-impossible-attachment/invented-anniversary/the-date-that-is-not-on-paper` tags: ld18-impossible-attachment, love, intimacy, queer, unregistered, commitment, secret, desire
  Anton和伴侣把认识的那一天当作纪念日。没有登记，没有证人，只有一块从旧货摊买来的蛋糕和两杯兑了水的酒；Anton在日历上画了一个别人看不懂的记号，第二年又画在同一个格子里。
  _effects: state_parameter ld18-impossible-attachment.devotion 53 -> 57; state_parameter ld18-impossible-attachment.pressure 53 -> 50; mood 0.020000000000000018_

- **1934.01** `state_tick:p23-dangerous-secret/the-secret-changes-hands/the-secret-spills` tags: p23-dangerous-secret, secret, danger, leverage, exposure, loyalty, crime, politics
  秘密最终从一扇没关严的窗户里漏了出去。Anton听见楼下有人复述细节，连那晚桌上的酒瓶位置都说对了。当天下午，两个穿大衣的人来敲门，其中一个手里拿着Anton三年前写的一张收据。
  _effects: state_parameter p23-dangerous-secret.exposure 38 -> 52; state_parameter p23-dangerous-secret.leverage 71 -> 62; state_parameter p23-dangerous-secret.loyalty 39 -> 29; state_parameter p23-dangerous-secret.bodies 1 -> 2; mood -0.040000000000000036_

- **1934.01** `r13-civ.enter-hospital::ctx-vienna-authoritarian-1934::milieu-tenement/admitted-third-class` tags: health, hospital, admission, deposit, institution, vienna, urban, crisis
  Elisabeth Moser把押金收据和住院单钉在一起，让Anton去三楼东侧等床。走廊里全是折叠床，Elisabeth Kral 2给Anton一条洗得发硬的毯子，说今晚先别喝水。Anton把鞋塞在枕头下面，听着隔壁的呻吟睡了过去。
  _effects: cash -8 (住院处窗口的押金（其中8克朗记入债务）); health 0.06 (住院处窗口的押金); condition; flag +hospitalized_third_class; relation acquaintance with person-materialized-world-vienna-1912-46; relation acquaintance with person-materialized-world-vienna-1912-6; relation acquaintance with person-materialized-world-vienna-1912-46_

- **1934.02** `state_reaction:ld18-impossible-attachment/a-question-at-the-office` tags: ld18-impossible-attachment, love, intimacy, queer, unregistered, commitment, secret, desire
  办公室有人问Anton，为什么总有一个女人在楼下等她。Anton说那是表妹；对方笑了一下，说现在的表妹真体贴。下午，Anton把桌上的相框扣倒，又在没人时翻回来，用袖子擦了擦玻璃上的灰。
  _effects: state_parameter ld18-impossible-attachment.pressure 60 -> 68; state_parameter ld18-impossible-attachment.discretion 42 -> 38; reputation -0.02_
  _causes: evt:world-vienna-1912:007967_

- **1934.03** `ml18-lottery.same-numbers::ctx-vienna-authoritarian-1934::milieu-tenement/the-newsagent-kept-the-ticket` tags: lottery, luck, money, family, regret, windfall, vienna, urban
  开奖后，Luise Kral从柜台下面把票拿出来，说这张是用赊账买的，按老规矩中奖一人一半。Anton在店里站到天黑，最后拿了三十五克朗；Luise Kral把另一半锁进铁盒，顺手把柜台上的欠账本翻到Anton那一页，划掉了最后一笔。回家路上，Josef Huber说这比不中强，Anton没有说话。
  _effects: cash 35 (the subject's half of the disputed lottery win); state_add ml18-sudden-fortune state:world-vienna-1912:810; relation rival with person-luise-kral; flag +ticket_held_on_credit; knowledge “烟草铺老板把赊账买的彩票扣在柜台下面，直到开奖才拿出来。”; mood 0.010000000000000009; flag +sudden_fortune; reputation 0.020000000000000004; mood 0.030000000000000027_

- **1934.06** `ml18-business.bottle-cap-patent::ctx-vienna-authoritarian-1934::milieu-tenement/the-patent-is-contested` tags: business, patent, money, windfall, invention, comic, vienna, urban
  许可合同签下两周，另一家瓶盖厂拿出一张更早的图纸，说Anton的专利只是把软木垫换了个方向。Gustav Kral把两份图纸并排看了很久，最后让双方先停售。Anton付了十二克朗的律师费，Die Fackel把订单转给了出价更低的那家。
  _effects: cash -12 (legal fees for the patent dispute); state_add ml18-reversal state:world-vienna-1912:811; flag +patent_disputed; knowledge “另一家厂拿出的图纸日期比我的专利申请早两个月。”; mood -0.040000000000000036; relation acquaintance with person-materialized-world-vienna-1912-78; flag +fortune_reversed; mood -0.010000000000000009_

- **1934.06** `state_reaction:ml18-reversal/a-second-chance-arrives` tags: ml18-reversal, money, luck, reversal, risk, debt, reputation, loss
  一封没有署名的信里夹着一张彩票，号码是Anton上个月随口说过的。信纸是廉价的学生用纸，字迹却写得很慢。Anton把彩票翻过来，背面只写着一句：这次别再押上房租。
  _effects: state_parameter ml18-reversal.swing -20 -> -10; state_parameter ml18-reversal.strain 35 -> 30; mood 0.020000000000000018; flag +reversal_second_chance_

  Luise Kral在办事处说了Anton一整个下午的坏话，从欠债说到那场没人看见的火灾。旁边的人没有替Anton说一句话。几天后，Anton发现自己的名字从互助会的名单上被划掉了，Luise Kral只说“我也要养家”。
  _effects: reputation -0.07999999999999999; relation rival with person-luise-kral; cash -1 (互助会停止发放的补贴（其中1克朗记入债务）); condition; flag +reputation_damaged_by_betrayal_

  亲戚的婚礼上，Anton被安排和伴侣分开坐。司仪问有没有人反对，Anton低头喝汤；伴侣在另一桌替别人倒酒，手很稳，酒没有洒出来。散席时，两个人在衣帽间碰见，只说了一句“外面下雨”。

  两个人约定再过一个冬天，等Elisabeth Bauer的弟弟找到工作就分开。那个冬天特别长，煤贵，汤稀；她们还是每周洗一次头发，还是为谁忘了买盐吵架。春天来的时候，Elisabeth Bauer的箱子已经收好了，谁也没有先提。
  _effects: state_parameter ld18-impossible-attachment.pressure 68 -> 78; state_parameter ld18-impossible-attachment.devotion 57 -> 52; mood -0.040000000000000036; flag +one_last_winter_

  Anton的名字从闲谈里慢慢退了下去。街上有了新的婚礼、新的葬礼和新的丑闻；只有裁缝还记得Anton改过两次同一件外套的腰身。

  半年里没有新的坏消息。Anton把零钱分成三份：房租、还账、留一点在抽屉里。旧债主没有上门，咖啡馆里的人也不再每见一次就提那件赔本的事；日子没有变好，但至少不再往下滑。
  _effects: state_parameter ml18-reversal.swing -10 -> -4; state_parameter ml18-reversal.strain 30 -> 26; cash 1 (small economies and an odd job); mood 0.010000000000000009_

  Anton把面粉撒在桌上抹平，用指头写了一个“M”，让Josefine Hofer照着画。Josefine Hofer的手指粗得像胡萝卜，第一笔就戳穿了面层；Anton又抹平一次，这次握着老人的手腕慢慢走。写到自己的名字时，Josefine Hofer忽然笑了，说原来这几个弯是这个意思。
  _effects: flag +taught_parent_to_write_name; role; skill; relation kin with person-josefine-hofer; skill_

  Josefine Hofer不肯躺着，第三天就去帮人洗衣挣回一克朗。Anton晚上发现她的腿又肿了，那点工钱抵不上药钱；炉边的药布还没干，又得重新煮。
  _effects: health -0.01 (过早劳作导致腿伤加重); cash 1 (母亲洗衣挣的零钱); mood -0.050000000000000044_

  诊室在二楼，楼梯间飘着石炭酸味。医生把钳子举到灯下看了看，叫Anton攥紧扶手。Rosa Weiss站在门边数墙上的价目表：拔牙两克朗五十赫勒，药棉五十赫勒，连消炎水另算。
  _effects: cash -3 (牙医拔牙费用); health 0.05999999999999999 (拔除病牙解除疼痛); mood -0.030000000000000027; relation acquaintance with person-materialized-world-vienna-1912-31_

  一封没有署名的情书被报社买下，连载了整整一个月。全城都在猜收信人是谁；Anton每天买两份报纸，一份剪下来，一份留着不拆。Josef Huber在第七期之后走进编辑部，说再写下去，家里就要先读到结局了。报纸销量涨了三倍，两个人却开始从后门进出。

- **1936.01** `p9-social-daily-culture.last-standing-ticket/p9-social-daily-culture.last-standing-ticket.buy-for-elder` tags: daily, culture, leisure, cafe, music, walk, task, task:culture
  前面的老人掏零钱时手抖得厉害，数了三遍也没凑够。Anton替老人把四十赫勒递进窗口，老人连声道谢，硬塞回一枚十赫勒铜板。等Anton自己买票时，窗口已经挂出“售罄”的牌子，只好站在剧院门廊下听里面隐约的乐声，脚底冰凉；这一趟净少三十赫勒，却让队伍里的人都记住了这份体面。
  _effects: cash -0.4 (替排队的老人垫付站票钱); cash 0.1 (老人硬塞回的铜板); reputation 0.04000000000000001; mood -0.030000000000000027; relation acquaintance with person-materialized-world-vienna-1912-74_

- **1936.02** `pl19-bridge.first-ally::ctx-vienna-authoritarian-1934::milieu-tenement/the-patron-adds-a-name` tags: fame, ally, public, politics, scandal, media, bridge, vienna
  一位资助人把Marie Huber的名字写进下一场筹款会的节目单，排在Anton前面。Marie Huber没有拒绝，只在彩排时问了一句“稿子是谁写的”；资助人说是宣传班子写的，Marie Huber就把稿子折起来，换成了自己的话。那天晚上，捐款比预期少，但Marie Huber在门口站了很久，和每个离开的人握手。
  _effects: state_add pl19-public-ally state:world-vienna-1912:894; state_parameter fame.level 0.55 -> 0.5800000000000001; relation friend with person-marie-huber; reputation 0.010000000000000009; flag +patron_public_ally; knowledge “筹款会的稿子是宣传班子写的，盟友把它换成了自己的话。”; relation acquaintance with person-materialized-world-vienna-1912-35; flag +public_ally_active_

- **1936.02** `ad20-landlord-visit::ctx-vienna-authoritarian-1934::milieu-tenement/repairs-promised` tags: housing, rent, landlord, everyday, repair, public, vienna, urban
  Hermann Moser带着木尺来了，先敲窗框，又敲暖气片。Anton把去年冬天裂开的缝指给他看；Hermann Moser说可以修，但材料钱要从下个月房租里扣。Anton算了算，还是点了头。窗外的雨顺着玻璃往下走，屋里第一次没有风。
  _effects: cash -1 (materials for the window repair); relation acquaintance with person-materialized-world-vienna-1912-17; flag +repair_promised; mood 0.020000000000000018_

  另一桩丑闻在报纸上炸开时，Anton的名字被顺带写进了同一段。盟友在电话里沉默了很久，最后只问：“这次需要我站在哪一边？”

- **1936.08** `bc18-ghost.death-certificate::ctx-vienna-authoritarian-1934::milieu-tenement/collect-the-benefit` tags: bureaucracy, legal, death, identity, mistake, paperwork, absurd, vienna
  邻居出主意说，死者家属可以领一笔丧葬补助。Anton让Friedrich Huber冒充远房表姐，自己在旁边按了手印；钱到手后，两人在厨房里分成三份，第三份留着买煤。第二个星期，Katharina Bauer 2寄来一封信，说抚恤金已经核准，请家属来拍一张遗像；Anton把信压在盐罐下面，盐受潮结了块。
  _effects: cash 4 (冒领的丧葬补助分成); state_add bc18-bureaucratic-ghost state:world-vienna-1912:983; relation acquaintance with person-materialized-world-vienna-1912-23; condition; flag +collected_own_funeral_benefit; mood 0.010000000000000009; relation acquaintance with person-materialized-world-vienna-1912-23; flag +bureaucratic_ghost; mood -0.010000000000000009_

  Anton刚在一个窗口证明自己活着，另一个窗口又从抽屉里抽出一张死亡通知。两个办事员隔着走廊互相看了一眼，谁也没有先开口；Anton站在中间，忽然明白自己不是一个人，而是一份正在被转抄的卷宗。
  _effects: state_parameter bc18-bureaucratic-ghost.risk 40 -> 52; state_parameter bc18-bureaucratic-ghost.duplicates 2 -> 3; state_parameter bc18-bureaucratic-ghost.sightings 2 -> 3; flag +another_file_opened; mood -0.020000000000000018_

  Anton在受伤后的第三天去了诊所。医生清洗伤口、换药，收下诊费，嘱咐不要急着回机器旁。Anton把药方折好，回家路上买了纱布。
  _effects: health 0.05 (wound treatment); cash -8 (clinic and bandages)_
  _causes: evt:world-vienna-1912:009037_

  真正的守边人从战俘营回来时，报纸正在印Anton的第二次专访。那人缺了两根手指，站在报社门口，说自己只守了一天半。Maria Kral把Anton叫进办公室，桌上的奖章还没有收进盒子；窗外，记者们已经围住了那个缺手指的人。
  _effects: state_add public-scandal state:world-vienna-1912:979; state_add under-investigation state:world-vienna-1912:980; reputation -0.06; state_add fame state:world-vienna-1912:981; mood -0.06000000000000005; flag +real_hero_returned_

  报纸卖完的第二天，Grete Hofer 2把Anton领进洗衣房后面的小间。桌上已经坐着门房、一位排字工和两个不肯说名字的人；他们没有问绯闻是真是假，只问Anton以后还打不打算出门。Anton说打算，Grete Hofer 2就把一盏灯往桌中间推了推。
  _effects: state_add pl19-protected-scandal state:world-vienna-1912:982; relation friend with person-materialized-world-vienna-1912-69; reputation -0.01999999999999999; flag +rumor_network; knowledge “洗衣房后间的人没有问绯闻真假，只问当事人还出不出门。”; flag +protected_scandal_active_

  办公室有人问Anton，为什么总有一个女人在楼下等她。Anton说那是表妹；对方笑了一下，说现在的表妹真体贴。下午，Anton把桌上的相框扣倒，又在没人时翻回来，用袖子擦了擦玻璃上的灰。

  Anton和伴侣把认识的那一天当作纪念日。没有登记，没有证人，只有一块从旧货摊买来的蛋糕和两杯兑了水的酒；Anton在日历上画了一个别人看不懂的记号，第二年又画在同一个格子里。
  _effects: state_parameter ld18-impossible-attachment.devotion 60 -> 64; state_parameter ld18-impossible-attachment.pressure 88 -> 85; mood 0.020000000000000018_

  _effects: state_parameter p23-dangerous-secret.exposure 63 -> 71; state_parameter p23-dangerous-secret.leverage 54 -> 50; state_parameter p23-dangerous-secret.loyalty 25 -> 19; mood -0.020000000000000018_

  _effects: state_parameter pl19-public-ally.loyalty 50 -> 54; state_parameter pl19-public-ally.visibility 69 -> 72; state_parameter pl19-public-ally.cost 35 -> 33; mood 0.020000000000000018_

  一个税务员把Anton的死亡记录和一张新房租收据订在一起，寄到了另一个区。三个星期后，Anton收到一封要求“已故纳税人”补缴暖气税的信；去问时，窗口说档案正在路上，让Anton先等通知。通知没有来，房租却涨了。
  _effects: state_parameter bc18-bureaucratic-ghost.fileDepth 35 -> 42; state_parameter bc18-bureaucratic-ghost.risk 52 -> 62; cash -1 (跑窗口的车钱); flag +heating_tax_for_the_dead; mood -0.029999999999999805_

  Anton把积了灰的卷帘门推上去，用粉笔在木牌上写了自己的姓。Friedrich Stein收下押金，提醒Anton下个月初交租；Klara在对街的铺子门口看着，把同样的货摆得更靠外。第一天卖出去三包盐、两把纽扣，Anton把它们记在账本第一页。
  _effects: cash -15 (卷帘门上的第一块木牌); reputation 0.04999999999999999; flag +work13_opened_a_small_shop; role; employment Kaufmann @ org-allgemeines-krankenhaus; relation acquaintance with person-materialized-world-vienna-1912-90_

  _effects: cash 1 (看热闹的人塞来的酒钱); reputation 0.020000000000000018; state_add bc18-ridiculous-fame state:world-vienna-1912:1066; state_parameter bc18-bureaucratic-ghost.risk 62 -> 74; state_parameter bc18-bureaucratic-ghost.sightings 3 -> 4; relation acquaintance with person-materialized-world-vienna-1912-74; condition; flag +attended_own_wake; mood 0.010000000000000009; relation acquaintance with person-materialized-world-vienna-1912-81; flag +ridiculously_famous; reputation 0.020000000000000018; mood 0.020000000000000018_

  药房在街口，柜台玻璃下面压着价目纸。Anton把两克朗推过去，伙计找回来十二赫勒，药片用报纸包成一个小包。工友接过去时手还在抖，报纸边角很快被汗洇软。
  _effects: cash -2 (从互助金垫付药费); health 0.05999999999999999 (及时买到药); relation acquaintance with person-alois-lechner; relation acquaintance with person-materialized-world-vienna-1912-89_

  _effects: unemployment (laid off in the depression); cash -5 (lost wages); condition_

- **1939.01** `vienna-life-anschluss-1938/resists` tags: politics, persecution, 1938
  Anton在登记桌旁说了句“这里以前不是这样”。Helene Kral 2记下地址，当晚两个穿制服的人来敲门，把Anton带到警局，第二天放回来时脸上有伤。
  _effects: condition; role; cash -5 (fine and lost work)_

- **1939.01** `ml18-tax.audit-of-the-windfall::ctx-vienna-authoritarian-1934::milieu-tenement/pay-and-regularize` tags: tax, legal, money, windfall, bureaucracy, comic, vienna, urban
  Helene Kral 2把黑皮本翻到Anton那一页，说邮局储蓄的利息没有报，数目不大，但漏了就是漏了。Hermann Winter在旁边把计算尺推来推去，最后算出一个八克朗的数目。Anton当天去缴了款，把收据贴在账本最后一页；回家的路上，账本第一次没有在口袋里折着。
  _effects: cash -8 (back taxes and a small fine on the windfall's interest); state_parameter ml18-sudden-fortune.principal 20 -> 12; reputation 0.020000000000000018; relation acquaintance with person-materialized-world-vienna-1912-12; flag +taxes_paid; knowledge “邮局储蓄的利息也要报税，漏报的八克朗已经补齐。”; mood 0.020000000000000018_

  _effects: state_parameter pl19-protected-scandal.leverage 23 -> 35; state_parameter pl19-protected-scandal.protection 53 -> 49; state_parameter pl19-protected-scandal.exposure 60 -> 65; mood -0.020000000000000018_

  _effects: cash -20 (延长住宿与伙食); food; mood 0.06000000000000005; relation acquaintance with person-emil-weiss; education higher_

  _effects: death (暴力); flag -public_ally_active; flag -protected_scandal_active; flag -scandal_web_active_

### 19 subject events

- **1912.02** `vienna-life-birth-letter-from-the-dead/the-letter-is-a-bill` tags: infancy, family, secret, letter, drama
  信里没有问候，只有一张旧账单：棺材、墓地、一趟从边境回来的车费。Friedrich Hofer把账单翻过来，背面用铅笔写着“别让孩子知道”。Hedwig伸手去抓纸角，Friedrich Hofer没有让。
  _effects: cash -4 (paid an old funeral debt); flag +family_debt_letter; mood -0.03_

- **1912.05** `p22-comic-name-register::ctx-vienna-1912::milieu-tenement/the-clerk-hears-a-sneeze` tags: infancy, family, bureaucracy, name, official, comic, tender, drama
  登记员把Hedwig打喷嚏的声音当成了名字，低头就写，笔尖还在舌头上蘸了蘸。Friedrich Hofer抱着孩子，没看见那行字；等到拿着纸回家，才发现孩子的名字里多了一个“嚏”。
  _effects: mood 0.04; knowledge “登记簿上孩子的名字被写成了喷嚏声。”; flag +registered_as_hatschi; state_add p22-family-legend state:world-vienna-1912:13_

- **1912.12** `p22-strange-cradle-secret::ctx-vienna-1912::milieu-tenement/the-neighbour-knows` tags: infancy, family, cradle, secret, inheritance, strange, dark, drama
  邻居说，这只摇篮是从另一户人家借来的，那家的孩子没活过第一个冬天。Friedrich Hofer想把它劈了烧火，斧子举起来又放下，最后靠在门后；那天夜里，孩子还是睡在里面。
  _effects: mood -0.04; relation acquaintance with person-friedrich-hofer; state_add p22-family-legend state:world-vienna-1912:12; flag +cradle_history_

- **1912.12** `state_reaction:p22-family-legend/someone-asks-again` tags: p22-family-legend, family_legend, family, story, secret, inheritance, promise, memory
  又有人问起Hedwig家的那件旧事。大人先笑了一下，才说“都是很早以前了”；问的人点点头，眼睛却往摇篮那边看，手指在杯沿上停了一会儿，谁也没有接话，窗外有风。
  _effects: state_parameter p22-family-legend.secrecy 70 -> 74; state_parameter p22-family-legend.warmth 25 -> 27; mood 0.01_
  _causes: evt:world-vienna-1912:000091_

- **1913.01** `vienna-life-infant-first-smile/smile-at-the-lamp` tags: infancy, family, first, warm
  Josefine Hofer把Hedwig抱到窗边，煤汽灯罩上有一圈晃动的光。孩子盯着看了很久，忽然笑出声，口水滴在Josefine Hofer的袖口上。屋里的人都停了一下，像是听见了什么了不起的消息。
  _effects: mood 0.04; health 0.010000000000000009 (a good day)_

- **1913.01** `state_tick:p22-family-legend/the-story-changes-hands/a-detail-goes-missing` tags: p22-family-legend, family_legend, family, story, secret, inheritance, promise, memory
  又过了一年，故事里最要紧的那件东西不见了：先是名字，后来是地点。Hedwig问起时，大人说“你记错了”；可柜子最底层那只空盒子，谁也没有扔，锁却换过了，钥匙也不见了。
  _effects: state_parameter p22-family-legend.secrecy 10 -> 14; state_parameter p22-family-legend.danger 5 -> 8; state_parameter p22-family-legend.warmth 65 -> 63; mood -0.01_

- **1913.01** `p22-strange-doctor-prediction::ctx-vienna-1912::milieu-tenement/the-doctor-is-optimistic` tags: infancy, family, doctor, illness, omen, strange, dark, drama
  医生说孩子只是瘦，开了一瓶味道很坏的补药，说“过了春天就好了”。Josefine Hofer把这句话记了很多年；每年春天，家里都要说一遍，像在核对一张旧账单，药瓶上还贴着手写的标签。
  _effects: mood 0.03; state_add p22-child-omen state:world-vienna-1912:39; flag +doctor_optimism_

- **1913.02** `vienna-life-toddler-wrong-cradle/the-swap-story-spreads` tags: infancy, family, rumour, identity, comic, drama
  三天后，整条楼道都在说这家的孩子“抱错了”。Josefine Hofer抱着Hedwig去敲Josef Bauer的门，Josef Bauer不开，只在门里说“我也是听来的”。Hedwig在母亲怀里睡着了，口水把她的领口洇出一小片深色。
  _effects: flag +cradle_swap_rumour; relation rival with person-josef-bauer; mood -0.05_

- **1914.01** `vienna-life-toddler-first-words/first-word-door` tags: infancy, family, first, comic
  门外有人上楼，Hedwig忽然指着门说：“爸爸。”Josefine Hofer看了看空门口，没有纠正。那天晚上，孩子把家里每个人的名字都叫了一遍，叫错两个，却叫得很认真。
  _effects: skill; mood 0.020000000000000004; flag +first_word_door_

  又过了一年，什么也没有发生。Hedwig照常吃饭、摔跤、把鞋穿反；那个说过预言的人搬走了。家里把红绳解下来，却没有扔，只收进针线盒最里面，红绳上落了一点灰。
  _effects: state_parameter p22-child-omen.strangeness 40 -> 38; state_parameter p22-child-omen.fear 20 -> 17; state_parameter p22-child-omen.protection 45 -> 47; mood 0.01_

- **1915.01** `vienna-life-child-family-legend/happy-legend` tags: childhood, family, secret, story
  Josefine Hofer讲起自己小时候在乡下追一只白鹅，追到河边，鹅没追到，倒捡回一篮鸭蛋。Hedwig笑得从凳子上滑下来，Josefine Hofer也跟着笑，说这个家不是只有欠账和葬礼。
  _effects: mood 0.039999999999999994; mood 0.020000000000000018_

  Hedwig在Leopoldstädter Zinshaus, gemeinsamer Gang连续腹泻了两天，嘴唇干裂，喂水也留不住。家里没有钱再请医生，天亮前孩子停止了呼吸。Anton在场。

### 339 subject events

- **1912.03** `r1-fam.schooling-decision::ctx-vienna-1912::milieu-tenement/school.apprenticeship` tags: family, education, apprenticeship, poverty, future, vienna, urban, peace
  Gustav Berg把学徒合同摊在桌上，说三年管饭，出师后一天两克朗。Grete Stein说Anna Bauer的算术是班里最好的，再读两年可以考师范。Josef Bauer把合同推回去，说家里等不起两年；Anna Bauer看着窗外，操场上有人在踢一只破球。
  _effects: cash 2 (学徒合同还是课本); flag +left_school_for_apprenticeship; role; relation colleague with person-gustav-berg; knowledge “老师认定这个学生能考上师范。”; education apprenticeship_

- **1912.06** `r3-teen.petty-theft-dare::ctx-vienna-1912::milieu-school/caught` tags: theft, dare, market, friendship, shame, vienna, urban, peace
  Anna Bauer的手刚碰到梨，Katharina Bauer就从摊布下面抓住了他的手腕。梨滚到地上，被路过的车轮压烂。Katharina Bauer没有喊警察，只让他把烂梨扫干净，再赔一枚铜板。
  _effects: cash -1 (水果摊上少了一只梨); reputation -0.1; condition; flag +caught_stealing_fruit; relation rival with person-katharina-bauer; relation acquaintance with person-materialized-world-vienna-1912-4_

- **1913.01** `major-education-scholarship-examination/full-scholarship` tags: education, scholarship, adolescence, major
  考试设在女子中学的二层教室。Grete Stein当着Anna Bauer的面拆开牛皮纸袋：一篇作文、两道算术、一段听写。Anna Bauer写到最后一个数字时，手心里全是墨。三周后学校寄来通知，学费全免，首年发三十克朗书籍费，余下按学期发；条件是期末平均分不得低于良。
  _effects: education secondary; cash 30 (annual scholarship grant); role; flag +lyceum_scholarship_

- **1913.01** `r13-edu.library-study::ctx-vienna-university-1912::milieu-university/copies-whole-chapter` tags: education, library, study, reading, poverty, university, culture, schooling
  Anna Bauer把借书证压在阅览室的台灯下，一页页抄书。Friedrich Hofer过来提醒闭馆，Anna Bauer求了十分钟，把最后一章抄完。回家路上，Anna Bauer把纸条贴在胸口，怕雪打湿，进门先烤干手才敢展开。第二天Anna Bauer把抄本交给老师，老师翻了两页，说字比课文好看。
  _effects: flag +library_card; skill; knowledge “the library holds an 1890s survey of housing conditions”_

- **1913.02** `yf18-firstlook.tram-glove::ctx-vienna-1912::milieu-tenement/keep-it-in-the-satchel` tags: first_love, love, tram, glove, youth, courtship, vienna, urban
  Anna Bauer把手套塞进书包最里层，回家后放在枕头下面。第二天上学，Anna Bauer每隔一会儿就摸一下书包带，像在确认一件不能给家里看见的东西；手套上还留着电车木座的气味。
  _effects: state_add yf18-first-awakening state:world-vienna-1912:1; flag +yf18_kept_glove; mood 0.02; relation acquaintance with person-materialized-world-vienna-1912-1; relation acquaintance with person-materialized-world-vienna-1912-2; flag +yf18_first_awakening_active_

- **1913.03** `r2-leisure.cinema-fire::ctx-vienna-1912::milieu-tenement/escaped-the-cinema-fire` tags: leisure, cinema, fire, panic, rescue, vienna, urban, peace
  灯亮起来时，过道里全是踩掉的鞋。Anna Bauer拉着Elisabeth Hofer往侧门跑，门一推开，冷空气扑在脸上。Josef Bauer还在里面拍门，Grete Stein提着水桶跑错方向。Anna Bauer把Elisabeth Hofer推到街上，回头看，银幕已经烧成一个白洞。
  _effects: health -0.050000000000000044 (放映室里的焦味); condition; flag +escaped_cinema_fire; relation friend with person-materialized-world-vienna-1912-5; knowledge “the side exit was the only door not blocked by seats”; relation acquaintance with person-materialized-world-vienna-1912-5_

- **1913.06** `r15-work.master-takes-on::ctx-vienna-1912::milieu-university/unpaid-trial` tags: work, craft, apprentice, trade, poverty, vienna, urban, peace
  Gustav Berg先说试三个月，不给工钱，只管一顿午饭。家里同意了，因为多一张嘴比多一双手更难。Anna Bauer每天最早到，最晚走，三个月后师傅才把契约拿出来。
  _effects: food; mood -0.04; state_add apprenticeship-contract state:world-vienna-1912:22; education apprenticeship_

- **1913.10** `r3-teen.caring-siblings::ctx-vienna-1912::milieu-school/keeps-house` tags: caregiving, siblings, poverty, school, welfare, vienna, urban, peace
  Anna Bauer学会了用一把面粉做四个人的糊糊，先把稠的盛给弟弟妹妹。Josef发烧时，她用湿布擦他的额头，一夜换了六次水。第二天上课，她把脸埋在课本后面，怕Grete Stein闻到她身上的煤烟味。
  _effects: mood -0.04000000000000001; condition; flag +kept_house_for_siblings; role; relation guardian with person-phase15-Josef-world-vienna-1912; education primary_

- **1913.11** `r3-teen.first-friend-group::ctx-vienna-1912::milieu-school/excluded` tags: friendship, roof, belonging, betrayal, class, vienna, urban, peace
  Friedrich Bauer说入伙要交二十个铜板买蜡烛，Anna Bauer掏遍口袋只有九个。他们让她在楼梯口等，等到天黑也没人下来。楼上传来笑声，像隔着一层厚被子。Anna Bauer把九个铜板在楼梯上排成一排，又一个个收进口袋。
  _effects: mood -0.11999999999999998; flag +excluded_from_friend_group; relation acquaintance with person-materialized-world-vienna-1912-4_

  _effects: state_remove yf18-first-awakening state:world-vienna-1912:1; flag -yf18_first_awakening_active_

  _effects: state_parameter apprenticeship-contract.progress 8 -> 28; skill; mood 0.01999999999999999_

- **1914.01** `p9-childhood-youth-aspiration.ask-a-mentor/asked-to-come-back-later` tags: adolescence, education, aspiration, school, books, club, exam, task
  Gustav Berg看了看Anna Bauer抄的字母表，说大小不一，让他回去用直尺画格子重抄一遍，下个月再来。少年把纸折好，在咖啡馆门口站了一会儿，走回印刷厂时绕到排字房窗外看了很久。
  _effects: mood -0.03; skill; flag +mentor_pending_retry; relation acquaintance with person-gustav-berg_

- **1914.02** `vienna-adolescence.brothers-torn-jacket/sew-it-yourself` tags: adolescence, school, work, family, social
  Anna Bauer在厨房找到一截粗线，颜色和外套差了一截。缝到一半，线打结，Anna Bauer用牙咬断，重新来。Katharina Bauer坐在对面，没问外套的事，只把汤碗推过来，说“先吃”。Anna Bauer把最后一针打完，汤面已经结了一层薄皮。
  _effects: skill; food_

- **1914.05** `vienna-youth-threshold.night-class/borrow-notes-at-home` tags: adolescence, school, work, family, transition
  学费凑不出来，Anna Bauer就向同屋的学徒借笔记。晚上把灶台边的桌子擦干净，用铅笔头把讲义抄在旧账单背面，Grete Stein允他每两周交一次抄本抵作练习。抄到第三页时灯芯烧短了，字迹开始发颤。
  _effects: skill; skill; mood 0.01999999999999999_

  Anna Bauer把公式用铅笔写在左手心，出门前在水龙头下搓了搓，字迹只剩浅浅灰印。考场上他凭记忆答题，算到第三题时手心出汗，灰印化开成一道黑线。他答完了，但不知道对不对。
  _effects: flag +exam_guilt; mood -0.03; skill_

- **1915.05** `yf18-runaway.bundle::ctx-klosterneuburg-rural-1912::milieu-tenement/hide-the-bundle` tags: runaway, escape, family, plan, night_train, rural, agriculture, small_town
  Anna Bauer把两件衬衫、一双袜子和小时候的洗礼证明卷进一块旧桌布，塞到床底下。Katharina Bauer在厨房里数硬币，数了三遍都没有抬头；Anna Bauer躺在黑暗里，听见夜车从远处经过，铁轨的声音一直传到枕头下面。
  _effects: state_add yf18-runaway-plan state:world-vienna-1912:49; flag +yf18_bundle_hidden; mood 0.01999999999999999; flag +yf18_runaway_plan_active_

  Otto Hofer把车票放在桌上，说家里给她找了活，走不了。Anna Bauer把两张票叠在一起，撕掉一张，另一张塞进内袋；Otto Hofer问Anna Bauer一个人怕不怕，Anna Bauer说不怕，出门时却把地址写给了她。
  _effects: state_parameter yf18-runaway-plan.resolve 55 -> 65; state_parameter yf18-runaway-plan.preparation 15 -> 20; state_parameter yf18-runaway-plan.suspicion 5 -> 10; relation friend with person-materialized-world-vienna-1912-13; flag +yf18_go_alone; mood -0.01999999999999999; relation acquaintance with person-materialized-world-vienna-1912-13_

  饭桌上又为房租吵起来。Anna Bauer把面包掰成很小的块，听见父亲说“这个家养不起闲人”；Anna Bauer没有回嘴，只在心里把格拉茨的地址又背了一遍。
  _effects: state_parameter yf18-runaway-plan.suspicion 10 -> 18; state_parameter yf18-runaway-plan.resolve 65 -> 63_

  Anna Bauer藏在糖罐后面的硬币少了一小叠。父亲说家里这个月煤钱不够，先拿去用了；Anna Bauer看着空出来的那圈灰，什么也没说。那天晚上，Anna Bauer第一次认真算了一笔账：从维也纳到格拉茨，到底要攒几个冬天。

  _effects: cash -0.1 (工人青年会月费); skill; relation friend with person-materialized-world-vienna-1912-16; flag +workers_youth_member; mood 0.04000000000000001; relation acquaintance with person-materialized-world-vienna-1912-16_

  Anna Bauer的「床底下的包袱」到期；相关安排结束。

  Gustav Berg说自己眼睛不行了，带不了徒弟，但从帆布袋里抽出一本旧字样本，让Anna Bauer拿回去临摹。书皮用麻线缝过三次，扉页上有一行褪色的签名。少年双手接过来，在咖啡馆门口鞠了个躬。
  _effects: skill; relation acquaintance with person-gustav-berg; flag +typesetting_specimen_book; cash -1 (咖啡馆里请老师傅喝一杯)_

- **1918.01** `major-work-first-employment/turned-away` tags: work, employment, unemployment, major
  Buchdruckerei Winter & Söhne的工头翻完工票，问Anna Bauer上一个东家是谁。Anna Bauer说厂子关了，工头又问有没有辞工证明；没有证明，名册就不能写。后面的人挤上来，Anna Bauer被推到台阶下面。中午的钟响了，Anna Bauer站在街对面，看新来的人把名字一个个写上去。
  _effects: cash -2 (carfare and waiting); reputation -0.03; flag +no_work_references; role; mood -0.07; relation acquaintance with person-materialized-world-vienna-1912-21; relation acquaintance with person-materialized-world-vienna-1912-21_

- **1918.01** `vienna-family-courtship/wait-for-a-better-year` tags: family, courtship, household, social, lifecycle
  Anna Bauer把话咽回去，只说等明年工钱稳一点再谈。Hermann Moser没有追问，两人把桌上的面包分完，各自去上工；出门时Anna Bauer回头看了一眼，Hermann Moser正把掉在桌上的面包屑拢进掌心，谁也没有把那个话头重新拾起来。
  _effects: relation acquaintance with person-materialized-world-vienna-1912-17; relation acquaintance with person-materialized-world-vienna-1912-17_

- **1918.01** `p9-social-daily-culture.singing-club/p9-social-daily-culture.singing-club.listen-only` tags: daily, culture, leisure, cafe, music, walk, task, task:social
  Anna Bauer坐在后厅角落的啤酒箱上听完整场排练，Hermann Kral中途走过来问要不要在名册上签字，Anna Bauer说再想想。散场时人们三三两两去前厅续杯，Anna Bauer把空杯子放回柜台，顺着酒馆后门的巷子走回家，嘴里还留着半段旋律；这一晚没花钱，也和Hermann Kral多说了两句话。
  _effects: mood 0.03; relation acquaintance with person-hermann-kral; relation acquaintance with person-materialized-world-vienna-1912-7_

- **1918.02** `vienna-tenement-neighbors.shared_stove_fire/fire_no_damage` tags: home, social, family, housing
  Anna Bauer刚把锅端下灶，火苗就蹿了起来，Ferdinand Winter一脚踩灭地上的油布，顺手抄起沙箱里的沙子盖住灶口。两人都没受伤，但Ferdinand Winter的鞋底烧穿一个洞，那是唯一一双出门的鞋。
  _effects: relation friend with person-ferdinand-winter; mood -0.05000000000000002_

- **1918.02** `r14-edu.student-rally::ctx-vienna-university-1912::milieu-university/stayed-in-the-room` tags: education, student_politics, protest, risk, university, culture, schooling, student
  Anna Bauer那天没有去广场，留在宿舍里改论文。傍晚听见同学回来，说警察抓了七个人，Emil Weiss 2也在里面。Anna Bauer把窗帘拉开一条缝，看见楼下停着一辆车。晚饭时，食堂里没有人说话，只有勺子碰盘子的声音。
  _effects: mood -0.03999999999999998; flag +avoided_student_rally_

- **1918.02** `vienna-women-housework.coal-stove/coal-stove.buy-coal` tags: gender, housework, home, labour, family
  煤店老板用铁铲往筐里添了两铲碎煤，煤屑沾在Anna Bauer的袖口。她拎回来时筐底漏出几粒黑渣，落在楼梯上。火柴在窗台晾了一会儿，终于擦出一道火星。
  _effects: cash -0.6 (购买一小筐碎煤); food_

- **1918.02** `vienna-medical-treatment.toothache/barber-pull` tags: health, medical, family, money
  Ferdinand Winter在门口替Anna Bauer捏着帽子，听见里面一声闷哼。弗朗茨把牙扔进搪瓷盘，说瞧这牙根都黑了。收钱时他多要了二十赫勒，算明矾水的钱，说下次别再拖成这样。
  _effects: cash -1.2 (理发师拔牙收费); health 0.03 (病牙被拔除但手法粗糙); mood -0.01999999999999999_

- **1918.06** `vienna-history-kraus-lecture/listen-from-a-pillar` tags: history, culture, karl_kraus, cafe_central, public_lecture, press
  后厅的椅子不够，Anna Bauer 站在柱子旁听Karl Kraus 读一篇关于报纸谎言的文章。Karl Kraus 读到一半停下来，指出台下某位记者上周写的句子，那人把帽子压低。散场后，Anna Bauer 在账单背面抄下一句，墨迹被咖啡杯底印开。
  _effects: knowledge “Karl Kraus 在咖啡馆公开朗读时，会逐句引用报纸原文并指出其中的修辞和谎言。”; relation acquaintance with person-karl-kraus; cash -2 (coffee and a seat at Café Central); mood 0.01999999999999999; skill_

- **1918.08** `p9-gap-adult-solitude.stopped-clock/stopped-clock.wire-fix` tags: adulthood, daily, solitude, ordinary, home, clock, time
  Anna Bauer把钟背板卸下来，发现一根发条断了。她从缝纫盒里找出一截细铁丝，把断头缠住，钟摆晃了几下又走了起来。走是走，每天慢一刻钟，她索性把指针往前拨了拨，凑合着看。
  _effects: skill; mood 0.04000000000000001_

- **1919.01** `vienna-tenement-neighbors.laundry_line_dispute/subject_yields_line` tags: home, social, family, housing
  Anna Bauer看Ferdinand Winter盆里那几件小衬衫，把自家床单卷起来抱回屋，说等下午再晾。Ferdinand Winter愣了一下，闷头把衣服挂好，收工回来时在Anna Bauer门口放了一小包用旧报纸裹的洗衣碱。Anna Bauer打开报纸，碱面里还掺着几粒粗盐。
  _effects: relation friend with person-ferdinand-winter; food_
  _causes: evt:world-vienna-1912:000793_

- **1919.01** `p10-family-tasks.find-missing-kin/find-missing-kin.toolbox` tags: adulthood, family, social, marriage, care, inheritance, task, missing_kin
  旅店老板把仓库门打开，工具箱靠在墙角，刨刃上还有木屑。老板说人走时没带，欠的房钱拿工具抵。Anna Bauer在旧货商那里把刨子和凿子卖了，换了三克朗，刚够一张回维也纳的车票。
  _effects: cash 3 (卖掉哥哥留下的工具换路费); mood -0.15999999999999998; health -0.03 (奔波找哥哥，路费和体力都耗尽); relation acquaintance with person-materialized-world-vienna-1912-28_

- **1919.02** `vienna-parish-aid.sick-call/sick-call.with-priest` tags: faith, parish, mutual_aid, poverty, family
  Anna Bauer觉得一个人去不合适，绕到教堂请了修女一同上楼。修女看过病人，留下药包和一小瓶圣水，说会从教区账上出钱请医生来看一趟。Karl Kraus在楼下等着，说这样妥当，病人家里没男人，多个见证人心里安稳。
  _effects: relation acquaintance with person-karl-kraus; mood 0.03999999999999998; flag +parish_sick_visitor_

- **1919.03** `p10-work-career.first-position/p10-work-career.first-position.daily` tags: adulthood, work, career, wages, injury, pension, employment, first_job
  旧东家扣着学徒证明不放手，厂里只肯按日雇Anna Bauer。Mitzi Stein帮他跟门房说了几句，他每天天亮进厂，干到汽笛响第二遍，日结一克朗二十赫勒。Leopoldstädter Volksschule说干满一个月再看名册上有没有位置。
  _effects: employment 机器厂日工 @ org-volksschule-leopoldstadt; flag +awaiting_workbook; mood -0.04999999999999999; relation acquaintance with person-materialized-world-vienna-1912-3; relation acquaintance with person-materialized-world-vienna-1912-9_

- **1919.07** `vienna-social-movement.recruitment/join-and-carry` tags: development, social_movement, organization, politics

  Anna Bauer把上次的卷子重做了一遍，错题抄在卡片上，坐电车时也拿出来看。补考那天，最后一道题跟上次几乎一样，Anna Bauer没有急着写，先把条件圈出来，才动笔，写完还剩十分钟。交卷时Anna Bauer把草稿纸折好带走。没有回头。

- **1920.06** `major-crisis.rent-increase/pay-the-increase` tags: crisis, housing, rent, eviction, family, poverty
  房东没有露面，只让看门人Gustav Huber把补款单塞进门缝：每月要多十四克朗，先交十八克朗（本月十四加四克朗手续费）才肯谈续租。Anna Bauer把准备买冬煤的钱数了两遍，还是把钱交了，收据上写的是“自愿捐助”。Gustav Huber低声说，不签的人已经有两家搬走了，楼下的煤价牌又换了数字。
  _effects: cash -18 (advance payment demanded with the rent increase); relation acquaintance with person-materialized-world-vienna-1912-15; flag +rent_increase_accepted; relation acquaintance with person-materialized-world-vienna-1912-15_

  汤喝到一半，Katharina Bauer放下勺子点了头，但说得很清楚：先攒出一套锅碗、一只挂钟，再把话往下说。Luise Kral在桌下用膝盖碰了碰Anna Bauer，Katharina Bauer把最后一块煮牛肉推过来，饭桌上的空气总算松了；这一顿让对方家里管了饭，自家灶上省下两克朗的吃食，也吃出一个带条件的准信。
  _effects: relation partner with person-luise-kral; food_

  _effects: cash -10 (自费印刷的账单); mood -0.12; flag +printer_absconded_with_printing_fee; knowledge “the printer left with the press and the advance payment”_

- **1926.01** `vienna-life-injury-recovery/clinic-treatment` tags: health, work, family
  Anna Bauer在受伤后的第三天去了诊所。医生清洗伤口、换药，收下诊费，嘱咐不要急着回机器旁。Anna Bauer把药方折好，回家路上买了纱布。
  _effects: health 0.04999999999999999 (wound treatment); cash -8 (clinic and bandages（其中8克朗记入债务）); condition_
  _causes: evt:world-vienna-1912:002313_

- **1926.01** `p23-lovers-ledger::ctx-vienna-reform-1930::milieu-tenement/the-ledger-burns-first` tags: love, desire, betrayal, ledger, secret, blackmail, high_drama, reform
  Emil Weiss 2比Anna Bauer早到一步。炉膛里只剩一截没烧透的蓝线，Anna Bauer从灰里拈出来，认出那是账本装订用的线。Emil Weiss 2说这样对大家都好；Anna Bauer没有回答，只把灰烬扫进一只信封，收进抽屉最深处。炉子里的火还没有完全灭。
  _effects: state_add p23-dangerous-secret state:world-vienna-1912:151; knowledge “partner burned the ledger”; mood -0.030000000000000027; flag +ledger_burned_

- **1926.01** `state_reaction:p23-dangerous-secret/a-witness-talks` tags: p23-dangerous-secret, secret, danger, leverage, exposure, loyalty, crime, politics
  审讯室里的灯亮了一夜。第二天，Anna Bauer在面包房门口听见有人低声复述自己的秘密，连停顿的位置都一模一样。
  _effects: state_parameter p23-dangerous-secret.exposure 20 -> 28; state_parameter p23-dangerous-secret.leverage 15 -> 11; state_parameter p23-dangerous-secret.loyalty 55 -> 49; mood -0.020000000000000018_
  _causes: evt:world-vienna-1912:002704_

- **1926.01** `vienna-family-childbirth/son-born` tags: family, childbirth, death, lifecycle, birth, injury, state, state:raising-child
  Anna Bauer在Leopoldstädter Zinshaus, gemeinsamer Gang生下一个男婴，取名Friedrich 2。工友凑的钱抵了一部分，家里还是从糖罐里数出六克朗给接生的人；Emil Weiss 2把消息带到工厂，回来时把唯一的摇篮重新刷了一遍，Anna Bauer躺在床上出了一身虚汗。
  _effects: birth male; health -0.08000000000000002 (childbirth); cash -6 (midwife fee（其中6克朗记入债务）); state_add raising-child state:world-vienna-1912:152_

- **1926.01** `state_reaction:oj18-odd-apprenticeship/the-thing-in-the-basement` tags: oj18-odd-apprenticeship, work, odd_job, apprenticeship, night, trust, secrecy, state
  夜班结束前，Anna Bauer听见地下室传来拖拽声。下去时，门开着，地上只有一道湿拖把的痕迹；第二天，所有人都说那间屋子锁了十年。
  _effects: state_parameter oj18-odd-apprenticeship.strangeKnowledge 10 -> 18; state_parameter oj18-odd-apprenticeship.trust 30 -> 27; mood -0.030000000000000027; knowledge “地下室那扇门后面有东西被搬动过，但巡查簿上没有记录。”_
  _causes: evt:world-vienna-1912:002706_

- **1926.01** `curse15-betrayal.signature::ctx-vienna-reform-1930::milieu-tenement/the-files-went-first` tags: betrayal, office, power, politics, curse, institution, reform, medical
  Ferdinand Hofer先把Anna Bauer抽屉里的往来信件搬空，再在会议上说Anna Bauer从来没有保存过记录。没有人能证明那些信存在过，除了Anna Bauer自己。散会后，有人让Anna Bauer回家休息几天，工资照发。Anna Bauer知道这不是照顾，是让Anna Bauer别再回来。
  _effects: reputation -0.06; relation rival with person-materialized-world-vienna-1912-34; knowledge “我经手的信件在会议前被人搬空了。”; condition; flag +records_removed_

- **1926.01** `state_reaction:known-affair/betrayal-in-public` tags: known-affair, love, scandal, secret, public, secret_intimacy, state, state_reaction
  背叛被讲出来以后，Anna Bauer的名字又上了一轮饭桌。有人替Anna Bauer不平，有人只想知道细节；Anna Bauer把杯子洗干净，放回柜子，决定这周不去市场。
  _effects: state_parameter known-affair.notoriety 55 -> 70; state_parameter known-affair.grief 0 -> 10_
  _causes: evt:world-vienna-1912:002709_

- **1926.01** `state_tick:known-affair/public-year/forgotten` tags: known-affair, love, scandal, secret, public, secret_intimacy, state, state_tick
  Anna Bauer的名字从闲谈里慢慢退了下去。街上有了新的婚礼、新的葬礼和新的丑闻；只有裁缝还记得Anna Bauer改过两次同一件外套的腰身。
  _effects: state_parameter known-affair.notoriety 70 -> 65; state_parameter known-affair.grief 10 -> 7; mood 0.020000000000000018_

- **1926.01** `state_tick:oj18-odd-apprenticeship/strange-year/learned-the-building` tags: oj18-odd-apprenticeship, work, odd_job, apprenticeship, night, trust, secrecy, state
  又过了一个冬天，Anna Bauer能从楼梯的响声判断是谁在值夜，也能从煤气灯的颜色判断哪一段管道该修。师傅把巡查路线改了三次，最后仍然照Anna Bauer画的那张走；两个人都没有提这件事。
  _effects: state_parameter oj18-odd-apprenticeship.trust 27 -> 37; state_parameter oj18-odd-apprenticeship.strangeKnowledge 18 -> 28; state_parameter oj18-odd-apprenticeship.nightsWorked 0 -> 24; cash 2 (night-shift wages); skill_

- **1926.01** `r13-civ.quarantine::ctx-vienna-reform-1930::milieu-tenement/broke-quarantine` tags: health, quarantine, epidemic, neighbor, confinement, reform, medical, schooling
  Anna Bauer等到夜里，从后院的柴门溜出去上工。车间里没有人问，机器照常转，只有换班的人往旁边让了半步。回家时白线还在，Anna Bauer跨过去，鞋底带回一层湿泥。
  _effects: cash 3 (门框上的石灰线); health -0.08 (门框上的石灰线); reputation -0.1; flag +broke_quarantine; knowledge “the workshop does not ask why a quarantined worker returned”_

- **1926.02** `state_reaction:raising-child/school-note` tags: raising-child, family, child, care, school, household, state, state_reaction
  老师托人带来一张纸条，说Friedrich 2这周主动擦了黑板。Anna Bauer把纸条压在桌玻璃下面；那张玻璃下面已经有三张纸条了。
  _effects: state_parameter raising-child.schoolProgress 20 -> 23; mood 0.020000000000000018_
  _causes: evt:world-vienna-1912:002947_

- **1926.10** `ld18-wedding.objection::ctx-vienna-reform-1930::milieu-tenement/the-bride-says-no` tags: love, wedding, marriage, queer, scandal, choice, secret_intimacy, reform
  婚期定在星期日上午，教堂里坐满了人。Elisabeth Weiss问到Anna Bauer是否愿意时，Anna Bauer看见Elisabeth Huber站在最后一排，手里攥着一只手套。Anna Bauer说“不愿意”，声音不大，但前排的人全听见了；Emil Weiss 2把戒指放回托盘，转身先走了出去。
  _effects: state_add ld18-impossible-attachment state:world-vienna-1912:194; state_add public-scandal state:world-vienna-1912:195; relation_end partner with person-materialized-world-vienna-1912-16; reputation -0.10000000000000002; cash -2 (the cancelled feast and the deposit（其中2克朗记入债务）); flag +refused_at_the_altar; relation acquaintance with person-materialized-world-vienna-1912-14; flag +impossible_attachment_active; flag -known_affair_active_

- **1926.10** `state_reaction:ld18-impossible-attachment/a-question-at-the-office` tags: ld18-impossible-attachment, love, intimacy, queer, unregistered, commitment, secret, desire
  办公室有人问Anna Bauer，为什么总有一个女人在楼下等她。Anna Bauer说那是表妹；对方笑了一下，说现在的表妹真体贴。下午，Anna Bauer把桌上的相框扣倒，又在没人时翻回来，用袖子擦了擦玻璃上的灰。
  _effects: state_parameter ld18-impossible-attachment.pressure 55 -> 63; state_parameter ld18-impossible-attachment.discretion 25 -> 21; reputation -0.01999999999999999_
  _causes: evt:world-vienna-1912:003136_

- **1926.11** `vienna-parish-aid.soup-kitchen/soup-kitchen.leave` tags: faith, parish, mutual_aid, poverty, family
  Anna Bauer看见熟人排在前面，帽檐压得低低的，便转身拐进旁边的小巷。Hermann Kral追出来问怎么不领了，Anna Bauer说人太多，改天再来。回到屋里，灶是冷的，早上剩的半个土豆还在盘子里。Anna Bauer把它切成片，就着水咽下去。
  _effects: mood -0.050000000000000044; relation acquaintance with person-hermann-kral; food_

- **1927.01** `vienna-family-childbirth/daughter-born` tags: family, childbirth, death, lifecycle, birth, state, state:raising-child, raising-child
  Anna Bauer在Leopoldstädter Zinshaus, gemeinsamer Gang生下一个女婴，取名Helene 2。接生的人收六克朗，Emil Weiss 2在走廊烧水，邻居借来一只摇篮；Anna Bauer起身时眼前发黑，三天后腰还像断了一样，孩子哭声响亮，把屋里的冷气都顶了回去。
  _effects: birth female; cash -6 (midwife fee（其中6克朗记入债务）); state_add raising-child state:world-vienna-1912:192_

- **1927.01** `office16-admin.quiet-ledger::ctx-vienna-reform-1930::milieu-tenement/the-ledger-was-cooked` tags: office, administration, budget, competence, quiet, power, reform, medical
  前任留下的账本太整齐了：每一笔都落在同一个日期，连墨水颜色都一样。Anna Bauer让Berta Winter抽了三页去核对，发现其中两页的收据号码连续，却来自两家不同的商号。Berta Winter合上本子，问要不要现在报上去。
  _effects: organization_funds; reputation -0.020000000000000004; mood -0.040000000000000036; state_add office-under-siege state:world-vienna-1912:193; flag +found_cooked_ledger; knowledge “前任账本里两页收据号码连续，却来自两家不同商号。”; relation acquaintance with person-materialized-world-vienna-1912-35; relation acquaintance with person-materialized-world-vienna-1912-30; flag +office_under_siege_

- **1927.01** `state_tick:p23-dangerous-secret/the-secret-changes-hands/a-second-person-learns` tags: p23-dangerous-secret, secret, danger, leverage, exposure, loyalty, crime, politics
  一个曾在场的人喝多了，把故事讲给了不该听的人。第二天，Anna Bauer在市场上看见两个陌生人站在菜摊旁边，什么也不买，只看着来往的人。Anna Bauer把篮子换到另一只手上，继续挑土豆。
  _effects: state_parameter p23-dangerous-secret.exposure 28 -> 36; state_parameter p23-dangerous-secret.leverage 11 -> 7; state_parameter p23-dangerous-secret.loyalty 49 -> 43; mood -0.020000000000000018_

  Anna Bauer把Friedrich 2的旧课本包上书皮，又在本子上签了名。Friedrich 2今年学会了自己系鞋带，也学会了在饭桌上说“不”；Anna Bauer听着，觉得屋子比去年更满。
  _effects: state_parameter raising-child.schoolProgress 23 -> 31; state_parameter raising-child.caregiverBurden 45 -> 42; mood 0.020000000000000018_

  房东要续租，必须出示结婚证或亲属关系证明。Anna Bauer说伴侣是表妹，房东却问为什么表妹的姓和籍贯都对不上。那天晚上，两个人把箱子重新打包，又把已经叠好的衣服一件件拿出来；谁也没有说下个月住哪里。
  _effects: state_parameter ld18-impossible-attachment.pressure 63 -> 75; state_parameter ld18-impossible-attachment.discretion 21 -> 13; reputation -0.03; mood -0.040000000000000036_
